## Response:
7/13/2026, 9:25:31 PM

Saya akan susun ini sebagai peta kemampuan praktis untuk microservice Java/Jersey, bukan sekadar daftar library. Saya juga akan pisahkan antara capability “AI/media” sebagai domain teknis dan concern produksi seperti privacy, latency, storage, audit, dan workflow orchestration.

Saya sudah cek sumber resmi untuk membedakan beberapa istilah yang sering tercampur: OCR/document extraction, face detection/recognition, Face ID/passkey, STT/transcription, TTS, dan media transcoding. Pembedaan ini penting karena arsitektur, risiko privasi, SLA, dan storage model-nya berbeda.

## Introduction

Yang Anda butuhkan adalah peta kemampuan untuk membangun **media/AI-capability microservice** di atas **Java 17+, Jersey/Jakarta REST, Maven, MyBatis, PostgreSQL, Docker/Kubernetes, Camunda, dan cloud/on-prem runtime**.

Kategori ini mencakup beberapa domain yang mirip tetapi sebenarnya berbeda:

- **OCR / document AI**: membaca teks dari image, PDF scan, dokumen semi-terstruktur, form, invoice, KTP, kontrak, dan hasil scan.
- **Face detection / face recognition / face verification**: mendeteksi wajah, membandingkan wajah, mencari kemiripan wajah, atau melakukan identity verification.
- **Face ID / biometric login**: sebaiknya dipahami sebagai **device-local authentication** atau **passkey/WebAuthn**, bukan backend menyimpan wajah user. Apple menjelaskan Face ID data diproteksi di Secure Enclave, dan FIDO passkeys menjaga biometric processing tetap di device; server hanya menerima bukti autentikasi berbasis kriptografi. ([Apple Support](https://support.apple.com/en-us/102381?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
- **Speech-to-text / transcription**: mengubah audio/video menjadi teks, baik batch maupun streaming real-time. AWS Transcribe dan Azure Speech sama-sama mendukung model batch dan real-time/streaming dalam dokumentasi resminya. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/streaming.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
- **Text-to-speech**: mengubah teks atau SSML menjadi audio. Google Cloud Text-to-Speech dan Amazon Polly menyediakan API untuk speech synthesis. ([Google Cloud Documentation](https://docs.cloud.google.com/text-to-speech/docs?utm_source=chatgpt.com))
- **Media processing**: upload, validasi MIME, transcoding, thumbnailing, waveform, metadata extraction, frame extraction, audio normalization, subtitle/caption generation. FFmpeg adalah fondasi umum karena dapat membaca banyak input, filter, transcode, dan output ke berbagai format. ([ffmpeg.org](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk Java/Jersey, pola sehatnya: **Jersey hanya menjadi API façade dan orchestration boundary**, sedangkan pekerjaan berat seperti OCR, transcription, transcoding, embedding generation, atau face matching dijalankan oleh worker async, job queue, Camunda workflow, atau service terpisah. Jakarta REST menyediakan API dasar untuk RESTful service, dan Jersey adalah framework production-grade untuk Jakarta REST/JAX-RS di Java. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Decomposition Map - Practical Categories / Domain / Skills

| No | Category / Skill Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Relevansi di Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **API Contract for Media AI Jobs** | Mendesain endpoint untuk submit job, cek status, ambil result, cancel job, retry job, callback/webhook. | Jersey resource sebaiknya tidak menunggu OCR/transcription selesai untuk file besar; return `jobId`, lalu proses async. |
| 2 | **Multipart Upload & Large File Handling** | `multipart/form-data`, chunked upload, streaming upload, pre-signed URL, limit ukuran file, timeout, backpressure. | Jangan load full file ke memory. Gunakan stream ke temp storage/object storage. Ini krusial untuk PDF besar, video, dan audio panjang. |
| 3 | **Media Validation & File Trust Boundary** | MIME sniffing, extension mismatch, magic number, checksum, antivirus scan, PDF bomb, decompression bomb. | Semua file upload harus dianggap untrusted. Validasi sebelum diproses oleh OCR/FFmpeg/OpenCV. |
| 4 | **Media Metadata Extraction** | Durasi audio/video, resolution, codec, frame rate, page count, orientation, EXIF, language hint. | Metadata menentukan pipeline: OCR image vs PDF, STT audio vs video, thumbnail generation, max duration policy. |
| 5 | **FFmpeg-Based Media Processing** | Transcode, extract audio, extract frame, normalize audio, trim, resize, generate thumbnail, subtitle burn-in. | FFmpeg adalah engine umum untuk membaca input, filter, dan transcode ke output format berbeda. ([ffmpeg.org](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 6 | **JavaCV / Native Binding Strategy** | Integrasi Java dengan OpenCV, FFmpeg, Tesseract lewat wrapper native. | JavaCV menyediakan wrapper/utilitas untuk OpenCV, FFmpeg, Tesseract, dan library vision/media lain di platform Java. ([GitHub](https://github.com/bytedeco/javacv?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 7 | **Image Preprocessing for OCR** | Resize, grayscale, thresholding, denoise, deskew, rotation correction, crop region, contrast enhancement. | Kualitas preprocessing sering menentukan akurasi OCR lebih besar daripada mengganti engine. |
| 8 | **Basic OCR** | Ekstraksi teks dari image/PDF scan, language pack, confidence score, bounding box. | Bisa pakai Tesseract/Tess4J untuk on-prem; Tess4J adalah Java JNA wrapper untuk Tesseract dan mendukung image/PDF formats. ([tess4j.sourceforge.net](https://tess4j.sourceforge.net/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 9 | **Cloud OCR / Document AI** | AWS Textract, Azure Vision/Document Intelligence, Google Vision/Document AI; pilih berdasarkan layout, form, table, handwriting, cost, residency. | AWS Textract menyediakan OCR untuk text/handwriting dan Analyze Document untuk forms, tables, queries; Azure OCR mendukung printed/handwritten text, location, confidence score, dan container deployment. ([Amazon Web Services, Inc.](https://aws.amazon.com/textract/pricing/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 10 | **Layout-Aware Extraction** | Page, block, paragraph, line, word, table, form key-value, reading order. | Jangan hanya simpan plain text; simpan structured JSON agar bisa audit, highlight, dan review manual. |
| 11 | **OCR Confidence & Human Review** | Confidence threshold, low-confidence field review, correction UI, audit correction, active learning feedback. | Cocok dipadukan dengan Camunda human task: OCR otomatis dulu, lalu operator review hanya field yang confidence rendah. |
| 12 | **Face Detection** | Deteksi lokasi wajah di image/video, landmark, pose, blur, occlusion, quality score. | Digunakan sebagai tahap awal sebelum face verification/recognition. OpenCV menyediakan computer vision library luas dan Java tutorial untuk face detection/tracking. ([OpenCV](https://opencv.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 13 | **Face Verification 1:1** | Membandingkan satu wajah dengan wajah referensi: “apakah orang ini sama?”. | Cocok untuk onboarding/KYC internal dengan consent eksplisit. AWS Rekognition `CompareFaces` membandingkan source image dengan target image dan menghasilkan similarity. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/rekognition/latest/dg/faces-comparefaces.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 14 | **Face Identification 1:N** | Mencari wajah terhadap koleksi wajah terdaftar. | Risiko lebih tinggi daripada 1:1 karena sifatnya mirip surveillance/search. Perlu policy, consent, audit, dan threshold ketat. |
| 15 | **Face ID / Passkey / WebAuthn Boundary** | Bedakan “login pakai biometrik di device” vs “backend melakukan face recognition”. | Untuk login modern, backend sebaiknya menerima WebAuthn/passkey assertion, bukan menerima template wajah. WebAuthn memakai credential public-key yang scoped ke relying party. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/webauthn-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 16 | **Liveness / Anti-Spoofing** | Deteksi foto, video replay, mask, deepfake, screen replay, challenge-response. | Wajib bila face verification dipakai untuk identity proofing. Jangan menganggap face match cukup untuk authentication. |
| 17 | **Biometric Privacy & Governance** | Consent, lawful basis, retention, purpose limitation, encryption, access control, deletion, audit trail. | Biometric termasuk data berisiko tinggi; GDPR mengatur biometric data dalam special category context, dan Indonesia memiliki regulasi PDP yang perlu dicek bersama legal/compliance. ([gdpr-info.eu](https://gdpr-info.eu/art-9-gdpr/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 18 | **Speech-to-Text / STT** | Audio format, sampling rate, language, punctuation, timestamps, confidence, profanity filtering, custom vocabulary. | STT bisa batch atau streaming; Azure Speech mendukung real-time dan batch transcription, Google Speech-to-Text menerima audio dan mengembalikan transcription. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/index-speech-to-text?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 19 | **Batch Transcription** | Proses audio/video panjang dari object storage, asynchronous job, polling/callback, result JSON. | Untuk meeting recording, call recording, interview, compliance archive. Pipeline: upload → extract audio → transcribe → index/search. |
| 20 | **Streaming Transcription** | WebSocket/gRPC bridge, audio chunks, partial result, final result, latency budget. | Jersey/JAX-RS biasa cocok untuk job API; untuk streaming real-time, pertimbangkan WebSocket atau service khusus. Amazon Transcribe streaming mengirim media real-time dan menerima transcript real-time. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/streaming.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 21 | **Speaker Diarization** | “Who spoke when”, speaker labels, channel separation, overlap handling. | Penting untuk call center, meeting summary, audit transcript. Simpan per segment: speaker, start time, end time, text, confidence. |
| 22 | **Text-to-Speech / TTS** | Voice selection, language, SSML, pronunciation, speed, pitch, output audio format. | Untuk notification voice, accessibility, IVR, generated audio. Google TTS menerima text/SSML dan menghasilkan natural speech audio. ([Google Cloud Documentation](https://docs.cloud.google.com/text-to-speech/docs?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 23 | **Subtitles / Captions** | SRT, VTT, timestamp alignment, speaker labels, multilingual subtitle. | Hasil STT dapat dikonversi menjadi subtitle untuk video processing pipeline. |
| 24 | **Provider Abstraction Layer** | Interface internal seperti `OcrProvider`, `SpeechToTextProvider`, `FaceVerificationProvider`, `TtsProvider`. | Hindari locking business logic ke AWS/Azure/Google/Tesseract. Provider bisa diganti via config/profile. |
| 25 | **On-Prem vs Cloud Decisioning** | Data residency, latency, cost, model quality, GPU availability, offline requirement. | Untuk data sensitif, on-prem Tesseract/OpenCV/ONNX bisa lebih defensible; cloud lebih cepat untuk managed capability. |
| 26 | **Model Inference in Java** | ONNX Runtime, model loading, tensor input/output, CPU/GPU provider, warmup, batching. | ONNX Runtime menyediakan Java binding untuk menjalankan inference ONNX di JVM. ([ONNX Runtime](https://onnxruntime.ai/docs/get-started/with-java.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 27 | **GPU / Accelerator Deployment** | GPU node pool, device plugin, resource request/limit, CUDA image, model warmup. | Kubernetes device plugin framework memungkinkan cluster expose GPU/NIC/FPGA ke kubelet; GPU scheduling didukung via device plugins. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/extend-kubernetes/compute-storage-net/device-plugins/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 28 | **Camunda Workflow Orchestration** | BPMN untuk upload → validate → process → review → approve/reject → notify. | Media AI cocok dimodelkan sebagai long-running process dengan retry, compensation, escalation, dan human review. |
| 29 | **Persistence with PostgreSQL + MyBatis** | Job state, media metadata, result JSONB, transcript segments, OCR fields, audit log, idempotency key. | MyBatis mendukung custom SQL, stored procedures, dan advanced mappings, cocok untuk schema yang eksplisit dan query-heavy. ([MyBatis](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| 30 | **Search & Indexing** | Full-text search, trigram, vector search, document metadata filter, time-range search. | OCR/transcript result sering perlu searchable: by text, speaker, document type, case ID, confidence, created date. |
| 31 | **Idempotency & Retry Safety** | Deduplicate upload/job, stable job key, retryable vs non-retryable failure, poison job handling. | Wajib karena OCR/STT/transcode bisa mahal; jangan memproses file yang sama berkali-kali tanpa kontrol. |
| 32 | **Observability** | Correlation ID, job duration, provider latency, queue lag, error class, confidence distribution, cost metric. | Ukur bukan hanya HTTP latency, tapi pipeline latency: ingest, normalize, process, review, result delivery. |
| 33 | **Security Hardening** | Encryption at rest/in transit, signed URLs, scoped access, temporary credentials, PII masking, rate limiting. | Media file sering berisi PII. Jangan expose raw object URL tanpa expiry dan authorization. |
| 34 | **Auditability & Explainability** | Store provider version, request hash, model version, confidence, bounding boxes, transcript timestamps. | Sangat penting untuk regulatory defensibility: hasil AI harus bisa ditelusuri ke input, engine, waktu, dan decision path. |
| 35 | **Cost & Quota Management** | Per-page OCR cost, per-minute transcription cost, storage cost, GPU cost, retry budget. | Terapkan quota per tenant/user/job type. File besar harus punya pre-check sebelum masuk pipeline mahal. |

## Mental Model Arsitektur yang Direkomendasikan

```text
Client
  │
  ▼
Jersey API
  │ submit job
  ▼
PostgreSQL job_state
  │
  ├── object storage: raw media
  │
  ▼
Camunda / Queue / Worker
  │
  ├── validate media
  ├── normalize media
  ├── choose provider
  ├── execute OCR/STT/TTS/Face/Media task
  ├── persist structured result
  ├── trigger human review if needed
  └── publish callback / notification
  │
  ▼
Result API / Search API / Admin API
```

**Invariant penting:** Jersey API tidak boleh menjadi tempat kerja CPU-heavy/long-running. Jersey cukup menjadi **control plane**: menerima request, membuat job, mengamankan akses, dan menyajikan hasil. Worker/media processor menjadi **execution plane**.

---

## Roadmap Alur Belajar yang Direkomendasikan

### Phase 1 - Foundation: Media Ingestion & Job Model

Mulai dari desain API:

```text
POST /media-jobs
GET  /media-jobs/{jobId}
GET  /media-jobs/{jobId}/result
POST /media-jobs/{jobId}/cancel
POST /media-jobs/{jobId}/retry
```

Fokus belajar:

- multipart upload vs object-storage handoff
- job state machine
- idempotency key
- file validation
- temp storage
- async processing
- PostgreSQL schema untuk job/result/audit

---

### Phase 2 - OCR & Document Processing

Pelajari:

- OCR raw text
- OCR layout-aware
- table/form extraction
- confidence score
- bounding box
- human review flow
- result JSON schema
- searchable OCR result

Target praktis:

```text
Upload scanned PDF
→ extract text
→ extract table/key-value
→ store result JSONB
→ expose result API
→ flag low-confidence fields
```

---

### Phase 3 - Speech & Transcription

Pelajari:

- audio extraction dari video
- batch transcription
- streaming transcription
- speaker diarization
- timestamp alignment
- subtitle generation
- custom vocabulary

Target praktis:

```text
Upload meeting recording
→ extract audio
→ transcribe
→ diarize speakers
→ generate transcript segments
→ generate SRT/VTT
→ enable text search
```

---

### Phase 4 - Media Processing

Pelajari:

- FFmpeg command model
- codec/container
- audio normalization
- thumbnail generation
- video frame extraction
- subtitle burn-in
- safe command execution

Target praktis:

```text
Upload video
→ validate
→ extract metadata
→ generate thumbnail
→ extract audio
→ transcribe
→ generate captions
```

---

### Phase 5 - Face / Vision Capability

Pelajari secara hati-hati:

- face detection
- face verification 1:1
- face identification 1:N
- liveness
- threshold tuning
- false match / false non-match
- consent and audit
- biometric retention policy

Target praktis yang relatif aman:

```text
Selfie + ID photo
→ detect face quality
→ compare 1:1
→ store decision metadata
→ route low-confidence case to manual review
```

Hindari langsung lompat ke 1:N face search karena governance dan risikonya lebih tinggi.

---

### Phase 6 - Production Hardening

Pelajari:

- provider abstraction
- retry strategy
- poison job
- cost limit
- quota
- GPU scheduling
- observability
- audit trail
- encryption
- data retention
- compliance review

Target praktis:

```text
Same OCR/STT API
→ can run via local provider or cloud provider
→ has audit logs
→ has retry safety
→ has cost metrics
→ has SLA dashboard
```

---

# Seri 1/20 - Mental Model & Architecture Overview  
## OCR, Face Recognition, Face ID, TTS, STT, Transcription, dan Media Processing di Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri pertama ini bertujuan membentuk **mental model arsitektur** sebelum masuk ke detail implementasi OCR, face recognition, speech-to-text, text-to-speech, dan media processing.

Dalam konteks stack Anda, **Jersey/Jakarta REST sebaiknya diposisikan sebagai API/control layer**, bukan tempat menjalankan proses berat seperti OCR, transcription, face matching, video transcoding, atau model inference. Jakarta REST menyediakan API dasar untuk membangun RESTful web services, sedangkan Jersey adalah framework Java production-grade untuk Jakarta REST/JAX-RS. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Prinsip utamanya:

```text
Jersey API = menerima request, validasi awal, membuat job, expose status/result.
Worker     = menjalankan OCR/STT/TTS/Face/FFmpeg/model inference.
Camunda    = orchestrate long-running workflow, retry, human review, escalation.
Postgres   = job state, metadata, result, audit, search index.
Storage    = raw media, normalized media, generated artifacts.
```

---

## 2. Big Picture Architecture

```text
Client / Frontend / External System
        │
        ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ Jersey / Jakarta REST API     │
│ - Upload request              │
│ - Submit processing job       │
│ - Check status                │
│ - Fetch result                │
│ - Cancel / retry              │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ PostgreSQL + MyBatis          │
│ - job table                   │
│ - job_event table             │
│ - media_asset table           │
│ - ocr_result / transcript     │
│ - audit trail                 │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ Object Storage                │
│ - raw uploaded file           │
│ - normalized image/audio      │
│ - thumbnails                  │
│ - transcript files            │
│ - generated audio/video       │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ Camunda / Queue / Worker      │
│ - validate media              │
│ - normalize media             │
│ - call provider               │
│ - persist result              │
│ - human review if needed      │
│ - publish callback            │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ AI / Media Providers          │
│ - Tesseract / OCR             │
│ - OpenCV / vision             │
│ - FFmpeg / media processing   │
│ - ONNX Runtime / local model  │
│ - AWS / Azure / Google APIs   │
└──────────────────────────────┘
```

FFmpeg cocok dipahami sebagai toolkit/layer untuk memproses audio, video, subtitles, dan metadata; Tesseract adalah OCR engine open-source; ONNX Runtime menyediakan Java binding untuk menjalankan model ONNX di JVM. ([GitHub](https://github.com/ffmpeg/ffmpeg?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Core Mental Model

### 3.1 Jangan desain sebagai “request-response biasa”

Untuk file kecil, request-response mungkin terlihat mudah:

```text
POST /ocr
→ upload file
→ proses OCR
→ return result
```

Tapi untuk produksi, ini cepat rusak karena:

| Masalah | Dampak |
|---|---|
| File besar | timeout HTTP, memory pressure |
| OCR/STT lambat | thread Jersey tertahan |
| Provider error | user harus retry manual |
| Cost tinggi | duplicate processing mahal |
| Hasil confidence rendah | perlu human review |
| Video/audio panjang | tidak cocok blocking API |
| Data sensitif | perlu audit dan retention |

Model yang lebih benar:

```text
POST /media-jobs
→ return jobId

GET /media-jobs/{jobId}
→ status

GET /media-jobs/{jobId}/result
→ result saat selesai
```

---

### 3.2 Pisahkan Control Plane dan Execution Plane

```text
Control Plane
├── Jersey Resource
├── Auth / permission
├── API contract
├── job creation
├── status API
└── result API

Execution Plane
├── worker OCR
├── worker STT
├── worker face verification
├── worker FFmpeg
├── worker TTS
└── model inference runtime
```

**Control plane** harus cepat, predictable, dan ringan.  
**Execution plane** boleh berat, asynchronous, retryable, dan horizontally scalable.

---

## 4. Decomposition Map Seri 1

| Area | Fungsi | Komponen Java/Jersey | Catatan Praktis |
|---|---|---|---|
| API Layer | Menerima request dari client | Jersey Resource, DTO, validation, exception mapper | Jangan lakukan OCR/transcode langsung di resource method |
| Upload Boundary | Menerima file/media | Multipart upload, pre-signed URL, stream handling | Untuk file besar, object storage handoff lebih aman |
| Job Management | Membuat dan melacak proses | `media_job`, `job_event`, status enum | Setiap proses harus punya `jobId` |
| Orchestration | Mengatur step panjang | Camunda BPMN / worker | Camunda job worker adalah service yang mengeksekusi task dalam process; Java client menyediakan worker yang polling job. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Persistence | Menyimpan metadata dan result | PostgreSQL + MyBatis | MyBatis cocok untuk SQL eksplisit dan mapped statement select/insert/update/delete. ([mybatis.org](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/java-api.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Storage | Menyimpan raw/generated media | S3, Azure Blob, MinIO, NFS | Database jangan dipakai untuk menyimpan file besar |
| AI Provider | Menjalankan OCR/STT/TTS/Face | Provider abstraction interface | Hindari hard-code ke satu vendor |
| Media Processor | Normalize/transcode/extract | FFmpeg, JavaCV, native process | Harus sandboxed dan dibatasi resource |
| Result Model | Menyimpan hasil structured | JSONB, relational table, full-text index | Simpan confidence, timestamp, bounding box, provider metadata |
| Audit | Traceability | audit table, correlation ID | Penting untuk data sensitif dan regulatory defensibility |
| Human Review | Validasi manual | Camunda user task / internal review UI | Diperlukan saat confidence rendah |
| Observability | Monitoring pipeline | metrics, logs, traces | Ukur job latency, queue lag, provider latency, error class |
| Governance | Privacy/security | encryption, consent, retention | Face/biometric perlu boundary lebih ketat |

---

## 5. Capability Families

### 5.1 OCR / Document Processing

```text
Input:
- image
- scanned PDF
- document photo
- form
- invoice
- ID card
- contract

Output:
- raw text
- lines / words
- bounding boxes
- table structure
- key-value fields
- confidence score
```

OCR tidak boleh dianggap hanya “ambil teks”. Untuk sistem enterprise, hasil OCR idealnya structured:

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_123",
  "documentType": "invoice",
  "pages": [
    {
      "pageNumber": 1,
      "text": "Invoice No ...",
      "fields": [
        {
          "name": "invoice_number",
          "value": "INV-2026-001",
          "confidence": 0.97
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

---

### 5.2 Speech-to-Text / Transcription

```text
Input:
- audio file
- video file
- live audio stream

Output:
- transcript
- segments
- speaker labels
- timestamps
- confidence
- subtitles
```

Model result yang baik:

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_456",
  "language": "id-ID",
  "segments": [
    {
      "speaker": "SPEAKER_1",
      "startMs": 1200,
      "endMs": 4600,
      "text": "Selamat pagi, kita mulai meeting.",
      "confidence": 0.91
    }
  ]
}
```

---

### 5.3 Text-to-Speech

```text
Input:
- plain text
- SSML
- language
- voice profile
- speed/pitch

Output:
- generated audio
- metadata
- duration
```

TTS biasanya lebih cocok sebagai async job juga, terutama jika:

- teks panjang
- perlu beberapa voice/language
- hasil perlu disimpan
- perlu audit/generated artifact

---

### 5.4 Face Detection / Face Verification / Face Recognition

Bedakan tiga hal ini:

| Capability | Pertanyaan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| Face Detection | “Ada wajah di gambar ini?” | Rendah-sedang |
| Face Verification 1:1 | “Apakah wajah A sama dengan wajah B?” | Tinggi |
| Face Recognition 1:N | “Orang ini siapa dari database wajah?” | Sangat tinggi |
| Face ID / Passkey | “User membuktikan identitas via device biometric?” | Backend tidak perlu menyimpan wajah |

OpenCV adalah library computer vision open-source yang umum digunakan untuk vision pipeline, sedangkan face verification/recognition di sistem enterprise harus dipisahkan dari login biometric device-local seperti Face ID/passkey. ([GitHub](https://github.com/opencv/opencv?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

### 5.5 Media Processing

```text
Common task:
- extract metadata
- generate thumbnail
- extract audio from video
- transcode video
- normalize audio
- cut/trim media
- generate subtitles
- burn subtitles
- redact/blur region
```

Contoh pipeline:

```text
Video upload
→ validate MIME and size
→ extract metadata
→ generate thumbnail
→ extract audio
→ run transcription
→ generate subtitle
→ persist result
```

---

## 6. Recommended Service Boundary

Untuk awal, jangan langsung pecah terlalu banyak microservice. Mulai dari boundary logis:

```text
media-api-service
├── Jersey REST API
├── auth/authorization
├── job creation
├── result API
└── admin API

media-worker-service
├── OCR worker
├── STT worker
├── TTS worker
├── FFmpeg worker
├── face worker
└── provider adapter

media-review-service
├── review queue
├── correction UI backend
├── reviewer assignment
└── audit decision
```

Kalau volume sudah tinggi, baru pecah lebih granular:

```text
ocr-worker
transcription-worker
vision-worker
media-transcode-worker
tts-worker
```

---

## 7. Job State Machine

Minimal state machine:

```text
SUBMITTED
  │
  ▼
VALIDATING
  │
  ├── invalid file ─────► REJECTED
  │
  ▼
STAGED
  │
  ▼
PROCESSING
  │
  ├── provider temporary error ─► RETRY_PENDING
  │
  ├── provider permanent error ─► FAILED
  │
  ▼
RESULT_READY
  │
  ├── low confidence ─► NEEDS_REVIEW
  │
  ▼
COMPLETED

Optional:
CANCEL_REQUESTED
CANCELLED
EXPIRED
ARCHIVED
```

Tabel status praktis:

| Status | Meaning | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `SUBMITTED` | Job dibuat | Simpan metadata dasar |
| `VALIDATING` | File dicek | MIME, size, checksum, malware |
| `STAGED` | File siap diproses | Sudah masuk object storage |
| `PROCESSING` | Worker sedang jalan | OCR/STT/TTS/Face/FFmpeg |
| `RETRY_PENDING` | Error sementara | Retry dengan backoff |
| `FAILED` | Error final | Simpan error class |
| `NEEDS_REVIEW` | Confidence rendah | Route ke human review |
| `COMPLETED` | Result final tersedia | Expose result API |
| `CANCELLED` | User/system cancel | Stop next step |
| `EXPIRED` | Retention habis | Hapus file sesuai policy |

---

## 8. API Shape yang Sehat

### Submit Job

```http
POST /media-jobs
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "type": "OCR",
  "source": {
    "storageKey": "uploads/raw/doc-123.pdf"
  },
  "options": {
    "language": "id",
    "extractTables": true,
    "requireHumanReviewBelowConfidence": 0.85
  },
  "idempotencyKey": "case-991-doc-123-ocr-v1"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_abc123",
  "status": "SUBMITTED"
}
```

---

### Check Status

```http
GET /media-jobs/job_abc123
```

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_abc123",
  "type": "OCR",
  "status": "PROCESSING",
  "progress": {
    "currentStep": "OCR_PAGE_EXTRACTION",
    "processedPages": 4,
    "totalPages": 10
  }
}
```

---

### Fetch Result

```http
GET /media-jobs/job_abc123/result
```

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_abc123",
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "resultType": "OCR_RESULT",
  "result": {
    "text": "...",
    "pages": []
  }
}
```

## 10. Provider Abstraction

Jangan buat business code langsung memanggil AWS/Azure/Google/Tesseract/FFmpeg. Buat interface internal:

```java
public interface OcrProvider {
    OcrResult extractText(OcrRequest request);
}

public interface SpeechToTextProvider {
    TranscriptResult transcribe(TranscriptionRequest request);
}

public interface TextToSpeechProvider {
    TtsResult synthesize(TtsRequest request);
}

public interface FaceVerificationProvider {
    FaceVerificationResult verify(FaceVerificationRequest request);
}

public interface MediaProcessor {
    MediaProcessingResult process(MediaProcessingRequest request);
}
```

Lalu implementasi:

```text
OcrProvider
├── TesseractOcrProvider
├── AwsTextractOcrProvider
├── AzureDocumentOcrProvider
└── GoogleDocumentOcrProvider

SpeechToTextProvider
├── AwsTranscribeProvider
├── AzureSpeechProvider
├── GoogleSpeechProvider
└── LocalWhisperProvider

MediaProcessor
├── FfmpegMediaProcessor
└── CloudMediaProcessor
```

Tujuannya:

| Benefit | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Vendor switch | Bisa pindah cloud/on-prem |
| Testability | Mock provider lebih mudah |
| Cost control | Pilih provider berdasarkan job type |
| Data residency | Sensitive data bisa on-prem |
| Failure isolation | Provider error tidak bocor ke API contract |

---

## 11. Failure Model

Dalam media/AI processing, error harus diklasifikasikan. Jangan hanya `500`.

| Error Class | Contoh | Retry? |
|---|---|---|
| `INVALID_MEDIA` | file corrupt, MIME mismatch | Tidak |
| `UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT` | codec tidak didukung | Tidak |
| `FILE_TOO_LARGE` | melebihi policy | Tidak |
| `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | vendor timeout | Ya |
| `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT` | quota habis | Ya, dengan backoff |
| `LOW_CONFIDENCE` | OCR/face/STT tidak yakin | Tidak otomatis; human review |
| `SECURITY_REJECTED` | malware / unsafe file | Tidak |
| `INTERNAL_PROCESSING_ERROR` | bug worker | Tergantung |
| `CANCELLED_BY_USER` | user cancel | Tidak |
| `RETENTION_EXPIRED` | file sudah dihapus | Tidak |

---

## 12. Anti-Pattern yang Harus Dihindari

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa buruk | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| OCR langsung di Jersey resource | Blocking, timeout, thread habis | Async job + worker |
| Simpan file besar di PostgreSQL | DB bloat, backup berat | Object storage |
| Tidak ada idempotency | Duplicate job mahal | Idempotency key |
| Result hanya plain text | Susah audit dan review | Structured JSON + confidence |
| Hard-code provider | Vendor lock-in | Provider abstraction |
| Face recognition tanpa consent | Risiko legal/compliance | Explicit consent + audit + retention |
| Semua error dianggap retryable | Cost meledak | Error classification |
| Tidak simpan provider/model version | Sulit audit | Persist provider metadata |
| Tidak ada retention policy | Privacy risk | TTL, archival, deletion workflow |
| Tidak ukur queue lag | SLA gelap | Metrics per pipeline stage |

---

## 13. Practical Checklist untuk Senior Engineer

Gunakan checklist ini saat review desain:

```text
API
[ ] Apakah upload dan processing dipisah?
[ ] Apakah API return jobId, bukan blocking result?
[ ] Apakah ada status, result, cancel, retry endpoint?
[ ] Apakah ada idempotency key?

Storage
[ ] Apakah raw file disimpan di object storage?
[ ] Apakah ada checksum?
[ ] Apakah ada retention policy?
[ ] Apakah URL file tidak permanen/public?

Processing
[ ] Apakah worker terpisah dari API?
[ ] Apakah CPU/GPU heavy task tidak jalan di request thread?
[ ] Apakah ada retry/backoff?
[ ] Apakah error diklasifikasikan?

Data
[ ] Apakah result structured, bukan hanya string?
[ ] Apakah confidence disimpan?
[ ] Apakah bounding box/timestamp disimpan?
[ ] Apakah provider/model version disimpan?

Governance
[ ] Apakah PII/biometric punya consent?
[ ] Apakah ada audit trail?
[ ] Apakah ada encryption?
[ ] Apakah ada deletion/expiry workflow?

Observability
[ ] Apakah job latency diukur?
[ ] Apakah provider latency diukur?
[ ] Apakah queue lag diukur?
[ ] Apakah cost per job bisa dihitung?
```

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 2/20**. Seri ini akan fokus ke upload boundary di Jersey: bagaimana menerima file tanpa membebani JVM/API thread, kapan pakai multipart, kapan pakai object-storage handoff, dan validasi apa saja yang wajib sebelum file masuk pipeline.

# Seri 2/20 - Media Upload API di Jersey  
## Multipart, Streaming, Pre-signed URL, Validation, File Trust Boundary, dan Object Storage Handoff

## 1. Introduction

Seri ini membahas **upload boundary** untuk microservice OCR / STT / TTS / face verification / media processing.

Masalah utama di domain ini bukan hanya “bagaimana menerima file”, tapi bagaimana menerima file secara aman, scalable, dan tidak membuat API layer menjadi bottleneck.

Untuk sistem Java Jersey/JAX-RS, ada dua pola utama:

```text
1. Direct upload via Jersey API
   Client → Jersey API → Object Storage → Job created

2. Object-storage handoff
   Client → Jersey API requests upload session
   Jersey API → returns pre-signed URL / SAS URL
   Client → uploads directly to object storage
   Client/API → creates processing job
```

Pola kedua biasanya lebih baik untuk file besar seperti PDF scan besar, audio panjang, video, atau batch media. AWS S3 mendukung upload dengan pre-signed URL agar pihak lain bisa upload tanpa memiliki credential AWS langsung; Azure Blob Storage juga menyediakan Java client untuk upload blob/file ke storage. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/PresignedUrlUploadObject.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model Upload Boundary

```text
Upload Boundary
│
├── 1. Accept request
│   ├── multipart upload
│   ├── binary stream upload
│   └── pre-signed object storage upload
│
├── 2. Validate request metadata
│   ├── tenant/user/case permission
│   ├── declared media type
│   ├── file name policy
│   ├── expected job type
│   └── size/duration/page constraints
│
├── 3. Validate file content
│   ├── MIME detection
│   ├── magic number sniffing
│   ├── checksum
│   ├── malware scan
│   ├── PDF/image/media sanity check
│   └── unsupported format rejection
│
├── 4. Store safely
│   ├── raw object storage
│   ├── temporary staging area
│   ├── encrypted bucket/container
│   └── no public permanent URL
│
├── 5. Create job
│   ├── media_job row
│   ├── media_asset row
│   ├── idempotency key
│   ├── status = SUBMITTED / STAGED
│   └── initial audit event
│
└── 6. Trigger pipeline
    ├── Camunda process
    ├── queue message
    ├── worker polling
    └── async provider execution
```

---

## 3. Upload Pattern Decision Matrix

| Pattern | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko / Catatan |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Multipart upload ke Jersey** | File kecil-menengah, simple form upload, internal tools | Mudah, single API call | Bisa membebani API pod jika file besar |
| **Raw binary stream ke Jersey** | Single file tanpa metadata kompleks | Lebih simple daripada multipart | Metadata tambahan harus via header/query/body terpisah |
| **Pre-signed URL / object-storage handoff** | Audio/video besar, batch PDF, mobile/web upload | API tidak menjadi data pipe besar | Perlu upload session, callback/finalize, expiry URL |
| **Chunked/resumable upload** | File sangat besar, koneksi tidak stabil | Lebih tahan gagal jaringan | Implementasi lebih kompleks |
| **Server-to-server pull** | File sudah ada di storage eksternal | API cukup menerima URI/reference | Perlu allowlist domain, SSRF protection |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text
Small file / internal admin tool  → multipart to Jersey
Large PDF / audio / video         → pre-signed URL
Untrusted external URL            → avoid direct pull unless heavily restricted
Batch ingestion                   → object storage manifest + async job
```

---

## 4. Diagram Arsitektur Upload

```text
Client
  │
  │ 1. Request upload session
  ▼
Jersey API
  │
  ├── authorize user / tenant / case
  ├── validate intended job type
  ├── create upload_session
  ├── create storage key
  └── return upload target
       │
       ▼
Object Storage
  │
  │ 2. Client uploads file directly
  ▼
Client
  │
  │ 3. Finalize upload / create job
  ▼
Jersey API
  │
  ├── verify object exists
  ├── detect MIME/content
  ├── compute/verify checksum
  ├── create media_asset
  ├── create media_job
  └── trigger Camunda/queue
       │
       ▼
Worker Pipeline
  ├── malware scan
  ├── metadata extraction
  ├── OCR/STT/FFmpeg/Face/TTS
  └── persist result
```

---

## 5. Jersey Multipart Support

Untuk Jersey, multipart biasanya memakai module `jersey-media-multipart`. Jersey menyediakan `MultiPartFeature` untuk mendaftarkan provider multipart, dan `FormDataMultiPart` memiliki dukungan khusus untuk `multipart/form-data`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/2.43/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/media/multipart/MultiPartFeature.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Maven dependency - konseptual

Versi harus disejajarkan dengan versi Jersey runtime Anda.

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
    <artifactId>jersey-media-multipart</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

### Register feature

```java
import org.glassfish.jersey.media.multipart.MultiPartFeature;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class ApiApplication extends ResourceConfig {
    public ApiApplication() {
        packages("com.company.media.api");
        register(MultiPartFeature.class);
    }
}
```

---

## 6. Direct Multipart Upload Endpoint

### API shape

```http
POST /media/uploads
Content-Type: multipart/form-data
```

Parts:

```text
file        = binary file
metadata    = JSON metadata
```

### Example Jersey resource

```java
@Path("/media/uploads")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class MediaUploadResource {

    private final MediaUploadService uploadService;

    public MediaUploadResource(MediaUploadService uploadService) {
        this.uploadService = uploadService;
    }

    @POST
    @Consumes(MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA)
    public Response upload(
            @FormDataParam("file") InputStream fileStream,
            @FormDataParam("file") FormDataContentDisposition fileMeta,
            @FormDataParam("metadata") String metadataJson,
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey
    ) {
        UploadRequest request = UploadRequest.builder()
                .originalFileName(fileMeta != null ? fileMeta.getFileName() : null)
                .metadataJson(metadataJson)
                .idempotencyKey(idempotencyKey)
                .contentStream(fileStream)
                .build();

        UploadResponse response = uploadService.acceptUpload(request);

        return Response.accepted(response).build();
    }
}
```

Catatan penting: jangan baca semua `InputStream` ke `byte[]` kecuali ukuran sangat kecil dan dibatasi ketat. Untuk media processing, stream langsung ke temporary file atau object storage.

---

## 7. Object Storage Handoff Pattern

### Step 1 - Create upload session

```http
POST /media/upload-sessions
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "fileName": "meeting-recording.mp4",
  "declaredContentType": "video/mp4",
  "sizeBytes": 524288000,
  "intendedJobType": "TRANSCRIPTION",
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "uploadSessionId": "upl_123",
  "storageKey": "tenant-a/raw/2026/07/upl_123/meeting-recording.mp4",
  "uploadUrl": "https://storage-provider/presigned-url",
  "expiresAt": "2026-07-13T15:30:00+07:00"
}
```

### Step 2 - Client uploads file directly

```text
Client → S3 / Azure Blob / MinIO
```

### Step 3 - Finalize upload and create processing job

```http
POST /media/upload-sessions/upl_123/finalize
```

```json
{
  "checksumSha256": "abc123...",
  "createJob": true,
  "jobOptions": {
    "language": "id-ID",
    "speakerDiarization": true
  }
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_789",
  "status": "SUBMITTED"
}
```

---

## 8. Kenapa Pre-signed URL Lebih Baik untuk File Besar

| Concern | Direct upload ke Jersey | Pre-signed upload |
|---|---|---|
| API bandwidth | Tinggi | Rendah |
| API pod memory risk | Lebih tinggi | Lebih rendah |
| Timeout risk | Lebih tinggi | Lebih rendah |
| Horizontal scaling | Lebih mahal | Lebih mudah |
| Upload retry | Perlu custom logic | Storage provider biasanya lebih siap |
| Large media | Kurang ideal | Lebih ideal |

Untuk S3, multipart upload memungkinkan satu object diunggah sebagai beberapa part yang dapat diunggah independen dan di-retry per part. Ini berguna untuk object besar atau koneksi tidak stabil. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/mpuoverview.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. File Trust Boundary

File upload adalah salah satu boundary paling berbahaya. OWASP menekankan bahwa unrestricted file upload dapat berdampak serius, mulai dari takeover, overload storage/database, serangan ke backend system, sampai client-side attack. OWASP juga menyarankan validasi ekstensi, content type, nama file, ukuran file, penyimpanan aman, permission, dan malware scanning. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Jangan percaya pada:

```text
- file extension
- Content-Type dari browser/client
- original filename
- metadata EXIF
- declared file size
- declared duration
- PDF page count dari client
- user-provided checksum tanpa verifikasi server-side
```

### Validasi minimal:

| Validasi | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Auth & authorization | User boleh upload untuk tenant/case ini atau tidak |
| File size limit | Cegah storage abuse dan processing cost abuse |
| Extension allowlist | Filter awal, bukan satu-satunya validasi |
| MIME detection | Cek jenis file aktual |
| Magic number sniffing | Deteksi mismatch extension/content |
| Checksum | Deduplication dan integrity |
| Malware scan | Cegah file berbahaya |
| Page/duration/resolution limit | Cegah processing cost meledak |
| File name normalization | Cegah path traversal dan injection |
| Storage isolation | Jangan simpan di web root / public path |
| Audit event | Traceability |

---

## 10. MIME Detection dengan Apache Tika

Apache Tika dapat melakukan content detection dengan kombinasi magic-based detection, filename hint, dan supplied content type. Magic-based detection dilakukan lebih dulu dari awal file, lalu detection dapat diperhalus dengan filename dan content type metadata. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/2.0.0/detection.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh konseptual:

```java
public final class MediaTypeDetector {

    private final Tika tika = new Tika();

    public DetectedMediaType detect(Path tempFile, String originalFileName) {
        try (InputStream in = Files.newInputStream(tempFile)) {
            String detected = tika.detect(in, originalFileName);

            return new DetectedMediaType(detected);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new MediaValidationException("Failed to detect media type", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Validation rule:

```text
declaredContentType = video/mp4
detectedContentType = application/pdf

→ reject as MIME_MISMATCH
```

---

## 11. Upload State Machine

```text
CREATED
  │
  ▼
WAITING_FOR_UPLOAD
  │
  ├── expired ─────────────► EXPIRED
  │
  ▼
UPLOADED
  │
  ▼
VALIDATING
  │
  ├── invalid content ─────► REJECTED
  │
  ├── malware detected ────► SECURITY_REJECTED
  │
  ▼
STAGED
  │
  ▼
JOB_CREATED
  │
  ▼
PROCESSING
```

Tabel status:

| Status | Meaning | Owner |
|---|---|---|
| `CREATED` | Upload session dibuat | API |
| `WAITING_FOR_UPLOAD` | Client belum upload file | Client/storage |
| `UPLOADED` | File sudah ada di storage | Storage/API |
| `VALIDATING` | Content sedang dicek | Worker/API |
| `REJECTED` | File tidak valid | API/worker |
| `SECURITY_REJECTED` | Malware/unsafe file | Security scanner |
| `STAGED` | File siap diproses | Worker |
| `JOB_CREATED` | Processing job sudah dibuat | API |
| `EXPIRED` | Upload session kadaluarsa | Scheduler |

---

## 12. PostgreSQL Schema Awal

### `media_upload_session`

```sql
CREATE TABLE media_upload_session (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id           UUID NOT NULL,
    case_id             VARCHAR(100),
    original_file_name  TEXT NOT NULL,
    declared_mime_type  TEXT,
    expected_size_bytes BIGINT,
    storage_provider    VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    bucket_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    object_key          TEXT NOT NULL,
    status              VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    intended_job_type   VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    expires_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    created_by          UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### `media_asset`

```sql
CREATE TABLE media_asset (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id           UUID NOT NULL,
    upload_session_id   UUID REFERENCES media_upload_session(id),
    storage_provider    VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    bucket_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    object_key          TEXT NOT NULL,
    original_file_name  TEXT NOT NULL,
    declared_mime_type  TEXT,
    detected_mime_type  TEXT,
    size_bytes          BIGINT NOT NULL,
    checksum_sha256     TEXT,
    width               INTEGER,
    height              INTEGER,
    duration_ms         BIGINT,
    page_count          INTEGER,
    validation_status   VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### `media_upload_event`

```sql
CREATE TABLE media_upload_event (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    upload_session_id   UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_upload_session(id),
    event_type          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    message             TEXT,
    metadata_json       JSONB,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

## 13. MyBatis Mapper Example

```java
public interface MediaUploadSessionMapper {

    void insert(MediaUploadSessionRecord record);

    Optional<MediaUploadSessionRecord> findById(UUID id);

    int updateStatus(
            @Param("id") UUID id,
            @Param("fromStatus") String fromStatus,
            @Param("toStatus") String toStatus
    );

    int markExpired(@Param("now") OffsetDateTime now);
}
```

```xml
<update id="updateStatus">
    UPDATE media_upload_session
    SET status = #{toStatus},
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE id = #{id}
      AND status = #{fromStatus}
</update>
```

Gunakan conditional update seperti ini supaya transisi state lebih aman terhadap race condition.

## 14. Validation Pipeline

```text
Uploaded file
  │
  ▼
1. Storage existence check
  │
  ▼
2. Size check
  │
  ▼
3. Extension allowlist
  │
  ▼
4. MIME / magic detection
  │
  ▼
5. Checksum calculation
  │
  ▼
6. Malware scan
  │
  ▼
7. Media sanity check
  │
  ├── image dimension
  ├── PDF page count
  ├── audio duration
  ├── video duration
  └── codec/container
  │
  ▼
8. Create media_asset
  │
  ▼
9. Create processing job
```

## 15. Practical Limits by Job Type

| Job Type | Typical Input | Hard Limits yang Perlu Ada |
|---|---|---|
| OCR image | JPG/PNG/TIFF | size, dimension, megapixel, supported MIME |
| OCR PDF | PDF scan | size, page count, encrypted PDF reject/handle |
| STT audio | WAV/MP3/M4A/FLAC | size, duration, sample rate |
| STT video | MP4/MOV/MKV | size, duration, audio stream existence |
| TTS | text/SSML | text length, language, voice, output format |
| Face verification | JPG/PNG | one face expected, min face size, blur score |
| Thumbnail generation | image/video | size, dimension, codec |
| Transcoding | video/audio | size, duration, codec, output profile |

```json
}
```

### Response

## 17. Idempotency Model

Untuk upload, idempotency perlu di dua tempat:

```text
Upload session idempotency
- mencegah user membuat banyak session untuk file/case/job yang sama

Job creation idempotency
- mencegah file yang sama diproses berkali-kali
```

Contoh idempotency key:

```text
tenant:{tenantId}:case:{caseId}:file:{sha256}:jobType:{OCR}:version:{v1}
```

Database constraint:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_media_job_idempotency
ON media_job (tenant_id, idempotency_key)
WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL;
```

---

## 18. Security Checklist

```text
Request/API
[ ] Authenticated user
[ ] Tenant/case authorization checked
[ ] Rate limit upload session creation
[ ] Rate limit finalize endpoint
[ ] Idempotency key supported

File name
[ ] Original filename never used as storage path directly
[ ] Normalize filename
[ ] Strip path separators
[ ] Store generated object key
[ ] Escape filename when displayed

Content
[ ] Extension allowlist
[ ] MIME detection
[ ] Magic number validation
[ ] Max size check
[ ] Max page/duration/resolution check
[ ] Malware scan
[ ] Reject unsupported encrypted/compressed formats if not handled

Storage
[ ] Private bucket/container
[ ] Encryption at rest
[ ] Short-lived upload URL
[ ] No permanent public URL
[ ] Retention policy
[ ] Raw and processed files separated

Processing
[ ] No heavy work in Jersey request thread
[ ] Worker has CPU/memory limit
[ ] FFmpeg/native tools sandboxed
[ ] Retry only safe failures
[ ] Audit every state change
```

---

## 19. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Risiko | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Upload file besar langsung ke Jersey | API pod jadi bottleneck | Pre-signed upload |
| Simpan original filename sebagai object key | Path traversal / collision / info leak | Generate object key |
| Percaya `Content-Type` dari client | MIME spoofing | Tika/magic detection |
| Simpan file di web root | Remote execution / public leak | Private object storage |
| Baca full file ke memory | OOM | Stream ke temp/object storage |
| Buat job sebelum file tervalidasi | Worker gagal belakangan | Upload → validate → job |
| Tidak ada expiry upload session | Orphaned session/object | TTL cleanup |
| Tidak ada malware scan | Security exposure | Scan sebelum processing |
| Semua format diterima | Attack surface besar | Allowlist per job type |

---

## 20. Minimal Production Flow

Untuk sistem Anda, flow yang paling sehat:

```text
1. Client minta upload session
2. API authorize tenant/case
3. API generate object key
4. API return pre-signed URL
5. Client upload file ke object storage
6. Client call finalize
7. API verify object exists
8. API run lightweight validation or enqueue validation worker
9. Worker detect MIME/checksum/malware/metadata
10. API/worker create media_asset
11. API/worker create media_job
12. Camunda/queue starts processing
13. Client polls job status or receives callback
```

---

## 21. Senior Engineer Review Questions

Gunakan pertanyaan ini saat review desain atau PR:

```text
[ ] Apakah file besar melewati API pod atau langsung ke object storage?
[ ] Apakah Jersey resource hanya melakukan control-plane work?
[ ] Apakah original filename tidak dipakai sebagai storage key?
[ ] Apakah MIME detection dilakukan berdasarkan content?
[ ] Apakah ada upload session expiry?
[ ] Apakah ada idempotency untuk upload dan job?
[ ] Apakah file tervalidasi sebelum masuk OCR/STT/FFmpeg/Face pipeline?
[ ] Apakah malware scan masuk ke flow?
[ ] Apakah storage private dan terenkripsi?
[ ] Apakah result/job bisa ditelusuri ke asset asli?
[ ] Apakah semua state transition tercatat di audit event?
[ ] Apakah ada cleanup orphaned upload/object?
```

## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:14:12 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 3/20**. Fokusnya sekarang adalah state machine pekerjaan media/AI: bagaimana job dibuat, diproses async, di-retry aman, dibatalkan, diklasifikasikan gagal, dan tetap audit-friendly.

# Seri 3/20 - Job State Machine  
## Async Processing, Idempotency, Retry, Cancellation, Poison Job, dan Failure Classification

## 1. Introduction

Dalam domain **OCR / STT / TTS / Face Verification / Media Processing**, pekerjaan utama hampir selalu bersifat:

```text id="rxh0gm"
long-running
resource-heavy
failure-prone
cost-sensitive
audit-sensitive
retryable only under certain conditions
```

Karena itu, model yang paling aman bukan:

```text id="x8vn71"
POST /ocr
→ tunggu sampai selesai
→ return result
```

Melainkan:

```text id="65i0ns"
POST /media-jobs
→ create job
→ return jobId

GET /media-jobs/{jobId}
→ check status

GET /media-jobs/{jobId}/result
→ fetch result when ready
```

Untuk pipeline yang memakai **Camunda**, konsep ini natural karena job worker mengambil pekerjaan, menyelesaikan job, atau menandai gagal dengan retry yang tersisa. Dokumentasi Camunda 8 menjelaskan bahwa worker dapat mengirim fail command dengan jumlah remaining retries, dan job yang tidak selesai dalam timeout bisa tersedia lagi untuk worker lain. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Core Mental Model

```text id="w3niyy"
Media job = durable intent to process media

Bukan:
- thread Java yang sedang berjalan
- request HTTP aktif
- temporary object semata

Tapi:
- state di database
- referensi ke input asset
- processing options
- audit trail
- retry policy
- result pointer
- ownership / tenant boundary
```

Dengan mental model ini, job harus tetap bisa dilacak walaupun:

| Failure | Sistem tetap harus bisa |
|---|---|
| API pod restart | job tidak hilang |
| Worker crash | job bisa diambil ulang |
| Provider timeout | job bisa retry aman |
| User submit ulang | duplicate dicegah |
| Camunda incident | state tetap bisa diaudit |
| File corrupt | job gagal dengan alasan jelas |
| Result confidence rendah | masuk human review |

---

## 3. Diagram State Machine

```text id="njzhxm"
                       ┌──────────────┐
                       │  SUBMITTED   │
                       └──────┬───────┘
                              │
                              ▼
                       ┌──────────────┐
                       │  VALIDATING  │
                       └──────┬───────┘
                              │
              ┌───────────────┼────────────────┐
              │               │                │
              ▼               ▼                ▼
        ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────────┐   ┌───────────────┐
        │ REJECTED │   │ SECURITY_    │   │   STAGED      │
        │          │   │ REJECTED     │   │               │
        └──────────┘   └──────────────┘   └──────┬────────┘
                                                  │
                                                  ▼
                                           ┌──────────────┐
                                           │  PROCESSING  │
                                           └──────┬───────┘
                                                  │
                 ┌────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┐
                 │                                │                             │
                 ▼                                ▼                             ▼
        ┌────────────────┐              ┌────────────────┐             ┌────────────┐
        │ RETRY_PENDING  │              │ NEEDS_REVIEW   │             │  FAILED    │
        └───────┬────────┘              └───────┬────────┘             └────────────┘
                │                               │
                ▼                               ▼
        ┌──────────────┐              ┌────────────────┐
        │  PROCESSING  │              │ REVIEWING      │
        └──────────────┘              └───────┬────────┘
                                              │
                                              ▼
                                      ┌────────────────┐
                                      │ RESULT_READY   │
                                      └───────┬────────┘
                                              │
                                              ▼
                                      ┌────────────────┐
                                      │  COMPLETED     │
                                      └────────────────┘

Optional terminal:
- CANCELLED
- EXPIRED
- ARCHIVED
```

---

## 4. State Table

| State | Meaning | Owner | Terminal? |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SUBMITTED` | Job dibuat, belum diproses | API | No |
| `VALIDATING` | File dan request sedang divalidasi | Worker/API | No |
| `REJECTED` | Input tidak valid | Validator | Yes |
| `SECURITY_REJECTED` | Malware/unsafe content/security policy fail | Security scanner | Yes |
| `STAGED` | Input sudah valid dan siap diproses | Worker | No |
| `PROCESSING` | OCR/STT/TTS/Face/FFmpeg sedang berjalan | Worker/Camunda | No |
| `RETRY_PENDING` | Gagal sementara, menunggu retry | Worker/Scheduler | No |
| `NEEDS_REVIEW` | Hasil butuh manual review | Camunda/Human task | No |
| `REVIEWING` | Sedang direview manusia | Review service | No |
| `RESULT_READY` | Result final sudah tersedia | Worker/Review service | No |
| `COMPLETED` | Job selesai secara final | System | Yes |
| `FAILED` | Gagal permanen | Worker/System | Yes |
| `CANCELLED` | Dibatalkan user/system | API/System | Yes |
| `EXPIRED` | Asset/result melewati retention | Scheduler | Yes |
| `ARCHIVED` | Result dipindahkan ke archive | Retention worker | Yes |

---

## 5. State Transition Rules

Jangan izinkan update status bebas. Pakai transisi eksplisit.

```text id="yz3q5b"
Allowed transitions:

SUBMITTED        → VALIDATING
VALIDATING       → REJECTED
VALIDATING       → SECURITY_REJECTED
VALIDATING       → STAGED
STAGED           → PROCESSING
PROCESSING       → RETRY_PENDING
RETRY_PENDING    → PROCESSING
PROCESSING       → NEEDS_REVIEW
NEEDS_REVIEW     → REVIEWING
REVIEWING        → RESULT_READY
PROCESSING       → RESULT_READY
RESULT_READY     → COMPLETED
PROCESSING       → FAILED
RETRY_PENDING    → FAILED

Any non-terminal state → CANCELLED
COMPLETED            → ARCHIVED
FAILED               → ARCHIVED
```

Contoh transition guard:

| Transition | Guard |
|---|---|
| `SUBMITTED → VALIDATING` | job belum diklaim worker |
| `VALIDATING → STAGED` | file exists, MIME valid, size valid, malware clear |
| `STAGED → PROCESSING` | worker lease/acquire berhasil |
| `PROCESSING → RETRY_PENDING` | error termasuk transient |
| `PROCESSING → FAILED` | retry habis atau error permanent |
| `PROCESSING → NEEDS_REVIEW` | confidence di bawah threshold |
| `RESULT_READY → COMPLETED` | result persisted dan audit event sukses |
| `* → CANCELLED` | belum terminal dan cancel policy mengizinkan |

---

## 6. Database Model

### `media_job`

```sql id="lb4j0w"
CREATE TABLE media_job (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id             UUID NOT NULL,
    case_id               VARCHAR(100),
    job_type              VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    status                VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    source_asset_id        UUID NOT NULL,
    result_asset_id        UUID,

    idempotency_key        TEXT,
    priority              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,

    attempt_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_attempts           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 3,
    next_attempt_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,

    locked_by              TEXT,
    locked_until           TIMESTAMPTZ,

    provider_name          VARCHAR(100),
    provider_operation_id  TEXT,
    provider_version       TEXT,

    error_class            VARCHAR(100),
    error_code             VARCHAR(100),
    error_message          TEXT,

    confidence_summary     NUMERIC(6, 5),
    processing_started_at  TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,

    created_by             UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Unique idempotency constraint

```sql id="uvhv9p"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_media_job_idempotency
ON media_job (tenant_id, idempotency_key)
WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL;
```

### `media_job_event`

```sql id="wdjb61"
CREATE TABLE media_job_event (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
    event_type      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    from_status     VARCHAR(50),
    to_status       VARCHAR(50),
    message         TEXT,
    metadata_json   JSONB,
    created_by      UUID,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### `media_job_failure`

```sql id="8dgbew"
CREATE TABLE media_job_failure (
    id                UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id            UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
    attempt_number    INTEGER NOT NULL,
    error_class       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    error_code        VARCHAR(100),
    error_message     TEXT,
    stack_trace_hash  TEXT,
    retryable         BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    provider_name     VARCHAR(100),
    provider_request_id TEXT,
    created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

---

## 7. PostgreSQL Worker Claim Pattern

Jika Anda memakai PostgreSQL sebagai queue ringan, gunakan row locking dengan hati-hati.

PostgreSQL mendukung `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`; dokumentasinya menjelaskan bahwa row yang tidak bisa langsung dikunci akan dilewati, dan pola ini cocok untuk beberapa consumer yang mengakses tabel seperti queue, walaupun tidak cocok untuk general-purpose consistent view. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh claim job:

```sql id="ohapnx"
WITH next_job AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM media_job
    WHERE status IN ('SUBMITTED', 'STAGED', 'RETRY_PENDING')
      AND (next_attempt_at IS NULL OR next_attempt_at <= now())
      AND (locked_until IS NULL OR locked_until < now())
    ORDER BY priority ASC, created_at ASC
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    LIMIT 1
)
UPDATE media_job j
SET status = 'PROCESSING',
    locked_by = :workerId,
    locked_until = now() + interval '10 minutes',
    attempt_count = attempt_count + 1,
    processing_started_at = COALESCE(processing_started_at, now()),
    updated_at = now()
FROM next_job
WHERE j.id = next_job.id
RETURNING j.*;
```

Pola ini cocok untuk worker sederhana. Jika flow sudah complex, gunakan Camunda sebagai orchestration layer dan database tetap menjadi source of truth untuk audit/result.

---

## 8. Optimistic State Transition dengan MyBatis

Gunakan conditional update agar transisi tidak saling tabrak.

```java id="eexw4y"
public interface MediaJobMapper {

    int transitionStatus(
            @Param("jobId") UUID jobId,
            @Param("fromStatus") String fromStatus,
            @Param("toStatus") String toStatus,
            @Param("updatedAt") OffsetDateTime updatedAt
    );

    Optional<MediaJobRecord> findById(@Param("jobId") UUID jobId);
}
```

```xml id="2v53zm"
<update id="transitionStatus">
    UPDATE media_job
    SET status = #{toStatus},
        updated_at = #{updatedAt}
    WHERE id = #{jobId}
      AND status = #{fromStatus}
</update>
```

Service pattern:

```java id="wjyvwh"
public void transition(UUID jobId, JobStatus from, JobStatus to) {
    int updated = mediaJobMapper.transitionStatus(
            jobId,
            from.name(),
            to.name(),
            OffsetDateTime.now()
    );

    if (updated != 1) {
        throw new InvalidJobTransitionException(jobId, from, to);
    }

    mediaJobEventMapper.insert(JobEvent.statusChanged(jobId, from, to));
}
```

---

## 9. Idempotency Model

Idempotency berarti request yang sama boleh dikirim ulang tanpa menciptakan efek samping ganda. Google Cloud menjelaskan operasi idempotent dapat dieksekusi berulang dan tetap menghasilkan final state yang sama; AWS juga menekankan idempotency key/token untuk membuat retry aman. ([Google Cloud Documentation](https://docs.cloud.google.com/storage/docs/retry-strategy?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk media AI pipeline, idempotency perlu di beberapa level:

```text id="5zjfhg"
1. Upload session idempotency
2. Media asset idempotency
3. Job creation idempotency
4. Provider request idempotency
5. Result persistence idempotency
6. Callback/webhook idempotency
```

### Idempotency key design

```text id="scnzdl"
tenant:{tenantId}
case:{caseId}
assetHash:{sha256}
jobType:{OCR}
optionsHash:{hash(options)}
pipelineVersion:{v1}
```

Contoh:

```text id="6u4sa1"
tenant:acme
case:CASE-2026-001
assetHash:93ac...
jobType:TRANSCRIPTION
optionsHash:82be...
pipelineVersion:v3
```

### API behavior

```text id="yut0oh"
First request:
POST /media-jobs with Idempotency-Key: abc
→ creates job_001
→ returns job_001

Retry same request:
POST /media-jobs with Idempotency-Key: abc
→ returns existing job_001

Same key but different payload:
POST /media-jobs with Idempotency-Key: abc
→ reject with IDEMPOTENCY_PAYLOAD_MISMATCH
```

AWS notes a common pattern: when an idempotency-enabled API returns a duplicate-request error on retry, that often means the first attempt already succeeded, so it should be handled as success rather than failure when appropriate. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/durable-execution/patterns/best-practices/idempotency/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Idempotency Table

Untuk audit lebih kuat, buat tabel khusus.

```sql id="mo3yq0"
CREATE TABLE idempotency_record (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id           UUID NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key     TEXT NOT NULL,
    request_hash        TEXT NOT NULL,
    resource_type       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    resource_id         UUID NOT NULL,
    response_json       JSONB,
    expires_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (tenant_id, idempotency_key)
);
```

Flow:

```text id="yiz01x"
Receive request
  │
  ▼
Check idempotency table
  │
  ├── not found
  │     ├── create job
  │     ├── store idempotency record
  │     └── return response
  │
  ├── found same request_hash
  │     └── return stored response / existing resource
  │
  └── found different request_hash
        └── reject
```

---

## 11. Retry Strategy

Retry tidak boleh asal. Harus dibedakan:

```text id="7mf2h4"
Transient failure   → retry
Permanent failure   → do not retry
Security failure    → do not retry
Business failure    → maybe human review
Unknown failure     → limited retry then incident
```

### Retryable examples

| Error | Retry? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Provider timeout | Ya | Bisa sementara |
| Provider rate limit | Ya | Tunggu backoff |
| Network error | Ya | Bisa sementara |
| Worker crash | Ya | Bisa diambil ulang |
| Temporary object storage failure | Ya | Infrastruktur sementara |
| Camunda job timeout | Ya | Worker mungkin mati |

### Non-retryable examples

| Error | Retry? | Alasan |
|---|---:|---|
| File corrupt | Tidak | Input rusak |
| Unsupported MIME | Tidak | Tidak akan berubah |
| Malware detected | Tidak | Security reject |
| PDF encrypted tanpa password | Tidak | Butuh input baru |
| No face detected | Tidak otomatis | Butuh user upload ulang |
| Multiple faces when one expected | Tidak otomatis | Butuh correction |
| Audio has no speech | Tidak otomatis | Result valid atau manual review |
| Payload exceeds policy | Tidak | Policy violation |

---

## 12. Exponential Backoff

Backoff membantu mencegah retry storm.

```text id="wphs7x"
attempt 1 → retry after 30 seconds
attempt 2 → retry after 2 minutes
attempt 3 → retry after 10 minutes
attempt 4 → retry after 30 minutes
attempt 5 → fail / incident
```

Contoh Java:

```java id="xdllxh"
public Duration computeBackoff(int attempt) {
    long seconds = switch (attempt) {
        case 1 -> 30;
        case 2 -> 120;
        case 3 -> 600;
        case 4 -> 1800;
        default -> 3600;
    };

    long jitter = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextLong(0, 15);
    return Duration.ofSeconds(seconds + jitter);
}
```

Update retry:

```sql id="pyfhml"
UPDATE media_job
SET status = 'RETRY_PENDING',
    next_attempt_at = now() + (:backoffSeconds || ' seconds')::interval,
    error_class = :errorClass,
    error_code = :errorCode,
    error_message = :errorMessage,
    locked_by = NULL,
    locked_until = NULL,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = :jobId
  AND status = 'PROCESSING';
```

---

## 13. Failure Classification

Failure harus menjadi domain model, bukan sekadar exception.

```java id="b3hf3z"
public enum FailureClass {
    INVALID_MEDIA,
    UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT,
    SECURITY_REJECTED,
    PROVIDER_TIMEOUT,
    PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT,
    PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE,
    LOW_CONFIDENCE,
    HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED,
    CANCELLED_BY_USER,
    RETENTION_EXPIRED,
    INTERNAL_PROCESSING_ERROR,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

Mapping:

| Failure Class | HTTP untuk status API | Job State | Retry |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `INVALID_MEDIA` | 422 | `REJECTED` | No |
| `UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT` | 415/422 | `REJECTED` | No |
| `SECURITY_REJECTED` | 422/403 | `SECURITY_REJECTED` | No |
| `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | 202 status pending | `RETRY_PENDING` | Yes |
| `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT` | 202 status pending | `RETRY_PENDING` | Yes |
| `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` | 202 status pending | `RETRY_PENDING` | Yes |
| `LOW_CONFIDENCE` | 200/202 | `NEEDS_REVIEW` | No |
| `CANCELLED_BY_USER` | 200 | `CANCELLED` | No |
| `INTERNAL_PROCESSING_ERROR` | 202/500 internal admin | `RETRY_PENDING` / `FAILED` | Limited |
| `UNKNOWN` | 202/500 internal admin | `RETRY_PENDING` / `FAILED` | Limited |

---

## 14. Poison Job

**Poison job** adalah job yang terus gagal walaupun sudah di-retry.

Contoh:

```text id="5cbq3j"
- file terlihat valid tapi membuat OCR engine crash
- video codec menyebabkan FFmpeg hang
- PDF tertentu memicu parser bug
- provider selalu return malformed response untuk input tertentu
- worker bug hanya muncul pada metadata tertentu
```

State handling:

```text id="sa0zg5"
PROCESSING
  │
  ├── fail attempt 1 → RETRY_PENDING
  ├── fail attempt 2 → RETRY_PENDING
  ├── fail attempt 3 → RETRY_PENDING
  └── fail attempt N → FAILED / POISONED
```

Tambahkan state opsional:

```text id="mpgozm"
POISONED
```

Atau gunakan:

```text id="mjwmk6"
FAILED with error_class = POISON_JOB
```

Praktisnya, saya lebih suka terminal state tetap `FAILED`, dengan detail:

```json id="npsbb2"
{
  "status": "FAILED",
  "errorClass": "POISON_JOB",
  "errorCode": "MAX_ATTEMPTS_EXCEEDED",
  "attemptCount": 5,
  "lastFailure": {
    "errorClass": "INTERNAL_PROCESSING_ERROR",
    "errorCode": "FFMPEG_EXIT_137"
  }
}
```

---

## 15. Cancellation Model

Cancellation tidak selalu bisa langsung menghentikan proses.

Bedakan:

| Mode | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `CANCEL_REQUESTED` | User/system meminta cancel |
| `CANCELLED` | Job benar-benar berhenti |
| `CANNOT_CANCEL` | Job sudah terminal atau provider tidak mendukung cancel |
| `CANCEL_AFTER_CURRENT_STEP` | Worker menyelesaikan step sekarang lalu berhenti |

State:

```text id="vo0qty"
SUBMITTED ──────────────► CANCELLED
STAGED ─────────────────► CANCELLED
RETRY_PENDING ──────────► CANCELLED
PROCESSING ─────────────► CANCEL_REQUESTED ───► CANCELLED
RESULT_READY ───────────► COMPLETED
COMPLETED ──────────────► cannot cancel
FAILED ─────────────────► cannot cancel
```

Cancellation endpoint:

```http id="p6hvsx"
POST /media-jobs/{jobId}/cancel
```

```json id="sgb86o"
{
  "reason": "User uploaded wrong document"
}
```

Response:

```json id="4y34yi"
{
  "jobId": "job_123",
  "status": "CANCEL_REQUESTED"
}
```

Worker harus check cancellation point:

```java id="kbmpas"
public void process(MediaJob job) {
    cancellationGuard.throwIfCancelRequested(job.id());

    validate(job);

    cancellationGuard.throwIfCancelRequested(job.id());

    normalize(job);

    cancellationGuard.throwIfCancelRequested(job.id());

    executeProvider(job);

    cancellationGuard.throwIfCancelRequested(job.id());

    persistResult(job);
}
```

---

## 16. Camunda Orchestration Model

Bila memakai Camunda, desain BPMN jangan terlalu detail sampai setiap internal function menjadi task. Buat task pada boundary yang benar:

```text id="5dkd7k"
Start
  │
  ▼
Validate Media
  │
  ├── invalid/security reject → End Rejected
  │
  ▼
Normalize Media
  │
  ▼
Execute AI/Media Provider
  │
  ├── transient error → retry
  ├── permanent error → End Failed
  ├── low confidence → Human Review
  │
  ▼
Persist Result
  │
  ▼
Publish Callback
  │
  ▼
End Completed
```

Camunda job worker model cocok untuk service task semacam `validate-media`, `normalize-media`, `execute-ocr`, `execute-transcription`, atau `persist-result`. Worker mengambil job berdasarkan type, mengunci job sementara, lalu complete/fail job sesuai hasil eksekusi. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 17. Camunda + Database Source of Truth

Ada dua pendekatan:

### Option A - Camunda as orchestration, DB as business source of truth

```text id="2t3bjq"
Camunda:
- process instance
- service task
- retries
- incidents
- human review flow

PostgreSQL:
- job status exposed to API
- media metadata
- result
- audit trail
- search
```

Ini paling cocok untuk stack Anda.

### Option B - PostgreSQL queue only

```text id="csjwsj"
PostgreSQL:
- job queue
- state transitions
- worker claim
- retry schedule

No Camunda:
- simpler
- good for straightforward pipelines
- weaker visual orchestration/human review
```

Rekomendasi:

```text id="wk4gk8"
Simple OCR/transcode batch     → PostgreSQL queue bisa cukup
Long-running regulated process → Camunda + PostgreSQL
Human review/escalation        → Camunda lebih cocok
```

## 19. Worker Processing Skeleton

```java id="lucobw"
public final class MediaJobWorker {

    private final MediaJobRepository jobRepository;
    private final MediaProcessorRegistry processorRegistry;
    private final FailureClassifier failureClassifier;
    private final RetryPolicy retryPolicy;

    public void execute(UUID jobId) {
        MediaJob job = jobRepository.getForProcessing(jobId);

        try {
            MediaJobProcessor processor = processorRegistry.resolve(job.jobType());

            ProcessingResult result = processor.process(job);

            if (result.requiresHumanReview()) {
                jobRepository.markNeedsReview(job.id(), result.confidenceSummary());
                return;
            }

            jobRepository.persistResult(job.id(), result);
            jobRepository.markCompleted(job.id());

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            ClassifiedFailure failure = failureClassifier.classify(ex);

            if (failure.retryable() && job.canRetry()) {
                Duration backoff = retryPolicy.nextBackoff(job.attemptCount());
                jobRepository.markRetryPending(job.id(), failure, backoff);
            } else {
                jobRepository.markFailed(job.id(), failure);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 20. Result Persistence Idempotency

Worker bisa crash setelah provider berhasil, tapi sebelum DB update selesai.

Contoh masalah:

```text id="i092tt"
1. Worker call OCR provider
2. OCR provider sukses
3. Worker simpan result sebagian
4. Worker crash sebelum mark COMPLETED
5. Retry jalan lagi
6. OCR provider dipanggil ulang
7. Cost double
```

Mitigasi:

```text id="b3thkn"
- simpan provider_operation_id
- simpan result dengan unique job_id
- persist result before final status
- check existing result before provider call if possible
- use idempotency token when provider supports it
- treat duplicate result insert as success if content matches
```

Schema:

```sql id="2qxbu8"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_ocr_result_job
ON ocr_result (job_id);
```

Code behavior:

```text id="3mjbml"
if result already exists for job:
    mark job RESULT_READY / COMPLETED
else:
    call provider and persist result
```

---

## 21. Webhook / Callback Idempotency

Jika setelah selesai sistem mengirim callback ke sistem lain:

```text id="9oh7pe"
Job completed
  │
  ▼
Send callback
  │
  ├── network timeout
  ├── receiver processed but response lost
  └── retry callback
```

Callback harus punya event ID.

```json id="ufehwh"
{
  "eventId": "evt_123",
  "eventType": "MEDIA_JOB_COMPLETED",
  "jobId": "job_123",
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T15:10:00+07:00"
}
```

Receiver harus bisa deduplicate berdasarkan `eventId`.

Minimal metrics:

| `media_job_created_total` | jumlah job dibuat |
| `media_job_completed_total` | jumlah job selesai |
| `media_job_failed_total` | jumlah job gagal |
| `media_job_retry_total` | jumlah retry |
| `media_job_duration_seconds` | end-to-end latency |
| `media_job_queue_lag_seconds` | waktu dari submitted ke processing |
| `media_provider_latency_seconds` | latency provider |
| `media_job_confidence_score` | confidence OCR/STT/face |
| `media_job_poison_total` | job yang melebihi retry |
| `media_job_cancelled_total` | job dibatalkan |

Log harus selalu membawa:

```text id="67goy2"
correlationId
tenantId
caseId
jobId
assetId
jobType
state
attempt
provider
errorClass
```


## 24. Senior Engineer Design Checklist

```text id="bg5u6l"
State Machine
[ ] Semua status punya definisi jelas
[ ] Semua transition eksplisit
[ ] Terminal state tidak bisa berubah sembarangan
[ ] Ada transition guard
[ ] Ada audit event per transition

Idempotency
[ ] Create job idempotent
[ ] Retry tidak membuat duplicate job
[ ] Result persistence idempotent
[ ] Callback idempotent
[ ] Same key different payload ditolak

Retry
[ ] Error diklasifikasikan
[ ] Retry hanya untuk transient error
[ ] Ada exponential backoff + jitter
[ ] Ada max attempts
[ ] Poison job ditangani

Cancellation
[ ] Ada cancel endpoint
[ ] Ada cancel requested state
[ ] Worker punya cancellation point
[ ] Terminal state tidak bisa cancel
[ ] Cancel tercatat di audit

Concurrency
[ ] Worker claim aman
[ ] Tidak ada double-processing
[ ] Lock punya timeout
[ ] Stuck job bisa recovery
[ ] Race condition dicegah conditional update

Observability
[ ] Queue lag diukur
[ ] Attempt count diukur
[ ] Failure class diukur
[ ] Provider latency diukur
[ ] Correlation ID konsisten
```

---

## 25. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Status hanya `PENDING/SUCCESS/FAILED` | Tidak cukup untuk audit dan recovery | State machine lengkap |
| Retry semua error | Cost naik, poison job | Failure classification |
| Tidak ada idempotency key | Duplicate job/result | Unique idempotency model |
| Worker update status bebas | Race condition | Conditional transition |
| Tidak ada `locked_until` | Job stuck selamanya | Lease timeout |
| Tidak ada audit event | Sulit investigasi | Append-only job event |
| Callback tanpa event ID | Duplicate side effect | Callback idempotency |
| Cancellation langsung dianggap selesai | Misleading state | `CANCEL_REQUESTED` lalu `CANCELLED` |
| Provider result tidak disimpan durable | Retry mahal | Persist result sebelum final state |
| Camunda dan DB status tidak sinkron | API membingungkan | Define source of truth |

---

Untuk tahap awal, implementasikan ini dulu:

```text id="4kg4n2"
4. explicit JobStatus enum
5. transition service
6. retry policy
7. failure classifier
8. worker claim mechanism
9. result persistence idempotency
10. cancellation endpoint
```

Minimal state yang cukup:

```text id="vg2hjy"
SUBMITTED
VALIDATING
STAGED
PROCESSING
RETRY_PENDING
NEEDS_REVIEW

FAILED

```
