## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:18:00 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 4/20**: object storage dan lifecycle file. Di sini fokusnya bagaimana raw file, normalized artifact, result file, retention, encryption, signed URL, dan cleanup dikelola agar pipeline OCR/STT/media tetap aman dan audit-friendly.

# Seri 4/20 - Object Storage & File Lifecycle  
## S3 / Azure Blob / MinIO, Storage Key Design, Raw vs Normalized Artifacts, Encryption, Retention, Cleanup, Signed URL, dan Audit Trail

## 1. Introduction

Untuk pipeline **OCR / STT / TTS / Face Verification / Media Processing**, object storage adalah tempat menyimpan:

```text
- raw uploaded file
- normalized image/audio/video
- thumbnails
- extracted audio
- OCR result artifact
- transcript JSON
- subtitle SRT/VTT
- generated TTS audio
- redacted media
- archived result
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text
PostgreSQL menyimpan metadata, state, audit, pointer.
Object storage menyimpan file besar dan artifact binary.
```

Jangan menyimpan file besar langsung di PostgreSQL kecuali ada alasan khusus yang sangat kuat. Untuk sistem enterprise, object storage memberi separation yang lebih baik antara **metadata/query layer** dan **binary/blob layer**.

AWS S3 mendukung pre-signed URL untuk upload/download tanpa memberikan AWS credential langsung ke client; S3 lifecycle dapat memindahkan object ke storage class lain atau menghapus object saat masa hidupnya selesai. Azure Blob Storage juga punya SAS untuk delegated access dan lifecycle management untuk memindahkan blob antar access tier atau menghapus blob. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/PresignedUrlUploadObject.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Object storage bukan sekadar folder file.

Object storage adalah:
- durable binary artifact store
- lifecycle-controlled storage
- audit-sensitive boundary
- access-controlled private repository
- input/output artifact registry untuk job pipeline
```

Dalam pipeline media AI, setiap file sebaiknya diperlakukan sebagai **asset**.

```text
media_asset
├── raw uploaded object
├── normalized object
├── derived object
├── result object
└── archived object
```

Setiap asset harus punya:

```text
- assetId
- tenantId
- caseId / business owner
- object key
- storage provider
- bucket/container
- MIME type
- checksum
- size
- lifecycle status
- retention date
- encryption metadata
- access classification
```

---

## 3. Diagram Arsitektur Object Storage

```text
Client / External System
        │
        │ upload via signed URL or API
        ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ Object Storage                │
│ - raw bucket/container        │
│ - normalized bucket/container │
│ - result bucket/container     │
│ - archive bucket/container    │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │ object key + metadata
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ PostgreSQL + MyBatis          │
│ - media_asset                 │
│ - media_asset_event           │
│ - media_job                   │
│ - retention_policy            │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ Worker / Camunda Pipeline     │
│ - validate                    │
│ - normalize                   │
│ - process OCR/STT/Face/TTS    │
│ - generate artifact           │
│ - update lifecycle status     │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ Result / Review / Search API  │
│ - signed download URL         │
│ - result metadata             │
│ - audit trail                 │
│ - retention enforcement       │
└──────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Raw vs Normalized vs Derived vs Result Artifact

Jangan campur semua file dalam satu konsep. Pisahkan berdasarkan peran.

| Artifact Type | Contoh | Purpose | Retention |
|---|---|---|---|
| `RAW_UPLOAD` | PDF asli, video asli, audio asli | Source of truth input | Sesuai policy/legal basis |
| `NORMALIZED_INPUT` | PDF page image, normalized WAV, resized image | Input yang siap diproses worker | Biasanya lebih pendek dari raw |
| `DERIVED_MEDIA` | thumbnail, extracted frame, extracted audio | Artifact pendukung | Sesuai kebutuhan UX/debug |
| `AI_RESULT` | OCR JSON, transcript JSON, face comparison result | Output structured | Biasanya mengikuti case retention |
| `EXPORT_FILE` | SRT, VTT, generated PDF, report | Output untuk user/download | Bisa lebih pendek |
| `REDACTED_MEDIA` | video blur, PDF redacted | Safe sharing artifact | Bisa lebih panjang dari raw |
| `ARCHIVE` | compressed final package | Long-term audit | Sesuai compliance |

Mental model:

```text
RAW_UPLOAD
  │
  ├── NORMALIZED_INPUT
  │       │
  │       ├── AI_RESULT
  │       └── DERIVED_MEDIA
  │
  └── REDACTED_MEDIA / EXPORT_FILE
```

---

## 5. Storage Layout

### Option A - bucket/container per artifact class

```text
media-raw
media-normalized
media-derived
media-result
media-archive
```

Kelebihan:

```text
- policy lebih mudah dipisah
- lifecycle per bucket lebih jelas
- access control lebih ketat
- raw sensitive data bisa diisolasi
```

Kekurangan:

```text
- lebih banyak bucket/container
- deployment/IaC lebih banyak
```

---

### Option B - single bucket dengan prefix

```text
media/
├── raw/
├── normalized/
├── derived/
├── result/
└── archive/
```

Kelebihan:

```text
- setup awal lebih sederhana
- prefix lifecycle rule bisa digunakan
```

Kekurangan:

```text
- salah policy lebih berisiko
- permission boundary lebih mudah bocor
```

Untuk enterprise/regulatory workload, saya lebih suka:

```text
bucket/container dipisah untuk raw sensitive data,
prefix dipakai untuk tenant/date/job partitioning.
```

---

## 6. Storage Key Design

Object key harus **generated**, bukan memakai filename user secara langsung.

### Jangan begini

```text
uploads/KTP Budi.png
uploads/../../etc/passwd
uploads/final.pdf
```

### Lebih aman

```text
tenant/{tenantId}/case/{caseId}/job/{jobId}/asset/{assetId}/raw/{safeFileId}.pdf
```

Contoh:

```text
tenant/tnt_001/case/CASE-2026-001/job/job_789/asset/ast_456/raw/ast_456.pdf
tenant/tnt_001/case/CASE-2026-001/job/job_789/asset/ast_457/normalized/page-0001.png
tenant/tnt_001/case/CASE-2026-001/job/job_789/result/ocr-result.json
```

### Recommended object key dimensions

| Dimension | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|
| `tenantId` | tenant isolation |
| `caseId` | business traceability |
| `jobId` | processing traceability |
| `assetId` | stable technical identity |
| `artifactType` | lifecycle/access policy |
| `version` | support reprocessing |
| `date partition` | lifecycle/reporting/ops |
| `safe extension` | debugging dan MIME clarity |

Contoh lebih lengkap:

```text
tenant/tnt_001/year/2026/month/07/case/CASE-001/job/job_123/raw/asset_ast_001_v1.pdf
tenant/tnt_001/year/2026/month/07/case/CASE-001/job/job_123/normalized/page_0001.png
tenant/tnt_001/year/2026/month/07/case/CASE-001/job/job_123/result/ocr_result_v1.json
```

---

## 7. Database Schema

### `media_asset`

```sql
CREATE TABLE media_asset (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id             UUID NOT NULL,
    case_id               VARCHAR(100),

    asset_type            VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    lifecycle_status      VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    storage_provider      VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    bucket_name           TEXT NOT NULL,
    object_key            TEXT NOT NULL,
    object_version        TEXT,

    original_file_name    TEXT,
    safe_file_name        TEXT,
    declared_mime_type    TEXT,
    detected_mime_type    TEXT,

    size_bytes            BIGINT NOT NULL,
    checksum_sha256       TEXT,
    encryption_mode       VARCHAR(50),
    kms_key_id            TEXT,

    width                 INTEGER,
    height                INTEGER,
    duration_ms           BIGINT,
    page_count            INTEGER,

    sensitivity_class     VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INTERNAL',
    retention_policy_id   UUID,
    retention_until       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    legal_hold            BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    created_by            UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (storage_provider, bucket_name, object_key)
);
```

### `media_asset_event`

```sql
CREATE TABLE media_asset_event (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    asset_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    event_type      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    from_status     VARCHAR(50),
    to_status       VARCHAR(50),
    message         TEXT,
    metadata_json   JSONB,
    created_by      UUID,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### `media_asset_relationship`

```sql
CREATE TABLE media_asset_relationship (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    parent_asset_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    child_asset_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    relationship_type   VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (parent_asset_id, child_asset_id, relationship_type)
);
```

Contoh relationship:

```text
RAW_UPLOAD → NORMALIZED_INPUT
RAW_UPLOAD → DERIVED_THUMBNAIL
NORMALIZED_INPUT → AI_RESULT
AI_RESULT → EXPORT_FILE
```

---

## 8. Lifecycle State Machine

```text
CREATED
  │
  ▼
UPLOADED
  │
  ▼
VALIDATING
  │
  ├── invalid ───────────► REJECTED
  ├── malware ───────────► QUARANTINED
  │
  ▼
ACTIVE
  │
  ├── normalized ────────► DERIVED
  ├── result generated ──► RESULT_LINKED
  │
  ▼
RETENTION_PENDING
  │
  ├── legal hold ────────► HELD
  │
  ▼
EXPIRED
  │
  ▼
DELETED

Optional:
ARCHIVED
RESTORED
```

Tabel status:

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `CREATED` | Metadata dibuat, object belum tentu ada |
| `UPLOADED` | Object sudah ada di storage |
| `VALIDATING` | Content sedang dicek |
| `ACTIVE` | Asset valid dan bisa dipakai |
| `DERIVED` | Asset hasil turunan dari asset lain |
| `RESULT_LINKED` | Asset terkait result job |
| `QUARANTINED` | Asset berbahaya/suspicious |
| `REJECTED` | Asset tidak valid |
| `RETENTION_PENDING` | Sudah masuk fase siap expire |
| `HELD` | Tidak boleh dihapus karena legal/compliance hold |
| `ARCHIVED` | Dipindah ke archive tier/storage |
| `EXPIRED` | Retention sudah selesai |
| `DELETED` | Object sudah dihapus dari storage |
| `RESTORED` | Archive object di-restore sementara |

---

## 9. Retention Policy Model

Retention tidak boleh hard-code di service method. Buat sebagai policy.

```sql
CREATE TABLE retention_policy (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    name                  VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    asset_type            VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    sensitivity_class     VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    retain_raw_days       INTEGER,
    retain_normalized_days INTEGER,
    retain_result_days    INTEGER,
    retain_archive_days   INTEGER,

    allow_user_delete     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    require_legal_review  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    created_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (name)
);
```

Contoh policy:

| Asset Type | Sensitivity | Retention |
|---|---|---:|
| `RAW_UPLOAD` | `BIOMETRIC` | 30 hari atau sesuai legal basis |
| `FACE_IMAGE` | `BIOMETRIC` | sangat pendek, kecuali legal hold |
| `OCR_RESULT` | `PII` | mengikuti case retention |
| `TRANSCRIPT` | `PII` | mengikuti case retention |
| `THUMBNAIL` | `INTERNAL` | 30-90 hari |
| `NORMALIZED_AUDIO` | `PII` | pendek, hanya untuk retry/debug |
| `REDACTED_MEDIA` | `INTERNAL` | bisa lebih panjang dari raw |

Catatan penting: untuk biometric/face data, retention harus jauh lebih ketat daripada artifact teknis biasa.

---

## 10. Lifecycle Rules di Storage Provider

### AWS S3

S3 Lifecycle mendukung aturan untuk transition, archive, atau expiration/delete object berdasarkan lifecycle configuration. S3 juga punya pertimbangan khusus untuk transisi ke kelas seperti Standard-IA/One Zone-IA yang tidak didukung dalam 30 hari pertama karena object baru sering masih sering diakses atau cepat dihapus. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/how-to-set-lifecycle-configuration-intro.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh konseptual:

```json
{
  "Rules": [
    {
      "ID": "expire-normalized-after-14-days",
      "Status": "Enabled",
      "Filter": {
        "Prefix": "normalized/"
      },
      "Expiration": {
        "Days": 14
      }
    },
    {
      "ID": "archive-results-after-90-days",
      "Status": "Enabled",
      "Filter": {
        "Prefix": "result/"
      },
      "Transitions": [
        {
          "Days": 90,
          "StorageClass": "GLACIER_IR"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

### Azure Blob Storage

Azure Blob lifecycle management dapat membuat automated rules untuk memindahkan data antar access tier seperti hot/cool/cold/archive atau menghapus blob di akhir lifecycle. Microsoft juga mencatat lifecycle policy ditulis/dibaca sebagai satu policy penuh, bukan partial update. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/storage/blobs/lifecycle-management-overview?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### MinIO / S3-Compatible On-Prem

MinIO AIStor Object Lifecycle Management mendukung rule untuk automatic transition ke remote tier atau expiry/delete object berbasis waktu/tanggal. MinIO Java SDK juga merupakan S3-compatible client untuk bucket/object operations, relevan untuk deployment on-prem yang ingin API kompatibel S3. ([MinIO AIStor Documentation](https://docs.min.io/aistor/administration/object-lifecycle-management/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Signed URL / Temporary Access

Untuk download/upload file, jangan expose object secara public. Gunakan short-lived signed URL.

### Use case

```text
- client upload raw file
- reviewer download preview
- frontend render thumbnail
- external system fetch export result
- user download generated transcript/audio
```

### AWS S3

Pre-signed URL dapat dipakai untuk memberikan akses upload/download object tanpa membagikan AWS credential langsung; URL tersebut terbatas oleh permission pembuat URL dan masa berlakunya. AWS juga mencatat bahwa pre-signed URL bisa digunakan beberapa kali sampai expired, dan upload ke key yang sudah ada dapat mengganti object tersebut. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/PresignedUrlUploadObject.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Azure Blob

Azure SAS digunakan untuk memberikan limited access ke Azure Storage resources, termasuk blob/container, tanpa memberikan account key langsung ke client. Untuk model yang lebih aman, user delegation SAS ditandatangani menggunakan user delegation key yang diperoleh dari Microsoft Entra credentials. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/storage/common/storage-sas-overview?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### MinIO

MinIO Java SDK menyediakan `getPresignedObjectUrl` untuk membuat pre-signed URL berdasarkan HTTP method, expiry time, dan parameter request tambahan. ([MinIO AIStor Documentation](https://docs.min.io/aistor/developers/sdk/java/api/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Signed URL Rules

| Rule | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Expiry | Pendek: 5-15 menit untuk download, upload sesuai ukuran file |
| Scope | Satu object key, bukan prefix luas |
| Method | Bedakan PUT/GET/DELETE |
| Permission | Ikuti permission service principal minimal |
| Audit | Catat siapa generate URL, untuk asset apa, expiry kapan |
| Filename | Pakai safe filename di response header |
| Content Type | Lock content type bila memungkinkan |
| Overwrite | Hindari reuse key untuk upload |
| Replay | Anggap URL bisa digunakan ulang sampai expired |
| Public Access | Disable public bucket/container access |

---

## 13. Java Abstraction Layer

Buat abstraction agar tidak hard-code AWS/Azure/MinIO.

```java
public interface ObjectStorageClient {

    PutObjectResult putObject(PutObjectCommand command);

    GetObjectResult getObject(GetObjectCommand command);

    boolean objectExists(ObjectRef objectRef);

    PresignedUrl createPresignedUploadUrl(PresignedUploadCommand command);

    PresignedUrl createPresignedDownloadUrl(PresignedDownloadCommand command);

    void deleteObject(ObjectRef objectRef);

    ObjectMetadata getMetadata(ObjectRef objectRef);
}
```

DTO:

```java
public record ObjectRef(
        String provider,
        String bucket,
        String key,
        String version
) {}

public record PresignedUploadCommand(
        ObjectRef objectRef,
        String contentType,
        long maxSizeBytes,
        Duration expiresIn,
        Map<String, String> requiredMetadata
) {}

public record PresignedUrl(
        URI url,
        OffsetDateTime expiresAt,
        String method
) {}
```

Implementasi:

```text
ObjectStorageClient
├── S3ObjectStorageClient
├── AzureBlobObjectStorageClient
├── MinioObjectStorageClient
└── LocalFilesystemObjectStorageClient only for dev/test
```

---

## 14. Storage Key Builder

```java
public final class MediaObjectKeyBuilder {

    public String rawObjectKey(RawObjectKeyRequest request) {
        return String.join("/",
                "tenant", request.tenantId(),
                "year", request.createdAt().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy")),
                "month", request.createdAt().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("MM")),
                "case", sanitizeSegment(request.caseId()),
                "job", request.jobId().toString(),
                "asset", request.assetId().toString(),
                "raw",
                request.assetId() + "." + request.safeExtension()
        );
    }

    private String sanitizeSegment(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return "none";
        }

        return value.replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]", "_");
    }
}
```

Design note:

```text
originalFileName hanya metadata display.
objectKey harus generated dan sanitized.
```

---

## 15. Upload Session → Asset Flow

```text
POST /media/upload-sessions
  │
  ├── create object key
  ├── create upload session row
  ├── generate signed upload URL
  └── return URL

Client uploads object
  │
  ▼

POST /media/upload-sessions/{id}/finalize
  │
  ├── check object exists
  ├── read storage metadata
  ├── calculate/verify checksum
  ├── detect MIME
  ├── create media_asset
  ├── mark upload session finalized
  └── create media_job
```

Contoh response finalize:

```json
{
  "uploadSessionId": "upl_123",
  "asset": {
    "assetId": "ast_456",
    "assetType": "RAW_UPLOAD",
    "lifecycleStatus": "ACTIVE",
    "detectedMimeType": "application/pdf",
    "sizeBytes": 10485760
  },
  "job": {
    "jobId": "job_789",
    "status": "SUBMITTED"
  }
}
```

## 16. Raw dan Normalized Artifact Relationship

Contoh OCR PDF:

```text
RAW_UPLOAD
└── ast_raw_pdf
    objectKey = raw/ast_raw_pdf.pdf

NORMALIZED_INPUT
├── ast_page_001
│   objectKey = normalized/page-0001.png
├── ast_page_002
│   objectKey = normalized/page-0002.png
└── ast_page_003
    objectKey = normalized/page-0003.png

AI_RESULT
└── ast_ocr_result
    objectKey = result/ocr-result.json
```

Database relationship:

```sql
INSERT INTO media_asset_relationship (
    id,
    parent_asset_id,
    child_asset_id,
    relationship_type
) VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    :rawPdfAssetId,
    :pageImageAssetId,
    'NORMALIZED_FROM'
);
```

---

## 17. Encryption Strategy

### AWS S3

Amazon S3 applies server-side encryption by default for new uploads using S3 managed keys, and AWS documents that server-side encryption protects data at rest by encrypting objects before storage and decrypting them on download. For stricter governance, teams often evaluate SSE-KMS/customer-managed keys depending on access control and audit requirements. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/UsingServerSideEncryption.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Azure Storage

Azure Storage encrypts data at rest transparently using 256-bit AES encryption, and Microsoft states all Azure Storage services support server-side encryption at rest. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/storage/common/storage-service-encryption?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Practical rule

```text
Default:
- provider-side encryption at rest enabled

For sensitive/biometric/regulated data:
- prefer customer-managed key where required
- separate key by environment/tenant/classification if justified
- log key id / encryption mode in media_asset
- ensure key rotation procedure exists
```

Schema fields:

```sql
encryption_mode VARCHAR(50),
kms_key_id TEXT
```

Possible values:

```text
SSE_S3
SSE_KMS
AZURE_MANAGED_KEY
AZURE_CUSTOMER_MANAGED_KEY
MINIO_SSE
CLIENT_SIDE_ENCRYPTED
```

---

## 18. Access Control Model

Object storage access jangan diberikan langsung berdasarkan object key tanpa domain authorization.

### Correct flow

```text
User requests asset download
  │
  ▼
Jersey API checks:
- authentication
- tenant membership
- case permission
- asset lifecycle status
- sensitivity class
- retention/legal hold
- purpose of access
  │
  ▼
API generates short-lived signed URL
```

### API

```http
POST /media-assets/{assetId}/download-url
```

Response:

```json
{
  "assetId": "ast_456",
  "url": "short-lived-signed-url",
  "expiresAt": "2026-07-13T16:15:00+07:00"
}
```

Jangan return bucket/key internal jika tidak diperlukan client.

---

## 19. Cleanup Worker

Lifecycle policy dari storage provider membantu, tapi aplikasi tetap perlu cleanup worker karena database state, audit, legal hold, dan cross-asset relationship perlu sinkron.

```text
Cleanup Worker
  │
  ├── find expired assets
  ├── skip legal_hold = true
  ├── verify no active job depends on asset
  ├── delete object from storage
  ├── mark asset DELETED
  ├── write asset event
  └── optionally compact/archive metadata
```

Query:

```sql
SELECT id, storage_provider, bucket_name, object_key
FROM media_asset
WHERE retention_until <= now()
  AND legal_hold = false
  AND lifecycle_status IN ('ACTIVE', 'RESULT_LINKED', 'ARCHIVED')
ORDER BY retention_until ASC
LIMIT 100;
```

Transition:

```sql
UPDATE media_asset
SET lifecycle_status = 'DELETED',
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = :assetId
  AND lifecycle_status <> 'DELETED'
  AND legal_hold = false;
```

---

## 20. Legal Hold

Legal hold harus override retention deletion.

```text
retention_until sudah lewat
BUT legal_hold = true
→ jangan delete
```

Schema:

```sql
ALTER TABLE media_asset
ADD COLUMN legal_hold_reason TEXT,
ADD COLUMN legal_hold_by UUID,
ADD COLUMN legal_hold_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
```

Event:

```json
{
  "eventType": "LEGAL_HOLD_APPLIED",
  "metadata": {
    "reason": "Regulatory investigation",
    "appliedBy": "user_123"
  }
}
```

---

## 21. Quarantine Flow

Untuk file berbahaya atau suspicious:

```text
UPLOADED
  │
  ▼
VALIDATING
  │
  ├── malware detected
  ▼
QUARANTINED
  │
  ├── block processing
  ├── revoke signed URL
  ├── alert security
  ├── audit event
  └── delete or retain per security policy
```

Jangan memproses file quarantined dengan FFmpeg, OCR engine, PDF parser, atau image library. Parser native/media sering menjadi attack surface.

---

## 22. Artifact Versioning

Reprocessing harus menghasilkan artifact baru, bukan overwrite diam-diam.

```text
ocr_result_v1.json
ocr_result_v2.json
ocr_result_v3.json
```

Contoh:

```text
job_123/result/ocr_result_v1.json
job_124/result/ocr_result_v2.json
```

Metadata:

```sql
ALTER TABLE media_asset
ADD COLUMN artifact_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
ADD COLUMN produced_by_job_id UUID,
ADD COLUMN produced_by_provider VARCHAR(100),
ADD COLUMN produced_by_provider_version TEXT;
```

Kenapa penting:

```text
- audit provider/model version
- compare result antar engine
- rollback hasil
- explain why extracted value changed
```

---

## 23. Object Metadata

Simpan metadata di dua tempat:

```text
1. Database
   - source of truth untuk application query

2. Object metadata/tagging
   - membantu lifecycle/storage ops
```

Contoh object metadata:

```text
tenant-id: tnt_001
case-id: CASE-2026-001
asset-id: ast_456
job-id: job_789
asset-type: RAW_UPLOAD
sensitivity: PII
checksum-sha256: abc123
```

Tapi jangan bergantung sepenuhnya pada object metadata untuk authorization. Authorization tetap lewat API/database.

---

## 24. PostgreSQL vs Object Storage Responsibility

| Concern | PostgreSQL | Object Storage |
|---|---|---|
| Job status | Ya | Tidak |
| Audit event | Ya | Tidak utama |
| Raw file | Tidak | Ya |
| OCR JSON kecil | Bisa JSONB | Bisa juga artifact |
| Transcript segment query | Ya | Tidak |
| Full transcript file | Optional | Ya |
| Thumbnail | Tidak | Ya |
| Signed URL | Metadata only | Ya |
| Retention decision | Ya | Lifecycle execution |
| Legal hold flag | Ya | Bisa mirror ke object lock/policy |
| Search | Ya / search engine | Tidak utama |

Praktik yang baik:

```text
Result structured yang sering di-query → PostgreSQL.
Result besar untuk download/export → object storage.
```

---

### Get asset metadata

```http
GET /media-assets/{assetId}
```

```json
{
  "assetId": "ast_456",
  "assetType": "RAW_UPLOAD",
  "lifecycleStatus": "ACTIVE",
  "originalFileName": "document.pdf",
  "detectedMimeType": "application/pdf",

  "checksumSha256": "abc123",

}
```

```http
POST /media-assets/{assetId}/download-url
```

```json
{
  "purpose": "REVIEW_PREVIEW"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "assetId": "ast_456",
  "downloadUrl": "short-lived-url",
  "expiresAt": "2026-07-13T16:15:00+07:00"
}
```

### Apply legal hold

```http
POST /media-assets/{assetId}/legal-hold
```

```json
{
  "reason": "Investigation case hold"
}
```

## 26. Storage Provider Decision Matrix

| Requirement | AWS S3 | Azure Blob | MinIO / S3-compatible |
|---|---|---|---|
| AWS-native workload | Sangat cocok | Bisa, tapi bukan native | Bisa untuk on-prem/hybrid |
| Azure-native workload | Bisa | Sangat cocok | Bisa untuk on-prem/hybrid |
| On-prem deployment | Tidak native | Tidak native | Cocok |
| S3-compatible API | Native | Tidak S3-native | Ya |
| Managed lifecycle | Ya | Ya | Ya, tergantung deployment |
| Signed temporary access | Pre-signed URL | SAS | Pre-signed URL |
| Enterprise key management | KMS | Key Vault | Tergantung setup |
| Local/dev parity | LocalStack/MinIO | Azurite | MinIO |

Untuk stack Anda yang mencakup **On-Prem/AWS/Azure**, desain abstraction `ObjectStorageClient` sangat penting supaya pipeline tidak tergantung penuh pada satu vendor.

---

## 27. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Simpan file besar di PostgreSQL | DB bloat, backup lambat | Object storage |
| Bucket public untuk preview/download | Data leak | Short-lived signed URL |
| Original filename jadi object key | Path traversal/collision | Generated object key |
| Semua artifact satu prefix tanpa policy | Retention kacau | Artifact-type prefix/bucket |
| Overwrite result lama | Audit hilang | Versioned artifact |
| Tidak ada checksum | Sulit integrity/dedup | SHA-256 |
| Tidak ada retention worker | Storage leak | Cleanup scheduler |
| Delete tanpa audit | Sulit investigasi | `media_asset_event` |
| Legal hold tidak diperiksa | Compliance breach | `legal_hold` guard |
| Signed URL expiry terlalu panjang | Leak window besar | Short expiry |
| Object key bocor ke client | Internal topology leak | Asset ID + signed URL API |
| Lifecycle hanya di storage, DB tidak sinkron | Metadata stale | App cleanup reconciliation |

---

## 28. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text
Storage Layout
[ ] Raw, normalized, result, archive artifact dipisahkan
[ ] Object key generated, bukan user filename
[ ] Tenant/case/job/asset traceability ada di key atau metadata
[ ] Bucket/container private
[ ] Public access disabled

Metadata
[ ] media_asset table menyimpan provider/bucket/key
[ ] checksum disimpan
[ ] detected MIME disimpan
[ ] size/duration/page count disimpan
[ ] asset relationship disimpan
[ ] provider/model version disimpan untuk result artifact

Security
[ ] Signed URL short-lived
[ ] Access via API authorization
[ ] Encryption at rest aktif
[ ] KMS/customer-managed key dievaluasi untuk sensitive data
[ ] Quarantine flow ada
[ ] Raw biometric/PII retention dibatasi

Lifecycle
[ ] Retention policy bukan hard-code
[ ] legal_hold override deletion
[ ] cleanup worker ada
[ ] storage lifecycle rule ada
[ ] DB dan storage direkonsiliasi
[ ] delete/archive punya audit event

Operations
[ ] Orphaned object cleanup ada
[ ] Missing object detection ada
[ ] Cost by artifact type bisa dihitung
[ ] Restore archive process jelas
[ ] Failed delete/retry handling ada
```

# Seri 5/20 - Media Metadata Extraction  
## MIME, EXIF, Codec, Duration, Resolution, PDF Page Count, Audio Stream Detection, Checksum, dan Media Sanity Validation

## 1. Introduction

Sebelum file masuk ke OCR, transcription, face verification, TTS, atau FFmpeg processing, sistem harus tahu dulu:

```text id="nr7qsg"
File ini sebenarnya apa?
Apakah aman diproses?
Apakah sesuai job type?
Apakah ukurannya masuk policy?
Apakah perlu OCR, STT, frame extraction, atau ditolak?
```

Di sinilah **media metadata extraction** berperan.

Metadata extraction bukan sekadar informasi tambahan. Dalam pipeline media/AI, metadata adalah **routing input**, **cost guardrail**, **security guardrail**, dan **audit evidence**.

Contoh:

```text id="n4jcg7"
User upload "document.pdf"
  │
  ├── detected MIME = application/pdf
  ├── page count = 1200 pages
  ├── encrypted = true
  └── intended job = OCR

Decision:
→ reject or require special policy because page count too high / encrypted PDF
```

Apache Tika dapat mendeteksi content type memakai magic-based detection, filename hint, dan supplied content type; FFprobe dapat menampilkan informasi stream dan container format; PDFBox menyediakan API untuk bekerja dengan PDF dan mengambil jumlah halaman. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/2.0.0/detection.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com), [ffmpeg.org](https://ffmpeg.org/ffprobe.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com), [pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/docs/2.0.2/javadocs/org/apache/pdfbox/pdmodel/PDDocument.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="6ejx1c"
Metadata extraction = technical triage layer

Input:
- raw uploaded object

Output:
- normalized technical facts
- validation decision
- routing decision
- risk flags
- audit record
```

Metadata extraction sebaiknya berjalan setelah upload selesai, tapi sebelum job berat dimulai:

```text id="v1313r"
UPLOAD_COMPLETED
  │
  ▼
METADATA_EXTRACTION
  │
  ├── MIME detection
  ├── checksum calculation
  ├── file size validation
  ├── PDF page count
  ├── image dimension
  ├── video/audio stream detection
  ├── EXIF extraction
  ├── codec/container detection
  └── sanity validation
  │
  ▼
STAGED / REJECTED / SECURITY_REJECTED
```

---

## 3. Diagram

```text id="m32fbc"
Raw Uploaded Asset
        │
        ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ Metadata Extraction Worker    │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
               ├── Checksum
               │   └── SHA-256
               │
               ├── File Type Detection
               │   ├── extension
               │   ├── declared MIME
               │   ├── magic bytes
               │   └── Apache Tika
               │
               ├── Image Metadata
               │   ├── width / height
               │   ├── EXIF orientation
               │   ├── color model
               │   └── GPS metadata flag
               │
               ├── PDF Metadata
               │   ├── page count
               │   ├── encrypted
               │   ├── text layer exists
               │   ├── scanned vs born-digital hint
               │   └── embedded files
               │
               ├── Audio / Video Metadata
               │   ├── duration
               │   ├── codec
               │   ├── container
               │   ├── bitrate
               │   ├── sample rate
               │   ├── channels
               │   ├── audio stream exists
               │   └── video stream exists
               │
               └── Sanity Validation
                   ├── allowed type
                   ├── max size
                   ├── max pages
                   ├── max duration
                   ├── max resolution
                   ├── supported codec
                   └── route/reject
```

---

## 4. Metadata Categories

| Category | Examples | Why it matters |
|---|---|---|
| File identity | extension, declared MIME, detected MIME, magic bytes | Prevent spoofing and route correctly |
| Integrity | SHA-256, object size, ETag if applicable | Deduplication, audit, retry safety |
| Image metadata | width, height, EXIF orientation, color profile | OCR/face preprocessing decision |
| PDF metadata | page count, encrypted flag, text layer hint | OCR cost and processing path |
| Audio metadata | duration, codec, sample rate, channel count | STT eligibility and normalization |
| Video metadata | duration, video codec, resolution, frame rate, audio stream | Transcoding, thumbnail, STT from video |
| Risk flags | too large, encrypted, unsupported, suspicious mismatch | Reject or route to manual/security |
| Routing hint | `OCR_IMAGE`, `OCR_PDF`, `STT_AUDIO`, `STT_VIDEO`, `FACE_IMAGE` | Choose next worker |
| Audit evidence | extractor version, extracted_at, errors | Defensibility and debugging |

---

## 5. Metadata Extraction Flow

```text id="8eucbg"
media_asset.lifecycle_status = UPLOADED
        │
        ▼
Claim metadata extraction job
        │
        ▼
Download/stream object to temp sandbox
        │
        ▼
Calculate checksum
        │
        ▼
Detect MIME using magic + filename hint
        │
        ▼
Run type-specific extractor
        │
        ├── image extractor
        ├── PDF extractor
        ├── audio/video extractor
        └── document extractor
        │
        ▼
Validate against policy
        │
        ├── valid       → ACTIVE / STAGED
        ├── invalid     → REJECTED
        └── suspicious  → SECURITY_REJECTED / QUARANTINED
        │
        ▼
Persist metadata + audit event
```

---

## 6. MIME Detection

Jangan percaya `Content-Type` dari client. Gunakan layered detection.

```text id="g8m0wo"
declared_mime_type  = from client/browser/header
extension_mime_type = inferred from filename
detected_mime_type  = detected from content
final_mime_type     = system decision
```

Apache Tika memakai `tika-mimetypes.xml` untuk magic-based dan filename-based detection. Tika melakukan magic detection di awal file lebih dulu, lalu memakai filename dan supplied content type sebagai hint tambahan bila tersedia. ([tika.apache.org](https://tika.apache.org/2.0.0/detection.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Java example

```java id="njscyr"
public final class MimeDetectionService {

    private final Tika tika = new Tika();

    public MimeDetectionResult detect(Path file, String originalFileName, String declaredMimeType) {
        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(file)) {
            String detectedMime = tika.detect(input, originalFileName);

            boolean mismatch = declaredMimeType != null
                    && !declaredMimeType.isBlank()
                    && !declaredMimeType.equalsIgnoreCase(detectedMime);

            return new MimeDetectionResult(
                    declaredMimeType,
                    detectedMime,
                    mismatch
            );
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new MetadataExtractionException("Failed to detect MIME type", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### MIME validation matrix

| Declared | Detected | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| `application/pdf` | `application/pdf` | Accept if policy allows |
| `image/png` | `image/png` | Accept if image job |
| `image/jpeg` | `application/pdf` | Reject or security review |
| `video/mp4` | `application/octet-stream` | Investigate / use ffprobe |
| missing | detected valid | Accept if policy allows |
| any | executable/script | Reject/security |

---

## 7. Checksum Extraction

Checksum berguna untuk:

```text id="2xr0tc"
- file integrity
- deduplication
- idempotency key
- audit traceability
- detecting object replacement
- result reproducibility
```

Gunakan SHA-256 sebagai default.

```java id="8z4suy"
public final class ChecksumService {

    public String sha256(Path file) {
        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(file)) {
            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");

            byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
            int read;

            while ((read = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                digest.update(buffer, 0, read);
            }

            byte[] hash = digest.digest();
            return HexFormat.of().formatHex(hash);

        } catch (IOException | NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new MetadataExtractionException("Failed to calculate SHA-256", e);
        }
    }
}
```

### Storage rule

```text id="2w6ru5"
checksum dari client = optional hint
checksum dari server = source of truth
```

---

## 8. Image Metadata Extraction

Untuk image OCR / face detection / face verification, metadata minimal:

```text id="qu8cse"
- width
- height
- megapixels
- format
- EXIF orientation
- color space
- has alpha channel
- GPS metadata present
- camera timestamp present
```

Java bisa memakai `ImageIO` untuk dimensi dasar, dan library seperti `metadata-extractor` untuk EXIF/IPTC/XMP. `metadata-extractor` adalah Java library untuk membaca metadata dari image, video, dan audio files. ([github.com](https://github.com/drewnoakes/metadata-extractor?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Basic dimension with ImageIO

```java id="2l2gso"
public ImageMetadata extractImageMetadata(Path file) {
    try (ImageInputStream input = ImageIO.createImageInputStream(file.toFile())) {
        Iterator<ImageReader> readers = ImageIO.getImageReaders(input);

        if (!readers.hasNext()) {
            throw new UnsupportedMediaException("No ImageIO reader available");
        }

        ImageReader reader = readers.next();

        try {
            reader.setInput(input);
            int width = reader.getWidth(0);
            int height = reader.getHeight(0);

            long megapixels = Math.multiplyExact((long) width, (long) height);

            return new ImageMetadata(width, height, megapixels);

        } finally {
            reader.dispose();
        }

    } catch (IOException e) {
        throw new MetadataExtractionException("Failed to extract image metadata", e);
    }
}
```

### EXIF orientation

```java id="sh19kz"
public Optional<Integer> readExifOrientation(Path file) {
    try {
        Metadata metadata = ImageMetadataReader.readMetadata(file.toFile());

        ExifIFD0Directory directory =
                metadata.getFirstDirectoryOfType(ExifIFD0Directory.class);

        if (directory == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        if (!directory.containsTag(ExifIFD0Directory.TAG_ORIENTATION)) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        return Optional.of(directory.getInt(ExifIFD0Directory.TAG_ORIENTATION));

    } catch (Exception e) {
        throw new MetadataExtractionException("Failed to read EXIF orientation", e);
    }
}
```

### Image sanity rules

| Rule | Example |
|---|---|
| min width/height | face verification may require at least 640×480 |
| max megapixels | reject 200 MP image to avoid decompression bomb |
| allowed formats | JPG/PNG/TIFF depending pipeline |
| orientation normalization | rotate before OCR/face detection |
| GPS stripping | remove or flag sensitive EXIF |
| alpha handling | convert PNG alpha if OCR engine needs RGB |
| color mode | convert CMYK/grayscale as needed |

---

## 9. PDF Metadata Extraction

PDF pipeline perlu tahu:

```text id="4vdohq"
- page count
- encrypted or not
- has text layer or scanned only
- embedded files
- page dimensions
- PDF version
- file size
- suspicious structure
```

Apache PDFBox adalah Java library open-source untuk membuat, memanipulasi, dan mengekstrak konten dari PDF. `PDDocument#getNumberOfPages()` mengembalikan total page count PDF. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/))

### Page count and encryption

```java id="fik7vh"
public PdfMetadata extractPdfMetadata(Path file) {
    try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(file.toFile())) {
        int pageCount = document.getNumberOfPages();
        boolean encrypted = document.isEncrypted();

        return new PdfMetadata(pageCount, encrypted);

    } catch (InvalidPasswordException e) {
        return PdfMetadata.encrypted();

    } catch (IOException e) {
        throw new MetadataExtractionException("Failed to extract PDF metadata", e);
    }
}
```

Catatan: PDFBox 3.x menggunakan loading API yang berbeda dari beberapa contoh lama PDFBox 2.x; bila codebase Anda memakai PDFBox 2.x, sesuaikan API-nya. PDFBox 3.0 migration guide menjelaskan perubahan penting untuk upgrade dari 2.x ke 3.x. ([pdfbox.apache.org](https://pdfbox.apache.org/3.0/migration.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Born-digital vs scanned PDF hint

```text id="ds4b2b"
Born-digital PDF:
- has extractable text layer
- OCR mungkin tidak perlu
- text extraction cukup

Scanned PDF:
- mostly page images
- OCR diperlukan
- perlu rasterization per page
```

Heuristic sederhana:

```java id="w1m6nq"
public boolean likelyHasTextLayer(Path file) {
    try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(file.toFile())) {
        PDFTextStripper stripper = new PDFTextStripper();
        stripper.setStartPage(1);
        stripper.setEndPage(Math.min(3, document.getNumberOfPages()));

        String text = stripper.getText(document);
        return text != null && text.trim().length() > 50;

    } catch (IOException e) {
        throw new MetadataExtractionException("Failed to inspect PDF text layer", e);
    }
}
```

### PDF sanity rules

| Rule | Decision |
|---|---|
| `page_count > max_pages` | reject or require async batch approval |
| encrypted PDF | reject unless password flow exists |
| no text layer + OCR job | valid |
| text layer exists + OCR requested | maybe skip OCR or compare |
| embedded files present | security review |
| page dimensions huge | reject or downscale |
| malformed PDF | reject |
| excessive object count | security review |

---

## 10. Audio / Video Metadata Extraction with FFprobe

Untuk audio/video, gunakan `ffprobe`, bukan parsing manual.

FFprobe adalah companion tool FFmpeg untuk mengumpulkan informasi dari multimedia streams; dokumentasinya menjelaskan opsi seperti `-show_streams`, `-show_format`, dan stream selection. ([ffmpeg.org](https://ffmpeg.org/ffprobe.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Command pattern

```bash id="d90g19"
ffprobe \
  -v error \
  -print_format json \
  -show_format \
  -show_streams \
  input.mp4
```

Output berisi:

```text id="6tu3q5"
format:
- duration
- size
- bit_rate
- format_name

streams:
- codec_type: audio/video/subtitle
- codec_name
- width/height for video
- sample_rate/channels for audio
- duration
- avg_frame_rate
```

### Java wrapper

```java id="9s17qk"
public final class FfprobeMetadataExtractor {

    public MediaProbeResult probe(Path inputFile) {
        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(
                "ffprobe",
                "-v", "error",
                "-print_format", "json",
                "-show_format",
                "-show_streams",
                inputFile.toAbsolutePath().toString()
        );

        builder.redirectErrorStream(false);

        try {
            Process process = builder.start();

            String stdout;
            String stderr;

            try (InputStream out = process.getInputStream();
                 InputStream err = process.getErrorStream()) {
                stdout = new String(out.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                stderr = new String(err.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            }

            boolean finished = process.waitFor(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

            if (!finished) {
                process.destroyForcibly();
                throw new MetadataExtractionException("ffprobe timed out");
            }

            if (process.exitValue() != 0) {
                throw new MetadataExtractionException("ffprobe failed: " + stderr);
            }

            return parseFfprobeJson(stdout);

        } catch (IOException | InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new MetadataExtractionException("Failed to run ffprobe", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Important production details:

```text id="7n5pxh"
- timeout wajib
- process memory/CPU limit idealnya di container
- file path harus controlled, bukan raw user input
- jangan shell concat command string
- parse JSON dengan strict DTO
- log ffprobe version
```

---

## 11. Audio Metadata

Untuk STT, metadata minimal:

| Field | Purpose |
|---|---|
| duration_ms | cost guardrail |
| codec_name | support decision |
| sample_rate | normalization decision |
| channels | mono/stereo/channel separation |
| bit_rate | quality hint |
| format_name | container handling |
| has_audio_stream | route/reject |
| speech_hint | optional later analysis |

Example normalized result:

```json id="sb70zr"
{
  "mediaKind": "AUDIO",
  "format": "mp3",
  "durationMs": 184000,
  "audio": {
    "codec": "mp3",
    "sampleRate": 44100,
    "channels": 2,
    "bitRate": 192000
  }
}
```

### STT sanity rules

| Rule | Decision |
|---|---|
| no audio stream | reject for STT |
| duration > max | reject or split/batch |
| unsupported codec | normalize/transcode first |
| sample rate too low | warn/normalize |
| multi-channel | decide diarization/channel handling |
| silence-only | possibly reject after audio analysis |

---

## 12. Video Metadata

Untuk video processing / STT-from-video / thumbnail:

| Field | Purpose |
|---|---|
| duration_ms | processing cost |
| video_codec | transcode compatibility |
| width/height | resolution policy |
| frame_rate | thumbnail/frame extraction |
| has_audio_stream | STT from video |
| has_video_stream | video processing |
| rotation | display/thumbnail correctness |
| bit_rate | quality/cost hint |
| subtitle streams | caption handling |

Example normalized result:

```json id="0mwwnr"
{
  "mediaKind": "VIDEO",
  "format": "mov,mp4,m4a,3gp,3g2,mj2",
  "durationMs": 3600000,
  "video": {
    "codec": "h264",
    "width": 1920,
    "height": 1080,
    "frameRate": 29.97
  },
  "audio": {
    "codec": "aac",
    "sampleRate": 48000,
    "channels": 2
  }
}
```

### Video sanity rules

| Rule | Decision |
|---|---|
| video duration too long | reject or async long job |
| no audio but STT requested | reject or skip transcription |
| resolution too high | transcode/downscale |
| unsupported codec | transcode first |
| variable frame rate | handle carefully for frame timestamps |
| rotation metadata | normalize before thumbnail |
| no video stream | treat as audio if audio exists |

---

## 13. Routing Decision

Metadata extraction should produce a routing decision.

```text id="6np4fj"
OCR_IMAGE
OCR_PDF_TEXT_LAYER
OCR_PDF_SCANNED
STT_AUDIO
STT_VIDEO
MEDIA_TRANSCODE
THUMBNAIL_IMAGE
THUMBNAIL_VIDEO
FACE_IMAGE
REJECT_UNSUPPORTED
SECURITY_REVIEW
```

Example:

```java id="a7a6uk"
public ProcessingRoute decideRoute(MediaMetadata metadata, JobType requestedJobType) {
    if (metadata.hasSecurityFlag()) {
        return ProcessingRoute.SECURITY_REVIEW;
    }

    if (requestedJobType == JobType.OCR && metadata.isImage()) {
        return ProcessingRoute.OCR_IMAGE;
    }

    if (requestedJobType == JobType.OCR && metadata.isPdf()) {
        if (metadata.pdf().encrypted()) {
            return ProcessingRoute.REJECT_UNSUPPORTED;
        }

        if (metadata.pdf().likelyHasTextLayer()) {
            return ProcessingRoute.OCR_PDF_TEXT_LAYER;
        }

        return ProcessingRoute.OCR_PDF_SCANNED;
    }

    if (requestedJobType == JobType.TRANSCRIPTION && metadata.hasAudioStream()) {
        return metadata.isVideo()
                ? ProcessingRoute.STT_VIDEO
                : ProcessingRoute.STT_AUDIO;
    }

    if (requestedJobType == JobType.FACE_VERIFICATION && metadata.isImage()) {
        return ProcessingRoute.FACE_IMAGE;
    }

    return ProcessingRoute.REJECT_UNSUPPORTED;
}
```

---

## 16. Validation Policy Model

Jangan hard-code limit di kode. Buat policy.

```sql id="va6icw"
CREATE TABLE media_validation_policy (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_type              VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    media_kind            VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    max_size_bytes        BIGINT,
    max_duration_ms       BIGINT,
    max_page_count        INTEGER,
    max_width             INTEGER,
    max_height            INTEGER,
    max_megapixels        BIGINT,

    allowed_mime_types    TEXT[] NOT NULL,
    allowed_codecs        TEXT[],
    reject_encrypted_pdf  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    require_audio_stream  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    require_video_stream  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    created_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Contoh policy:

| Job Type | Media Kind | Key Policy |
|---|---|---|
| OCR | IMAGE | max 20MB, max 50MP, JPG/PNG/TIFF |
| OCR | PDF | max 100MB, max 100 pages, reject encrypted |
| STT | AUDIO | max 2GB, max 4 hours, require audio |
| STT | VIDEO | max 5GB, max 4 hours, require audio |
| FACE_VERIFICATION | IMAGE | max 10MB, one image, JPG/PNG |
| THUMBNAIL | VIDEO | max duration 2 hours, require video |

## 17. Sanity Validation Result

```java id="o17s1t"
public record ValidationResult(
        ValidationStatus status,
        ProcessingRoute route,
        List<String> warnings,
        List<ValidationError> errors
) {
    public boolean accepted() {
        return status == ValidationStatus.PASSED
                || status == ValidationStatus.PASSED_WITH_WARNINGS;
    }
}
```

Error examples:

```text id="zpcwg8"
MIME_MISMATCH
UNSUPPORTED_MIME
FILE_TOO_LARGE
PDF_TOO_MANY_PAGES
PDF_ENCRYPTED
IMAGE_TOO_LARGE
VIDEO_TOO_LONG
AUDIO_STREAM_MISSING
VIDEO_STREAM_MISSING
UNSUPPORTED_CODEC
METADATA_EXTRACTION_FAILED
SUSPICIOUS_FILE
```

---

## 18. Security Flags

Metadata extraction harus menghasilkan security flags.

| Flag | Meaning | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `MIME_MISMATCH` | declared and detected MIME differ | reject/review |
| `DOUBLE_EXTENSION` | e.g. `invoice.pdf.exe` | reject/review |
| `EXCESSIVE_DIMENSIONS` | image bomb risk | reject |
| `PDF_ENCRYPTED` | cannot inspect safely | reject unless supported |
| `PDF_EMBEDDED_FILES` | embedded payload risk | review |
| `NO_READER_AVAILABLE` | parser cannot read | reject |
| `UNEXPECTED_EXECUTABLE` | executable/script detected | reject |
| `GPS_EXIF_PRESENT` | privacy-sensitive metadata | strip/flag |
| `UNKNOWN_BINARY` | no confident detection | reject/review |
| `FFPROBE_TIMEOUT` | suspicious/heavy media | reject/retry once |

OWASP’s File Upload Cheat Sheet recommends layered defenses including extension allowlisting, content-type validation, filename safety, file size limits, storage restrictions, permissions, and malware scanning. ([cheatsheetseries.owasp.org](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 19. Metadata Extraction Worker Skeleton

```java id="x9ox2k"
public final class MetadataExtractionWorker {

    private final MediaAssetRepository assetRepository;
    private final ObjectStorageClient objectStorageClient;
    private final MimeDetectionService mimeDetectionService;
    private final ChecksumService checksumService;
    private final ImageMetadataExtractor imageExtractor;
    private final PdfMetadataExtractor pdfExtractor;
    private final FfprobeMetadataExtractor ffprobeExtractor;
    private final MediaValidationService validationService;

    public void extract(UUID assetId) {
        MediaAsset asset = assetRepository.get(assetId);

        Path localFile = objectStorageClient.downloadToTemp(asset.objectRef());

        try {
            String checksum = checksumService.sha256(localFile);
            MimeDetectionResult mime = mimeDetectionService.detect(
                    localFile,
                    asset.originalFileName(),
                    asset.declaredMimeType()
            );

            MediaMetadata metadata = switch (mime.detectedMimeType()) {
                case "application/pdf" -> pdfExtractor.extract(localFile, mime, checksum);
                case "image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/tiff" -> imageExtractor.extract(localFile, mime, checksum);
                case "audio/mpeg", "audio/wav", "audio/x-wav", "audio/flac" -> ffprobeExtractor.extract(localFile, mime, checksum);
                case "video/mp4", "video/quicktime", "video/x-matroska" -> ffprobeExtractor.extract(localFile, mime, checksum);
                default -> MediaMetadata.unsupported(mime, checksum);
            };

            ValidationResult validation = validationService.validate(asset, metadata);

            assetRepository.saveMetadata(assetId, metadata, validation);

            if (validation.accepted()) {
                assetRepository.transition(assetId, "VALIDATING", "ACTIVE");
            } else {
                assetRepository.transition(assetId, "VALIDATING", "REJECTED");
            }

        } finally {
            secureDeleteBestEffort(localFile);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 20. Failure Handling

| Failure | Job/Asset State | Retry? |
|---|---|---|
| temporary object storage error | `RETRY_PENDING` | Yes |
| ffprobe timeout | `RETRY_PENDING` then reject | Limited |
| PDF parser crash | `REJECTED` / `SECURITY_REVIEW` | No or limited |
| MIME unsupported | `REJECTED` | No |
| checksum mismatch | `REJECTED` | No |
| temp disk full | `RETRY_PENDING` | Yes |
| worker crash | reclaim by queue/Camunda | Yes |
| malware detected | `SECURITY_REJECTED` / `QUARANTINED` | No |

---

## 23. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Percaya filename extension | MIME spoofing | Tika/magic detection |
| Percaya client `Content-Type` | false routing | server-side detection |
| Tidak hitung checksum | sulit dedup/audit | SHA-256 |
| OCR PDF tanpa cek page count | cost explosion | page limit policy |
| STT video tanpa cek audio stream | wasted processing | audio stream detection |
| Face verification tanpa cek image dimension | poor quality/error | dimension/quality check |
| FFprobe tanpa timeout | worker hang | process timeout |
| Shell concat ffprobe command | command injection risk | `ProcessBuilder` args |
| Semua metadata hanya JSONB | query lambat | denormalize hot fields |
| EXIF GPS tidak dipedulikan | privacy leak | strip/flag GPS EXIF |
| Encrypted PDF diproses normal | parser failure/security risk | reject/password flow |
| Tidak simpan extractor version | audit gap | persist version |

## 25. Minimal Implementation untuk Tahap Awal

Implementasikan dulu:

```text id="afktw5"
1. MIME detection dengan Apache Tika
2. SHA-256 checksum
3. Image dimension + EXIF orientation
4. PDF page count + encrypted flag
5. FFprobe JSON extraction untuk audio/video
6. media_validation_policy table
7. validation result JSON
8. routing_hint field
9. metadata_json field
10. audit events
```

Minimal routing:

```text id="vtml6w"
image/jpeg, image/png → OCR_IMAGE or FACE_IMAGE
application/pdf       → OCR_PDF
audio/*               → STT_AUDIO
video/* with audio    → STT_VIDEO
video/* no audio      → MEDIA_PROCESSING_ONLY
unknown/mismatch      → REJECTED / SECURITY_REVIEW
```

7/13/2026, 10:24:50 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 6/20**: image preprocessing untuk OCR. Fokusnya adalah meningkatkan kualitas input sebelum OCR, karena akurasi sering lebih banyak ditentukan oleh preprocessing daripada sekadar mengganti OCR engine.

# Seri 6/20 - Image Preprocessing untuk OCR  
## Resize, Denoise, Deskew, Thresholding, Rotation, Crop, PDF Rasterization, dan Quality Scoring sebelum OCR

## 1. Introduction

Image preprocessing adalah tahap untuk mengubah image/PDF scan mentah menjadi input yang lebih mudah dibaca OCR engine.

Dalam sistem OCR production, akurasi OCR biasanya ditentukan oleh kombinasi:

```text id="ymylsy"
Input quality
+ preprocessing
+ OCR engine configuration
+ language model
+ layout detection
+ post-processing
+ human review threshold
```

Jadi jangan berpikir:

```text id="nwjtju"
OCR buruk → langsung ganti OCR engine
```

Lebih tepat:

```text id="gc51l8"
OCR buruk
→ cek resolution / DPI
→ cek rotation / skew
→ cek noise / blur
→ cek contrast
→ cek crop / margin
→ cek thresholding
→ cek language / page segmentation mode
→ baru evaluasi engine/provider
```

Tesseract sendiri mendokumentasikan bahwa image quality penting; Tesseract bekerja baik pada image dengan DPI minimal sekitar 300 dpi, dan untuk Tesseract 4.x lebih aman menggunakan dark text on light background. Tesseract juga menyebut preprocessing internal seperti thresholding dan deskewing, tetapi tetap merekomendasikan perbaikan image input bila output buruk. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="uacgjb"
Raw image / scanned PDF
        │
        ▼
Image quality assessment
        │
        ▼
Preprocessing pipeline selection
        │
        ├── rotate
        ├── crop
        ├── resize / DPI normalize
        ├── grayscale
        ├── denoise
        ├── contrast normalize
        ├── threshold / binarize
        ├── deskew
        ├── morphology cleanup
        └── border handling
        │
        ▼
OCR-ready image
        │
        ▼
OCR engine
        │
        ▼
OCR result + confidence
```

Preprocessing bukan satu pipeline tetap untuk semua image. Pipeline harus dipilih berdasarkan karakter input.

Contoh:

| Input | Pipeline berbeda |
|---|---|
| Scan dokumen kantor | deskew + threshold + OCR |
| Foto HP miring | perspective correction + crop + denoise |
| PDF born-digital | extract text dulu, OCR mungkin tidak perlu |
| PDF scan | rasterize page ke image lalu OCR |
| Receipt buram | denoise + sharpen + adaptive threshold |
| KTP/ID card | crop ROI + face/text region separation |
| Dokumen berwarna | grayscale/contrast normalize, jangan selalu threshold agresif |

---

## 3. Diagram

```text id="3qqdp7"
OCR Image Preprocessing Pipeline
│
├── 1. Input Classification
│   ├── image upload
│   ├── scanned PDF page
│   ├── camera photo
│   ├── document scan
│   └── structured document / ID / invoice
│
├── 2. Quality Assessment
│   ├── resolution / DPI
│   ├── width / height
│   ├── blur score
│   ├── brightness
│   ├── contrast
│   ├── skew angle
│   ├── noise estimate
│   └── text density
│
├── 3. Geometric Correction
│   ├── EXIF orientation
│   ├── rotation
│   ├── deskew
│   ├── perspective correction
│   ├── crop border
│   └── page segmentation
│
├── 4. Pixel-Level Enhancement
│   ├── grayscale
│   ├── resize / DPI normalize
│   ├── denoise
│   ├── contrast adjustment
│   ├── sharpening
│   ├── thresholding
│   └── morphology cleanup
│
├── 5. OCR Variant Generation
│   ├── original normalized
│   ├── thresholded
│   ├── adaptive thresholded
│   ├── denoised
│   └── cropped ROI
│
├── 6. OCR Execution
│   ├── Tesseract/Tess4J
│   ├── cloud OCR
│   ├── layout OCR
│   └── field-specific OCR
│
└── 7. Result Quality Feedback
    ├── confidence score
    ├── CER/WER sample if ground truth exists
    ├── low-confidence route
    └── human review
```

---

## 4. Practical Skill Map

| Skill Area | Yang dipelajari | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| PDF rasterization | Convert PDF page menjadi image dengan DPI tertentu | PDF scan harus jadi image sebelum OCR |
| DPI normalization | Target sekitar 300 DPI atau text height memadai | Tesseract lebih stabil pada resolusi cukup ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Orientation correction | EXIF orientation, 90/180/270 rotation | Banyak foto HP salah orientasi |
| Deskew | Koreksi kemiringan teks/page | Skew kecil bisa menurunkan OCR |
| Crop / border removal | Hilangkan border, background, meja, margin berlebih | Mengurangi noise dan salah segmentasi |
| Grayscale conversion | Simplify channel | Basis untuk thresholding/denoise |
| Thresholding | Binary image: text vs background | OpenCV menyediakan simple, adaptive, dan Otsu thresholding. ([opencv24-python-tutorials.readthedocs.io](https://opencv24-python-tutorials.readthedocs.io/en/latest/py_tutorials/py_imgproc/py_thresholding/py_thresholding.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Denoising | Hilangkan salt-pepper/noise/fax artifacts | Meningkatkan text clarity |
| Morphology | Erosion/dilation/opening/closing | Membersihkan noise atau menyambung stroke |
| Perspective correction | Koreksi foto dokumen miring | Penting untuk mobile capture |
| Quality scoring | blur, contrast, brightness, resolution | Putuskan reject, retry capture, atau human review |
| Multi-variant OCR | Jalankan beberapa versi preprocessing lalu pilih result terbaik | Berguna untuk dokumen heterogen |
| Audit artifact | Simpan preprocessing metadata dan image versi final | Debug dan regulatory traceability |

---

## 5. Tooling di Java Ecosystem

| Tool / Library | Fungsi | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| **OpenCV Java / JavaCV** | Image processing: resize, threshold, morphology, contour, deskew | Cocok untuk preprocessing kompleks |
| **Tesseract / Tess4J** | OCR lokal/on-prem | Tess4J adalah wrapper Java JNA untuk Tesseract |
| **Apache PDFBox** | Render PDF page ke image, inspect PDF | `PDFRenderer` dapat render page sebagai image; PDFBox 3.0.5 API menyediakan `renderImageWithDPI`. ([Javadoc](https://javadoc.io/static/org.apache.pdfbox/pdfbox/3.0.5/index.html?org%2Fapache%2Fpdfbox%2Frendering%2FPDFRenderer.html=&utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| **ImageIO** | Basic image read/write | Cukup untuk dimensi/format sederhana |
| **metadata-extractor** | EXIF orientation/GPS/camera metadata | Berguna untuk foto HP |
| **ImageMagick** | Image conversion/enhancement via external process | Harus dibatasi dengan security policy/resource limits; ImageMagick mendokumentasikan policy untuk memory, paths, max images, runtime, disk usage, dan coder allow/deny. ([ImageMagick](https://imagemagick.org/security-policy/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| **Cloud OCR preprocessor** | Kadang provider sudah punya preprocessing internal | Tetap perlu input validation dan quality scoring |

```text id="qxxlkh"
Raw image
  │
```

Contoh sederhana:

```text id="1xyh4h"
→ rotate based on EXIF
→ crop document region
→ convert grayscale
→ denoise
→ adaptive threshold
→ deskew
→ Tesseract OCR
```

## 7. PDF Rasterization

Untuk scanned PDF, OCR engine biasanya memproses image per halaman. Maka PDF perlu di-render menjadi image.

```text id="v0h25x"
PDF
  │
  ├── page 1 → page-0001.png
  ├── page 2 → page-0002.png
  ├── page 3 → page-0003.png
  └── ...
```

### Java dengan PDFBox

```java id="4rh12m"
public final class PdfRasterizer {

    public List<Path> renderPages(Path pdfFile, Path outputDir, float dpi) {
        try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(pdfFile.toFile())) {
            PDFRenderer renderer = new PDFRenderer(document);

            List<Path> pageImages = new ArrayList<>();

            for (int pageIndex = 0; pageIndex < document.getNumberOfPages(); pageIndex++) {
                BufferedImage image = renderer.renderImageWithDPI(
                        pageIndex,
                        dpi,
                        ImageType.RGB
                );

                Path output = outputDir.resolve(
                        "page-" + String.format("%04d", pageIndex + 1) + ".png"
                );

                ImageIO.write(image, "png", output.toFile());
                pageImages.add(output);
            }

            return pageImages;

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new OcrPreprocessingException("Failed to rasterize PDF", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Practical defaults:

| Use Case | DPI |
|---|---:|
| Normal text OCR | 300 |
| Small text / low quality scan | 400 |
| Large page / memory constrained | 200-300 |
| Preview thumbnail only | 72-150 |

Trade-off:

```text id="6zfxq1"
Higher DPI
→ better OCR potential
→ larger image
→ higher memory
→ slower preprocessing
→ higher storage
```

---

## 8. DPI / Resize Strategy

Tesseract documentation menyebut Tesseract bekerja baik pada image setidaknya 300 dpi. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tapi di sistem nyata, metadata DPI kadang:

```text id="0uz1eg"
- tidak ada
- salah
- tidak relevan untuk foto HP
- berubah setelah resize
```

Jadi gunakan kombinasi:

```text id="9t22zb"
1. target DPI for PDF rendering
2. minimum text height heuristic
3. max megapixel guardrail
4. OCR confidence feedback
```

### Rule praktis

| Kondisi | Action |
|---|---|
| image terlalu kecil | upscale moderat |
| text height terlalu kecil | upscale |
| image sangat besar | downscale |
| PDF scan | render 300 DPI |
| KTP/ID card | crop ROI lalu upscale text region |
| receipt kecil | upscale + denoise + adaptive threshold |

Jangan upscaling ekstrem karena bisa membuat noise ikut membesar.

---

## 9. Grayscale Conversion

Grayscale membuat banyak operasi lebih sederhana.

```java id="n5z5o3"
public Mat toGrayscale(Mat input) {
    Mat gray = new Mat();

    if (input.channels() == 1) {
        return input.clone();
    }

    Imgproc.cvtColor(input, gray, Imgproc.COLOR_BGR2GRAY);
    return gray;
}
```

Kapan tidak cukup?

```text id="ryj9tx"
- dokumen dengan warna sebagai informasi penting
- stamp/signature detection
- colored form fields
- highlight/annotation extraction
```

Untuk OCR teks biasa, grayscale biasanya aman.

---

## 10. Thresholding / Binarization

Thresholding mengubah grayscale menjadi hitam-putih.

OpenCV menyediakan beberapa teknik thresholding, termasuk simple thresholding, adaptive thresholding, dan Otsu thresholding. ([opencv24-python-tutorials.readthedocs.io](https://opencv24-python-tutorials.readthedocs.io/en/latest/py_tutorials/py_imgproc/py_thresholding/py_thresholding.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Simple threshold

Cocok jika pencahayaan merata.

```java id="c5ebxz"
public Mat simpleThreshold(Mat gray) {
    Mat binary = new Mat();

    Imgproc.threshold(
            gray,
            binary,
            127,
            255,
            Imgproc.THRESH_BINARY
    );

    return binary;
}
```

### Otsu threshold

Cocok jika histogram foreground/background cukup terpisah.

```java id="ksr1do"
public Mat otsuThreshold(Mat gray) {
    Mat binary = new Mat();

    Imgproc.threshold(
            gray,
            binary,
            0,
            255,
            Imgproc.THRESH_BINARY | Imgproc.THRESH_OTSU
    );

    return binary;
}
```

### Adaptive threshold

Cocok untuk dokumen dengan pencahayaan tidak merata, misalnya foto HP.

```java id="mpr08l"
public Mat adaptiveThreshold(Mat gray) {
    Mat binary = new Mat();

    Imgproc.adaptiveThreshold(
            gray,
            binary,
            255,
            Imgproc.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C,
            Imgproc.THRESH_BINARY,
            31,
            15
    );

    return binary;
}
```

### Decision guide

| Kondisi image | Threshold method |
|---|---|
| scan bersih | Otsu |
| foto HP dengan shadow | Adaptive |
| document high contrast | Simple/Otsu |
| background textured | Adaptive + denoise |
| receipt/fax noisy | denoise + adaptive/otsu trial |

---

## 11. Denoising

Noise umum:

```text id="fr9qer"
- salt and pepper noise
- JPEG artifacts
- scanner dust
- fax artifacts
- shadow
- paper texture
- compression artifacts
```

Teknik:

| Technique | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| Median blur | salt-and-pepper noise |
| Gaussian blur | smoothing ringan sebelum threshold |
| Bilateral filter | reduce noise sambil menjaga edge |
| Morphological opening | remove small white/black noise |
| Non-local means | image denoising lebih kuat tapi lebih lambat |

### Median blur

```java id="08pa6h"
public Mat medianDenoise(Mat gray) {
    Mat denoised = new Mat();
    Imgproc.medianBlur(gray, denoised, 3);
    return denoised;
}
```

### Bilateral filter

```java id="9jsciz"
public Mat bilateralDenoise(Mat gray) {
    Mat denoised = new Mat();

    Imgproc.bilateralFilter(
            gray,
            denoised,
            9,
            75,
            75
    );

    return denoised;
}
```

Trade-off:

```text id="m7oesy"
Too little denoise → OCR sees noise as characters.
Too much denoise   → thin strokes disappear.
```

---

## 12. Deskew

Deskew mengoreksi kemiringan halaman/teks.

```text id="8n1ixn"
Input skewed text line:
////////////////////

After deskew:
--------------------
```

Pendekatan umum:

```text id="ic9ma4"
1. Convert to binary
2. Find foreground pixels or contours
3. Estimate angle using minAreaRect / Hough line
4. Rotate image by negative angle
```

### Deskew skeleton with OpenCV

```java id="rrcm7y"
public Mat deskew(Mat binaryOrGray) {
    Mat binary = new Mat();

    Imgproc.threshold(
            binaryOrGray,
            binary,
            0,
            255,
            Imgproc.THRESH_BINARY_INV | Imgproc.THRESH_OTSU
    );

    Mat points = new Mat();
    Core.findNonZero(binary, points);

    if (points.empty()) {
        return binaryOrGray.clone();
    }

    RotatedRect box = Imgproc.minAreaRect(new MatOfPoint2f(points));

    double angle = box.angle;

    if (angle < -45) {
        angle = 90 + angle;
    }

    if (Math.abs(angle) < 0.3) {
        return binaryOrGray.clone();
    }

    return rotate(binaryOrGray, angle);
}

private Mat rotate(Mat input, double angleDegrees) {
    Point center = new Point(input.width() / 2.0, input.height() / 2.0);
    Mat rotationMatrix = Imgproc.getRotationMatrix2D(center, angleDegrees, 1.0);

    Mat rotated = new Mat();
    Imgproc.warpAffine(
            input,
            rotated,
            rotationMatrix,
            input.size(),
            Imgproc.INTER_CUBIC,
            Core.BORDER_REPLICATE,
            new Scalar(255, 255, 255)
    );

    return rotated;
}
```

Caveat:

```text id="5pqbcn"
- minAreaRect bisa salah untuk dokumen multi-column
- table-heavy document bisa mempengaruhi angle
- logo/stamp besar bisa mengganggu
- perlu guardrail: jangan rotate jika confidence angle rendah
```

---

## 13. Rotation & EXIF Orientation

Banyak foto dari mobile menyimpan orientasi di EXIF, bukan memutar pixel actual.

Flow:

```text id="h5z5j3"
Read EXIF orientation
  │
  ├── 1 = normal
  ├── 3 = rotate 180
  ├── 6 = rotate 90 CW
  └── 8 = rotate 270 CW
```

Jika EXIF tidak diterapkan, OCR bisa membaca halaman sideways.

Practical rule:

```text id="q0dfr0"
Apply EXIF orientation before:
- OCR
- face detection
- crop
- thumbnail generation
```

---

## 14. Crop & Border Removal

Tesseract documentation menyebut terlalu besar border dapat menyebabkan masalah, sementara border kecil dapat membantu; missing border juga bisa menyebabkan masalah segmentation. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/ImproveQuality.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tujuan crop:

```text id="p42fxj"
- hilangkan meja/background
- hilangkan shadow tepi
- fokus ke document region
- kurangi noise
- kurangi ukuran processing
```

Tapi jangan crop terlalu agresif:

```text id="opnq6w"
Aggressive crop
→ text terpotong
→ OCR kehilangan awal/akhir kata
```

Practical approach:

```text id="1y7f93"
1. Detect document contour
2. Crop document area
3. Add safe margin/padding
4. Normalize perspective if needed
```

---

## 15. Perspective Correction

Untuk foto dokumen dari HP:

```text id="ckm4g5"
Document appears trapezoid
→ detect four corners
→ apply perspective transform
→ rectangular document
```

Pipeline:

```text id="2njnxw"
grayscale
→ blur
→ edge detection
→ find contours
→ choose largest quadrilateral
→ order corners
→ perspective transform
```

Caveat:

```text id="8a927f"
- gagal jika background ramai
- dokumen putih di atas meja putih sulit detect
- shadow bisa salah dianggap edge
- table/box besar bisa dianggap document contour
```

Untuk production, simpan fallback:

```text id="lvngml"
if perspective correction confidence low:
    use original normalized image
    or route to manual recapture/review
```

---

## 16. Morphological Operations

Morphology berguna setelah thresholding.

Contoh:

| Operation | Efek |
|---|---|
| Erosion | mengecilkan foreground |
| Dilation | membesarkan foreground |
| Opening | erosion lalu dilation; remove small noise |
| Closing | dilation lalu erosion; close small gaps |
| Top-hat | highlight bright small elements |
| Black-hat | highlight dark small elements |

OpenCV mendokumentasikan thresholding, dan morphological operations tersedia sebagai transformasi image processing umum di OpenCV; gunakan dengan hati-hati karena dapat mengubah bentuk karakter. ([opencv24-python-tutorials.readthedocs.io](https://opencv24-python-tutorials.readthedocs.io/en/latest/py_tutorials/py_imgproc/py_thresholding/py_thresholding.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Opening noise cleanup

```java id="3krvgh"
public Mat morphologicalOpening(Mat binary) {
    Mat cleaned = new Mat();

    Mat kernel = Imgproc.getStructuringElement(
            Imgproc.MORPH_RECT,
            new Size(2, 2)
    );

    Imgproc.morphologyEx(
            binary,
            cleaned,
            Imgproc.MORPH_OPEN,
            kernel
    );

    return cleaned;
}
```

### Closing broken strokes

```java id="mt906l"
public Mat morphologicalClosing(Mat binary) {
    Mat closed = new Mat();

    Mat kernel = Imgproc.getStructuringElement(
            Imgproc.MORPH_RECT,
            new Size(2, 1)
    );

    Imgproc.morphologyEx(
            binary,
            closed,
            Imgproc.MORPH_CLOSE,
            kernel
    );

    return closed;
}
```

---

## 17. Quality Scoring sebelum OCR

Quality scoring membantu menentukan:

```text id="fahod5"
- accept
- reject
- ask user recapture
- run enhanced preprocessing
- route to human review
- lower confidence expectation
```

### Metrics praktis

| Metric | Cara umum | Makna |
|---|---|---|
| Resolution | width × height / DPI | terlalu rendah → OCR buruk |
| Blur score | variance of Laplacian | rendah → image blur |
| Brightness | mean pixel intensity | terlalu gelap/terang |
| Contrast | standard deviation / histogram spread | rendah → teks sulit dibaca |
| Skew angle | text line angle | perlu deskew |
| Noise score | local variation / connected components | preprocessing perlu denoise |
| Text density | ratio foreground pixels / contours | empty/cropped wrong |
| Border ratio | whitespace margin | terlalu besar/kecil |
| Face/text conflict | face present in OCR doc | route doc type carefully |

### Blur score example

```java id="1v1lvx"
public double blurScore(Mat gray) {
    Mat laplacian = new Mat();
    Imgproc.Laplacian(gray, laplacian, CvType.CV_64F);

    MatOfDouble mean = new MatOfDouble();
    MatOfDouble stddev = new MatOfDouble();

    Core.meanStdDev(laplacian, mean, stddev);

    double sigma = stddev.toArray()[0];
    return sigma * sigma;
}
```

Interpretasi blur score harus dikalibrasi terhadap dataset Anda. Jangan memakai angka threshold universal tanpa sampling.

---

## 18. Multi-Variant OCR Strategy

Untuk dokumen heterogen, satu preprocessing pipeline sering tidak cukup.

Strategi:

```text id="4ctjsk"
Generate variants:
1. normalized grayscale
2. Otsu threshold
3. adaptive threshold
4. denoised + Otsu
5. sharpened + adaptive

Run OCR on selected variants
Choose result by:
- OCR confidence
- text length sanity
- expected field match
- language dictionary
- regex validation
```

Contoh:

```text id="lskkjk"
invoice_number expected pattern = INV-\d+

Variant A OCR:
"1NV-2026-001" confidence 0.71

Variant B OCR:
"INV-2026-001" confidence 0.89

Choose Variant B
```

Caveat:

```text id="vy99se"
Multi-variant OCR increases:
- CPU cost
- latency
- storage artifacts
- complexity
```

Gunakan untuk:

```text id="n9lkxj"
- low-confidence retry
- high-value documents
- field-specific extraction
- human-review assistance
```

---

## 20. Database Model

### `ocr_preprocessing_run`

```sql id="zx0p5a"
CREATE TABLE ocr_preprocessing_run (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id                UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
    source_asset_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    output_asset_id        UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    pipeline_name          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    pipeline_version       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    status                 VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    page_number            INTEGER,
    dpi                    INTEGER,
    width                  INTEGER,
    height                 INTEGER,

    blur_score             NUMERIC(12, 4),
    brightness_score       NUMERIC(12, 4),
    contrast_score         NUMERIC(12, 4),
    skew_angle_degrees     NUMERIC(8, 4),

    preprocessing_json     JSONB NOT NULL,
    error_code             VARCHAR(100),
    error_message          TEXT,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

### `ocr_image_variant`

```sql id="upyouy"
CREATE TABLE ocr_image_variant (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    preprocessing_run_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES ocr_preprocessing_run(id),
    variant_name             VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    asset_id                 UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    priority                 INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
    quality_json             JSONB,
    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (preprocessing_run_id, variant_name)
);
```

## 21. Pipeline Policy

Jangan hard-code pipeline untuk semua dokumen. Buat policy berdasarkan document type/job type.

```sql id="jd54yj"
CREATE TABLE ocr_preprocessing_policy (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    name                  VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    document_type          VARCHAR(100),
    media_kind             VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    target_dpi             INTEGER,
    max_megapixels         BIGINT,
    apply_exif_orientation BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    apply_deskew           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    apply_denoise          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    threshold_strategy     VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    generate_variants      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Contoh:

| Policy | Input | Strategy |
|---|---|---|
| `STANDARD_SCAN_OCR` | scanned PDF | 300 DPI, grayscale, Otsu, deskew |
| `MOBILE_DOCUMENT_OCR` | phone photo | orientation, crop, perspective, adaptive threshold |
| `RECEIPT_OCR` | receipt image | denoise, adaptive threshold, multi-variant |
| `ID_CARD_OCR` | KTP/passport | crop ROI, avoid aggressive threshold, field-specific OCR |
| `LOW_QUALITY_RETRY` | failed OCR | generate multiple variants |
