## 23. Worker Skeleton

```java id="gruilz"
public final class OcrPreprocessingWorker {

    private final ObjectStorageClient storageClient;
    private final MediaAssetRepository assetRepository;
    private final OcrPreprocessingPolicyRepository policyRepository;
    private final PdfRasterizer pdfRasterizer;
    private final ImagePreprocessor imagePreprocessor;
    private final OcrPreprocessingRunRepository runRepository;

    public void execute(UUID jobId, UUID sourceAssetId) {
        MediaAsset sourceAsset = assetRepository.get(sourceAssetId);
        OcrPreprocessingPolicy policy = policyRepository.resolve(sourceAsset);

        UUID runId = runRepository.start(jobId, sourceAssetId, policy);

        Path sourceFile = storageClient.downloadToTemp(sourceAsset.objectRef());

        try {
            List<Path> inputImages = sourceAsset.isPdf()
                    ? pdfRasterizer.renderPages(sourceFile, tempDir(), policy.targetDpi())
                    : List.of(sourceFile);

            List<PreprocessedImage> outputs = new ArrayList<>();

            for (int i = 0; i < inputImages.size(); i++) {
                PreprocessedImage processed = imagePreprocessor.preprocess(
                        inputImages.get(i),
                        policy,
                        i + 1
                );

                outputs.add(processed);
            }

            List<MediaAsset> outputAssets = storageClient.uploadPreprocessedImages(outputs);

            runRepository.markCompleted(runId, outputs, outputAssets);

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            runRepository.markFailed(runId, ex);
            throw ex;

        } finally {
            cleanupTempFiles(sourceFile);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 24. Image Preprocessor Skeleton

```java id="uif3ij"
public final class ImagePreprocessor {

    public PreprocessedImage preprocess(
            Path input,
            OcrPreprocessingPolicy policy,
            int pageNumber
    ) {
        Mat image = Imgcodecs.imread(input.toAbsolutePath().toString());

        if (image.empty()) {
            throw new OcrPreprocessingException("Unable to read image");
        }

        List<PreprocessingStep> steps = new ArrayList<>();

        Mat current = image;

        if (policy.applyExifOrientation()) {
            current = applyOrientation(input, current);
            steps.add(PreprocessingStep.of("APPLY_EXIF_ORIENTATION"));
        }

        current = normalizeResolution(current, policy);
        steps.add(PreprocessingStep.of("NORMALIZE_RESOLUTION"));

        Mat gray = toGrayscale(current);
        steps.add(PreprocessingStep.of("GRAYSCALE"));

        QualityScores before = assessQuality(gray);

        if (policy.applyDenoise()) {
            gray = medianDenoise(gray);
            steps.add(PreprocessingStep.of("DENOISE_MEDIAN"));
        }

        Mat binary = switch (policy.thresholdStrategy()) {
            case OTSU -> otsuThreshold(gray);
            case ADAPTIVE -> adaptiveThreshold(gray);
            case NONE -> gray;
        };

        steps.add(PreprocessingStep.of("THRESHOLD_" + policy.thresholdStrategy()));

        if (policy.applyDeskew()) {
            binary = deskew(binary);
            steps.add(PreprocessingStep.of("DESKEW"));
        }

        QualityScores after = assessQuality(binary);

        return new PreprocessedImage(
                pageNumber,
                binary,
                steps,
                before,
                after
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 25. Failure Model

| Failure | Meaning | Retry? |
|---|---|---|
| `IMAGE_READ_FAILED` | image corrupt / unsupported | No |
| `PDF_RENDER_FAILED` | PDF cannot render | No/limited |
| `PDF_PAGE_TOO_LARGE` | rendered page exceeds memory limit | No or lower DPI retry |
| `PREPROCESSING_TIMEOUT` | OpenCV/native operation stuck | Limited |
| `TEMP_STORAGE_FULL` | node disk full | Yes |
| `IMAGE_TOO_LARGE` | decompression bomb / huge dimensions | No |
| `LOW_QUALITY_INPUT` | blur/contrast/resolution below policy | No, ask recapture/review |
| `NO_TEXT_REGION_DETECTED` | cannot find text/document | No or human review |
| `NATIVE_LIBRARY_ERROR` | OpenCV/ImageMagick issue | Limited |
| `SECURITY_POLICY_REJECTED` | ImageMagick/PDF policy reject | No |

Fallback example:

```text id="8whgqo"
PDF_PAGE_TOO_LARGE at 400 DPI
→ retry rasterization at 300 DPI
→ if still too large, reject page/job with clear error
```

---

## 26. Security Concerns

Image preprocessing sering memakai native libraries atau external binaries. Ini harus dianggap risk boundary.

### Rules

```text id="2x8irm"
- Jangan run ImageMagick/FFmpeg/PDF tools tanpa timeout
- Jangan concat command string dengan user input
- Jalankan di container dengan CPU/memory/temp disk limit
- Batasi file size dan pixel count sebelum decode
- Gunakan private temp directory
- Cleanup temp file
- Jangan simpan preprocessed artifact lebih lama dari perlu
- Strip sensitive EXIF jika output dipakai downstream/share
```

ImageMagick menyediakan security policy untuk membatasi resource seperti memory, allowed paths, number of images, workflow runtime, disk usage, dan coder allow/deny; ini penting bila ImageMagick dipakai untuk memproses untrusted file. ([ImageMagick](https://imagemagick.org/security-policy/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

```json id="8vb3ke"
{
  "eventType": "IMAGE_DESKEW_APPLIED",
  "jobId": "job_789",
  "assetId": "ast_page_001",
  "metadata": {
    "pageNumber": 1,
    "angleDegrees": -1.42,
    "pipelineVersion": "ocr-preprocess-v1"
  }
}
```

---

## 29. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| OCR langsung raw image | Akurasi rendah | preprocessing pipeline |
| Satu pipeline untuk semua dokumen | over/under-processing | policy per document type |
| Render PDF 600 DPI default | memory/cost tinggi | 300 DPI default + adaptive |
| Threshold agresif untuk semua image | stroke hilang | choose threshold by quality |
| Tidak apply EXIF orientation | OCR sideways | orientation correction |
| Deskew tanpa guardrail | image malah rusak | angle threshold/confidence |
| Crop terlalu agresif | text terpotong | safe padding |
| Tidak simpan preprocessing version | sulit audit | persist pipeline version |
| Tidak ada quality scoring | gagal baru ketahuan di OCR | pre-OCR triage |
| External binary tanpa timeout | worker hang | timeout/resource limit |
| Simpan semua variant selamanya | storage bloat | retention per artifact |
| Percaya DPI metadata foto HP | salah scaling | use pixel/text heuristic |

---

## 30. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="8zhpux"
Input
[ ] image/PDF sudah tervalidasi MIME dan checksum
[ ] page count/resolution sudah diketahui
[ ] max size/max megapixel policy ada
[ ] encrypted PDF sudah ditangani

Preprocessing
[ ] EXIF orientation diterapkan
[ ] PDF rasterization DPI jelas
[ ] grayscale conversion ada
[ ] denoise strategy jelas
[ ] threshold strategy jelas
[ ] deskew punya guardrail
[ ] crop/perspective correction tidak merusak text
[ ] output image format jelas

Quality
[ ] blur score dihitung
[ ] brightness/contrast dihitung
[ ] skew angle dicatat
[ ] low-quality input bisa ditolak/review
[ ] OCR confidence feedback dipakai

Persistence
[ ] preprocessing run disimpan
[ ] output asset disimpan sebagai derived artifact
[ ] relationship raw → preprocessed disimpan
[ ] pipeline version disimpan
[ ] preprocessing params disimpan

Security/Ops
[ ] native tool timeout ada
[ ] temp file cleanup ada
[ ] container resource limit ada
[ ] ImageMagick/FFmpeg/PDF tool policy ada jika digunakan
[ ] metrics dan audit event ada
```

## 31. Minimal Implementation untuk Tahap Awal

Implementasi awal yang realistis:

```text id="xvye74"
1. PDF rasterization with PDFBox at 300 DPI
2. EXIF orientation correction
3. grayscale conversion
4. Otsu threshold for clean scan
5. adaptive threshold for mobile/photo input
6. median denoise
7. basic deskew
8. blur/contrast/skew quality scoring
9. preprocessing_run table
10. output asset + artifact relationship
```

Minimal pipeline:

```text id="gy9x76"
PDF/image input
→ validate metadata
→ rasterize if PDF
→ orientation correction
→ grayscale
→ denoise
→ threshold
→ deskew
→ save OCR-ready image
→ run OCR
```

7/13/2026, 10:27:00 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 7/20**: OCR basic dengan Tesseract/Tess4J. Fokusnya integrasi OCR lokal/on-prem di Java worker, bukan endpoint blocking di Jersey.

# Seri 7/20 - OCR Basic dengan Tesseract/Tess4J  
## Raw Text Extraction, Language Pack, PSM/OEM, Confidence Score, Structured Result, dan Java Worker Integration

## 1. Introduction

Seri ini membahas **OCR basic**: bagaimana menjalankan OCR lokal/on-prem menggunakan **Tesseract** melalui **Tess4J** dalam arsitektur Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice.

Posisi arsitekturnya tetap sama:

```text id="7khs6a"
Jersey API
  → create OCR job
  → return jobId

OCR Worker
  → ambil preprocessed image/PDF page
  → jalankan Tesseract/Tess4J
  → ambil text + confidence + bounding data
  → simpan result
  → update job state
```

Tess4J adalah Java JNA wrapper untuk Tesseract OCR API, tersedia dengan lisensi Apache 2.0 dan di Maven Central; fiturnya mencakup OCR untuk TIFF, JPEG, GIF, PNG, BMP, multi-page TIFF, dan PDF document format. ([tess4j.sourceforge.net](https://tess4j.sourceforge.net/))

Untuk versi dependency, jangan copy versi dari contoh lama. MvnRepository mencatat group `net.sourceforge.tess4j` dan last release pada **May 27, 2026**, jadi versi harus dipin dari Maven Central/repository perusahaan saat implementasi. ([Maven Repository](https://mvnrepository.com/artifact/net.sourceforge.tess4j))

---

## 2. Mental Model OCR Basic

```text id="8lbmb3"
OCR Basic
│
├── Input
│   ├── preprocessed image
│   ├── rasterized PDF page
│   └── normalized grayscale/binary image
│
├── Tesseract Configuration
│   ├── tessdata path
│   ├── language pack
│   ├── OEM: OCR Engine Mode
│   ├── PSM: Page Segmentation Mode
│   ├── character whitelist/blacklist
│   └── custom variables
│
├── Execution
│   ├── doOCR image
│   ├── doOCR region of interest
│   ├── per page OCR
│   └── timeout / resource isolation
│
├── Output
│   ├── plain text
│   ├── word-level confidence
│   ├── line/block/page structure
│   ├── bounding box
│   ├── HOCR/TSV optional
│   └── searchable PDF optional
│
└── Persistence
    ├── ocr_result
    ├── ocr_page_result
    ├── ocr_word_result
    ├── confidence summary
    └── audit event
```

Tesseract command-line default OCR sederhana adalah `tesseract imagename outputbase`; default-nya memakai English dan Page Segmentation Mode `3`, dan file `eng.traineddata` serta `osd.traineddata` perlu berada di folder `tessdata` atau dikonfigurasi lewat `TESSDATA_PREFIX`. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html))

---

## 3. Diagram OCR Worker

```text id="qqiwcg"
media_job: OCR
   │
   ▼
OCR Worker
   │
   ├── load job
   ├── load source asset / preprocessed page asset
   ├── resolve OCR policy
   ├── resolve tessdata path
   ├── configure language
   ├── configure PSM/OEM
   ├── run Tess4J
   ├── extract text
   ├── extract confidence
   ├── normalize result
   ├── persist OCR result
   └── update job state
        │
        ├── COMPLETED
        ├── NEEDS_REVIEW
        └── FAILED
```

---

## 4. OCR Basic Skill Map

| Skill | Yang perlu dikuasai | Relevansi praktis |
|---|---|---|
| Tesseract runtime | native binary/library, `tessdata`, language packs | OCR worker tidak stabil kalau runtime path tidak jelas |
| Tess4J integration | Java wrapper, `Tesseract` instance, native dependencies | Cara paling langsung menjalankan OCR lokal di Java |
| Language config | `eng`, `ind`, `eng+ind`, script model | Bahasa salah = akurasi turun |
| OEM | LSTM vs legacy engine | Tesseract 5 memakai `--oem 1` untuk LSTM/neural network; legacy model bergantung pada traineddata tertentu. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html)) |
| PSM | page segmentation mode | Layout salah = text urutan kacau |
| Preprocessed input | 300 DPI, grayscale/binary, deskew | Kualitas input sangat menentukan OCR |
| Confidence score | word/page confidence | Routing ke human review |
| Bounding box | posisi kata/baris/block | Highlight UI, review, field extraction |
| Structured result | page/line/word model | Jangan simpan plain text saja |
| Timeout & isolation | native processing guardrail | Cegah worker hang |
| Error classification | invalid image, missing tessdata, timeout | Job retry/failure jelas |
| Auditability | provider version, language, PSM/OEM, confidence | Penting untuk traceability |

---

## 5. Maven Dependency

Gunakan versi aktual dari Maven Central/internal repository saat implementasi.

```xml id="epstse"
<dependency>
    <groupId>net.sourceforge.tess4j</groupId>
    <artifactId>tess4j</artifactId>
    <version>${tess4j.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Prinsip versioning:

```text id="4kd0s4"
- pin exact version, jangan floating
- align dengan Java 17 runtime
- test di Linux container target
- pastikan native library compatible
- simpan versi Tess4J/Tesseract di result metadata
```

Tess4J dikembangkan dan dites pada Windows dan Linux; di Linux, library `libtesseract.so` harus tersedia lewat install/build Tesseract, sedangkan language data pack perlu diletakkan di folder `tessdata`. ([tess4j.sourceforge.net](https://tess4j.sourceforge.net/usage.html))

## 6. Runtime Layout di Docker/Kubernetes

Contoh layout container:

```text id="g2xcbs"
/app
  ├── app.jar
  └── config

/opt/tesseract
  └── tessdata
      ├── eng.traineddata
      ├── ind.traineddata
      └── osd.traineddata

/usr/lib
  └── libtesseract.so
```

Environment:

```text id="um5lng"
TESSDATA_PREFIX=/opt/tesseract
OCR_DEFAULT_LANG=ind+eng
OCR_DEFAULT_PSM=3
OCR_DEFAULT_OEM=1
```

Catatan penting:

```text id="o4vsfb"
TESSDATA_PREFIX menunjuk ke parent folder dari tessdata,
bukan ke tessdata langsung, tergantung cara library/runtime membaca path.
```

## 7. Dockerfile Contoh

Contoh konseptual untuk worker Linux:

```dockerfile id="4q036q"
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
       tesseract-ocr \
       tesseract-ocr-eng \
       tesseract-ocr-ind \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

ENV TESSDATA_PREFIX=/usr/share/tesseract-ocr/5/tessdata

WORKDIR /app

COPY target/media-ocr-worker.jar /app/app.jar

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/app.jar"]
```

Catatan:

```text id="qfceix"
Path tessdata berbeda antar distro/base image.
Pastikan diverifikasi dengan integration test di image final.
```

## 8. Basic Tess4J Usage

```java id="x9rqr4"
import net.sourceforge.tess4j.ITesseract;
import net.sourceforge.tess4j.Tesseract;
import net.sourceforge.tess4j.TesseractException;

import java.io.File;

public final class BasicOcrService {

    public String extractText(File imageFile) {
        ITesseract tesseract = new Tesseract();

        tesseract.setDatapath("/usr/share/tesseract-ocr/5/tessdata");
        tesseract.setLanguage("ind+eng");

        try {
            return tesseract.doOCR(imageFile);
        } catch (TesseractException e) {
            throw new OcrExecutionException("Tesseract OCR failed", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Namun untuk produksi, jangan instantiate dan configure sembarangan di banyak tempat. Buat provider abstraction.

---

## 9. OCR Provider Abstraction

```java id="qegj33"
public interface OcrProvider {
    OcrProviderResult extract(OcrProviderRequest request);
}
```

```java id="o1b5hs"
public record OcrProviderRequest(
        UUID jobId,
        UUID assetId,
        Path imagePath,
        String language,
        int pageSegmentationMode,
        int ocrEngineMode,
        Map<String, String> variables
) {}
```

```java id="o9g3e3"
public record OcrProviderResult(
        String providerName,
        String providerVersion,
        String text,
        double meanConfidence,
        List<OcrWord> words,
        Map<String, Object> rawMetadata
) {}
```

Implementasi:

```java id="qjxj0n"
public final class Tess4jOcrProvider implements OcrProvider {

    private final Tess4jFactory factory;

    public Tess4jOcrProvider(Tess4jFactory factory) {
        this.factory = factory;
    }

    @Override
    public OcrProviderResult extract(OcrProviderRequest request) {
        ITesseract tesseract = factory.create(request);

        try {
            String text = tesseract.doOCR(request.imagePath().toFile());

            return new OcrProviderResult(
                    "TESSERACT_TESS4J",
                    detectProviderVersion(),
                    text,
                    -1.0,
                    List.of(),
                    Map.of(
                            "language", request.language(),
                            "psm", request.pageSegmentationMode(),
                            "oem", request.ocrEngineMode()
                    )
            );

        } catch (TesseractException e) {
            throw new OcrExecutionException("Tess4J OCR failed", e);
        }
    }

    private String detectProviderVersion() {
        return "runtime-detected-or-configured";
    }
}
```

---

## 10. Tess4J Factory

Buat factory agar config konsisten.

```java id="gnah56"
public final class Tess4jFactory {

    private final OcrRuntimeConfig config;

    public Tess4jFactory(OcrRuntimeConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
    }

    public ITesseract create(OcrProviderRequest request) {
        Tesseract tesseract = new Tesseract();

        tesseract.setDatapath(config.tessdataPath());
        tesseract.setLanguage(request.language());

        tesseract.setOcrEngineMode(request.ocrEngineMode());
        tesseract.setPageSegMode(request.pageSegmentationMode());

        request.variables().forEach(tesseract::setVariable);

        return tesseract;
    }
}
```

Config:

```java id="tkia0v"
public record OcrRuntimeConfig(
        String tessdataPath,
        String defaultLanguage,
        int defaultPsm,
        int defaultOem,
        Duration timeout
) {}
```

---

## 11. Language Pack Strategy

Tesseract memakai language code seperti:

```text id="iuwi6v"
eng = English
ind = Indonesian
osd = Orientation and script detection
```

Tesseract mendukung penggunaan beberapa bahasa dengan format `LANG+LANG`; dokumentasi command-line menunjukkan contoh `eng+deu`, dan urutan bahasa dapat mempengaruhi waktu OCR maupun output. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html))

Untuk konteks Indonesia/enterprise:

| Dokumen | Language config awal |
|---|---|
| Dokumen Bahasa Indonesia | `ind` |
| Dokumen campuran Indonesia + English | `ind+eng` |
| Invoice/vendor global | `eng+ind` atau policy per vendor |
| KTP/ID lokal | `ind` + whitelist field tertentu |
| Dokumen multilingual | detect language dulu atau route by metadata |

Prinsip:

```text id="n44qxd"
Lebih banyak language pack ≠ selalu lebih akurat.
Lebih banyak language pack bisa memperlambat OCR dan memunculkan ambiguity.
```

---

## 12. OEM - OCR Engine Mode

Untuk Tesseract 5, dokumentasi menyebut `--oem 1` untuk LSTM/neural network dan `--oem 0` untuk legacy Tesseract; legacy model hanya tersedia pada traineddata tertentu. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html))

Praktisnya:

| OEM | Meaning | Kapan dipakai |
|---:|---|---|
| `1` | LSTM/neural network | default modern |
| `0` | legacy | hanya kalau traineddata dan use case mendukung |
| `3` | default engine mode | biarkan Tesseract pilih, tapi kurang eksplisit |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="1bqwmf"
Gunakan OEM=1 sebagai default untuk modern deployment,
kecuali ada benchmark internal yang menunjukkan mode lain lebih baik.
```

---

## 13. PSM - Page Segmentation Mode

PSM menentukan bagaimana Tesseract menganggap layout halaman.

Default command-line sederhana memakai Page Segmentation Mode `3`. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html))

Mental model:

```text id="r7ftwp"
PSM salah
→ layout salah
→ reading order kacau
→ confidence turun
→ hasil ekstraksi field buruk
```

Practical mapping:

| Use Case | PSM yang sering dicoba |
|---|---:|
| full page document | `3` |
| single uniform block of text | `6` |
| single text line | `7` |
| single word | `8` |
| single character | `10` |
| sparse text / label | `11` |
| orientation/script detection | perlu `osd` / config terkait |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="i01zsf"
Jangan satu PSM untuk semua.
Buat OCR policy per document type / field type.
```

Contoh field OCR:

```text id="c5svs3"
Full page invoice → PSM 3
Cropped invoice number region → PSM 7 atau 8
Cropped amount field → PSM 7
Captcha-like/single char field → PSM 10, tapi hati-hati policy/safety
```

---

## 14. Character Whitelist / Field-Specific OCR

Untuk field tertentu, whitelist bisa membantu.

Contoh invoice number:

```java id="hph6i7"
tesseract.setVariable(
        "tessedit_char_whitelist",
        "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789-/."
);
```

Contoh amount:

```java id="r7vdxt"
tesseract.setVariable(
        "tessedit_char_whitelist",
        "0123456789.,"
);
```

Gunakan whitelist hanya untuk **ROI/field-level OCR**, bukan full page.

Anti-pattern:

```text id="xlum91"
Full document OCR dengan whitelist angka
→ semua teks non-angka hilang
→ result tidak bisa diaudit dengan baik
```

---

Jangan hanya simpan:

```text id="y77j6a"
String text
```

## 16. Getting Word-Level Data

Tess4J biasanya dapat mengambil hasil lebih detail memakai API result iterator / word data, tergantung versi API yang dipakai. Secara arsitektur, bentukkan output internal seperti ini:

```java id="2553ph"
public record OcrWord(
        String text,
        double confidence,
        int x,
        int y,
        int width,
        int height,
        int pageNumber,
        int lineNumber
) {}
```

Output word-level penting untuk:

```text id="ybkjt7"
- highlight hasil OCR di UI
- human review
- field extraction
- confidence aggregation
- debugging OCR buruk
- layout-aware processing
```

---

## 17. Confidence Score

Confidence score jangan dianggap mutlak benar. Gunakan sebagai sinyal.

```text id="aerfw8"
High confidence    → likely acceptable
Medium confidence  → maybe accepted with validation
Low confidence     → human review / retry preprocessing
```

Contoh aggregation:

```java id="ebk6hj"
public double meanConfidence(List<OcrWord> words) {
    return words.stream()
            .filter(word -> word.confidence() >= 0)
            .mapToDouble(OcrWord::confidence)
            .average()
            .orElse(-1.0);
}
```

Weighted confidence lebih baik:

```java id="gfhtqf"
public double weightedConfidence(List<OcrWord> words) {
    double totalChars = 0;
    double weighted = 0;

    for (OcrWord word : words) {
        int length = Math.max(1, word.text().length());
        totalChars += length;
        weighted += word.confidence() * length;
    }

    return totalChars == 0 ? -1.0 : weighted / totalChars;
}
```

Threshold awal:

| Confidence | Action |
|---:|---|
| `>= 0.90` | accept untuk raw text |
| `0.75-0.90` | accept with validation atau review sebagian |
| `< 0.75` | review / retry preprocessing |
| missing | treat as unknown, jangan auto-approve |

Kalibrasikan threshold terhadap dataset perusahaan. Jangan percaya angka default universal.

---

## 18. Plain Text vs HOCR vs TSV vs Searchable PDF

Tesseract dapat menghasilkan output default text; dokumentasi juga menunjukkan searchable PDF output dengan config `pdf` dan HOCR output dengan config `hocr`, yang berisi struktur seperti page, area, paragraph, line, word, dan confidence word. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html))

| Output | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|
| Plain text | simple search, simple extraction |
| HOCR | layout-aware UI, bounding boxes, word confidence |
| TSV | tabular post-processing |
| Searchable PDF | user-facing document artifact |
| JSON internal | normalized application result |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="4vse6o"
Internal system result → normalized JSON.
User export          → searchable PDF / text / CSV.
Review UI            → JSON + bounding boxes.
```

---

## 19. Database Schema

### `ocr_result`

```sql id="kx7yf7"
CREATE TABLE ocr_result (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id                UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
    source_asset_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    provider_name          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    provider_version       VARCHAR(100),
    tess4j_version         VARCHAR(100),
    language_config        VARCHAR(100),
    page_segmentation_mode INTEGER,
    ocr_engine_mode        INTEGER,

    full_text              TEXT,
    mean_confidence        NUMERIC(6, 5),
    result_json            JSONB NOT NULL,

    requires_review        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    review_reason          TEXT,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (job_id)
);
```

### `ocr_page_result`

```sql id="ocv9gk"
CREATE TABLE ocr_page_result (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    ocr_result_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES ocr_result(id),
    page_number            INTEGER NOT NULL,
    source_asset_id        UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    text                   TEXT,
    mean_confidence        NUMERIC(6, 5),
    width                  INTEGER,
    height                 INTEGER,
    word_count             INTEGER,

    result_json            JSONB NOT NULL,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (ocr_result_id, page_number)
);
```

### `ocr_word_result`

```sql id="r9b48v"
CREATE TABLE ocr_word_result (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    ocr_page_result_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES ocr_page_result(id),

    word_index             INTEGER NOT NULL,
    text                   TEXT NOT NULL,
    confidence             NUMERIC(6, 5),

    bbox_x                 INTEGER,
    bbox_y                 INTEGER,
    bbox_width             INTEGER,
    bbox_height            INTEGER,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Full-text search

```sql id="kzyzy5"
ALTER TABLE ocr_result
ADD COLUMN search_vector tsvector;

CREATE INDEX idx_ocr_result_search
ON ocr_result USING GIN (search_vector);
```

Untuk Bahasa Indonesia, PostgreSQL built-in full-text configuration mungkin tidak sekuat English stemming. Untuk awal, gunakan `simple`, lalu evaluasi search engine atau analyzer khusus jika search menjadi core feature.

## 20. MyBatis Mapper

```java id="qme1pw"
public interface OcrResultMapper {

    void insertOcrResult(OcrResultRecord record);

    void insertPageResult(OcrPageResultRecord record);

    void insertWordResults(@Param("words") List<OcrWordResultRecord> words);

    Optional<OcrResultRecord> findByJobId(@Param("jobId") UUID jobId);
}
```

Idempotent insert pattern:

```sql id="ajic33"
INSERT INTO ocr_result (
    id,
    job_id,
    source_asset_id,
    provider_name,
    provider_version,
    tess4j_version,
    language_config,
    page_segmentation_mode,
    ocr_engine_mode,
    full_text,
    mean_confidence,
    result_json,
    requires_review,
    review_reason
) VALUES (
    #{id},
    #{jobId},
    #{sourceAssetId},
    #{providerName},
    #{providerVersion},
    #{tess4jVersion},
    #{languageConfig},
    #{pageSegmentationMode},
    #{ocrEngineMode},
    #{fullText},
    #{meanConfidence},
    #{resultJson}::jsonb,
    #{requiresReview},
    #{reviewReason}
)
ON CONFLICT (job_id) DO NOTHING
```

## 21. OCR Policy

Jangan hard-code language/PSM/OEM di service.

```sql id="wk8nmw"
CREATE TABLE ocr_policy (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    name                    VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    document_type            VARCHAR(100),
    media_kind               VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    language_config          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    page_segmentation_mode   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    ocr_engine_mode          INTEGER NOT NULL,

    mean_confidence_threshold NUMERIC(6,5),
    enable_word_result        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    enable_hocr_output        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    enable_searchable_pdf     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    character_whitelist       TEXT,
    character_blacklist       TEXT,

    created_at                TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at                TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Contoh policy:

| Policy | Document | Language | PSM | OEM |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `STANDARD_ID_OCR` | ID/KTP | `ind` | `6` | `1` |
| `FULL_PAGE_DOC_OCR` | general document | `ind+eng` | `3` | `1` |
| `INVOICE_FIELD_OCR` | invoice field ROI | `eng+ind` | `7` | `1` |
| `SINGLE_WORD_OCR` | cropped label | `eng` | `8` | `1` |

## 22. Worker Flow

```text id="0i3b8s"
OCR job claimed
  │
  ▼
load OCR policy
  │
  ▼
load preprocessed image assets
  │
  ▼
for each page:
  ├── configure Tesseract
  ├── run OCR
  ├── extract text/words/confidence
  ├── persist page result
  └── append full text
  │
  ▼
calculate confidence summary
  │
  ├── confidence low → NEEDS_REVIEW
  └── confidence ok  → COMPLETED
```

---

## 23. OCR Worker Skeleton

```java id="tw72y5"
public final class OcrWorker {

    private final MediaJobRepository jobRepository;
    private final MediaAssetRepository assetRepository;
    private final ObjectStorageClient storageClient;
    private final OcrPolicyRepository policyRepository;
    private final OcrProvider ocrProvider;
    private final OcrResultRepository resultRepository;

    public void execute(UUID jobId) {
        MediaJob job = jobRepository.get(jobId);

        if (resultRepository.existsByJobId(jobId)) {
            jobRepository.markCompleted(jobId);
            return;
        }

        OcrPolicy policy = policyRepository.resolve(job);

        List<MediaAsset> pageAssets = assetRepository.findOcrReadyAssets(job.sourceAssetId());

        List<OcrPageOutput> pages = new ArrayList<>();

        for (MediaAsset pageAsset : pageAssets) {
            Path imagePath = storageClient.downloadToTemp(pageAsset.objectRef());

            try {
                OcrProviderRequest request = new OcrProviderRequest(
                        job.id(),
                        pageAsset.id(),
                        imagePath,
                        policy.languageConfig(),
                        policy.pageSegmentationMode(),
                        policy.ocrEngineMode(),
                        policy.toTesseractVariables()
                );

                OcrProviderResult result = ocrProvider.extract(request);

                pages.add(OcrPageOutput.from(pageAsset, result));

            } finally {
                cleanupTempFile(imagePath);
            }
        }

        OcrAggregateResult aggregate = OcrAggregateResult.from(job, policy, pages);

        resultRepository.persist(aggregate);

        if (aggregate.requiresReview(policy.meanConfidenceThreshold())) {
            jobRepository.markNeedsReview(jobId, aggregate.meanConfidence(), "LOW_OCR_CONFIDENCE");
        } else {
            jobRepository.markCompleted(jobId);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 24. Timeout Strategy

Tess4J API langsung sulit dibatalkan jika native call stuck. Untuk workload production dengan untrusted files, ada dua pendekatan:

### Option A - In-process Tess4J

```text id="h2dbm2"
Java worker JVM
  └── Tess4J native call
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="43y47f"
- mudah dipakai
- overhead rendah
- result langsung di memory
```

Risiko:

```text id="e73osg"
- native crash bisa berdampak ke JVM
- timeout/cancel lebih sulit
- memory leak native lebih berbahaya
```

### Option B - Out-of-process Tesseract CLI

```text id="80qrhv"
Java worker
  └── ProcessBuilder: tesseract input output ...
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="75rq29"
- timeout bisa destroy process
- crash isolated
- lebih aman untuk untrusted heavy input
```

Risiko:

```text id="ahet7l"
- parsing output lebih manual
- overhead process
- perlu file IO
```

Untuk production regulated/untrusted upload, saya cenderung memilih:

```text id="eklf56"
Tess4J untuk controlled/internal/simple input.
Tesseract CLI subprocess untuk high-risk/untrusted/heavy input.
```

---

## 25. CLI Provider Alternative

Tesseract command-line dapat menghasilkan text, HOCR, atau searchable PDF. Dokumentasi menunjukkan output default text, config `hocr` untuk HOCR, dan config `pdf` untuk searchable PDF. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html))

Contoh command:

```bash id="p71x9u"
tesseract input.png stdout -l ind+eng --oem 1 --psm 3
```

Java wrapper:

```java id="ztsabq"
public String runTesseractCli(Path imagePath, OcrPolicy policy) {
    ProcessBuilder pb = new ProcessBuilder(
            "tesseract",
            imagePath.toAbsolutePath().toString(),
            "stdout",
            "-l", policy.languageConfig(),
            "--oem", String.valueOf(policy.ocrEngineMode()),
            "--psm", String.valueOf(policy.pageSegmentationMode())
    );

    pb.redirectErrorStream(false);

    try {
        Process process = pb.start();

        boolean finished = process.waitFor(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS);

        if (!finished) {
            process.destroyForcibly();
            throw new OcrTimeoutException("Tesseract timed out");
        }

        String stdout = new String(process.getInputStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        String stderr = new String(process.getErrorStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        if (process.exitValue() != 0) {
            throw new OcrExecutionException("Tesseract failed: " + stderr);
        }

        return stdout;

    } catch (IOException e) {
        throw new OcrExecutionException("Failed to start Tesseract", e);
    } catch (InterruptedException e) {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        throw new OcrExecutionException("Interrupted while running Tesseract", e);
    }
}
```

Security note:

```text id="p1qpz2"
Gunakan ProcessBuilder arg list.
Jangan shell-concat command string.
```

---

## 26. Failure Classification

| Failure | Meaning | Retry? |
|---|---|---|
| `TESSDATA_NOT_FOUND` | language data/path salah | No, deployment/config issue |
| `UNSUPPORTED_LANGUAGE` | traineddata tidak ada | No |
| `IMAGE_READ_FAILED` | image corrupt | No |
| `OCR_TIMEOUT` | native/CLI terlalu lama | Limited |
| `NATIVE_LIBRARY_LOAD_FAILED` | libtesseract/JNA issue | No, deployment issue |
| `OCR_ENGINE_CRASHED` | native crash/process exit abnormal | Limited, then poison |
| `LOW_CONFIDENCE` | OCR selesai tapi buruk | No, route review/retry preprocessing |
| `EMPTY_TEXT` | output kosong | No/depends, maybe review |
| `TEMP_FILE_ERROR` | disk/permission issue | Yes if infra transient |
| `OUT_OF_MEMORY` | memory limit hit | Limited with smaller page batch/DPI |

---

## 27. Empty Text Handling

Output kosong tidak selalu error teknis.

Possible meanings:

```text id="to2jur"
- blank page
- wrong orientation
- text too small
- thresholding too aggressive
- wrong language
- wrong PSM
- image is not a document
- OCR failed silently
```

Handling:

```text id="s8pjwl"
if blank-page detection true:
    mark page as BLANK
else if quality low:
    retry preprocessing variant
else:
    mark NEEDS_REVIEW / FAILED depending policy
```

---

## 28. Confidence-Based Workflow

```text id="gzjhnv"
OCR completed
  │
  ▼
calculate confidence
  │
  ├── high confidence
  │      └── COMPLETED
  │
  ├── medium confidence
  │      ├── run field validation
  │      └── maybe COMPLETED_WITH_WARNINGS
  │
  └── low confidence
         ├── retry alternate preprocessing
         └── NEEDS_REVIEW
```

Example:

```java id="3esaf6"
public boolean requiresReview(OcrAggregateResult result, OcrPolicy policy) {
    if (result.fullText().isBlank()) {
        return true;
    }

    if (result.meanConfidence() < policy.meanConfidenceThreshold()) {
        return true;
    }

    if (result.hasCriticalFieldFailures()) {
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}
```

## 31. Performance Considerations

| Concern | Practical control |
|---|---|
| Large PDF | OCR per page, batch pages |
| High DPI image | cap megapixels |
| Native memory | container memory limit |
| Thread contention | bounded worker pool |
| Language pack count | avoid unnecessary multi-language |
| Multiple variants | use only on retry/low quality |
| OCR per field | crop ROI, smaller input |
| Searchable PDF | generate only when needed |
| Result word table | optional for large documents |

Important:

```text id="2w8td0"
Do not run unlimited OCR tasks in parallel inside one JVM.
Native OCR work is CPU-heavy and memory-sensitive.
```

---

## 32. Kubernetes Deployment Notes

```yaml id="zqlgvo"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: media-ocr-worker
spec:
  replicas: 3
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: worker
          image: company/media-ocr-worker:1.0.0
          env:
            - name: TESSDATA_PREFIX
              value: /usr/share/tesseract-ocr/5/tessdata
            - name: OCR_DEFAULT_LANG
              value: ind+eng
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "1Gi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "4Gi"
```

Ops rule:

```text id="ptojv6"
Scale OCR worker separately from Jersey API.
OCR worker CPU-heavy; Jersey API latency-sensitive.
```

## 33. Security Considerations

Tesseract memproses file yang sudah melewati preprocessing/validation. Jangan kirim file upload mentah langsung ke OCR engine tanpa validation.

Checklist:

```text id="b9n4sb"
[ ] input sudah MIME-validated
[ ] input sudah page/dimension-limited
[ ] input sudah malware-scanned jika policy mewajibkan
[ ] OCR worker berjalan di container terbatas
[ ] temp file private dan dibersihkan
[ ] OCR result disanitasi sebelum ditampilkan sebagai HTML
[ ] HOCR tidak langsung dirender tanpa sanitization
[ ] raw text tidak dipakai untuk SQL/string command
```

---

## 35. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| OCR langsung di Jersey resource | API timeout/thread exhaustion | async OCR worker |
| Simpan plain text saja | sulit review/audit | structured JSON + word confidence |
| Satu language config untuk semua | akurasi turun | OCR policy per doc type |
| Satu PSM untuk semua | layout kacau | PSM per full page/ROI |
| Tidak simpan provider version | audit gap | persist runtime metadata |
| Tidak cek tessdata di startup | failure runtime | startup health check |
| Unlimited OCR parallelism | CPU/memory collapse | bounded worker concurrency |
| Tidak ada timeout | stuck native call | CLI subprocess or external timeout |
| Confidence dianggap absolute truth | false acceptance/rejection | calibration + validation |
| HOCR dirender langsung | XSS risk | sanitize output |
| Tidak pakai preprocessing | akurasi rendah | OCR-ready image pipeline |
| Multi-language default tanpa alasan | lambat/ambigu | language by policy |

---

## 37. Minimal Implementation untuk Tahap Awal

Implementasi awal yang realistis:

```text id="uvm5ku"
1. OCR worker service terpisah dari Jersey API
2. Tess4J dependency pinned
3. Docker image dengan Tesseract + ind/eng tessdata
4. OCR policy config: language, PSM, OEM, confidence threshold
5. OCR per preprocessed page image
6. Persist full text + page text + mean confidence
7. Optional word-level result
8. Low-confidence route to NEEDS_REVIEW
9. Audit event per page/job
10. Metrics: duration, confidence, failure
```

Minimal API tetap:

```text id="t9na17"
POST /media-jobs
GET  /media-jobs/{jobId}
GET  /media-jobs/{jobId}/result
```

Jangan expose endpoint seperti `/ocr-now` yang blocking untuk file besar.

## 38. Roadmap Singkat Setelah Seri 7

Urutan berikutnya:

```text id="pjwfjw"
Seri 8  → Layout-Aware OCR: table, form, key-value, bounding box, reading order
Seri 9  → Document AI Pipeline: invoice, form, ID card, contract, scanned PDF
Seri 10 → Human Review Workflow dengan Camunda
Seri 11 → Speech-to-Text Batch Transcription
```

OCR basic adalah fondasi, tetapi untuk aplikasi enterprise Anda hampir pasti butuh seri berikutnya: **layout-aware OCR**, karena raw text saja tidak cukup untuk invoice, form, ID card, contract, atau case document.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:30:02 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 8/20**: layout-aware OCR. Seri ini naik satu level dari “ambil text” menjadi “pahami struktur dokumen”: page, block, line, word, bounding box, table, key-value, reading order, dan hasil JSON yang bisa dipakai workflow/review.

# Seri 8/20 - Layout-Aware OCR  
## Page / Block / Line / Word Model, Bounding Box, Table Extraction, Key-Value Extraction, Reading Order, HOCR/TSV Parsing, dan Structured Document Result

## 1. Introduction

Di seri sebelumnya, OCR basic fokus pada:

```text id="5d4dt1"
image/PDF page
→ OCR engine
→ plain text
```

Seri ini naik level menjadi **layout-aware OCR**, yaitu OCR yang tidak hanya mengambil teks, tetapi juga memahami **posisi, struktur, urutan baca, table, form field, key-value pair, checkbox/selection mark, dan confidence per elemen**.

Ini penting karena di aplikasi enterprise, output seperti ini tidak cukup:

```text id="7gkzng"
Invoice No INV-2026-001 Date 2026-07-13 Total 12,500,000 ...
```

Yang dibutuhkan adalah:

```json id="ceua6g"
{
  "fields": {
    "invoiceNumber": {
      "value": "INV-2026-001",
      "confidence": 0.97,
      "bbox": { "x": 412, "y": 118, "width": 180, "height": 24 }
    },
    "invoiceDate": {
      "value": "2026-07-13",
      "confidence": 0.94,
      "bbox": { "x": 420, "y": 156, "width": 130, "height": 22 }
    }
  },
  "tables": [
    {
      "name": "line_items",
      "rows": []
    }
  ]
}
```

Tesseract mendukung output seperti hOCR dan TSV dari command-line, sementara cloud document AI seperti AWS Textract, Azure Document Intelligence, dan Google Document AI menyediakan model yang lebih langsung untuk layout, forms, tables, key-value pairs, dan document structure. Tesseract command-line docs menjelaskan penggunaan config `hocr` dan `tsv`; AWS Textract `AnalyzeDocument` mendukung forms, tables, signatures, queries, dan layout; Azure layout model mengekstrak text, tables, selection marks, dan document structure; Google Enterprise Document OCR mendeteksi block, paragraph, line, word, dan symbol dari PDF/image. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="r9dgw1"
OCR Basic:
Document image
→ plain text

Layout-Aware OCR:
Document image
→ page/block/paragraph/line/word structure
→ coordinates
→ tables
→ key-value pairs
→ reading order
→ confidence
→ reviewable structured result
```

Output layout-aware harus bisa menjawab:

```text id="odokaj"
Teks ini muncul di halaman berapa?
Koordinatnya di mana?
Masuk baris apa?
Masuk cell table mana?
Ini label atau value?
Ini field penting atau noise?
Confidence-nya berapa?
Perlu human review atau tidak?
```

---

## 3. Diagram

```text id="i5n36o"
Layout-Aware OCR Pipeline
│
├── 1. Input
│   ├── OCR-ready page image
│   ├── scanned PDF page
│   └── born-digital PDF text layer
│
├── 2. OCR / Document AI Provider
│   ├── Tesseract hOCR / TSV
│   ├── AWS Textract
│   ├── Azure Document Intelligence
│   ├── Google Document AI
│   └── custom model / ONNX layout model
│
├── 3. Layout Normalization
│   ├── page
│   ├── block
│   ├── paragraph
│   ├── line
│   ├── word/token
│   ├── bounding box
│   ├── confidence
│   └── reading order
│
├── 4. Structure Extraction
│   ├── table detection
│   ├── row/column reconstruction
│   ├── cell merging
│   ├── key-value pair extraction
│   ├── checkbox/selection marks
│   └── section/header/footer detection
│
├── 5. Domain Mapping
│   ├── invoice fields
│   ├── ID card fields
│   ├── contract clauses
│   ├── application form fields
│   └── case document metadata
│
├── 6. Validation
│   ├── required field check
│   ├── regex/type validation
│   ├── cross-field validation
│   ├── confidence threshold
│   └── duplicate/ambiguity detection
│
└── 7. Persistence / Review
    ├── structured JSON
    ├── normalized relational tables
    ├── searchable text
    ├── bounding box for UI highlight
    └── human review task
```

---

## 4. Practical Skill Map

| Skill Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| Page model | page number, size, DPI, rotation | Semua coordinate harus punya referensi page |
| Text hierarchy | block, paragraph, line, word/token | Untuk reading order dan visual highlight |
| Bounding box | x, y, width, height / polygon | Untuk review UI dan spatial reasoning |
| Confidence model | per word/line/field/table | Untuk review threshold |
| hOCR parsing | HTML-like OCR output | Tesseract layout extraction |
| TSV parsing | tabular OCR output | Lebih mudah diparse daripada hOCR untuk word/line |
| Reading order | urutan baca multi-column, header/footer | Plain OCR sering kacau di layout kompleks |
| Table extraction | detect table, row, column, cell | Invoice, statements, reports |
| Key-value extraction | label-value mapping | Forms, invoices, KYC docs |
| Selection mark | checkbox/radio extraction | Application forms |
| Layout normalization | provider-specific → internal canonical model | Hindari vendor lock-in |
| Domain mapping | generic OCR element → business field | Yang dibutuhkan workflow bukan hanya text |
| Validation | regex, type, required, cross-field | Deteksi hallucinated/misread field |
| Review UX | bounding box + original image overlay | Human correction cepat dan defensible |
| Auditability | provider/model/config/result version | Traceability |

---

## 5. Canonical Internal Model

Jangan simpan output provider langsung sebagai satu-satunya model. Buat model internal yang stabil.

```text id="wwjsu7"
Provider output:
- Tesseract hOCR
- Tesseract TSV
- AWS Textract Blocks
- Azure Document Intelligence result
- Google Document AI Document object

        │ normalize
        ▼

Canonical layout model:
- Document
- Page
- Block
- Line
- Word
- Table
- Cell
- KeyValuePair
- SelectionMark
- Field
```

Tujuannya:

| Benefit | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Vendor abstraction | AWS/Azure/Google/Tesseract bisa diganti |
| Stable API | Client tidak bergantung format provider |
| Easier review UI | Semua provider punya coordinate format sama |
| Easier validation | Field extraction rules konsisten |
| Audit | Raw provider response tetap disimpan sebagai artifact |

## 7. Bounding Box Model

Gunakan model yang bisa mendukung rectangle dan polygon.

```java id="db1uol"
public record BoundingBox(
        double x,
        double y,
        double width,
        double height,
        CoordinateUnit unit,
        List<Point2D> polygon
) {}

public enum CoordinateUnit {
    PIXEL,
    NORMALIZED_0_1
}
```

Kenapa polygon penting?

```text id="m89w5v"
- dokumen miring
- rotated text
- perspective-corrected region
- provider cloud sering return polygon
```

Namun untuk UI overlay sederhana, rectangle cukup:

```text id="zfdmh1"
x, y, width, height, pageNumber
```

---

## 8. Coordinate Normalization

Provider berbeda bisa memakai coordinate berbeda.

| Provider / Source | Coordinate biasanya |
|---|---|
| Tesseract TSV | pixel coordinate |
| hOCR | `bbox x0 y0 x1 y1` pixel |
| AWS Textract | normalized ratio 0-1 |
| Azure Document Intelligence | polygon/page coordinate |
| Google Document AI | bounding poly / layout anchors |

Canonical rule:

```text id="xrj8eh"
Simpan dua hal jika memungkinkan:
1. raw provider geometry
2. normalized internal geometry
```

Recommended internal:

```text id="cxik4k"
- use PIXEL coordinates for rendered review image
- keep page width/height
- convert normalized coordinates to pixels when rendering
```

Formula:

```java id="g4m3e1"
public BoundingBox normalizedToPixel(
        double left,
        double top,
        double width,
        double height,
        int pageWidth,
        int pageHeight
) {
    return new BoundingBox(
            left * pageWidth,
            top * pageHeight,
            width * pageWidth,
            height * pageHeight,
            CoordinateUnit.PIXEL,
            List.of()
    );
}
```

## 9. Tesseract hOCR dan TSV

Tesseract bisa menghasilkan hOCR dan TSV output dengan menambahkan config `hocr` atau `tsv` di command-line. Dokumentasi Tesseract menunjukkan contoh `tesseract images/eurotext.png - -l eng hocr` dan `tesseract images/eurotext.png - -l eng tsv`. ([Tesseract OCR](https://tesseract-ocr.github.io/tessdoc/Command-Line-Usage.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### hOCR

hOCR adalah HTML-like output dengan class seperti:

```text id="aqe2wi"
ocr_page
ocr_carea
ocr_par
ocr_line
ocrx_word
```

Biasanya berisi title:

```text id="3mboph"
bbox 100 200 180 220; x_wconf 95
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="gmmqzk"
- rich hierarchy
- word confidence
- bounding boxes
- bisa dipakai untuk overlay
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="8f6irp"
- parsing HTML-ish
- perlu sanitization
- format detail bisa berubah
- tidak cocok langsung sebagai API contract
```

### TSV

TSV lebih mudah diparse.

Kolom umum:

```text id="31abom"
level
page_num
block_num
par_num
line_num
word_num
left
top
width
height
conf
text
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="mj37qp"
- mudah diparse
- cocok untuk line/word extraction
- output tabular
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="hf44qj"
- table/key-value tetap perlu rekonstruksi sendiri
- reading order masih perlu validasi
```

---

## 10. Tesseract TSV Parsing Example

### Command

```bash id="8ioodb"
tesseract page-0001.png stdout -l ind+eng --oem 1 --psm 3 tsv
```

### Java parser skeleton

```java id="ng1tqs"
public final class TesseractTsvParser {

    public OcrPageLayout parse(UUID pageAssetId, int pageNumber, String tsv) {
        List<OcrWord> words = new ArrayList<>();
        List<OcrLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();

        String[] rows = tsv.split("\\R");

        if (rows.length <= 1) {
            return OcrPageLayout.empty(pageAssetId, pageNumber);
        }

        for (int i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
            String row = rows[i];

            if (row.isBlank()) {
                continue;
            }

            String[] columns = row.split("\\t", -1);

            if (columns.length < 12) {
                continue;
            }

            int level = Integer.parseInt(columns[0]);
            int blockNum = Integer.parseInt(columns[2]);
            int parNum = Integer.parseInt(columns[3]);
            int lineNum = Integer.parseInt(columns[4]);
            int wordNum = Integer.parseInt(columns[5]);

            int left = Integer.parseInt(columns[6]);
            int top = Integer.parseInt(columns[7]);
            int width = Integer.parseInt(columns[8]);
            int height = Integer.parseInt(columns[9]);
            double confidence = Double.parseDouble(columns[10]);
            String text = columns[11];

            if (level == 5 && !text.isBlank()) {
                words.add(new OcrWord(
                        UUID.randomUUID(),
                        pageNumber,
                        blockNum,
                        parNum,
                        lineNum,
                        wordNum,
                        text,
                        confidence / 100.0,
                        new BoundingBox(left, top, width, height, CoordinateUnit.PIXEL, List.of())
                ));
            }
        }

        lines = groupWordsIntoLines(words);

        return new OcrPageLayout(pageAssetId, pageNumber, lines, words);
    }

    private List<OcrLine> groupWordsIntoLines(List<OcrWord> words) {
        return words.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                        word -> word.blockNumber() + ":" + word.paragraphNumber() + ":" + word.lineNumber(),
                        LinkedHashMap::new,
                        Collectors.toList()
                ))
                .values()
                .stream()
                .map(OcrLine::fromWords)
                .toList();
    }
}
```

---

## 11. Reading Order

Reading order adalah salah satu problem tersulit.

Contoh layout:

```text id="hm0hgs"
Column A                     Column B
Line A1                      Line B1
Line A2                      Line B2
Line A3                      Line B3
```

OCR naïve bisa menghasilkan:

```text id="abylib"
Line A1 Line B1 Line A2 Line B2 Line A3 Line B3
```

Padahal reading order yang benar bisa:

```text id="9bbau0"
Line A1 Line A2 Line A3 Line B1 Line B2 Line B3
```

### Heuristic sederhana

```text id="u7xn2t"
1. group words into lines
2. group lines into blocks/columns
3. sort blocks top-to-bottom, left-to-right
4. within block, sort lines by y
5. within line, sort words by x
```

### Practical rule

| Document type | Reading order strategy |
|---|---|
| simple form | top-to-bottom |
| invoice | section-aware |
| two-column contract | column detection |
| table-heavy doc | table region separate |
| ID card | template/ROI-based |
| receipt | vertical line order |

Jangan mengandalkan satu global sorting untuk semua dokumen.

---

## 12. Line Reconstruction

Word-level OCR harus direkonstruksi menjadi line.

```java id="j8s2iv"
public record OcrLine(
        int pageNumber,
        int blockNumber,
        int paragraphNumber,
        int lineNumber,
        String text,
        double meanConfidence,
        BoundingBox bbox,
        List<OcrWord> words
) {
    public static OcrLine fromWords(List<OcrWord> words) {
        String text = words.stream()
                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(w -> w.bbox().x()))
                .map(OcrWord::text)
                .collect(Collectors.joining(" "));

        double confidence = words.stream()
                .mapToDouble(OcrWord::confidence)
                .average()
                .orElse(-1.0);

        BoundingBox bbox = BoundingBox.union(
                words.stream().map(OcrWord::bbox).toList()
        );

        OcrWord first = words.get(0);

        return new OcrLine(
                first.pageNumber(),
                first.blockNumber(),
                first.paragraphNumber(),
                first.lineNumber(),
                text,
                confidence,
                bbox,
                words
        );
    }
}
```

---

## 13. Table Extraction

Tesseract basic OCR tidak benar-benar memahami table sebagai table. Untuk table, ada tiga pendekatan:

```text id="6xjhsz"
1. Rule-based from OCR coordinates
2. Cloud Document AI provider
3. Custom model / layout model
```

AWS Textract table extraction dapat mengekstrak cells, merged cells, column headers, titles, section titles, footers, structured/semistructured table type, dan summary cells. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/textract/latest/dg/how-it-works-tables.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Azure Document Intelligence layout model mengekstrak structured representations termasuk text, tables, selection marks, dan document structure. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/document-intelligence/prebuilt/layout?view=doc-intel-4.0.0&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Table model

```java id="dykqux"
public record OcrTable(
        UUID id,
        int pageNumber,
        String name,
        BoundingBox bbox,
        List<OcrTableRow> rows,
        double confidence
) {}

public record OcrTableRow(
        int rowIndex,
        List<OcrTableCell> cells
) {}

public record OcrTableCell(
        int rowIndex,
        int columnIndex,
        int rowSpan,
        int columnSpan,
        String text,
        double confidence,
        BoundingBox bbox,
        List<OcrWord> words
) {}
```

---

## 14. Rule-Based Table Reconstruction

Untuk table sederhana:

```text id="t7ms3y"
Input:
words with x/y/bbox

Process:
1. detect table region
2. group words into rows by y-overlap
3. infer columns from x gaps/alignment
4. assign words to cells
5. merge cells if large span
6. validate header/body consistency
```

### Row grouping heuristic

```java id="l5pvqj"
public List<List<OcrWord>> groupIntoRows(List<OcrWord> words, double yTolerance) {
    List<OcrWord> sorted = words.stream()
            .sorted(Comparator
                    .comparing((OcrWord w) -> w.bbox().y())
                    .thenComparing(w -> w.bbox().x()))
            .toList();

    List<List<OcrWord>> rows = new ArrayList<>();

    for (OcrWord word : sorted) {
        Optional<List<OcrWord>> matchingRow = rows.stream()
                .filter(row -> Math.abs(rowCenterY(row) - wordCenterY(word)) <= yTolerance)
                .findFirst();

        if (matchingRow.isPresent()) {
            matchingRow.get().add(word);
        } else {
            rows.add(new ArrayList<>(List.of(word)));
        }
    }

    rows.forEach(row -> row.sort(Comparator.comparing(w -> w.bbox().x())));

    return rows;
}
```

### Limitasi rule-based

| Problem | Dampak |
|---|---|
| table tanpa garis | column inference sulit |
| merged cell | row/column index salah |
| multi-line cell | dianggap beberapa row |
| rotated table | coordinate heuristic gagal |
| multi-page table | continuity sulit |
| handwritten table | OCR + layout sama-sama sulit |

Untuk invoice/statement serius, pertimbangkan provider/layout model khusus.

---

## 15. Key-Value Pair Extraction

Key-value extraction mencari pasangan seperti:

```text id="x1gtgt"
Invoice No: INV-2026-001
Date: 2026-07-13
Customer Name: PT ABC
Total Amount: 12,500,000
```

AWS Textract Analyze Document Forms mengekstrak key-value pairs; pricing page AWS juga menjelaskan Analyze Document Forms mengekstrak data seperti key-value pair “First Name” dan value terkait. ([Amazon Web Services, Inc.](https://aws.amazon.com/textract/pricing/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Azure general document model/document intelligence juga menyediakan key-value pair, tables, selection marks, text, dan structure extraction untuk structured/semi-structured/unstructured documents. ([docs.azure.cn](https://docs.azure.cn/en-us/ai-services/document-intelligence/prebuilt/general-document?view=doc-intel-4.0.0&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Key-value model

```java id="m913e9"
public record OcrKeyValuePair(
        String keyText,
        String valueText,
        double confidence,
        BoundingBox keyBox,
        BoundingBox valueBox,
        int pageNumber,
        String extractionMethod
) {}
```

### Rule-based heuristic

```text id="ha7lgn"
1. detect candidate key labels
2. normalize label text
3. find nearby value:
   - right side
   - below label
   - same line after colon
4. validate value type
5. choose best candidate by distance + confidence + regex match
```

Example:

```text id="2q1t00"
Line:
"Invoice No: INV-2026-001"

Key:
Invoice No

Value:
INV-2026-001
```

Field config:

```json id="lm84p1"
{
  "fieldName": "invoiceNumber",
  "labels": ["Invoice No", "Invoice Number", "No. Invoice", "Nomor Invoice"],
  "valuePattern": "[A-Z]{2,5}-\\d{4}-\\d+",
  "searchStrategy": "SAME_LINE_OR_RIGHT",
  "required": true
}
```

---

## 16. Selection Marks / Checkbox

Selection marks biasanya muncul di forms:

```text id="hqudpd"
[ ] Yes
[x] No
```

Cloud layout models sering punya selection mark detection. Azure layout model secara eksplisit menyebut extraction untuk selection marks bersama text, tables, dan document structure. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/document-intelligence/prebuilt/layout?view=doc-intel-4.0.0&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Rule-based local approach:

```text id="u8cm9c"
1. detect small square/box contours
2. inspect fill/mark ratio
3. associate nearest label text
4. output selected/unselected
```

Model:

```java id="mno0fh"
public record SelectionMark(
        int pageNumber,
        String state,
        String nearbyLabel,
        BoundingBox bbox,
        double confidence
) {}
```

States:

```text id="nq7qqr"
SELECTED
UNSELECTED
UNKNOWN
```

---

## 17. Provider Comparison

| Capability | Tesseract TSV/hOCR | AWS Textract | Azure Document Intelligence | Google Document AI |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Raw text | Ya | Ya | Ya | Ya |
| Word bbox | Ya | Ya | Ya | Ya |
| Line/block | Ya | Ya | Ya | Ya |
| Table | Manual/rule-based | Native | Native | Model-dependent |
| Key-value | Manual/rule-based | Native Forms | Native/general/custom | Model-dependent |
| Selection mark | Manual/custom | Supported in document analysis contexts | Native layout/general | Model-dependent |
| On-prem | Ya | Tidak native | Container support depends service/model | Tidak native untuk managed |
| Cost | infra sendiri | per page/API | per page/API | per page/API |
| Data residency | on-prem possible | cloud region | cloud/selected deployment options | cloud region |
| Custom schema | manual | adapters/queries/custom patterns | custom models | processors/custom processors |

Google Enterprise Document OCR supports blocks, paragraphs, lines, words, and symbols, and can deskew documents for better accuracy. Google Document AI response handling documentation describes how OCR result elements map into a `Document` object JSON. ([Google Cloud Documentation](https://docs.cloud.google.com/document-ai/docs/enterprise-document-ocr?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="74ynmm"
CREATE TABLE document_layout_result (

    provider_name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
```

## 21. Field Extraction Policy

Buat policy per document type.

```sql id="2fzbmz"
CREATE TABLE document_field_definition (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    document_type            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    field_name               VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    labels                   TEXT[] NOT NULL,
    value_type               VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    value_pattern            TEXT,
    required                 BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    search_strategy           VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    confidence_threshold      NUMERIC(6,5),

    created_at                TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at                TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (document_type, field_name)
);
```

Example:

| Field | Labels | Strategy | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|
| `invoiceNumber` | Invoice No, No. Invoice | same-line/right | regex |
| `invoiceDate` | Date, Tanggal | same-line/right/below | date parse |
| `customerName` | Customer, Bill To | below/right block | non-empty |
| `totalAmount` | Total, Grand Total | right/below, bottom-weighted | currency parse |
| `nik` | NIK | same-line/right | 16 digits |
| `fullName` | Nama, Name | below/right | non-empty |

## 22. Field Extraction Flow

```text id="7ocgzw"
Canonical layout result
  │
  ▼
Load document field definitions
  │
  ▼
For each field definition:
  ├── find candidate labels
  ├── find candidate values spatially
  ├── score candidates
  ├── validate type/regex
  ├── choose best value
  └── mark review if ambiguous/low confidence
  │
  ▼
Persist document_extracted_field
```

Candidate scoring:

```text id="ahmcdy"
score =
  label_similarity_score
+ spatial_distance_score
+ OCR_confidence_score
+ regex_match_score
+ document_zone_score
- ambiguity_penalty
```

```text id="923klj"
- plain text reconstruction
- RAG/chunking
- document preview
- legal/regulatory review
- clause extraction
```

Text reconstruction:

```java id="ox3td5"
public String reconstructText(List<DocumentElement> elements) {
    return elements.stream()
            .filter(e -> e.type() == ElementType.LINE)
            .sorted(Comparator
                    .comparing(DocumentElement::pageNumber)
                    .thenComparing(DocumentElement::readingOrderIndex))
            .map(DocumentElement::text)
            .collect(Collectors.joining("\n"));
}
```

## 27. Review UI Integration

Layout-aware OCR sangat berguna untuk human review.

```text id="lmj4i7"
Original page image
  │
  ├── overlay word boxes
  ├── highlight extracted fields
  ├── highlight low confidence words
  ├── click field → jump to coordinate
  ├── edit extracted value
  └── write correction audit
```

Review data requirement:

```text id="qtuxjm"
fieldName
fieldValue
confidence
pageNumber
bbox
sourceWords
validationErrors
reviewStatus
```

Correction should store:

```text id="7hngag"
old value
new value
reviewer
reason
timestamp
source bbox
```

---

## 28. Validation Rules

Field validation examples:

| Field | Validation |
|---|---|
| invoice number | regex, uniqueness per vendor |
| invoice date | parse date, not future beyond policy |
| due date | >= invoice date |
| tax ID | checksum/length if applicable |
| NIK | 16 digits |
| currency amount | parse decimal, non-negative |
| table total | sum line items equals total |
| name | non-empty, not likely label |
| address | multi-line allowed |
| checkbox | selected/unselected only |

Cross-field validation:

```text id="mku3wu"
subtotal + tax = total
invoice date <= due date
NIK length = 16
line item amount = quantity * unit price
document name matches case party
```

---

## 29. Failure Model

| Failure | Meaning | Retry? |
|---|---|---|
| `LAYOUT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | provider timeout | Yes |
| `LAYOUT_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT` | quota/rate limit | Yes |
| `HOCR_PARSE_FAILED` | invalid hOCR output | Limited |
| `TSV_PARSE_FAILED` | malformed TSV | Limited |
| `NO_WORDS_EXTRACTED` | OCR empty | No/retry preprocessing |
| `TABLE_RECONSTRUCTION_FAILED` | table ambiguous | No, review |
| `FIELD_REQUIRED_MISSING` | field not found | No, review |
| `FIELD_VALIDATION_FAILED` | extracted field invalid | No, review |
| `LOW_LAYOUT_CONFIDENCE` | confidence below threshold | No, review |
| `COORDINATE_NORMALIZATION_FAILED` | geometry mapping error | Bug/config |
| `PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED_DOCUMENT` | provider cannot handle | No/fallback |

---

## 30. Fallback Strategy

```text id="3mzz9q"
Primary provider fails
  │
  ├── retry transient failure
  ├── fallback to another provider
  ├── fallback to OCR basic
  ├── fallback to manual review
  └── mark failed if unsupported/security
```

Example policy:

| Document Type | Primary | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| invoice | AWS/Azure document model | Tesseract TSV + field rules |
| generic PDF | Tesseract TSV | basic text OCR |
| ID card | custom template/field OCR | manual review |
| contract | text layer + layout lines | OCR if scanned |
| table-heavy statement | cloud layout provider | manual review |

## 33. Security Considerations

Layout-aware OCR output can contain PII, biometric-adjacent document info, signatures, addresses, IDs, financial data.

Checklist:

```text id="b8n6o5"
[ ] raw provider response stored private
[ ] hOCR/HTML output sanitized before rendering
[ ] field values access-controlled
[ ] bbox overlays do not expose unauthorized pages
[ ] extracted text encrypted at rest if required
[ ] logs do not dump full OCR text
[ ] PII redaction policy exists
[ ] retention follows source document policy
[ ] provider selection considers data residency
```

Important: hOCR is HTML-like. Do not render it directly in browser without sanitization.

---

## 34. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Simpan plain text saja | Tidak bisa table/field/review | canonical layout model |
| Expose provider raw response as API | vendor lock-in | normalized contract |
| Tidak simpan coordinate | review UI sulit | bbox per word/field |
| Treat reading order as solved | text reconstruction kacau | document-type strategy |
| Table extraction pakai regex text saja | row/cell hilang | coordinate/table model |
| Key-value hanya regex global | salah field | spatial + label strategy |
| Confidence page-level saja | field risk tersembunyi | confidence per field |
| hOCR langsung render | XSS risk | sanitize/normalize |
| Tidak simpan raw provider result | audit/debug sulit | raw artifact private |
| Tidak versi-kan extraction policy | hasil berubah tanpa jejak | policy version |
| Semua low confidence dianggap failed | workload buruk | route human review |
| Semua provider disamakan | capability mismatch | provider decision matrix |

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 9/20**: Document AI pipeline. Fokusnya adalah mengubah OCR/layout result menjadi output bisnis yang bisa dipakai sistem: klasifikasi dokumen, field extraction, validation, confidence threshold, dan routing ke human review.

# Seri 9/20 - Document AI Pipeline  
## Invoice, Form, ID Card, Contract, Scanned PDF, Document Classification, Field Extraction, Validation, Confidence Threshold, dan Human Review Routing

## 1. Introduction

Di seri sebelumnya, kita sudah membahas **layout-aware OCR**: page, line, word, table, key-value, bounding box, dan reading order.

Seri ini membahas lapisan di atasnya: **Document AI Pipeline**.

Bedanya:

```text
OCR Basic
→ “Apa teks di dokumen ini?”

Layout-Aware OCR
→ “Teks itu ada di mana, pada baris/cell/field apa?”

Document AI Pipeline
→ “Dokumen ini jenis apa, field bisnis apa yang bisa dipercaya, mana yang perlu divalidasi, dan apakah bisa lanjut workflow?”
```

Untuk sistem enterprise, output akhir yang dibutuhkan biasanya bukan sekadar OCR text, tapi **business-ready structured result**:

```json
{
  "documentType": "INVOICE",
  "confidence": 0.94,
  "fields": {
    "invoiceNumber": "INV-2026-001",
    "invoiceDate": "2026-07-13",
    "vendorName": "PT Contoh Vendor",
    "totalAmount": "12500000"
  },
  "tables": {
    "lineItems": []
  },
  "validationStatus": "PASSED",
  "routingDecision": "AUTO_ACCEPT"
}
```

Cloud provider sudah menyediakan beberapa building block. AWS Textract `AnalyzeDocument` mendukung fitur seperti forms, tables, signatures, queries, dan layout; Azure Document Intelligence general document model mengekstrak key-value pairs, tables, selection marks, text, dan document structure; Google Document AI OCR mendeteksi blocks, paragraphs, lines, words, dan symbols dari PDF/image. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/textract/latest/dg/how-it-works-tables.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Document AI Pipeline = OCR + Layout + Classification + Extraction + Validation + Workflow Decision
```

Pipeline ideal:

```text
Raw document
  │
  ▼
Metadata extraction
  │
  ▼
Preprocessing
  │
  ▼
OCR / layout extraction
  │
  ▼
Document classification
  │
  ▼
Field/table extraction
  │
  ▼
Normalization
  │
  ▼
Validation
  │
  ├── valid + high confidence     → auto-accept
  ├── valid + medium confidence   → conditional review
  ├── invalid / missing required  → human review
  └── unsupported / suspicious    → reject/security review
  │
  ▼
Persist structured result + audit
```

Kunci desainnya: **AI/OCR tidak boleh langsung dianggap final decision**. Output AI harus melewati validation, confidence threshold, dan bila perlu human review.

---

```text
├── 1. Ingestion
```

## 4. Practical Category Map

| Area | Skill yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| Document taxonomy | Menentukan jenis dokumen yang didukung | Extraction rule berbeda per jenis dokumen |
| Classification | Rule-based, template-based, ML/LLM-assisted, provider classifier | Pipeline harus tahu schema mana yang dipakai |
| Canonical schema | Field standar per document type | Output stabil walaupun provider berubah |
| Field extraction | Label-value, spatial rule, regex, provider key-value | Mengubah OCR menjadi data bisnis |
| Table extraction | Header, row, cell, line item, totals | Invoice/statement/report sangat bergantung table |
| Normalization | Date, currency, ID number, phone, address | Downstream system butuh value canonical |
| Validation | Required, type, regex, cross-field, business rule | Mencegah OCR error masuk workflow |
| Confidence scoring | Field-level, table-level, document-level | Dasar auto-accept vs review |
| Human review routing | Camunda user task, review queue, correction | AI output tidak selalu reliable |
| Audit trail | raw input, provider output, extracted value, correction | Defensibility dan debugging |
| Provider abstraction | AWS/Azure/Google/Tesseract/custom | Hindari vendor lock-in |
| Versioning | schema version, extraction policy version, provider version | Hasil bisa berubah antar versi pipeline |
| Observability | extraction success rate, review rate, validation error | Mengukur kualitas pipeline |

---

## 5. Document Types dan Pipeline Strategy

| Document Type | Input umum | Extraction focus | Strategy |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INVOICE` | PDF/image | invoice no, date, vendor, buyer, total, tax, line items | layout + key-value + table validation |
| `RECEIPT` | image/photo | merchant, date, total, tax, payment method | OCR + receipt-specific rules |
| `ID_CARD` | image/photo | ID number, name, DOB, address, face/photo region | template/ROI + strict validation |
| `CONTRACT` | PDF scan/text | parties, effective date, clauses, signatures | text layer/layout + section extraction |
| `APPLICATION_FORM` | PDF/image | form fields, checkboxes, signatures | key-value + selection marks |
| `BANK_STATEMENT` | PDF/image | account info, transaction table, balance | table-heavy extraction |
| `SUPPORTING_DOCUMENT` | any | classify, summarize metadata, searchable text | generic OCR/layout |
| `UNKNOWN` | any | detect type or route review | classification fallback |

---

## 6. Document Classification

Classification menjawab:

```text
Dokumen ini termasuk jenis apa?
Schema extraction mana yang harus dipakai?
Apakah dokumen ini sesuai expected job/case?
```

### Classification approaches

| Approach | Cocok untuk | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Filename/metadata rule | internal upload terkontrol | cepat | mudah salah |
| Keyword rule | invoice/receipt/form sederhana | explainable | brittle |
| Template matching | dokumen vendor/form tetap | akurat untuk template | gagal pada template baru |
| Layout similarity | form/statement/invoice repetitif | bagus untuk template family | perlu dataset |
| Provider classifier | cloud document AI | cepat implementasi | vendor lock-in/cost |
| ML/LLM classifier | dokumen beragam | fleksibel | perlu guardrail/eval |

Practical starting point:

```text
1. expectedDocumentType dari request/case
2. keyword + layout heuristic
3. provider classifier bila ada
4. confidence threshold
5. unknown → human review
```

---

```text
LOW_CONFIDENCE
```
