## 8. Canonical Document Schema

Setiap document type butuh schema.

```json
{
  "documentType": "INVOICE",
  "schemaVersion": "invoice-v1",
  "requiredFields": [
    "invoiceNumber",
    "invoiceDate",
    "vendorName",
    "totalAmount"
  ],
  "optionalFields": [
    "dueDate",
    "taxAmount",
    "purchaseOrderNumber"
  ],
  "tables": [
    "lineItems"
  ]
}
```

Jangan biarkan field bebas tanpa definisi karena downstream akan sulit dikontrol.

---

## 9. Schema Table

```sql
CREATE TABLE document_schema (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    document_type          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    schema_version         VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    description            TEXT,
    active                 BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    schema_json            JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (document_type, schema_version)
);
```

Field definition:

```sql
CREATE TABLE document_schema_field (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    schema_id              UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES document_schema(id),

    field_name             VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    display_name           TEXT NOT NULL,
    value_type             VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    required               BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    labels                 TEXT[],
    value_pattern          TEXT,
    confidence_threshold   NUMERIC(6,5),
    validation_rules_json  JSONB,

    extraction_strategy    VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (schema_id, field_name)
);
```

---

## 10. Extraction Strategy per Field

| Strategy | Contoh | Cocok untuk |
|---|---|---|
| `SAME_LINE_AFTER_LABEL` | `Invoice No: INV-001` | label-value satu baris |
| `RIGHT_OF_LABEL` | label kiri, value kanan | form/invoice |
| `BELOW_LABEL` | label atas, value bawah | form vertikal |
| `REGEX_GLOBAL` | cari pola nomor tertentu | backup/fallback |
| `DOCUMENT_ZONE` | field ada di region tertentu | ID card/template |
| `TABLE_HEADER_BASED` | line item columns | invoice/table |
| `PROVIDER_KEY_VALUE` | AWS/Azure key-value | managed document AI |
| `QUERY_BASED` | ask provider “What is invoice number?” | Textract Queries/provider query |
| `CUSTOM_MODEL` | trained extractor | dokumen kompleks |

AWS Textract Queries memungkinkan menanyakan informasi tertentu dari dokumen, sementara `AnalyzeDocument` dapat memakai feature seperti Forms, Tables, Queries, Signatures, dan Layout. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/textract/latest/dg/queryresponse.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Invoice Pipeline

Invoice adalah contoh paling umum karena mengandung key-value dan table.

```text
Invoice document
  │
  ├── classify as INVOICE
  ├── extract header fields
  │   ├── invoiceNumber
  │   ├── invoiceDate
  │   ├── dueDate
  │   ├── vendorName
  │   ├── buyerName
  │   ├── taxId
  │   └── purchaseOrderNumber
  │
  ├── extract line item table
  │   ├── description
  │   ├── quantity
  │   ├── unitPrice
  │   ├── tax
  │   └── amount
  │
  ├── extract totals
  │   ├── subtotal
  │   ├── taxAmount
  │   ├── discount
  │   └── totalAmount
  │
  └── validate
      ├── subtotal + tax - discount = total
      ├── qty * unit price = line amount
      ├── invoice date <= due date
      └── invoice number not duplicate
```

### Invoice result shape

```json
{
  "documentType": "INVOICE",
  "schemaVersion": "invoice-v1",
  "fields": {
    "invoiceNumber": {
      "value": "INV-2026-001",
      "confidence": 0.96,
      "validationStatus": "PASSED"
    },
    "invoiceDate": {
      "value": "2026-07-13",
      "confidence": 0.93,
      "validationStatus": "PASSED"
    },
    "totalAmount": {
      "value": "12500000.00",
      "currency": "IDR",
      "confidence": 0.91,
      "validationStatus": "PASSED"
    }
  },
  "tables": {
    "lineItems": {
      "rows": [
        {
          "description": "Service A",
          "quantity": "2",
          "unitPrice": "5000000.00",
          "amount": "10000000.00"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "validation": {
    "status": "PASSED_WITH_WARNINGS",
    "warnings": [
      "TAX_AMOUNT_MISSING"
    ]
  }
}
```

---

## 12. Form Pipeline

Form biasanya berisi label-value, checkbox, radio, signature.

Azure Document Intelligence general document model mendukung extraction key-value pairs, selection marks, text, tables, dan structure, sehingga cocok sebagai managed provider untuk form-like documents. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/document-intelligence/prebuilt/general-document?view=doc-intel-4.0.0&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Application form
  │
  ├── classify as APPLICATION_FORM
  ├── extract key-value fields
  ├── extract selection marks
  ├── extract signature presence
  ├── validate required sections
  └── route incomplete form to review
```

Example:

```json
{
  "documentType": "APPLICATION_FORM",
  "fields": {
    "applicantName": {
      "value": "Budi Santoso",
      "confidence": 0.94
    },
    "consentGiven": {
      "value": true,
      "source": "SELECTION_MARK",
      "confidence": 0.89
    }
  },
  "signatures": [
    {
      "pageNumber": 2,
      "present": true,
      "confidence": 0.86
    }
  ]
}
```

---

## 13. ID Card Pipeline

ID card berbeda dari invoice/form karena lebih template/ROI-based.

```text
ID Card
  │
  ├── detect document orientation
  ├── detect card boundary
  ├── crop normalized card
  ├── classify ID card type
  ├── extract text fields by region
  ├── detect face/photo region
  ├── validate ID number format
  ├── validate DOB/date fields
  └── route mismatch/blur/low confidence to review
```

Field examples:

```text
- NIK / ID number
- full name
- birth place/date
- gender
- address
- province/city
- expiry/lifetime marker
```

Practical caution:

```text
ID card data is highly sensitive.
Do not log extracted fields.
Do not retain raw images longer than needed.
Do not use face/photo region for recognition unless there is explicit lawful basis and consent.
```

---

## 14. Contract Pipeline

Contract processing tidak selalu field extraction sederhana. Fokusnya sering pada sections, clauses, parties, dates, signatures, and obligations.

```text
Contract
  │
  ├── detect born-digital vs scanned
  ├── extract text layer or OCR
  ├── reconstruct reading order
  ├── detect sections/clauses
  ├── extract parties
  ├── extract effective date / expiry date
  ├── detect signature blocks
  ├── classify clause types
  └── store searchable structured sections
```

Contract output:

```json
{
  "documentType": "CONTRACT",
  "fields": {
    "partyA": {
      "value": "PT Alpha",
      "confidence": 0.88,
      "requiresReview": true
    },
    "effectiveDate": {
      "value": "2026-07-01",
      "confidence": 0.92
    }
  },
  "sections": [
    {
      "title": "Termination",
      "pageStart": 8,
      "pageEnd": 9,
      "text": "...",
      "classification": "TERMINATION_CLAUSE"
    }
  ]
}
```

For contracts, “correct enough” often requires legal/business review, not fully automated acceptance.

---

## 15. Scanned PDF Pipeline

Scanned PDF is usually a container of page images.

```text
Scanned PDF
  │
  ├── metadata extraction
  │   ├── page count
  │   ├── encrypted?
  │   └── text layer?
  │
  ├── rasterize pages
  ├── preprocess each page
  ├── OCR/layout each page
  ├── classify document type
  ├── extract fields/tables
  ├── combine multi-page result
  └── persist searchable result
```

Important distinctions:

| PDF type | Pipeline |
|---|---|
| Born-digital PDF | extract text layer first |
| Scanned PDF | rasterize + OCR |
| Hybrid PDF | use text layer + OCR only for image regions |
| Encrypted PDF | reject or password workflow |
| Huge PDF | page count/cost policy |

---

## 16. Provider Strategy

| Need | Recommended provider strategy |
|---|---|
| On-prem/simple OCR | Tesseract + TSV/hOCR |
| Tables/forms quick start | AWS Textract / Azure Document Intelligence |
| Google Cloud environment | Google Document AI |
| ID card template | custom ROI + OCR + validation |
| Regulated data residency | on-prem OCR/layout where possible |
| Complex vendor invoices | managed Document AI or trained/custom model |
| High audit defensibility | preserve raw response + normalized result |

AWS Textract provides table-related structures such as cells, merged cells, headers, titles, footers, and table type metadata; Azure Document Intelligence response is async and extracts content/layout/semantic elements from images, PDFs, and other document files; Google Document AI response includes OCR elements such as blocks, paragraphs, tokens, and symbols. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/textract/latest/dg/how-it-works-tables.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 17. Provider Abstraction

```java
public interface DocumentAiProvider {
    DocumentAiProviderResult analyze(DocumentAiProviderRequest request);
}
```

```java
public record DocumentAiProviderRequest(
        UUID jobId,
        UUID sourceAssetId,
        Path documentPath,
        String expectedDocumentType,
        DocumentAiOptions options
) {}
```

```java
public record DocumentAiOptions(
        boolean classifyDocument,
        boolean extractLayout,
        boolean extractKeyValuePairs,
        boolean extractTables,
        boolean extractSelectionMarks,
        boolean extractSignatures,
        boolean preserveRawProviderResponse
) {}
```

Implementations:

```text
DocumentAiProvider
├── TesseractDocumentAiProvider
├── AwsTextractDocumentAiProvider
├── AzureDocumentIntelligenceProvider
├── GoogleDocumentAiProvider
├── TemplateBasedDocumentAiProvider
└── CompositeFallbackDocumentAiProvider
```

---

## 18. Pipeline Orchestrator

```java
public final class DocumentAiPipeline {

    private final DocumentClassifier classifier;
    private final DocumentAiProviderRegistry providerRegistry;
    private final DocumentSchemaRepository schemaRepository;
    private final FieldExtractionEngine fieldExtractionEngine;
    private final TableExtractionEngine tableExtractionEngine;
    private final DocumentValidationEngine validationEngine;
    private final ReviewRoutingService reviewRoutingService;
    private final DocumentResultRepository resultRepository;

    public DocumentAiResult process(DocumentAiPipelineRequest request) {
        DocumentClassification classification = classifier.classify(request);

        DocumentSchema schema = schemaRepository.resolve(
                classification.predictedDocumentType()
        );

        DocumentAiProvider provider = providerRegistry.resolve(schema, request);

        DocumentAiProviderResult providerResult = provider.analyze(
                request.toProviderRequest(schema)
        );

        CanonicalDocumentLayout layout = providerResult.canonicalLayout();

        ExtractedFields fields = fieldExtractionEngine.extract(schema, layout);

        ExtractedTables tables = tableExtractionEngine.extract(schema, layout);

        DocumentValidationResult validation = validationEngine.validate(
                schema,
                fields,
                tables,
                classification
        );

        ReviewDecision reviewDecision = reviewRoutingService.decide(
                classification,
                fields,
                tables,
                validation
        );

        DocumentAiResult result = DocumentAiResult.of(
                classification,
                schema,
                layout,
                fields,
                tables,
                validation,
                reviewDecision
        );

        resultRepository.persist(result);

        return result;
    }
}
```

---

```java
        UUID jobId,
        UUID sourceAssetId,
        String documentType,

        CanonicalDocumentLayout layout,
        Map<String, ExtractedField> fields,

        DocumentValidationResult validation,
        ReviewDecision reviewDecision,
        ProviderMetadata providerMetadata
) {}
```

Field:

```java
public record ExtractedField(
        String fieldName,
        String rawValue,
        String normalizedValue,
        String valueType,
        double confidence,
        BoundingBox bbox,
        int pageNumber,
        String extractionMethod,
        ValidationStatus validationStatus,
        List<ValidationError> validationErrors,
        boolean requiresReview
) {}
```

---

## 20. Validation Engine

Validation harus dipisah dari extraction. Extraction menjawab “apa yang mungkin terbaca”; validation menjawab “apakah value ini masuk akal dan boleh dipakai”.

```text
Extract:
invoiceDate = "13/07/2026"

Normalize:
invoiceDate = "2026-07-13"

Validate:
- required? yes
- date parse? yes
- not too far future? yes
- due date >= invoice date? yes
```

### Validation categories

| Category | Example |
|---|---|
| Required field | invoice number wajib ada |
| Type validation | amount harus decimal |
| Format validation | NIK harus 16 digit |
| Range validation | date tidak boleh di luar policy |
| Cross-field validation | subtotal + tax = total |
| Referential validation | vendor terdaftar di master data |
| Duplicate validation | invoice number belum pernah diproses |
| Consistency validation | document type sesuai expected |
| Confidence validation | confidence di atas threshold |
| Completeness validation | required table columns ada |

---

## 21. Validation Result Model

```json
{
  "validation": {
    "status": "FAILED",
    "errors": [
      {
        "code": "REQUIRED_FIELD_MISSING",
        "field": "invoiceNumber",
        "severity": "ERROR"
      },
      {
        "code": "TOTAL_MISMATCH",
        "field": "totalAmount",
        "severity": "ERROR",
        "details": {
          "computedTotal": "12000000.00",
          "extractedTotal": "12500000.00"
        }
      }
    ],
    "warnings": [
      {
        "code": "LOW_CONFIDENCE_OPTIONAL_FIELD",
        "field": "purchaseOrderNumber",
        "severity": "WARNING"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Statuses:

```text
PASSED
PASSED_WITH_WARNINGS
FAILED
NEEDS_REVIEW
REJECTED
```

---

## 22. Confidence Scoring

Confidence harus dihitung berlapis:

```text
word confidence
→ line confidence
→ field confidence
→ table row confidence
→ document confidence
→ routing decision
```

### Field confidence formula contoh

```text
fieldConfidence =
  0.50 * OCR confidence
+ 0.20 * label match confidence
+ 0.15 * spatial confidence
+ 0.15 * validation confidence
```

Field dengan regex/type valid harus lebih dipercaya daripada sekadar OCR confidence tinggi.

Example:

| Field | OCR Conf | Label Conf | Regex Valid | Final |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| invoiceNumber | 0.84 | 0.95 | yes | 0.89 |
| totalAmount | 0.92 | 0.88 | yes | 0.91 |
| taxId | 0.96 | 0.30 | no | 0.55 |

---

## 23. Routing Decision

```text
AUTO_ACCEPT
AUTO_ACCEPT_WITH_WARNING
HUMAN_REVIEW
REQUEST_RECAPTURE
REJECT_UNSUPPORTED
SECURITY_REVIEW
FAILED
```

Decision matrix:

| Condition | Decision |
|---|---|
| all required fields present, valid, high confidence | `AUTO_ACCEPT` |
| required valid, optional low confidence | `AUTO_ACCEPT_WITH_WARNING` |
| required field missing | `HUMAN_REVIEW` |
| required field invalid | `HUMAN_REVIEW` |
| document type mismatch | `HUMAN_REVIEW` / `REJECT_UNSUPPORTED` |
| image blur too high | `REQUEST_RECAPTURE` |
| malware/suspicious content | `SECURITY_REVIEW` |
| unsupported file type | `REJECT_UNSUPPORTED` |
| provider transient failure | retry |
| provider permanent failure | `FAILED` / fallback |

Camunda user tasks are appropriate for human review because a user task represents work done by a human with workflow engine assistance, and the process waits until the user task is completed before continuing. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 24. Camunda BPMN Flow

```text
Start Document AI Process
  │
  ▼
Validate Asset
  │
  ├── invalid/security → Reject End
  │
  ▼
Preprocess Document
  │
  ▼
Run OCR / Layout AI
  │
  ▼
Classify Document
  │
  ├── unknown/mismatch → Human Review
  │
  ▼
Extract Fields / Tables
  │
  ▼
Validate Extracted Data
  │
  ├── passed high confidence → Auto Accept
  │
  ├── warnings only          → Auto Accept With Warning
  │
  ├── missing/invalid        → Human Review
  │
  └── suspicious             → Security Review
  │
  ▼
Persist Final Result
  │
  ▼
Publish Event / Continue Case Workflow
```

Camunda best practice generally recommends assigning human tasks to groups rather than fixed individuals, which fits review queues such as `document-reviewer`, `kyc-reviewer`, or `finance-reviewer`. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/architecture/understanding-human-tasks-management/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql
CREATE TABLE document_ai_result (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id                  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
```

## 31. Failure Classification

| Failure Class | Meaning | Retry? | Routing |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `DOCUMENT_TYPE_UNKNOWN` | classifier tidak yakin | No | human review |
| `DOCUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH` | expected vs predicted berbeda | No | review/reject |
| `LAYOUT_EXTRACTION_FAILED` | provider/layout failed | Maybe | retry/fallback |
| `FIELD_EXTRACTION_FAILED` | extraction engine error | Maybe | retry/fallback |
| `REQUIRED_FIELD_MISSING` | field wajib tidak ditemukan | No | human review |
| `FIELD_VALIDATION_FAILED` | field invalid | No | human review |
| `TABLE_EXTRACTION_FAILED` | table ambiguous | No | review |
| `TOTAL_MISMATCH` | invoice totals inconsistent | No | review |
| `LOW_CONFIDENCE` | confidence bawah threshold | No | review |
| `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT` | cloud quota/rate limit | Yes | retry |
| `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | timeout | Yes | retry |
| `UNSUPPORTED_DOCUMENT` | schema/provider tidak mendukung | No | reject/manual |
| `SECURITY_REJECTED` | suspicious/malware/unsafe | No | security review |

## 33. Audit Checklist

For every final extracted field, you should be able to answer:

```text
Field value came from which source document?
Which page?
Which bounding box?
Which OCR/provider?
Which model/config version?
What was the confidence?
Which validation rules passed/failed?
Was it corrected by a human?
Who corrected it?
When?
Why?
What value was changed?
```

This is critical for regulated systems.

---

## 34. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Treat OCR output as final truth | wrong data enters workflow | validation + review |
| One schema for all documents | extraction chaos | schema per document type |
| No document classification | wrong extraction rules | classifier + expected type |
| No field-level confidence | cannot review intelligently | confidence per field |
| No bounding box | review UI weak | store bbox/source elements |
| No correction audit | defensibility gap | correction table/event |
| Raw provider response as API | vendor lock-in | canonical result model |
| Regex-only extraction | brittle | spatial + layout + validation |
| No cross-field validation | invoice total errors pass | validation engine |
| Auto-accept low confidence | data quality risk | threshold routing |
| Review everything | operational bottleneck | selective review |
| No schema version | result drift | versioned schema/policy |
| Log full OCR text | PII leak | structured redacted logging |

---

## 35. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text
Document Taxonomy
[ ] Supported document types defined
[ ] UNKNOWN fallback exists
[ ] Expected vs predicted document type checked
[ ] Schema per document type exists

Pipeline
[ ] Metadata extraction before AI processing
[ ] OCR/layout provider abstraction exists
[ ] Classification step explicit
[ ] Extraction step separate from validation
[ ] Routing decision explicit

Schema & Extraction
[ ] Required fields defined
[ ] Optional fields defined
[ ] Field labels/synonyms defined
[ ] Extraction strategy per field
[ ] Table schema defined where needed
[ ] Selection mark/signature handling defined

Validation
[ ] Type/regex validation
[ ] Cross-field validation
[ ] Required field validation
[ ] Duplicate/reference validation
[ ] Confidence threshold per field
[ ] Document-level validation status

Review
[ ] Human review route exists
[ ] Review reason structured
[ ] Review payload includes bbox/page image
[ ] Corrections audited
[ ] Review completion resumes workflow

Persistence
[ ] canonical result JSON stored
[ ] raw provider result stored privately
[ ] hot fields normalized into table
[ ] schema version stored
[ ] provider/model version stored

Ops/Security
[ ] PII not dumped to logs
[ ] access control by tenant/case
[ ] metrics: auto-accept/review/correction
[ ] cost and provider latency tracked
[ ] retention policy follows source document
```

---

## 36. Minimal Implementation untuk Tahap Awal

Implementasi awal yang realistis:

```text
1. document_schema table
2. document_schema_field table
3. document classifier sederhana: expected type + keyword/layout rules
4. canonical DocumentAiResult model
5. field extraction engine untuk key-value sederhana
6. invoice schema v1
7. validation engine: required/type/regex/cross-field
8. routing decision: AUTO_ACCEPT vs HUMAN_REVIEW
9. Camunda human review task
10. correction audit table
```

Start dengan satu document type dulu, misalnya:

```text
INVOICE v1
```

Jangan langsung mendukung semua dokumen. Pilih satu schema, ukur auto-accept rate dan correction rate, lalu baru scale ke document type lain.

## 37. Roadmap Singkat Setelah Seri 9

Urutan berikutnya:

```text
Seri 10 → Human Review Workflow dengan Camunda
Seri 11 → Speech-to-Text Batch Transcription
Seri 12 → Streaming Transcription
Seri 13 → Speaker Diarization, Timestamp Alignment, Subtitle SRT/VTT
Seri 14 → Text-to-Speech
```

Document AI pipeline adalah titik di mana OCR berubah menjadi **business automation**. Fokus senior engineer bukan hanya “bisa extract”, tetapi “bisa dipercaya, divalidasi, diaudit, dan dioperasikan”.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:34:58 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 10/20**: human review workflow dengan Camunda. Fokusnya adalah bagaimana hasil OCR/Document AI yang tidak cukup yakin masuk ke review queue, dikoreksi manusia, diaudit, lalu workflow dilanjutkan secara defensible.

# Seri 10/20 - Human Review Workflow dengan Camunda  
## Review Queue, Assignment, Correction, Approval/Rejection, SLA, Escalation, Audit, dan Feedback Loop

## 1. Introduction

Dalam pipeline **OCR / Document AI**, human review adalah mekanisme untuk menangani output AI yang:

```text id="wra3r7"
- confidence rendah
- field wajib hilang
- validasi gagal
- dokumen tidak cocok dengan expected type
- OCR membaca value ambigu
- table total tidak konsisten
- input blur / miring / tidak lengkap
- perlu keputusan bisnis manusia
```

Human review bukan sekadar “manual approval”. Dalam sistem yang defensible, human review adalah **controlled state transition**:

```text id="z1i7t"
AI result
→ review task
→ reviewer decision
→ correction/audit
→ final structured result
→ workflow lanjut
```

Camunda cocok untuk ini karena **User Task** merepresentasikan pekerjaan yang dilakukan manusia dengan bantuan workflow engine, dan process menunggu sampai task tersebut selesai sebelum lanjut. Camunda juga mendukung job worker untuk service task, retry, dan incident ketika retries habis. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="5i2v6p"
Human Review Workflow
=
AI uncertainty resolution
+ business validation
+ correction capture
+ audit trail
+ process continuation
```

Jangan desain human review sebagai tabel status sederhana tanpa workflow. Yang dibutuhkan:

```text id="7gx82y"
Review reason
Reviewer assignment
Review payload
Field correction
Decision
Audit
SLA
Escalation
Feedback to extraction quality
```

Human review sebaiknya hanya muncul bila ada alasan jelas. Kalau semua dokumen masuk review, automation tidak memberi nilai.

---

## 3. Diagram

```text id="quirdm"
Document AI Pipeline
        │
        ▼
Extraction + Validation
        │
        ├── high confidence + valid
        │       ▼
        │   Auto Accept
        │
        ├── low confidence / missing / invalid
        │       ▼
        │   Human Review Required
        │       │
        │       ▼
        │   Camunda User Task
        │       │
        │       ├── Reviewer accepts AI result
        │       ├── Reviewer corrects fields
        │       ├── Reviewer rejects document
        │       ├── Reviewer requests recapture
        │       └── Reviewer escalates
        │
        ▼
Final Decision
        │
        ├── Persist corrected result
        ├── Update job status
        ├── Publish domain event
        └── Continue downstream workflow
```

---

## 4. Human Review Skill Map

| Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| Review trigger | Kapan task manusia dibuat | Menghindari review overload |
| Review reason taxonomy | Missing field, low confidence, mismatch, total error | Task bisa diprioritaskan |
| Camunda User Task | BPMN user task, assignee/candidate group, variables | Orchestration manusia |
| Review queue | Claim, assign, release, pause, resume | Operasional reviewer |
| Review payload | Fields, bbox, page image, validation errors | Reviewer bisa kerja cepat |
| Correction model | old value, new value, reason, source bbox | Audit dan feedback |
| Decision model | accept, correct, reject, recapture, escalate | Workflow branch |
| SLA | due date, priority, escalation | Case tidak stuck |
| Authorization | reviewer group, tenant/case permission | Data sensitif |
| Audit trail | siapa mengubah apa, kapan, kenapa | Regulatory defensibility |
| Feedback loop | correction data untuk tuning OCR/rules | Quality improvement |
| Metrics | review rate, correction rate, aging, backlog | Operability |

---

## 5. Review Trigger Taxonomy

Human review harus punya alasan eksplisit.

| Review Reason | Contoh | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `LOW_DOCUMENT_CONFIDENCE` | document confidence < threshold | Review semua field utama |
| `LOW_FIELD_CONFIDENCE` | invoice number confidence 0.62 | Review field terkait |
| `REQUIRED_FIELD_MISSING` | total amount tidak ditemukan | Reviewer isi/cari manual |
| `FIELD_VALIDATION_FAILED` | NIK bukan 16 digit | Correct/reject |
| `TOTAL_MISMATCH` | line item sum beda dengan total | Correct table/total |
| `DOCUMENT_TYPE_UNKNOWN` | classifier tidak yakin | Pilih document type |
| `DOCUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH` | expected invoice, detected receipt | Confirm/reject |
| `IMAGE_QUALITY_POOR` | blur/low contrast | Request recapture |
| `MULTIPLE_CANDIDATES` | dua kemungkinan invoice number | Pilih kandidat |
| `PROVIDER_ERROR_FALLBACK` | provider gagal tapi basic OCR ada | Review result |
| `SECURITY_SUSPICIOUS` | MIME mismatch / embedded file | Security review |
| `BUSINESS_RULE_EXCEPTION` | vendor tidak dikenal | Escalate/business review |

---

## 6. BPMN Review Flow

```text id="ll0cyn"
Start
  │
  ▼
Run Document AI
  │
  ▼
Evaluate Extraction Result
  │
  ├── AUTO_ACCEPT
  │      ▼
  │   Persist Final Result
  │      ▼
  │   End Completed
  │
  ├── HUMAN_REVIEW
  │      ▼
  │   Create Review Task
  │      ▼
  │   Reviewer Completes Task
  │      ▼
  │   Apply Corrections
  │      ▼
  │   Re-validate Corrected Result
  │      ├── valid
  │      │    ▼
  │      │  Persist Final Result
  │      │    ▼
  │      │  End Completed
  │      │
  │      └── invalid
  │           ▼
  │         Escalate / Reject / Request Recapture
  │
  └── SECURITY_REVIEW
         ▼
      Security Review Task
         ▼
      Reject / Allow Continue
```

Dalam Camunda 8, user task dapat dimodelkan di BPMN; Tasklist dan task APIs menyediakan lifecycle seperti update candidate users/groups, due date, follow-up date, dan action semacam start/pause/resume atau custom audit action. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Recommended BPMN Structure

Jangan jadikan semua detail correction sebagai BPMN node. BPMN cukup menangkap business milestones.

```text id="n34xl9"
Service Task: Run Document AI
Service Task: Evaluate Review Need
Exclusive Gateway: Routing Decision
User Task: Review Extracted Document
Service Task: Apply Human Corrections
Service Task: Revalidate Corrected Data
Exclusive Gateway: Review Outcome
Service Task: Persist Final Result
End Event
```

Detail field correction, bbox, candidate values, dan UI interaction lebih baik di application layer / review service.

## 8. Process Variables

Minimal process variables:

```json id="67ly88"
{
  "jobId": "job_789",
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "tenantId": "tnt_001",
  "sourceAssetId": "ast_123",
  "documentType": "INVOICE",
  "schemaVersion": "invoice-v1",
  "routingDecision": "HUMAN_REVIEW",
  "reviewReason": "LOW_FIELD_CONFIDENCE",
  "reviewTaskId": "rev_123",
  "reviewPriority": "HIGH",
  "reviewGroup": "finance-document-reviewer"
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="tw41g9"
Process variable menyimpan pointer dan routing state.
Payload besar seperti OCR JSON, image, table detail tetap di database/object storage.
```

Jangan memasukkan full OCR text besar, raw provider JSON besar, atau base64 image sebagai Camunda variables.

## 9. Review Task Data Model

### `document_review_task`

```sql id="6ouutu"
CREATE TABLE document_review_task (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id                UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
    document_ai_result_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES document_ai_result(id),

    tenant_id             UUID NOT NULL,
    case_id               VARCHAR(100),
    document_type          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    status                VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    priority              VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',

    review_reason          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    review_reason_details  JSONB,

    assigned_to            UUID,
    candidate_group        VARCHAR(100),
    claimed_at             TIMESTAMPTZ,
    due_at                 TIMESTAMPTZ,
    escalated_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,

    camunda_process_key    TEXT,
    camunda_user_task_key  TEXT,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

### Status

```text id="9hbbf6"
CREATED
AVAILABLE
CLAIMED
IN_REVIEW
PENDING_INFO
ESCALATED
COMPLETED
REJECTED
CANCELLED
EXPIRED
```

## 11. Review Decision Model

Top-level task decision:

```text id="3mblpy"
ACCEPT_ALL
ACCEPT_WITH_CORRECTIONS
REJECT_DOCUMENT
REQUEST_RECAPTURE
ESCALATE
MARK_UNSUPPORTED
SECURITY_REJECT
```

Decision meaning:

| Decision | Meaning | Next workflow |
|---|---|---|
| `ACCEPT_ALL` | AI result dianggap benar | Persist final |
| `ACCEPT_WITH_CORRECTIONS` | Reviewer mengoreksi beberapa field | Revalidate lalu persist |
| `REJECT_DOCUMENT` | Dokumen tidak valid | End rejected |
| `REQUEST_RECAPTURE` | Input blur/salah/tidak lengkap | Notify uploader |
| `ESCALATE` | Butuh reviewer senior/business/legal/security | Escalation task |
| `MARK_UNSUPPORTED` | Dokumen tidak didukung pipeline | Manual fallback/reject |
| `SECURITY_REJECT` | Dokumen suspicious/unsafe | Security terminal |

## 16. Apply Corrections Service

Setelah user task selesai, service task berikutnya menerapkan correction.

```java id="lpvxlo"
public final class ApplyReviewCorrectionsWorker {

    private final ReviewTaskRepository reviewTaskRepository;
    private final DocumentAiResultRepository resultRepository;
    private final DocumentValidationEngine validationEngine;
    private final MediaJobRepository jobRepository;

    public void execute(UUID reviewTaskId) {
        DocumentReviewTask task = reviewTaskRepository.get(reviewTaskId);

        ReviewDecision decision = task.decision();

        switch (decision.type()) {
            case ACCEPT_ALL -> {
                resultRepository.markFinal(task.documentAiResultId());
                jobRepository.markCompleted(task.jobId());
            }

            case ACCEPT_WITH_CORRECTIONS -> {
                CorrectedDocumentResult corrected = resultRepository.applyCorrections(
                        task.documentAiResultId(),
                        task.reviewedFields()
                );

                DocumentValidationResult validation = validationEngine.validate(corrected);

                if (validation.passed()) {
                    resultRepository.markFinalCorrected(corrected.id(), validation);
                    jobRepository.markCompleted(task.jobId());
                } else {
                    reviewTaskRepository.createEscalationTask(task, validation);
                    jobRepository.markNeedsReview(task.jobId(), "CORRECTION_VALIDATION_FAILED");
                }
            }

            case REQUEST_RECAPTURE -> {
                jobRepository.markRejected(task.jobId(), "RECAPTURE_REQUIRED");
            }

            case REJECT_DOCUMENT -> {
                jobRepository.markRejected(task.jobId(), "HUMAN_REJECTED_DOCUMENT");
            }

            case ESCALATE -> {
                reviewTaskRepository.createEscalationTask(task, task.escalationReason());
                jobRepository.markNeedsReview(task.jobId(), "ESCALATED");
            }

            case SECURITY_REJECT -> {
                jobRepository.markSecurityRejected(task.jobId(), "HUMAN_SECURITY_REJECT");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 17. Camunda Completion Pattern

In Camunda 8, service workers complete jobs by sending variables that are merged into the process instance; if a job fails and retries reach zero, an incident is raised until it is resolved. This makes it natural to pass only compact review outcome variables back to the process. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Example completion variables:

```json id="e3zcg5"
{
  "reviewDecision": "ACCEPT_WITH_CORRECTIONS",
  "reviewTaskId": "rev_123",
  "reviewCompletedBy": "user_123",
  "correctedFieldCount": 1,
  "reviewValidationStatus": "PASSED",
  "routingDecision": "PERSIST_FINAL_RESULT"
}
```

---

## 18. SLA Model

Review tasks need SLA.

```sql id="eozldg"
CREATE TABLE review_sla_policy (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    document_type          VARCHAR(100),
    review_reason          VARCHAR(100),
    priority               VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    due_duration_minutes   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    warning_before_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL,
    escalation_group       VARCHAR(100),

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Example policy:

| Review Reason | Priority | Due |
|---|---|---:|
| `SECURITY_SUSPICIOUS` | Critical | 1 hour |
| `TOTAL_MISMATCH` | High | 4 hours |
| `LOW_FIELD_CONFIDENCE` | Normal | 1 business day |
| `DOCUMENT_TYPE_UNKNOWN` | Normal | 1 business day |
| `IMAGE_QUALITY_POOR` | Low | 2 business days |

---

## 19. Escalation Flow

```text id="m4twe9"
Review Task Created
  │
  ├── no claim before warning time
  │      └── notify review group
  │
  ├── not completed by due time
  │      └── escalate to senior group
  │
  ├── reviewer chooses ESCALATE
  │      └── create escalation task
  │
  └── security reason
         └── route security group directly
```

Camunda can model escalation with timer boundary events or separate scheduler/service. In practice, keep SLA state queryable in your database, because review managers need backlog and aging dashboards.

---

## 20. Review Queue Query

```sql id="fvk19g"
SELECT
    id,
    job_id,
    case_id,
    document_type,
    review_reason,
    status,
    priority,
    assigned_to,
    candidate_group,
    due_at,
    created_at
FROM document_review_task
WHERE tenant_id = :tenantId
  AND status IN ('AVAILABLE', 'CLAIMED', 'IN_REVIEW', 'ESCALATED')
  AND (
      assigned_to = :currentUserId
      OR assigned_to IS NULL
  )
  AND candidate_group = ANY(:userGroups)
ORDER BY
    CASE priority
        WHEN 'CRITICAL' THEN 1
        WHEN 'HIGH' THEN 2
        WHEN 'NORMAL' THEN 3
        WHEN 'LOW' THEN 4
        ELSE 5
    END,
    due_at ASC NULLS LAST,
    created_at ASC
LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset;
```

Important note: Camunda Tasklist behavior differs across versions/configurations; in Camunda 8 Tasklist V2, candidate users/groups are not evaluated by Tasklist for visibility/assignment in the way V1 did, so for production application-specific review queues, implement authorization and queue filtering in your own domain service as the source of truth. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 21. Authorization Model

Human review often exposes sensitive PII.

Minimum checks:

```text id="ces760"
- authenticated reviewer
- tenant membership
- candidate group membership
- case/document permission
- sensitivity class permission
- action permission: view, claim, correct, reject, escalate
```

Example permission matrix:

| Role | View | Claim | Correct | Reject | Escalate | Security Reject |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `document-reviewer` | Yes | Yes | Yes | Limited | Yes | No |
| `senior-reviewer` | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | No |
| `security-reviewer` | Yes | Yes | No | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `case-owner` | Yes | No/Maybe | No | No | Maybe | No |
| `admin` | Yes | Maybe | Maybe | Maybe | Yes | Maybe |

---

## 22. Review UI Requirements

Reviewer UI should not show only a form. It should show context.

```text id="m5uipl"
Left panel:
- page image/PDF preview
- bbox overlay
- low confidence highlights
- selected field highlight

Right panel:
- extracted fields
- confidence
- validation errors
- candidate values
- correction input
- action buttons

Bottom/audit panel:
- previous corrections
- review reason
- document classification
- job history
```

Field interaction:

```text id="mlxbee"
Click field → jump to bbox
Click bbox → focus field
Hover word → show confidence
Correct value → record correction reason
Submit → validate before complete
```

---

For ambiguous fields, show candidates.

```json id="q64fe4"
{
  "fieldName": "invoiceNumber",

    {

    },
    {

      "value": "INV-2026-001",
      "confidence": 0.58,
      "bbox": { "x": 412, "y": 118, "width": 180, "height": 24 }
    }
  ]
}
```

This reduces typing and creates better training feedback.

## 24. Feedback Loop

Correction data is valuable. Use it to improve extraction.

```text id="owhjyn"
Human correction
  │
  ├── update field label synonyms
  ├── tune regex/pattern
  ├── adjust confidence threshold
  ├── improve preprocessing
  ├── identify vendor template
  ├── create training dataset
  └── evaluate provider/model accuracy
```

Feedback metrics:

| Metric | Meaning |
|---|---|
| correction rate by field | field extractor quality |
| correction rate by document type | schema/provider fit |
| correction rate by provider | provider comparison |
| low confidence but accepted rate | threshold too strict |
| high confidence but corrected rate | threshold too loose / overconfidence |
| missing field rate | extraction strategy gap |
| review aging | ops capacity issue |
| reviewer disagreement | ambiguous business rule |

---

Use cases:

```text id="my21tk"
- evaluate OCR model
- tune extraction rules
- create labeled data
- audit false positives
- compare provider versions
```

---

## 26. Revalidation After Correction

Correction is not automatically valid. Revalidate.

```text id="r1r3iy"
Reviewer corrected totalAmount = 12,500,000
  │
  ▼
Normalize currency
  │
  ▼
Validate decimal
  │
  ▼
Cross-check subtotal + tax
  │
  ├── pass → complete
  └── fail → require confirmation/escalation
```

Example:

```java id="v2yjue"
public DocumentValidationResult revalidateAfterReview(
        DocumentAiResult original,
        ReviewDecision decision
) {
    CorrectedDocumentResult corrected = correctionApplier.apply(original, decision);

    DocumentValidationResult validation = validationEngine.validate(corrected);

    if (validation.hasBlockingErrors()) {
        return validation.withRoutingDecision("ESCALATE");
    }

    return validation.withRoutingDecision("FINALIZE");
}
```

---

## 27. Event-Driven Integration

Publish events after review.

```text id="59sfmf"
DOCUMENT_REVIEW_REQUIRED
DOCUMENT_REVIEW_CLAIMED
DOCUMENT_FIELD_CORRECTED
DOCUMENT_REVIEW_COMPLETED
DOCUMENT_REVIEW_ESCALATED
DOCUMENT_REVIEW_REJECTED
DOCUMENT_RECAPTURE_REQUESTED
```

Example final event:

```json id="oq6nx2"
{
  "eventId": "evt_789",
  "eventType": "DOCUMENT_REVIEW_COMPLETED",
  "jobId": "job_123",
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "documentType": "INVOICE",
  "decision": "ACCEPT_WITH_CORRECTIONS",
  "correctedFieldCount": 1,
  "completedBy": "user_123",
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-13T18:00:00+07:00"
}
```

---

## 28. Failure Handling

| Failure | Meaning | Handling |
|---|---|---|
| `REVIEW_TASK_NOT_FOUND` | stale UI/task | reload task |
| `TASK_ALREADY_CLAIMED` | reviewer race | show current assignee |
| `TASK_ALREADY_COMPLETED` | duplicate submit | idempotent success if same decision |
| `INVALID_CORRECTION` | value fails type/regex | reject submit |
| `UNAUTHORIZED_REVIEW_ACTION` | wrong group/tenant | deny |
| `CAMUNDA_TASK_COMPLETE_FAILED` | engine issue | retry service action |
| `CORRECTION_REVALIDATION_FAILED` | corrected data invalid | keep task open/escalate |
| `ASSET_NOT_AVAILABLE` | page preview missing | ops incident |
| `SLA_BREACHED` | review overdue | escalate |
| `CONCURRENT_UPDATE` | two reviewers edited | optimistic lock conflict |

---

## 29. Optimistic Locking

Use version column.

```sql id="3uf1o8"
ALTER TABLE document_review_task
ADD COLUMN version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
```

Update pattern:

```sql id="v2ykqo"
UPDATE document_review_task
SET status = :newStatus,
    assigned_to = :assignedTo,
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE id = :reviewTaskId
  AND version = :expectedVersion;
```

If zero rows updated:

```text id="esvrlk"
→ task changed by another reviewer
→ reload latest state
```

---

## 30. Idempotent Complete

Review complete endpoint should be idempotent.

```text id="l5n6dm"
First complete:
- applies corrections
- completes Camunda user task
- marks task completed
- publishes event

Retry same request:
- returns existing completion result

Retry different request:
- reject conflict
```

Table:

```sql id="q7r8ln"
CREATE TABLE review_completion_request (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    review_task_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES document_review_task(id),
    idempotency_key        TEXT NOT NULL,
    request_hash           TEXT NOT NULL,
    response_json          JSONB,
    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (review_task_id, idempotency_key)
);
```

---

## 31. Camunda Incident Handling

If service task after review fails, e.g. `Apply Corrections` or `Persist Final Result`, Camunda job retries should be used for transient errors. If retries are exhausted, Camunda creates an incident, and official docs describe resolving job-related incidents by updating variables if needed, increasing retries, and marking incident resolved. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/incidents/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Operational implication:

```text id="k6miv4"
Human already completed review.
Do not ask reviewer to redo work just because downstream persistence failed.
Retry/idempotently resume from saved review decision.
```

---

| Metric | Meaning |

| `document_review_created_total` | review volume |

| `document_review_escalation_total` | complexity/unclear rules |
| `document_review_task_duration_seconds` | reviewer handling time |
| `document_review_field_correction_rate_by_field` | field quality |

Best dashboard:

```text id="klymfh"
- backlog by priority
- aging by candidate group
- SLA breach trend
- correction rate by document type
- correction rate by field
- auto-accept vs review trend
- reviewer throughput
```

## 33. Audit Requirements

For every reviewed document, you should answer:

```text id="il5wh1"
Why did this document enter review?
Who claimed the task?
Who completed it?
What fields were changed?
What were the old and new values?
What was the source bounding box?
What validation errors existed?
What decision was made?
Was the decision escalated?
What process instance continued afterward?
```

That is the core defensibility requirement.

---

## 34. Security and Privacy

Human review often has broader data exposure than automated extraction.

Checklist:

```text id="0spxpw"
[ ] reviewers see only tenant/case they are allowed to see
[ ] sensitive fields masked unless needed
[ ] raw page images served via short-lived signed URL
[ ] review UI does not cache sensitive pages uncontrolled
[ ] audit logs do not expose full PII unnecessarily
[ ] screenshots/downloads controlled where required
[ ] correction comments sanitized
[ ] hOCR/HTML output never rendered raw
[ ] reviewer action is logged
[ ] retention policy applies to review payload and corrections
```

---

## 35. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Review everything | automation value hilang | threshold-based selective review |
| Review task tanpa reason | reviewer bingung | structured review reason |
| Correction overwrite tanpa audit | defensibility gap | append-only correction audit |
| Complete task tanpa revalidation | invalid final data | revalidate after correction |
| Store big OCR payload in Camunda variable | engine bloat | DB/object storage pointer |
| Assignment hard-coded to user | bottleneck | candidate group / review queue |
| No SLA | stuck task | due date + escalation |
| No idempotency on complete | duplicate side effects | idempotency key |
| No optimistic lock | conflicting edits | version check |
| UI without bbox | slow review | page overlay/highlight |
| No feedback loop | quality stagnant | correction analytics |
| Trust Camunda Tasklist visibility only | domain auth gap | app-level authorization |

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:37:03 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 11/20**: Speech-to-Text batch transcription. Fokusnya adalah pipeline audio/video non-realtime: upload media, extract/normalize audio, jalankan provider STT, simpan transcript segment, confidence, timestamps, retry, dan search.

# Seri 11/20 - Speech-to-Text Batch Transcription  
## Audio/Video Upload, Audio Extraction, Normalization, Provider Abstraction, Transcript Result Model, Confidence, Retry, dan Searchable Transcript

## 1. Introduction

Seri ini membahas **Speech-to-Text batch transcription**, yaitu proses mengubah audio/video yang sudah di-upload menjadi transcript secara asynchronous.

Bedakan dulu:

```text
Batch transcription
→ file audio/video sudah tersedia
→ proses asynchronous
→ result diambil setelah selesai

Streaming transcription
→ audio dikirim real-time
→ partial transcript keluar real-time
→ latency menjadi concern utama
```

Untuk stack Java/Jersey/JAX-RS, batch transcription sebaiknya tidak dilakukan langsung dalam request thread. Jersey API cukup membuat job dan mengembalikan `jobId`; worker/Camunda yang menjalankan audio extraction, normalization, provider call, result parsing, dan persistence.

Cloud provider besar mendukung batch transcription. AWS Transcribe menyimpan hasil batch transcript ke S3 atau service-managed bucket, Azure Batch Transcription menerima audio batch dan hasilnya bisa diambil secara asynchronous dari storage, sedangkan Google Speech-to-Text mendukung konfigurasi seperti word time offsets untuk timestamp per kata. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/how-input.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text
Batch STT Pipeline
=
media ingestion
+ metadata extraction
+ audio extraction
+ audio normalization
+ transcription provider
+ transcript normalization
+ searchable persistence
+ review/correction if needed
```

Input bisa berupa:

```text
- WAV
- MP3
- M4A
- FLAC
- OGG
- MP4 video
- MOV video
- MKV video
- meeting recording
- call recording
- interview audio
- evidence audio/video
```

Output yang baik bukan hanya plain text:

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_123",
  "language": "id-ID",
  "durationMs": 184000,
  "segments": [
    {
      "speaker": "SPEAKER_1",
      "startMs": 1200,
      "endMs": 4600,
      "text": "Selamat pagi, kita mulai meeting.",
      "confidence": 0.91
    }
  ],
  "fullText": "Selamat pagi, kita mulai meeting..."
}
```

---

## 3. Diagram

```text
Speech-to-Text Batch Pipeline
│
├── 1. Input Asset
│   ├── audio file
│   ├── video file
│   ├── call recording
│   └── meeting recording
│
├── 2. Metadata Extraction
│   ├── duration
│   ├── codec
│   ├── sample rate
│   ├── channels
│   ├── audio stream exists
│   └── file size
│
├── 3. Audio Preparation
│   ├── extract audio from video
│   ├── transcode unsupported codec
│   ├── normalize sample rate
│   ├── mono/stereo handling
│   ├── volume normalization
│   ├── silence detection
│   └── chunking for long audio
│
├── 4. STT Provider
│   ├── AWS Transcribe
│   ├── Azure Speech Batch Transcription
│   ├── Google Speech-to-Text
│   ├── Whisper/local model
│   └── custom/on-prem ASR
│
├── 5. Transcript Normalization
│   ├── full text
│   ├── segments
│   ├── words
│   ├── timestamps
│   ├── speaker labels
│   ├── confidence
│   └── language metadata
│
├── 6. Persistence
│   ├── transcript_job_result
│   ├── transcript_segment
│   ├── transcript_word
│   ├── transcript_artifact
│   └── search index
│
└── 7. Workflow / Review
    ├── low confidence review
    ├── speaker correction
    ├── redaction
    ├── subtitle generation
    └── downstream case workflow
```

---

## 4. Practical Skill Map

| Skill Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| Audio/video metadata | duration, codec, sample rate, channels | Routing dan cost guardrail |
| Audio extraction | video → audio track | STT biasanya butuh audio stream |
| Audio normalization | WAV/FLAC, sample rate, mono/stereo | Akurasi dan provider compatibility |
| Provider abstraction | AWS/Azure/Google/local | Hindari vendor lock-in |
| Batch job lifecycle | submit, poll, callback, fetch result | STT provider biasanya async |
| Transcript model | full text, segment, word, timestamp | Search, subtitle, review |
| Speaker diarization | siapa bicara kapan | Meeting/call transcript |
| Custom vocabulary | domain terms, product names, names | Meningkatkan akurasi istilah domain |
| Confidence scoring | word/segment/job confidence | Review routing |
| Chunking | audio panjang dipotong | Limit provider, retry, parallelism |
| Subtitle generation | SRT/VTT | Output video/caption |
| Search indexing | searchable transcript | Case/evidence search |
| PII redaction | masking sensitive speech | Compliance |
| Retry safety | provider timeout/rate limit | Cost dan reliability |
| Audit | provider version, input hash, options | Defensibility |

---

## 5. Batch vs Streaming

| Aspect | Batch Transcription | Streaming Transcription |
|---|---|---|
| Input | file sudah ada | audio live chunks |
| API style | async job | WebSocket/gRPC/event stream |
| Latency | menit/jam tergantung durasi | near real-time |
| Use case | meeting recording, call archive, evidence | live captioning, live assistant |
| Retry | lebih mudah | lebih kompleks |
| Result quality | biasanya lebih kaya | partial/final merge perlu hati-hati |
| Jersey fit | sangat cocok sebagai REST job API | Jersey REST kurang ideal; gunakan WebSocket/service khusus |

AWS sendiri membedakan batch transcription yang memakai media file dengan streaming transcription yang mengirim media secara real time dan mengembalikan transcript real time. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/streaming.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Recommended Architecture

```text
Client
  │
  ▼
Jersey API
  │ submit transcription job
  ▼
media_job table
  │
  ▼
Camunda / Queue
  │
  ▼
Transcription Worker
  │
  ├── load media asset
  ├── inspect metadata
  ├── extract audio if video
  ├── normalize audio
  ├── submit provider job
  ├── poll/fetch result
  ├── normalize transcript
  ├── persist result
  └── update job state
```

Jersey endpoint sebaiknya seperti ini:

```text
POST /transcription-jobs
GET  /transcription-jobs/{jobId}
GET  /transcription-jobs/{jobId}/result
GET  /transcription-jobs/{jobId}/segments
GET  /transcription-jobs/{jobId}/search?q=...
```

Bukan seperti ini:

```text
POST /transcribe-now
→ upload 2GB video
→ wait 1 hour
→ timeout
```

---

## 7. Input Validation untuk STT

Sebelum job STT dibuat, minimal cek:

| Validation | Reason |
|---|---|
| file exists | asset valid |
| MIME supported | route audio/video |
| duration known | cost guardrail |
| max duration | mencegah job terlalu mahal |
| max size | storage/cost guardrail |
| audio stream exists | STT butuh audio |
| codec supported | perlu transcode atau reject |
| encrypted/protected media | reject atau special flow |
| language option valid | provider compatibility |
| tenant/case permission | security |

Contoh rejection:

```json
{
  "status": "REJECTED",
  "errorClass": "AUDIO_STREAM_MISSING",
  "message": "The uploaded video does not contain an audio stream."
}
```

---

## 8. Audio Extraction dari Video

Untuk video input, pipeline umum:

```text
video.mp4
  │
  ▼
ffprobe metadata
  │
  ├── has audio stream? no → reject for STT
  └── has audio stream? yes
       │
       ▼
ffmpeg extract/normalize audio
       │
       ▼
audio.wav / audio.flac
       │
       ▼
STT provider
```

FFmpeg adalah universal media converter yang bisa membaca berbagai input, filter, dan transcode output; untuk pipeline ini, FFmpeg/FFprobe menjadi tools utama untuk ekstraksi audio dan metadata media. ([FFmpeg](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh command konseptual:

```bash
ffmpeg \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -vn \
  -ac 1 \
  -ar 16000 \
  -c:a pcm_s16le \
  output.wav
```

Arti:

| Option | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `-i input.mp4` | input video |
| `-vn` | disable video stream |
| `-ac 1` | mono channel |
| `-ar 16000` | sample rate 16 kHz |
| `-c:a pcm_s16le` | WAV PCM 16-bit |
| `output.wav` | normalized audio output |

Catatan: jangan selalu convert ke WAV besar jika provider mendukung compressed format seperti FLAC/MP3. WAV bagus untuk compatibility, tapi ukuran file bisa besar.

---

## 9. Audio Normalization Strategy

Tujuan normalization:

```text
- format kompatibel provider
- sample rate konsisten
- channel handling jelas
- volume terlalu rendah/tinggi dikoreksi
- noise/silence dianalisis
- transcript lebih stabil
```

| Concern | Strategy |
|---|---|
| unsupported codec | transcode |
| stereo call recording | preserve channels atau diarization |
| meeting audio | mono bisa cukup |
| low sample rate | jangan upsample berlebihan |
| noisy audio | optional denoise |
| long silence | detect/trim/chunk |
| very long recording | chunking |
| volume too low | loudness normalization |

Contoh FFmpeg normalization:

```bash
ffmpeg \
  -i input.m4a \
  -ac 1 \
  -ar 16000 \
  -af loudnorm \
  -c:a pcm_s16le \
  normalized.wav
```

Caveat:

```text
Denoise/loudness normalization bisa membantu,
tapi juga bisa merusak signal jika agresif.
Benchmark dengan sample audio nyata sebelum dijadikan default.
```

---

## 10. Provider Decision Matrix

| Requirement | AWS Transcribe | Azure Speech Batch | Google Speech-to-Text | Local/Whisper/on-prem |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AWS environment | sangat cocok | bisa | bisa | tergantung |
| Azure environment | bisa | sangat cocok | bisa | tergantung |
| Google Cloud | bisa | bisa | sangat cocok | tergantung |
| On-prem/data sensitive | kurang ideal | tergantung deployment | kurang ideal | cocok |
| Batch transcription | ya | ya | ya | ya |
| Streaming | ya | ya | ya | tergantung |
| Subtitle output | AWS supports SRT/VTT output | bisa dibuat dari timestamps/result | bisa dibuat dari timestamps/result | tergantung |
| Diarization | supported | supported in service capabilities | supported/config-dependent | model-dependent |
| Custom vocabulary | supported | supported | supported | model-dependent |
| Cost control | provider billing | provider billing | provider billing | infra cost |
| Vendor lock-in | tinggi jika raw API bocor | tinggi jika raw API bocor | tinggi jika raw API bocor | model/runtime lock-in |

AWS Transcribe batch transcripts dapat disimpan ke S3 bucket milik user atau service-managed bucket; AWS juga mendukung subtitle output SRT/VTT untuk batch video transcription. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/how-input.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 11. Provider Abstraction

Jangan biarkan business code langsung tergantung AWS/Azure/Google result shape.

```java
public interface SpeechToTextProvider {
    TranscriptionProviderJob submit(TranscriptionProviderRequest request);

    TranscriptionProviderStatus getStatus(String providerJobId);

    TranscriptionProviderResult fetchResult(String providerJobId);
}
```

Request:

```java
public record TranscriptionProviderRequest(
        UUID jobId,
        UUID assetId,
        URI mediaUri,
        String languageCode,
        boolean enableSpeakerDiarization,
        boolean enableWordTimestamps,
        boolean enablePunctuation,
        boolean enableProfanityFilter,
        boolean enablePiiRedaction,
        Integer expectedSpeakerCount,
        Map<String, String> customVocabulary
) {}
```

Result:

```java
public record TranscriptionProviderResult(
        String providerName,
        String providerJobId,
        String languageCode,
        Duration duration,
        String fullText,
        List<TranscriptSegment> segments,
        List<TranscriptWord> words,
        Map<String, Object> rawMetadata
) {}
```

Implementations:

```text
SpeechToTextProvider
├── AwsTranscribeProvider
├── AzureBatchSpeechProvider
├── GoogleSpeechToTextProvider
├── LocalWhisperProvider
└── CompositeFallbackSpeechProvider
```

---

## 12. Batch Job Lifecycle

```text
SUBMITTED
  │
  ▼
MEDIA_VALIDATING
  │
  ▼
AUDIO_PREPARING
  │
  ├── video input → extract audio
  ├── unsupported codec → transcode
  └── long audio → chunk
  │
  ▼
PROVIDER_SUBMITTING
  │
  ▼
PROVIDER_RUNNING
  │
  ├── poll status
  ├── wait callback
  └── handle timeout
  │
  ▼
RESULT_FETCHING
  │
  ▼
RESULT_NORMALIZING
  │
  ▼
RESULT_PERSISTING
  │
  ├── low confidence → NEEDS_REVIEW
  └── valid → COMPLETED
```

Provider state perlu dimapping ke internal state:

| Internal State | Provider State Example |
|---|---|
| `PROVIDER_RUNNING` | queued / in progress |
| `RESULT_FETCHING` | completed and result URI available |
| `FAILED` | provider failed |
| `RETRY_PENDING` | timeout/rate limit/network issue |

---

## 13. AWS Transcribe Pattern

AWS Transcribe batch pattern:

```text
1. upload media to S3
2. StartTranscriptionJob with media URI
3. poll GetTranscriptionJob
4. fetch transcript from output location
5. normalize transcript
```

AWS docs note that batch transcripts are stored in S3; if user specifies their own bucket, result remains there until user removes it, while service-managed bucket temporary URI is valid for 15 minutes and can be refreshed by `GetTranscriptionJob`. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/how-input.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Feature notes:

| Feature | AWS Transcribe |
|---|---|
| batch transcription | yes |
| speaker diarization | supported for batch and streaming |
| subtitle output | SRT/VTT output |
| output storage | S3 |
| custom vocabulary | supported |
| language config | supported |

AWS diarization docs describe speaker partitioning for batch transcription, and subtitle docs describe WebVTT/SubRip output. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/diarization.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 14. Azure Batch Transcription Pattern

Azure Batch Transcription pattern:

```text
1. provide audio URI / storage input
2. create batch transcription
3. service transcribes asynchronously
4. retrieve result from storage/container
5. normalize transcript
```

Microsoft’s batch transcription docs state that you submit audio data in batch, the service transcribes it, stores results in a storage container, and then you retrieve results from that container. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/batch-transcription-create?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Azure Speech-to-Text docs also describe speech-to-text capabilities including real-time, fast, and batch transcription options. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/speech-to-text?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15. Google Speech-to-Text Pattern

Google Speech-to-Text pattern:

```text
1. upload audio to Cloud Storage or provide content
2. create recognition request / long-running recognition
3. wait for operation result
4. normalize result
```

For transcript timestamps, Google Speech-to-Text supports word time offsets when `enableWordTimeOffsets` is true, returning start and end time offsets for recognized words in the top result. ([Google for Developers](https://developers.google.com/resources/api-libraries/documentation/speech/v1/java/latest/com/google/api/services/speech/v1/model/RecognitionConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16. Local / On-Prem Provider Pattern

Local STT provider cocok jika:

```text
- data tidak boleh keluar environment
- cloud cost terlalu tinggi
- offline/on-prem requirement
- latency bisa dikontrol dengan GPU
- model ingin dikustomisasi
```

Model umum:

```text
Java worker
  │
  ├── prepare audio
  ├── call local STT service via HTTP/gRPC
  ├── or run model subprocess
  ├── parse result
  └── persist transcript
```

Rekomendasi arsitektur:

```text
Jangan load model besar langsung di Jersey API.
Buat dedicated stt-worker atau stt-inference-service.
```

---

## 17. Transcript Result Model

Transcript harus mendukung beberapa level:

```text
Transcript
├── full text
├── segments / utterances
├── words
├── speakers
├── channels
├── confidence
├── timestamps
├── artifacts
└── provider metadata
```

Canonical JSON:

```json
{
  "jobId": "job_123",
  "sourceAssetId": "ast_audio_456",
  "provider": {
    "name": "AWS_TRANSCRIBE",
    "providerJobId": "transcribe-job-abc",
    "modelVersion": "provider-managed"
  },
  "languageCode": "id-ID",
  "durationMs": 184000,
  "fullText": "Selamat pagi, kita mulai meeting...",
  "segments": [
    {
      "segmentIndex": 0,
      "speakerLabel": "SPEAKER_1",
      "channelLabel": null,
      "startMs": 1200,
      "endMs": 4600,
      "text": "Selamat pagi, kita mulai meeting.",
      "confidence": 0.91
    }
  ],
  "words": [
    {
      "wordIndex": 0,
      "speakerLabel": "SPEAKER_1",
      "startMs": 1200,
      "endMs": 1650,
      "text": "Selamat",
      "confidence": 0.93
    }
  ],
  "quality": {
    "meanConfidence": 0.89,
    "lowConfidenceSegmentCount": 3,
    "requiresReview": false
  }
}
```

## 18. Database Schema

### `transcription_result`

```sql
CREATE TABLE transcription_result (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id                  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
    source_asset_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    normalized_audio_asset_id UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    provider_name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    provider_job_id          TEXT,
    provider_version         TEXT,
    model_name               TEXT,
    model_version            TEXT,

    language_code            VARCHAR(50),
    duration_ms              BIGINT,

    full_text                TEXT,
    mean_confidence          NUMERIC(6,5),
    requires_review          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    review_reason            TEXT,

    result_json              JSONB NOT NULL,
    raw_provider_asset_id    UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (job_id)
);
```

### `transcript_segment`

```sql
CREATE TABLE transcript_segment (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    transcription_result_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES transcription_result(id),

    segment_index            INTEGER NOT NULL,
    speaker_label            VARCHAR(100),
    channel_label            VARCHAR(100),

    start_ms                 BIGINT NOT NULL,
    end_ms                   BIGINT NOT NULL,

    text                     TEXT NOT NULL,
    confidence               NUMERIC(6,5),

    metadata_json            JSONB,
    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (transcription_result_id, segment_index)
);
```

### `transcript_word`

```sql
CREATE TABLE transcript_word (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    transcription_result_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES transcription_result(id),
    segment_id               UUID REFERENCES transcript_segment(id),

    word_index               INTEGER NOT NULL,
    speaker_label            VARCHAR(100),

    start_ms                 BIGINT,
    end_ms                   BIGINT,

    text                     TEXT NOT NULL,
    confidence               NUMERIC(6,5),

    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (transcription_result_id, word_index)
);
```

## 19. Full-Text Search

Transcript perlu searchable.

```sql
ALTER TABLE transcription_result
ADD COLUMN search_vector tsvector;

CREATE INDEX idx_transcription_result_search
ON transcription_result USING GIN (search_vector);
```

Update:

```sql
UPDATE transcription_result
SET search_vector = to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(full_text, ''))
WHERE id = :id;
```

Segment-level search juga berguna:

```sql
ALTER TABLE transcript_segment
ADD COLUMN search_vector tsvector;

CREATE INDEX idx_transcript_segment_search
ON transcript_segment USING GIN (search_vector);
```

Search API:

```http
GET /transcription-jobs/{jobId}/search?q=pembayaran
```

Response:

```json
{
  "query": "pembayaran",
  "matches": [
    {
      "segmentIndex": 12,
      "speakerLabel": "SPEAKER_2",
      "startMs": 84200,
      "endMs": 90100,
      "text": "Untuk pembayaran, kita perlu menunggu approval.",
      "highlights": ["pembayaran"]
    }
  ]
}
```

### `transcription_chunk`

```sql
CREATE TABLE transcription_chunk (
    id                    UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id                UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_job(id),
    chunk_index            INTEGER NOT NULL,

    source_asset_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    start_ms               BIGINT NOT NULL,
    end_ms                 BIGINT NOT NULL,

    provider_job_id        TEXT,
    status                 VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    result_json            JSONB,

    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,

    UNIQUE (job_id, chunk_index)

```

Chunking penting untuk audio panjang, retry partial, dan provider limit.

---

## 21. Chunking Strategy

Untuk audio panjang:

```text
3-hour meeting
  │
  ├── chunk 0: 00:00-15:00
  ├── chunk 1: 15:00-30:00
  ├── chunk 2: 30:00-45:00
  └── ...
```

Rule praktis:

| Concern | Strategy |
|---|---|
| chunk too short | context hilang |
| chunk too long | retry mahal |
| word near boundary | overlap 1-5 seconds |
| speaker diarization | speaker labels bisa reset per chunk |
| final merge | adjust timestamp offset |
| provider billing | per duration tetap, tapi retry lebih hemat |

Merge logic:

```text
chunk result timestamp + chunk.startMs = global timestamp
```

Overlap dedup:

```text
if word/segment appears in overlap window:
    keep higher confidence / earlier stable segment
```

---

## 22. Speaker Diarization

Diarization menjawab:

```text
Siapa bicara kapan?
```

AWS Transcribe supports speaker diarization for batch transcription and real-time streams. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/diarization.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Model output:

```json
{
  "speakerLabel": "SPEAKER_1",
  "startMs": 1200,
  "endMs": 4600,
  "text": "Selamat pagi."
}
```

Caveats:

```text
- speaker label bukan identitas orang
- SPEAKER_1 tidak berarti nama user
- label bisa berubah antar chunk
- overlap speech sulit
- noise/crosstalk menurunkan kualitas
```

Untuk identitas speaker:

```text
Diarization = speaker separation
Speaker identification = mengenali siapa orangnya

Jangan campur dua hal ini.
```

Seri 13 akan membahas diarization lebih detail.

---

## 23. Channel Identification vs Diarization

| Concept | Meaning | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| Channel identification | audio channel kiri/kanan atau channel terpisah | call center stereo: agent/customer |
| Speaker diarization | model membedakan speaker dari mono/mixed audio | meeting recording |
| Speaker identification | mencocokkan suara ke orang tertentu | biometric/high-risk |

Jika call recording punya channel terpisah:

```text
channel 0 = agent
channel 1 = customer
```

Lebih stabil daripada diarization. Untuk meeting audio mono, diarization lebih relevan.

---

## 24. Custom Vocabulary

Custom vocabulary berguna untuk:

```text
- nama produk
- nama perusahaan
- istilah domain
- nama orang
- acronym
- istilah teknis
- istilah regulasi
```

Example:

```text
Camunda
Jersey
MyBatis
PL/pgSQL
Kubernetes
Order Management
Case ID
```

Provider biasanya punya mekanisme vocabulary/phrase hints. Jangan masukkan PII sensitif sembarangan ke vocabulary shared/global.

Policy:

```text
- vocabulary per tenant/domain bila perlu
- version vocabulary
- audit vocabulary used
- test accuracy before rollout
```

---

## 25. Punctuation, Profanity, Redaction

Transcription options umum:

| Option | Purpose |
|---|---|
| automatic punctuation | transcript readable |
| profanity filter | masking kata tertentu |
| PII redaction | privacy/compliance |
| vocabulary | domain accuracy |
| language identification | multi-language input |
| word timestamps | subtitle/search |
| diarization | speaker labels |
| channel identification | call center |

Azure batch transcription docs list punctuation handling options through `punctuationMode`, including none, dictated, automatic, or both. ([Azure Documentation](https://docs.azure.cn/en-us/ai-services/speech-service/batch-transcription-create?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 26. Transcript Normalizer

Provider result berbeda-beda. Normalizer harus membuat canonical model.

```java
public interface TranscriptNormalizer<T> {
    CanonicalTranscript normalize(T providerResult, TranscriptNormalizationContext context);
}
```

Context:

```java
public record TranscriptNormalizationContext(
        UUID jobId,
        UUID sourceAssetId,
        Duration mediaDuration,
        String languageCode,
        Duration chunkOffset,
        String providerName
) {}
```

Canonical model:

```java
public record CanonicalTranscript(
        String fullText,
        List<TranscriptSegment> segments,
        List<TranscriptWord> words,
        double meanConfidence,
        Map<String, Object> providerMetadata
) {}
```

---

## 27. Worker Skeleton

```java
public final class BatchTranscriptionWorker {

    private final MediaJobRepository jobRepository;
    private final MediaAssetRepository assetRepository;
    private final ObjectStorageClient storageClient;
    private final AudioPreparationService audioPreparationService;
    private final SpeechToTextProviderRegistry providerRegistry;
    private final TranscriptResultRepository transcriptRepository;
    private final TranscriptReviewRoutingService reviewRoutingService;

    public void execute(UUID jobId) {
        MediaJob job = jobRepository.get(jobId);

        if (transcriptRepository.existsByJobId(jobId)) {
            jobRepository.markCompleted(jobId);
            return;
        }

        MediaAsset sourceAsset = assetRepository.get(job.sourceAssetId());

        AudioPreparationResult preparedAudio =
                audioPreparationService.prepare(job, sourceAsset);

        SpeechToTextProvider provider =
                providerRegistry.resolve(job, preparedAudio);

        TranscriptionProviderJob providerJob =
                provider.submit(buildProviderRequest(job, preparedAudio));

        jobRepository.markProviderRunning(jobId, provider.name(), providerJob.providerJobId());

        TranscriptionProviderResult providerResult =
                provider.awaitOrFetch(providerJob.providerJobId());

        CanonicalTranscript transcript =
                provider.normalize(providerResult, preparedAudio);

        transcriptRepository.persist(job, preparedAudio, transcript);

        ReviewDecision reviewDecision = reviewRoutingService.decide(transcript);

        if (reviewDecision.requiresReview()) {
            jobRepository.markNeedsReview(jobId, reviewDecision.reason());
        } else {
            jobRepository.markCompleted(jobId);
        }
    }
}
```

For long-running provider jobs, do not block one worker thread for hours. Better pattern:

```text
Service Task 1: submit provider job
Timer / polling worker: check provider status
Service Task 2: fetch result
Service Task 3: normalize/persist result
```

---

## 28. Polling Provider Jobs

Polling pattern:

```text
submit provider job
  │
  ▼
store providerJobId
  │
  ▼
schedule next poll
  │
  ├── provider still running → poll later
  ├── completed → fetch result
  ├── failed → classify failure
  └── not found/expired → fail/recover
```

Poll state table fields:

```sql
ALTER TABLE media_job
ADD COLUMN provider_job_id TEXT,
ADD COLUMN provider_status VARCHAR(100),
ADD COLUMN provider_submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
ADD COLUMN provider_last_polled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
ADD COLUMN provider_next_poll_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
```

Backoff:

```text
first 5 min: poll every 30-60s
long jobs: poll every 2-5 min
after expected duration exceeded: poll less often / timeout
```

---

## 29. Retry and Idempotency

Important failure cases:

| Failure | Retry? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| provider rate limit | yes | backoff |
| provider timeout on submit | maybe | must check if job was created |
| provider job failed due invalid audio | no | permanent |
| network error fetching result | yes | idempotent |
| transcript parse failed | limited | maybe provider format issue |
| normalized audio missing | yes/no | depends storage |
| FFmpeg extraction failed | depends | codec vs infra |
| unsupported language | no | config/user error |

Idempotency:

```text
- internal job has idempotency key
- provider job id stored
- result insert unique by job_id
- normalized audio asset unique by source hash + options
- callback/event idempotent
```

Provider submit risk:

```text
submit request timed out
→ provider may or may not have created job
→ use deterministic provider job name if provider supports it
→ otherwise store request token and query/list if possible
```

---

## 30. Result Persistence Idempotency

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_transcription_result_job
ON transcription_result (job_id);
```

Behavior:

```text
if transcription_result exists:
    treat as already completed
else:
    persist result
```

For segments:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_transcript_segment_index
ON transcript_segment (transcription_result_id, segment_index);
```

For words:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_transcript_word_index
ON transcript_word (transcription_result_id, word_index);
```

---

## 31. Confidence and Review Routing

Confidence can be:

```text
- word confidence
- segment confidence
- provider confidence
- absent/unavailable
```

Decision logic:

```text
if transcript empty:
    review or fail depending silence detection

if mean confidence low:
    review

if critical keywords detected with low confidence:
    review

if language mismatch:
    review

if speaker diarization failed but required:
    review

if PII redaction required but unavailable:
    fail/review
```

Example:

```java
public ReviewDecision decide(CanonicalTranscript transcript, TranscriptionPolicy policy) {
    if (transcript.fullText().isBlank()) {
        return ReviewDecision.required("EMPTY_TRANSCRIPT");
    }

    if (transcript.meanConfidence() >= 0
            && transcript.meanConfidence() < policy.meanConfidenceThreshold()) {
        return ReviewDecision.required("LOW_TRANSCRIPTION_CONFIDENCE");
    }

    long lowConfidenceSegments = transcript.segments().stream()
            .filter(s -> s.confidence() >= 0)
            .filter(s -> s.confidence() < policy.segmentConfidenceThreshold())
            .count();

    if (lowConfidenceSegments > policy.maxLowConfidenceSegments()) {
        return ReviewDecision.required("MANY_LOW_CONFIDENCE_SEGMENTS");
    }

    return ReviewDecision.notRequired();
}
```

---

## 32. Transcript Review

Human review untuk transcript berbeda dari document review.

Reviewer mungkin perlu:

```text
- play audio at segment timestamp
- see transcript segment
- correct text
- correct speaker label
- mark inaudible
- mark crosstalk
- redact sensitive phrase
- split/merge segment
```

Review payload:

```json
{
  "reviewTaskId": "rev_transcript_123",
  "jobId": "job_123",
  "reason": "LOW_TRANSCRIPTION_CONFIDENCE",
  "segments": [
    {
      "segmentId": "seg_12",
      "speakerLabel": "SPEAKER_1",
      "startMs": 84200,
      "endMs": 90100,
      "text": "Untuk pembayaran ...",
      "confidence": 0.61,
      "audioPreviewUrl": "short-lived-url-with-time-range"
    }
  ]
}
```

Correction model:

```sql
CREATE TABLE transcript_correction (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    transcription_result_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES transcription_result(id),
    segment_id               UUID REFERENCES transcript_segment(id),
    word_id                  UUID REFERENCES transcript_word(id),

    correction_type          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    old_text                 TEXT,
    new_text                 TEXT,
    old_speaker_label        VARCHAR(100),
    new_speaker_label        VARCHAR(100),

    start_ms                 BIGINT,
    end_ms                   BIGINT,

    reviewer_id              UUID NOT NULL,
    reason                   TEXT,
    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

## 33. Subtitle Generation

Subtitle output common:

```text
- SRT
- WebVTT
```

AWS Transcribe supports WebVTT and SubRip output for batch video transcription jobs; if both are requested, both subtitle files and regular transcript file are produced. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/subtitles.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

If provider does not produce subtitles, generate from segments:

### SRT example

```text
1
00:00:01,200 --> 00:00:04,600
Selamat pagi, kita mulai meeting.

2
00:00:05,100 --> 00:00:08,300
Agenda hari ini adalah review dokumen.
```

### VTT example

```text
WEBVTT

00:00:01.200 --> 00:00:04.600
Selamat pagi, kita mulai meeting.

00:00:05.100 --> 00:00:08.300
Agenda hari ini adalah review dokumen.
```

Seri 13 akan membahas subtitle lebih detail.
