## 37. Provider Capability Notes

| Provider | Capability Notes |
|---|---|
| AWS Transcribe | Speaker partitioning for batch and streaming; speaker labels available; subtitle SRT/VTT supported for batch video transcription. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/diarization.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Google Speech-to-Text | Word time offsets can return beginning and end time for each spoken word; Google also documents speaker diarization for detecting and labeling different speakers. ([Google Cloud Documentation](https://docs.cloud.google.com/speech-to-text/docs/v1/async-time-offsets?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Azure Speech | Real-time and batch transcription are available; Azure batch transcription can store results in a storage container. Use provider-specific docs for exact diarization/channel capabilities per SDK/API version. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/transcribe/latest/dg/conversation-diarization-streaming-med.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Local/on-prem ASR | Capabilities depend on model/runtime; diarization often requires separate model or pipeline. |

---

## 38. Security and Privacy

Transcript + speaker labels can be sensitive.

Checklist:

```text id="x0v93b"
[ ] transcript access controlled by tenant/case
[ ] speaker labels not treated as legal identity
[ ] speaker identity mapping requires explicit policy
[ ] raw audio and subtitle artifacts private
[ ] short-lived signed download URLs
[ ] transcript and subtitle files follow retention policy
[ ] correction history access controlled
[ ] full transcript not logged
[ ] sensitive terms redacted if policy requires
```

Important:

```text id="s76h2k"
Subtitle files expose the transcript.
Treat .srt and .vtt files as sensitive artifacts, not harmless metadata.
```

---

## 39. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Treat `SPEAKER_1` as real identity | privacy/accuracy risk | label only, map via review |
| Append partial transcript into subtitle | duplicate/bad subtitle | final segments only |
| Generate subtitle from huge segments | unreadable subtitles | cue splitting |
| No timestamp numeric storage | search/playback hard | store `startMs/endMs` |
| Ignore chunk offsets | wrong timing | apply global offset |
| Ignore overlap duplication | repeated words | overlap dedup |
| Assume speaker labels stable across chunks | wrong attribution | full-audio diarization/review |
| No correction model | cannot fix diarization | correction tables |
| Store only SRT/VTT | no structured search/review | segment/word tables |
| Log full subtitle/transcript | PII leak | redacted logs |
| Use diarization for performance analytics blindly | fairness/compliance risk | quality/policy review |

---

## 41. Minimal Implementation untuk Tahap Awal

Implementasi awal yang realistis:

```text id="vvtjr2"
1. Store transcript segments with startMs/endMs
2. Store words if provider returns word timestamps
3. Store speakerLabel per segment/word if available
4. Build SRT generator
5. Build WebVTT generator
6. Add subtitle artifact storage
7. Add search result → playback timestamp
8. Add correction tables for text/speaker/timing
9. Add quality flags
10. Add review payload for low-confidence segments
```

Mulai dari final segments dulu:

```text id="7ftg54"
Provider result
→ canonical segments
→ canonical words if available
→ subtitle cues
→ SRT/VTT artifact
→ review/search/playback
```

Jangan langsung memaksakan perfect diarization. Diarization harus dianggap probabilistik dan reviewable.

# Seri 14/20 - Text-to-Speech  
## SSML, Voice Profile, Language/Locale, Pronunciation, Output Format, Generated Audio Asset, Provider Abstraction, Caching, Consent, dan Audit

## 1. Introduction

**Text-to-Speech / TTS** adalah proses mengubah text menjadi audio. Dalam arsitektur Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, TTS sebaiknya diperlakukan seperti media generation job:

```text id="k2venv"
Input:
- plain text
- SSML
- language / locale
- voice profile
- output format
- speaking style
- pronunciation rules

Process:
- validate text
- normalize / chunk text
- synthesize audio
- store generated audio
- persist metadata
- expose download/stream URL

Output:
- MP3 / WAV / OGG / PCM / Opus
- duration
- provider metadata
- speech marks / viseme optional
- audit trail
```

SSML adalah XML-based markup language untuk mengontrol speech synthesis; W3C SSML 1.1 mendeskripsikannya sebagai markup language untuk membantu generasi synthetic speech, sementara provider seperti Amazon Polly, Azure Speech, dan Google Cloud TTS mendukung plain text maupun SSML dengan variasi fitur per provider. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/speech-synthesis11/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="nyq2ix"
TTS Pipeline
=
Text normalization
+ SSML generation/validation
+ voice/provider selection
+ synthesis execution
+ audio artifact storage
+ cache/idempotency
+ audit/governance
```

Jangan desain TTS hanya sebagai:

```text id="xxtm85"
String text → byte[] audio
```

Untuk production, desainnya harus seperti:

```text id="nfwctr"
TTS request
  │
  ├── validate text / SSML
  ├── resolve policy
  ├── select provider + voice
  ├── generate deterministic synthesis key
  ├── check cache
  ├── synthesize if cache miss
  ├── store generated audio as media_asset
  ├── persist metadata
  └── return assetId / signed URL
```

---

## 3. Diagram

```text id="3emqnr"
Text-to-Speech Pipeline
│
├── 1. API Layer
│   ├── POST /tts/jobs
│   ├── GET /tts/jobs/{jobId}
│   ├── GET /tts/jobs/{jobId}/result
│   └── POST /tts/preview
│
├── 2. Input Validation
│   ├── text length
│   ├── SSML well-formedness
│   ├── allowed tags
│   ├── language/locale
│   ├── voice permission
│   ├── content policy
│   └── consent/license check
│
├── 3. Text/SSML Processing
│   ├── normalize whitespace
│   ├── pronunciation hints
│   ├── lexicon
│   ├── chunk long text
│   ├── paragraph/sentence boundary
│   └── SSML provider adaptation
│
├── 4. Provider Layer
│   ├── AWS Polly
│   ├── Azure Speech
│   ├── Google Cloud TTS
│   ├── local/on-prem TTS
│   └── custom voice provider
│
├── 5. Audio Generation
│   ├── MP3
│   ├── WAV/PCM
│   ├── OGG/Opus
│   ├── sample rate
│   ├── bitrate
│   └── duration metadata
│
├── 6. Persistence
│   ├── tts_job
│   ├── tts_result
│   ├── generated audio asset
│   ├── speech marks / visemes optional
│   └── audit events
│
└── 7. Governance
    ├── cache policy
    ├── retention
    ├── consent
    ├── watermark/disclosure
    ├── cost tracking
    └── access control
```

---

## 4. Practical Skill Map

| Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| Plain text synthesis | Text sederhana jadi audio | Baseline TTS |
| SSML | break, prosody, say-as, voice, pronunciation | Kontrol cara bicara |
| Voice profile | locale, gender/persona/style/provider | Konsistensi suara |
| Language/locale | `id-ID`, `en-US`, multilingual | Salah locale → pronunciation buruk |
| Pronunciation | phoneme, lexicon, acronym, product names | Nama domain sering salah dibaca |
| Output format | MP3/WAV/Opus/PCM, sample rate | Playback, telephony, storage |
| Chunking | long text → chunks | Provider limit dan latency |
| Audio stitching | gabung chunk audio | Output panjang |
| Speech marks | word/sentence timing | highlight text, karaoke, avatar |
| Visemes | mouth movement events | avatar/lip-sync |
| Provider abstraction | AWS/Azure/Google/local | Hindari vendor lock-in |
| Cache/idempotency | reuse audio untuk input sama | Cost control |
| Consent/governance | custom/personal voice risk | Legal/privacy |
| Audit | input hash, voice, provider, result asset | Traceability |

---

## 5. TTS Use Cases di Microservice

| Use Case | Contoh | Design Note |
|---|---|---|
| Notification audio | “Pembayaran berhasil” | Cache tinggi |
| Voice bot response | dynamic prompt | Latency penting |
| Accessibility | bacakan dokumen | Long-form/chunking |
| Training content | generated narration | Quality/style penting |
| IVR/telephony | phone audio prompt | 8kHz/μ-law atau provider telephony format |
| Video narration | voice over | subtitle/timing alignment |
| Multilingual app | Indonesian/English prompt | locale + pronunciation |
| Avatar/lip-sync | speech + viseme | speech marks/viseme events |
| Document readout | OCR result dibacakan | PII/access control |
| Case workflow | generated instruction audio | audit dan retention |

---

## 6. Plain Text vs SSML

| Input Type | Kapan dipakai | Kelebihan | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|
| Plain text | prompt sederhana | simple | kontrol pronunciation kecil |
| SSML | production voice output | pause/prosody/pronunciation/style | provider compatibility harus dijaga |
| Template SSML | reusable notification | konsisten | template injection risk |
| Provider-specific SSML | fitur style/voice khusus | kualitas lebih baik | vendor lock-in |
| Internal canonical SSML | abstraction | bisa adapt ke provider | butuh mapper |

Google Cloud TTS menyatakan SSML dapat memberi customization untuk pauses, audio formatting, acronyms, dates, times, abbreviations, atau text yang perlu dicensor; Azure SSML dapat menentukan voice, language, name, style, role, multiple voices, emphasis, rate, pitch, volume, dan prerecorded audio; Amazon Polly juga memakai SSML untuk memberi kontrol tambahan terhadap generated speech. ([Google Cloud Documentation](https://docs.cloud.google.com/text-to-speech/docs/ssml?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. SSML Basic Shape

Canonical SSML:

```xml id="61y4in"
<speak>
  Halo, selamat datang.
  <break time="500ms"/>
  Silakan pilih menu yang tersedia.
</speak>
```

Dengan voice:

```xml id="7bc6oe"
<speak>
  <voice name="id-ID-Standard-A">
    Halo, pesanan Anda sedang diproses.
  </voice>
</speak>
```

Dengan pronunciation:

```xml id="nxei96"
<speak>
  Nomor tiket Anda adalah
  <say-as interpret-as="characters">ABC123</say-as>.
</speak>
```

Catatan penting: tidak semua provider mendukung tag yang sama. Amazon Polly secara eksplisit menyatakan unsupported SSML tags dapat menghasilkan error; Azure juga mencatat beberapa voice tidak mendukung semua tag SSML, termasuk beberapa voice HD/personal/embedded. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/polly/latest/dg/supportedtags.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. SSML Feature Map

| SSML Feature | Fungsi | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `<break>` | pause | jeda antar kalimat |
| `<prosody>` | rate, pitch, volume | bicara lebih lambat |
| `<say-as>` | baca angka/tanggal/acronym | “ABC123” dieja |
| `<phoneme>` | pronunciation presisi | nama brand/orang |
| `<sub>` | substitution alias | “JAX-RS” dibaca “Jakarta REST” |
| `<emphasis>` | penekanan | kata penting |
| `<p>` / `<s>` | paragraph/sentence | struktur narasi |
| `<voice>` | pilih suara | multi-voice |
| `<audio>` | insert prerecorded sound | sound effect |
| `<mark>` / bookmark | event marker | sync UI/avatar |
| provider style tag | expressive style | cheerful, sad, calm |

Provider-specific style support berbeda. Azure, misalnya, mendukung penggunaan SSML untuk style/role dan multiple voices dalam satu dokumen, sementara Amazon Polly memiliki engine dan supported tags yang berbeda tergantung voice/engine. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/speech-synthesis-markup-voice?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. Voice Profile Model

Voice profile adalah konfigurasi business-level, bukan sekadar provider voice name.

```json id="disw2q"
{
  "voiceProfileId": "voice_customer_service_id_female_v1",
  "displayName": "Customer Service Indonesia Female",
  "provider": "AZURE_SPEECH",
  "locale": "id-ID",
  "providerVoiceName": "id-ID-GadisNeural",
  "style": "friendly",
  "speakingRate": "0%",
  "pitch": "0%",
  "outputFormat": "audio-24khz-48kbitrate-mono-mp3",
  "active": true
}
```

Kenapa perlu voice profile?

```text id="ww7fpr"
- voice konsisten antar service
- provider voice bisa diganti tanpa ubah caller
- governance per use case
- cache key stabil
- audit jelas
- style/rate/format tidak hard-code
```

---

## 10. Database Schema - Voice Profile

```sql id="tpcg2i"
CREATE TABLE tts_voice_profile (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    profile_key              VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    display_name             TEXT NOT NULL,

    provider_name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    provider_voice_name      TEXT NOT NULL,

    locale                   VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    gender_label             VARCHAR(50),
    style                    VARCHAR(100),
    role_name                VARCHAR(100),

    speaking_rate            VARCHAR(50),
    pitch                    VARCHAR(50),
    volume                   VARCHAR(50),

    default_output_format    VARCHAR(100),
    sample_rate_hz           INTEGER,

    allowed_use_cases        TEXT[],
    sensitivity_class        VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INTERNAL',

    active                   BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    version                  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    metadata_json            JSONB,
    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (profile_key, version)
);
```

---

## 11. Provider Decision Matrix

| Requirement | AWS Polly | Azure Speech | Google Cloud TTS | Local/On-Prem TTS |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AWS environment | sangat cocok | bisa | bisa | tergantung |
| Azure environment | bisa | sangat cocok | bisa | tergantung |
| Google Cloud environment | bisa | bisa | sangat cocok | tergantung |
| SSML | supported | supported | supported | engine-dependent |
| Voice styles | provider/voice-dependent | strong style/role support | voice/model-dependent | model-dependent |
| Speech marks / viseme | Polly speech marks incl. word/sentence/viseme/ssml | Azure viseme events via SDK | timepoints/marks depending API | model-dependent |
| On-prem requirement | kurang cocok | container/custom depends offering | kurang cocok | cocok |
| Cost control | cache important | cache important | cache important | infra cost |
| Vendor lock-in | high if raw API leaks | high if raw API leaks | high if raw API leaks | runtime/model lock-in |

Amazon Polly speech marks dapat mencakup sentence, word, viseme, dan ssml; Azure Speech docs menjelaskan viseme events untuk facial animation data; Google Cloud TTS mendukung audio creation dari text atau SSML dan voice/audio configuration dalam request. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/polly/latest/dg/using-speechmarks.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Output Format Strategy

Output format harus mengikuti use case.

| Use Case | Format Umum | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Web playback | MP3 / OGG Opus | ukuran kecil |
| Mobile app | MP3 / AAC / Opus | compatibility |
| Telephony/IVR | PCM 8kHz / μ-law | phone quality |
| High-quality narration | WAV/PCM / high bitrate MP3 | storage lebih besar |
| Real-time voice bot | streaming PCM/Opus | latency |
| Avatar/lip-sync | audio + viseme/speech marks | sync event penting |
| Archival | WAV/FLAC optional | lebih besar |

Azure REST TTS menggunakan header `X-Microsoft-OutputFormat` untuk format audio streaming/non-streaming, dan dokumentasinya menyebut dukungan output 48kHz, 24kHz, 16kHz, dan 8kHz; Google Cloud TTS create-audio docs juga menjelaskan pembuatan file audio dari text atau SSML. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/rest-text-to-speech?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 13. TTS Job State Machine

```text id="o15qme"
SUBMITTED
  │
  ▼
VALIDATING_INPUT
  │
  ├── invalid text/SSML ─────► REJECTED
  │
  ▼
RESOLVING_POLICY
  │
  ▼
CHECKING_CACHE
  │
  ├── cache hit ─────────────► RESULT_READY
  │
  ▼
SYNTHESIZING
  │
  ├── provider transient ────► RETRY_PENDING
  ├── provider permanent ────► FAILED
  │
  ▼
STORING_AUDIO
  │
  ▼
RESULT_READY
  │
  ▼
COMPLETED
```

Optional states:

```text id="a24f8g"
NEEDS_APPROVAL
CONSENT_REQUIRED
CANCELLED
EXPIRED
ARCHIVED
```

---

## 14. API Contract

### Create TTS Job

```http id="gds2iy"
POST /tts/jobs
Idempotency-Key: tenant-case-text-hash-voice-v1
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json id="v72hmy"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "inputType": "SSML",
  "text": "<speak>Halo <break time=\"300ms\"/> pesanan Anda berhasil dibuat.</speak>",
  "voiceProfileKey": "voice_customer_service_id_female",
  "output": {
    "format": "MP3",
    "sampleRateHz": 24000
  },
  "options": {
    "cacheEnabled": true,
    "generateSpeechMarks": true
  }
}
```

Response:

```json id="wwqp2h"
{
  "jobId": "job_tts_123",
  "status": "SUBMITTED"
}
```

### Get Result

```http id="f3msfs"
GET /tts/jobs/{jobId}/result
```

```json id="xjduvm"
{
  "jobId": "job_tts_123",
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "result": {
    "audioAssetId": "ast_audio_456",
    "format": "MP3",
    "durationMs": 3200,
    "downloadUrl": "short-lived-url"
  }
}
```

---

## 15. Preview vs Durable Job

Ada dua mode:

| Mode | Endpoint | Purpose | Persistence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Preview | `POST /tts/preview` | UI testing / short audio | short TTL cache |
| Durable Job | `POST /tts/jobs` | production artifact | media asset + audit |
| Streaming TTS | WebSocket/HTTP stream | voice bot / realtime | optional |
| Batch Long-form | async long text | audiobook/document readout | durable artifact |

Preview jangan otomatis disamakan dengan final production artifact karena retention, audit, dan approval bisa berbeda.

---

## 16. Text Normalization

Sebelum TTS, text perlu dinormalisasi.

```text id="z63p78"
Input:
"Order ID ABC123 berhasil. Total Rp1250000."

Normalized/SSML:
<speak>
  Order ID <say-as interpret-as="characters">ABC123</say-as> berhasil.
  Total <say-as interpret-as="currency">Rp1250000</say-as>.
</speak>
```

Normalization concerns:

| Concern | Example |
|---|---|
| whitespace | remove excessive spaces |
| unsafe control chars | reject |
| acronym | “API”, “KYC”, “JAX-RS” |
| number | 123, 1.5, 01 |
| currency | Rp, USD, decimal |
| date | 13/07/2026 |
| phone/account number | digit-by-digit |
| product name | custom pronunciation |
| language switch | Indonesian + English |
| sensitive content | policy/redaction |

---

## 17. SSML Validation

SSML harus divalidasi sebelum dikirim ke provider.

Validation layers:

```text id="lngkod"
1. XML well-formedness
2. root element allowed
3. allowed tag allowlist
4. max length / max nesting
5. provider compatibility
6. no external entity
7. no untrusted raw injection
```

Example allowlist:

```text id="kiw308"
speak
voice
break
prosody
say-as
sub
phoneme
p
s
emphasis
mark
```

Do not allow arbitrary XML features.

Java XML parser hardening matters:

```java id="ekxlid"
DocumentBuilderFactory factory = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance();
factory.setFeature("http://apache.org/xml/features/disallow-doctype-decl", true);
factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-general-entities", false);
factory.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/external-parameter-entities", false);
factory.setXIncludeAware(false);
factory.setExpandEntityReferences(false);
```

---

## 18. Provider-Specific SSML Adapter

Internal canonical SSML may not map 1:1 to provider.

```text id="fr4i58"
Canonical TTS request
  │
  ├── AWS Polly adapter
  ├── Azure Speech adapter
  ├── Google TTS adapter
  └── Local TTS adapter
```

Example interface:

```java id="yw7hvv"
public interface SsmlAdapter {
    ProviderSynthesisInput adapt(TtsSynthesisRequest request, TtsVoiceProfile voiceProfile);
}
```

Why adapter is needed:

```text id="acuvrp"
- supported tags differ
- voice tag format differs
- style tags differ
- output format names differ
- pronunciation features differ
- speech mark features differ
```

---

```java id="jrh9o1"
public interface TextToSpeechProvider {

    TtsProviderResult synthesize(TtsProviderRequest request);

    List<TtsVoiceInfo> listVoices(TtsVoiceQuery query);

    boolean supports(TtsCapability capability);
}
```

Request:

```java id="6pvfvx"
public record TtsProviderRequest(
        UUID jobId,
        String inputType,
        String textOrSsml,
        String locale,
        String providerVoiceName,
        String outputFormat,
        Integer sampleRateHz,
        boolean generateSpeechMarks,

) {}
```

```java id="wkoal5"
        String providerName,
        String providerRequestId,
        byte[] audioBytes,
        String contentType,
        String outputFormat,
        Long durationMs,
        List<SpeechMark> speechMarks,
        Map<String, Object> rawMetadata
) {}
```

Implementations:

```text id="tbtqdi"
TextToSpeechProvider
├── AwsPollyTtsProvider
├── AzureSpeechTtsProvider
├── GoogleCloudTtsProvider
├── LocalTtsProvider
└── CompositeFallbackTtsProvider
```

## 20. TTS Policy

Jangan hard-code voice, provider, output format.

```sql id="lw9q2l"
CREATE TABLE tts_policy (
    id                          UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    policy_key                   VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,

    use_case                     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    default_voice_profile_id      UUID REFERENCES tts_voice_profile(id),

    allow_plain_text              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    allow_ssml                    BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    allow_custom_voice            BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    max_input_chars               INTEGER NOT NULL,
    max_ssml_chars                INTEGER NOT NULL,

    cache_enabled                 BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    cache_ttl_days                INTEGER,

    require_approval              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    require_disclosure             BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,

    retention_days                INTEGER,

    created_at                    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at                    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (policy_key)
);
```

Policy examples:

| Policy | Use Case | Key Rules |
|---|---|---|
| `NOTIFICATION_TTS` | short notification | cache on, short text |
| `IVR_PROMPT_TTS` | phone prompt | telephony output, strict SSML |
| `DOCUMENT_READOUT_TTS` | long document readout | async, chunking, approval optional |
| `VOICEBOT_REALTIME_TTS` | dynamic response | low latency, cache partial |
| `CUSTOM_VOICE_TTS` | branded/personal voice | consent + approval required |

## 23. Generated Audio Asset

Generated audio adalah `media_asset`.

```text id="z0ol36"
asset_type = GENERATED_TTS_AUDIO
lifecycle_status = ACTIVE
storage_provider = S3/AzureBlob/MinIO
detected_mime_type = audio/mpeg / audio/wav / audio/ogg
checksum_sha256 = ...
retention_until = ...
```

Object key example:

```text id="yr5i7n"
tenant/tnt_001/year/2026/month/07/case/CASE-001/job/job_tts_123/result/tts_audio_v1.mp3
tenant/tnt_001/year/2026/month/07/case/CASE-001/job/job_tts_123/result/speech_marks_v1.json
```

---

## 24. Cache / Idempotency Strategy

TTS is expensive enough to cache, but cache must be correct.

Cache key should include:

```text id="llm1ds"
- normalized text hash
- SSML hash
- voice profile key/version
- provider name
- provider voice name
- output format
- sample rate
- pronunciation lexicon version
- provider options
- policy version
```

Example:

```text id="23h4j0"
sha256(
  normalizedText
  + voiceProfileKey
  + voiceProfileVersion
  + outputFormat
  + sampleRate
  + lexiconVersion
)
```

Do not cache across tenants unless policy explicitly allows it and text is non-sensitive.

---

## 25. TTS Cache Table

```sql id="1jh2av"
CREATE TABLE tts_cache_entry (
    id                          UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id                   UUID,
    cache_scope                 VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    cache_key                   TEXT NOT NULL,
    audio_asset_id               UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    speech_marks_asset_id        UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    voice_profile_key            VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    voice_profile_version        INTEGER NOT NULL,
    output_format                VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    hit_count                    BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    expires_at                   TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at                   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    last_used_at                 TIMESTAMPTZ,

    UNIQUE (cache_scope, tenant_id, cache_key)
);
```

Cache scopes:

```text id="3ekaw2"
GLOBAL_PUBLIC_TEMPLATE
TENANT
CASE
USER
NO_CACHE
```

---

## 26. Chunking Long Text

Provider limits and latency often require chunking.

```text id="dw2n9r"
Long text
  │
  ├── paragraph 1
  ├── paragraph 2
  ├── paragraph 3
  └── ...
```

Chunking rules:

```text id="tsbyq6"
- split at paragraph/sentence boundary
- avoid splitting inside SSML tag
- preserve voice/prosody context
- keep chunk below provider limit
- synthesize chunks independently
- stitch audio if final output needed
```

Chunk table:

```sql id="nh2uzh"
CREATE TABLE tts_chunk (
    id                          UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    job_id                      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES tts_job(id),

    chunk_index                 INTEGER NOT NULL,
    input_text_hash              TEXT NOT NULL,
    input_char_count             INTEGER NOT NULL,

    status                      VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    audio_asset_id               UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    duration_ms                  BIGINT,

    error_class                  VARCHAR(100),
    error_message                TEXT,

    created_at                   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at                 TIMESTAMPTZ,

    UNIQUE (job_id, chunk_index)
);
```

---

## 27. Audio Stitching

Jika long-form TTS menghasilkan banyak chunks, bisa digabung.

Options:

| Approach | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|
| Return playlist/manifest | simple, no re-encode | client must handle |
| Concatenate same codec | faster | format constraints |
| FFmpeg concat | robust | processing cost |
| Generate single provider long-form | quality/context better | provider-specific/async |

Manifest example:

```json id="11u2ur"
{
  "type": "TTS_AUDIO_MANIFEST",
  "chunks": [
    {
      "index": 0,
      "assetId": "ast_chunk_001",
      "durationMs": 4300
    },
    {
      "index": 1,
      "assetId": "ast_chunk_002",
      "durationMs": 5200
    }
  ],
  "totalDurationMs": 9500
}
```

---

## 28. Speech Marks / Timepoints / Visemes

Speech marks help synchronize audio with text/avatar.

Use cases:

```text id="su7a7p"
- highlight spoken word
- karaoke-style captions
- avatar mouth movement
- accessibility playback
- audio-text alignment
```

Amazon Polly can generate speech marks for sentence, word, viseme, and ssml elements; Azure Speech SDK viseme events can generate facial animation data; SSML bookmark/mark-like events vary by provider. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/polly/latest/dg/using-speechmarks.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Speech mark model:

```java id="ee4ys4"
public record SpeechMark(
        String type,
        long timeMs,
        Integer startChar,
        Integer endChar,
        String value
) {}
```

Example:

```json id="eq8v4c"
[
  {
    "type": "word",
    "timeMs": 120,
    "startChar": 0,
    "endChar": 4,
    "value": "Halo"
  },
  {
    "type": "viseme",
    "timeMs": 160,
    "value": "p"
  }
]
```

---

## 29. Pronunciation and Lexicon

Problem umum:

```text id="lcmgrh"
- nama orang salah dibaca
- acronym dibaca sebagai kata
- product name salah pronunciation
- nomor tiket dibaca sebagai angka biasa
- bahasa campuran Indonesia/English
```

Solutions:

| Technique | Example |
|---|---|
| `say-as` | nomor, tanggal, currency |
| `phoneme` | pronunciation presisi |
| `sub` | “JAX-RS” → “Jakarta REST” |
| custom lexicon | domain terms |
| language switch | phrase English dalam text Indonesia |
| provider vocabulary | provider-specific |

Azure pronunciation docs menjelaskan SSML dapat dipakai untuk pronunciation melalui phonemes dan custom lexicon, serta `say-as` untuk mengatur bagaimana kata, angka, atau ekspresi matematika dibaca. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/speech-service/speech-synthesis-markup-pronunciation?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Lexicon table:

```sql id="75s3h2"
CREATE TABLE tts_pronunciation_lexicon (
    id                          UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id                   UUID,
    lexicon_key                  VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    locale                      VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    term                        TEXT NOT NULL,
    pronunciation                TEXT NOT NULL,
    pronunciation_type           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    active                      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    version                     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    created_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (tenant_id, lexicon_key, term, version)
);
```

---

## 30. Worker Skeleton

```java id="p3j6bo"
public final class TtsWorker {

    private final TtsJobRepository jobRepository;
    private final TtsPolicyRepository policyRepository;
    private final TtsVoiceProfileRepository voiceProfileRepository;
    private final TtsCacheRepository cacheRepository;
    private final TextToSpeechProviderRegistry providerRegistry;
    private final ObjectStorageClient storageClient;
    private final TtsResultRepository resultRepository;

    public void execute(UUID jobId) {
        TtsJob job = jobRepository.get(jobId);

        if (resultRepository.existsByJobId(jobId)) {
            jobRepository.markCompleted(jobId);
            return;
        }

        TtsPolicy policy = policyRepository.resolve(job);
        TtsVoiceProfile voiceProfile = voiceProfileRepository.resolve(job.voiceProfileId());

        ValidatedTtsInput input = validateAndNormalize(job, policy, voiceProfile);

        String cacheKey = buildCacheKey(input, voiceProfile, job.outputFormat());

        Optional<TtsCacheEntry> cached = cacheRepository.find(cacheKey, job.tenantId());

        if (cached.isPresent() && policy.cacheEnabled()) {
            resultRepository.persistFromCache(job, cached.get());
            cacheRepository.incrementHit(cached.get().id());
            jobRepository.markCompleted(jobId);
            return;
        }

        TextToSpeechProvider provider = providerRegistry.resolve(voiceProfile.providerName());

        TtsProviderRequest providerRequest = buildProviderRequest(job, input, voiceProfile);

        TtsProviderResult providerResult = provider.synthesize(providerRequest);

        MediaAsset audioAsset = storageClient.storeGeneratedAudio(
                job,
                providerResult.audioBytes(),
                providerResult.contentType(),
                job.outputFormat()
        );

        TtsResult result = resultRepository.persist(job, voiceProfile, providerResult, audioAsset);

        if (policy.cacheEnabled()) {
            cacheRepository.put(cacheKey, audioAsset, result);
        }

        jobRepository.markCompleted(jobId);
    }
}
```

---

## 31. Java Provider Example - Conceptual

```java id="2oxgve"
public final class AwsPollyTtsProvider implements TextToSpeechProvider {

    @Override
    public TtsProviderResult synthesize(TtsProviderRequest request) {
        // Build AWS Polly request:
        // - text or SSML
        // - voiceId
        // - outputFormat
        // - engine if needed
        // - speech mark types if needed
        //
        // Execute provider call.
        // Convert provider response into canonical TtsProviderResult.

        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Conceptual skeleton");
    }

    @Override
    public List<TtsVoiceInfo> listVoices(TtsVoiceQuery query) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Conceptual skeleton");
    }

    @Override
    public boolean supports(TtsCapability capability) {
        return switch (capability) {
            case SSML -> true;
            case SPEECH_MARKS -> true;
            case VISEMES -> true;
            default -> false;
        };
    }
}
```

Implementation detail should stay inside provider adapter, not leak into business service.

---

## 32. Error Classification

| Error Class | Meaning | Retry? |
|---|---|---:|
| `INVALID_TEXT` | empty/too long/unsafe chars | No |
| `INVALID_SSML` | malformed XML/unsupported tags | No |
| `VOICE_NOT_FOUND` | voice profile invalid | No |
| `VOICE_NOT_ALLOWED` | policy disallows voice | No |
| `UNSUPPORTED_LOCALE` | locale not supported | No |
| `UNSUPPORTED_OUTPUT_FORMAT` | provider cannot produce format | No |
| `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT` | quota/rate limit | Yes |
| `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | transient timeout | Yes |
| `PROVIDER_SYNTHESIS_FAILED` | provider error | Depends |
| `AUDIO_STORE_FAILED` | object storage failure | Yes |
| `CACHE_CONFLICT` | same key different metadata | No/manual |
| `CONSENT_REQUIRED` | custom/personal voice needs consent | No until resolved |
| `CONTENT_POLICY_REJECTED` | text not allowed by policy | No |

---

## 33. Consent and Voice Governance

TTS governance menjadi penting jika menggunakan:

```text id="x9idwc"
- custom voice
- personal voice
- cloned voice
- branded persona
- voice resembling real person
- generated speech for external users
```

Governance rules:

```text id="co57wk"
- require explicit consent for personal/custom voice
- track who approved voice usage
- restrict allowed use cases
- disclose synthetic voice where required
- prevent arbitrary user text with sensitive voices
- audit every generation
- support takedown/deactivation
```

Voice consent table:

```sql id="tmeceq"
CREATE TABLE tts_voice_consent (
    id                          UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    voice_profile_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES tts_voice_profile(id),

    subject_type                 VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    subject_reference            TEXT,

    consent_status               VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    allowed_use_cases            TEXT[],
    expires_at                   TIMESTAMPTZ,

    approved_by                  UUID,
    approved_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ,

    evidence_asset_id            UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    created_at                   TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

---

## 34. Disclosure and Watermarking

For user-facing synthetic audio, consider:

```text id="90btgl"
- “This audio is AI-generated”
- metadata tag in asset
- internal watermarking if provider supports it
- audit event
- use-case-specific disclosure policy
```

Metadata example:

```json id="7fan1j"
{
  "syntheticAudio": true,
  "provider": "AZURE_SPEECH",
  "voiceProfile": "customer-service-id-v1",
  "generatedAt": "2026-07-13T20:00:00+07:00",
  "disclosureRequired": true
}
```

## 37. Security and Privacy

Generated audio bisa mengandung PII, instruksi sensitif, atau representasi suara yang perlu governance.

Checklist:

```text id="6ebf84"
[ ] input text/SSML access-controlled
[ ] SSML XML parser hardened
[ ] unsupported tags rejected
[ ] generated audio private
[ ] signed URL short-lived
[ ] cache scope does not leak tenant data
[ ] custom/personal voice requires consent
[ ] full text not logged
[ ] provider region/data residency reviewed
[ ] retention policy applied to audio and speech marks
[ ] disclosure policy defined
```

Important:

```text id="c8xekp"
TTS audio is not harmless because it may reveal the original text.
Protect generated audio at least as strongly as the input text.
```

---

## 38. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Generate audio in Jersey request for long text | timeout/thread exhaustion | async TTS job |
| Hard-code provider voice name | vendor lock-in | voice profile |
| No SSML validation | provider error/security risk | allowlist + XML hardening |
| Cache by text only | wrong voice/format reuse | full synthesis cache key |
| Global cache for sensitive text | data leak | tenant/case-scoped cache |
| Store full input text in logs | PII leak | hash/redacted preview |
| No consent for custom voice | legal/privacy risk | consent table |
| No output format policy | playback incompatibility | use-case-specific format |
| No provider abstraction | migration hard | `TextToSpeechProvider` |
| No speech marks model | hard to sync UI/avatar | store speech marks |
| Treat generated voice as human voice | misleading UX | synthetic disclosure |
| No retention policy | storage/privacy bloat | lifecycle policy |

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:48:09 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 15/20**: FFmpeg media processing. Fokusnya adalah pipeline operasional untuk transcode, thumbnail, audio extraction, trim, watermark, blur/redaction, codec/container strategy, worker isolation, timeout, dan lifecycle artifact.

# Seri 15/20 - FFmpeg Media Processing  
## Transcode, Thumbnail, Audio Extraction, Trim, Watermark, Blur/Redaction, Codec/Container Strategy, Worker Isolation, Timeout, dan Artifact Lifecycle

## 1. Introduction

Dalam pipeline **OCR / STT / TTS / Face / Media Processing**, FFmpeg adalah komponen teknis utama untuk memproses audio/video:

```text id="7eegqc"
- extract audio from video
- transcode video/audio
- generate thumbnail
- trim clip
- resize video
- normalize audio
- burn subtitle
- add watermark
- blur/redact region
- convert container/codec
- inspect media stream with ffprobe
```

FFmpeg sendiri mendeskripsikan proyeknya sebagai solusi cross-platform untuk record, convert, dan stream audio/video; dokumentasi `ffmpeg` juga menegaskan bahwa FFmpeg dapat melakukan transcode stream audio/video/subtitle, dan `-c copy` dipakai jika ingin stream copy tanpa re-encode. ([FFmpeg](https://www.ffmpeg.org/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Di arsitektur Java Jersey/JAX-RS, FFmpeg **jangan dijalankan langsung di request thread** untuk file besar. Treat FFmpeg sebagai **media worker job**:

```text id="qmc6sd"
Jersey API
→ create media processing job
→ return jobId

Media Worker
→ download/stage source asset
→ run ffprobe
→ run ffmpeg with timeout/resource limit
→ upload generated artifact
→ persist metadata/result
→ update job status
```

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="gjdxmq"
FFmpeg Media Processing
=
input media asset
+ metadata inspection
+ processing policy
+ isolated execution
+ output artifact
+ audit trail
```

FFmpeg bukan sekadar command-line utility. Dalam sistem production, FFmpeg adalah **untrusted media processing boundary** karena input berasal dari user/external system.

Prinsip utama:

```text id="vtv4ad"
1. Validate before processing
2. Never trust filename / Content-Type
3. Run FFmpeg in worker, not API thread
4. Use timeout and resource limit
5. Store outputs as media_asset
6. Persist command template and result metadata
7. Classify failure clearly
```

OWASP File Upload Cheat Sheet merekomendasikan layered defense seperti extension allowlist, validasi file type, tidak mempercayai `Content-Type`, generated filename, file size limit, authorization, dan malware scanning. Prinsip itu sangat relevan sebelum media masuk FFmpeg. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

```text id="gyf4mg"
Media Processing Pipeline
│
├── 1. Input Asset
│   ├── uploaded video
│   ├── uploaded audio

│

│
├── 3. Policy Resolution
│   ├── transcode profile

│   └── output retention
│
├── 4. Worker Execution
│   ├── download/stage input

│
├── 5. Output Artifact

│
└── 6. Persistence / Audit
```

## 4. Practical Skill Map

| Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| `ffprobe` metadata | codec, duration, stream, bitrate | Routing dan validation |
| Audio extraction | video → audio | STT pipeline |
| Transcoding | codec/container conversion | Compatibility web/mobile |
| Thumbnail | preview image | UI/review/search |
| Trim/clip | segment extraction | evidence/case workflow |
| Resize/scale | reduce resolution | storage/cost/playback |
| Watermark | visual ownership/disclosure | governance |
| Blur/redaction | hide face/PII/region | privacy/compliance |
| Subtitle processing | burn-in or sidecar | accessibility/video workflow |
| Filter graph | scale, crop, overlay, blur, drawtext | complex media transformation |
| Worker isolation | process timeout, CPU/mem limit | safety/reliability |
| Artifact lifecycle | output asset retention | storage/cost/privacy |
| Idempotency | avoid duplicate expensive output | cost control |
| Observability | latency/failure/output size | operations |

FFmpeg filters are provided by the `libavfilter` library; official filter docs include filters such as `scale`, `crop`, `overlay`, and many others used in real media pipelines. ([FFmpeg](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg-filters.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Use Case | Input | Output | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| Audio extraction | MP4/MOV/MKV | WAV/MP3/FLAC | STT from video |
| Video transcode | MOV/AVI/MKV | MP4 H.264/AAC | web playback |
| Thumbnail | video | JPG/PNG | preview card |
| Trim clip | video/audio | shorter clip | case evidence excerpt |
| Resize | 4K video | 720p/1080p | reduce size |
| Watermark | video | watermarked video | generated/sensitive media |
| Blur face/region | video/image | redacted artifact | privacy |
| Burn subtitle | video + SRT/VTT | video with subtitle | sharing/export |
| Normalize audio | audio/video | mono 16k WAV | STT |
| Extract frame | video timestamp | image | review/OCR/face detection |
| Convert audio | M4A/OGG | MP3/WAV | playback/provider compatibility |

## 6. Codec vs Container

Ini fundamental.

```text id="fj5a06"
Container:
- MP4
- MOV
- MKV
- AVI
- WebM

Codec:
- H.264
- H.265/HEVC
- VP9
- AV1
- AAC
- MP3
- Opus
- PCM
```

Contoh:

```text id="72ikmy"
file.mp4 = container MP4
video stream = H.264
audio stream = AAC
subtitle stream = optional
```

Jangan bilang “convert ke MP4” saja. Harus jelas:

```text id="1y5u86"
container = MP4
video codec = H.264
audio codec = AAC
pixel format = yuv420p
resolution = 1280x720
```

---

## 7. Recommended Output Profiles

| Profile | Container | Video | Audio | Use Case |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WEB_MP4_720P` | MP4 | H.264 | AAC | default web preview |
| `WEB_MP4_1080P` | MP4 | H.264 | AAC | high-quality playback |
| `MOBILE_LOW` | MP4 | H.264 lower bitrate | AAC | mobile bandwidth |
| `AUDIO_STT_WAV` | WAV | none | PCM 16kHz mono | STT normalization |
| `AUDIO_MP3_PREVIEW` | MP3 | none | MP3 | web audio preview |
| `THUMBNAIL_JPG` | JPG | image | none | video preview |
| `REDACTED_MP4` | MP4 | H.264 | AAC/copy | shareable redacted video |
| `ARCHIVE_COPY` | original | copy | copy | preservation |

Important:

```text id="i5kmzk"
Use stream copy only when codec/container are already acceptable.
Use transcode when compatibility, resize, filter, watermark, or blur is needed.
```

FFmpeg docs explicitly distinguish transcoding from stream copy with `-c copy`; by default FFmpeg may transcode streams unless copy is specified. ([FFmpeg](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```sql id="s4goje"
CREATE TABLE media_processing_job (

    job_type              VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    output_asset_id        UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    attempt_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    error_class            VARCHAR(100),
    error_code             VARCHAR(100),
    error_message          TEXT,

    created_by             UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,

    UNIQUE (tenant_id, idempotency_key)
);
```

## 12. FFprobe Metadata First

Sebelum FFmpeg processing, jalankan `ffprobe`.

Command:

```bash id="d89rhu"
ffprobe \
  -v error \
  -print_format json \
  -show_format \
  -show_streams \
  input.mp4
```

Output yang perlu dinormalisasi:

```text id="j8ufey"
format:
- duration
- size
- bit_rate
- format_name

streams:
- codec_type
- codec_name
- width
- height
- avg_frame_rate
- sample_rate
- channels
- duration
```

Gunakan metadata untuk:

```text id="3xee2m"
- reject unsupported input
- choose transcode/copy
- check audio stream exists
- calculate timeout
- estimate output size/cost
- choose thumbnail timestamp
```

---

## 13. Java FFprobe Wrapper

```java id="t07tnm"
public final class FfprobeService {

    public MediaProbeResult probe(Path inputFile, Duration timeout) {
        ProcessBuilder pb = new ProcessBuilder(
                "ffprobe",
                "-v", "error",
                "-print_format", "json",
                "-show_format",
                "-show_streams",
                inputFile.toAbsolutePath().toString()
        );

        pb.redirectErrorStream(false);

        try {
            Process process = pb.start();

            boolean finished = process.waitFor(timeout.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);

            if (!finished) {
                process.destroyForcibly();
                throw new MediaProcessingException("FFPROBE_TIMEOUT");
            }

            String stdout = new String(process.getInputStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            String stderr = new String(process.getErrorStream().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

            if (process.exitValue() != 0) {
                throw new MediaProcessingException("FFPROBE_FAILED: " + sanitize(stderr));
            }

            return parseProbeJson(stdout);

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new MediaProcessingException("FFPROBE_START_FAILED", e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new MediaProcessingException("FFPROBE_INTERRUPTED", e);
        }
    }

    private String sanitize(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return value.length() > 4000 ? value.substring(0, 4000) : value;
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Audio Extraction for STT

Input video:

```bash id="j3dzr7"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -vn \
  -ac 1 \
  -ar 16000 \
  -c:a pcm_s16le \
  output.wav
```

Purpose:

| Option | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `-vn` | disable video |
| `-ac 1` | mono |
| `-ar 16000` | 16kHz |
| `-c:a pcm_s16le` | PCM WAV |
| `output.wav` | normalized STT input |

Use case:

```text id="d51dj8"
Video upload
→ extract normalized audio
→ send to STT provider
```

---

## 15. Generate Thumbnail

Basic thumbnail at specific timestamp:

```bash id="pm4m7f"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -ss 00:00:05 \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -frames:v 1 \
  -q:v 2 \
  thumbnail.jpg
```

Better strategy:

```text id="170q4d"
- avoid first frame because it may be black
- choose 5s or 10% of duration
- fallback if duration short
- verify output image exists and non-empty
```

Thumbnail policy:

| Input Duration | Timestamp |
|---:|---|
| < 3 sec | 0.5 sec |
| 3-30 sec | 2 sec |
| > 30 sec | 10% of duration or 5 sec |
| black frame detected | retry later timestamp |

---

## 16. Video Transcode to Web MP4

```bash id="vpmf1t"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mov \
  -vf "scale='min(1280,iw)':-2" \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -preset medium \
  -crf 23 \
  -pix_fmt yuv420p \
  -c:a aac \
  -b:a 128k \
  output.mp4
```

Explanation:

| Option | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `scale='min(1280,iw)':-2` | max width 1280, preserve aspect ratio |
| `libx264` | H.264 encoder |
| `crf 23` | quality/size tradeoff |
| `pix_fmt yuv420p` | broad playback compatibility |
| `aac` | common MP4 audio |
| `b:a 128k` | audio bitrate |

---

## 17. Stream Copy When Possible

If source is already compatible:

```bash id="c9fk9n"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -c copy \
  output.mp4
```

Use `-c copy` only when:

```text id="kg6f6w"
- container is acceptable
- codecs are acceptable
- no filter needed
- no resize/watermark/blur
- no audio normalization required
```

If filters are used, re-encode is usually required.

---

## 18. Trim Media

Fast trim with stream copy:

```bash id="62vrst"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -ss 00:01:00 \
  -to 00:02:00 \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -c copy \
  clip.mp4
```

More accurate trim with re-encode:

```bash id="2zk9gz"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -ss 00:01:00 \
  -to 00:02:00 \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -c:a aac \
  clip.mp4
```

Trade-off:

| Method | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|
| `-c copy` | fast | keyframe accuracy limitation |
| re-encode | accurate | slower, quality/cost impact |

---

## 19. Resize / Scale

Simple 720p max height:

```bash id="x4p5lz"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -vf "scale=-2:720" \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -c:a aac \
  output-720p.mp4
```

Scale max width without upscaling:

```bash id="m1fka5"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -vf "scale='min(1280,iw)':-2" \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -c:a aac \
  output.mp4
```

FFmpeg filter docs include `scale` and broader filter graph concepts; filters are the right abstraction for resizing, cropping, overlaying, blurring, and similar transformations. ([FFmpeg](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg-filters.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 20. Watermark

Image watermark overlay:

```bash id="9moppu"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -i watermark.png \
  -filter_complex "overlay=W-w-20:H-h-20" \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -c:a copy \
  output-watermarked.mp4
```

Text watermark:

```bash id="vq2bs5"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -vf "drawtext=text='CONFIDENTIAL':x=20:y=20:fontsize=32:fontcolor=white" \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -c:a copy \
  output-watermarked.mp4
```

Security note:

```text id="9ejy1i"
Do not inject user text directly into drawtext expression.
Escape carefully or use controlled templates.
```

---

## 21. Blur / Redaction

Static region blur:

```bash id="no2wo8"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -filter_complex "[0:v]crop=300:200:100:80,boxblur=20[blurred];[0:v][blurred]overlay=100:80" \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -c:a copy \
  output-redacted.mp4
```

Concept:

```text id="4mal38"
1. crop sensitive region
2. apply blur
3. overlay blurred region back
```

Use cases:

```text id="iqvvx2"
- blur face
- blur license plate
- blur ID number on video
- blur screen region
- redact document preview
```

Caveat:

```text id="0q7qbk"
Static blur works only if region does not move.
Moving face/object requires detection + tracking + dynamic filter generation.
```

---

## 22. Dynamic Redaction

For moving object:

```text id="k1qj9y"
Face detector/tracker
  │
  ▼
per-frame bounding boxes
  │
  ▼
generate redaction timeline
  │
  ▼
FFmpeg filter graph / frame processor
  │
  ▼
redacted video
```

Redaction timeline:

```json id="lvj6ka"
[
  {
    "startMs": 1000,
    "endMs": 3000,
    "x": 120,
    "y": 80,
    "width": 300,
    "height": 200
  },
  {
    "startMs": 3000,
    "endMs": 5000,
    "x": 160,
    "y": 90,
    "width": 310,
    "height": 210
  }
]
```

For production, consider frame-level processing with OpenCV/JavaCV when FFmpeg filter expression becomes too complex.

---

## 23. Burn Subtitle into Video

```bash id="q0oryi"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -vf "subtitles=transcript.srt" \
  -c:v libx264 \
  -c:a copy \
  output-subtitled.mp4
```

Sidecar subtitle is usually better when player supports it:

```text id="w432eq"
video.mp4
transcript.vtt
```

Burn-in subtitle:

| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| works everywhere | cannot turn off |
| easy sharing | re-encode required |
| visual consistency | text permanently embedded |

Sidecar subtitle:

| Pros | Cons |
|---|---|
| selectable | player support needed |
| no video re-encode | separate artifact |
| accessible/multilingual | sync management needed |

---

## 24. Extract Frames

Single frame:

```bash id="7j87hb"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -ss 00:00:10 \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -frames:v 1 \
  frame-10s.jpg
```

Frames every second:

```bash id="08tsvb"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.mp4 \
  -vf fps=1 \
  frames/frame-%04d.jpg
```

Use cases:

```text id="u3rffz"
- OCR on video frames
- face detection sampling
- thumbnail selection
- moderation/review
- scene analysis
```

Guardrail:

```text id="bvtfy6"
Frame extraction can explode storage.
Always set fps / max frame count / retention.
```

## 25. Normalize Audio

For speech processing:

```bash id="ygr6ud"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input_audio_or_video \
  -vn \
  -ac 1 \
  -ar 16000 \
  -af loudnorm \
  -c:a pcm_s16le \
  normalized.wav
```

For web preview MP3:

```bash id="q8z4ge"
ffmpeg \
  -y \
  -i input.wav \
  -c:a libmp3lame \
  -b:a 128k \
  preview.mp3
```

Practical rule:

```text id="92jfup"
STT normalization ≠ playback optimization.
Use different profiles.
```

## 26. Java FFmpeg Runner

```java id="65u2sk"
public final class FfmpegRunner {

    public FfmpegRunResult run(List<String> args, Duration timeout) {
        List<String> command = new ArrayList<>();
        command.add("ffmpeg");
        command.addAll(args);

        ProcessBuilder pb = new ProcessBuilder(command);
        pb.redirectErrorStream(false);

        Instant started = Instant.now();

        try {
            Process process = pb.start();

            CompletableFuture<String> stderrFuture = CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() ->
                    readLimited(process.getErrorStream(), 16_000)
            );

            CompletableFuture<String> stdoutFuture = CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() ->
                    readLimited(process.getInputStream(), 16_000)
            );

            boolean finished = process.waitFor(timeout.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);

            if (!finished) {
                process.destroyForcibly();
                return FfmpegRunResult.timeout(
                        Duration.between(started, Instant.now()).toMillis()
                );
            }

            int exitCode = process.exitValue();

            return new FfmpegRunResult(
                    exitCode,
                    stdoutFuture.join(),
                    stderrFuture.join(),
                    Duration.between(started, Instant.now()).toMillis()
            );

        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new MediaProcessingException("FFMPEG_START_FAILED", e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new MediaProcessingException("FFMPEG_INTERRUPTED", e);
        }
    }

    private String readLimited(InputStream input, int limit) {
        try {
            byte[] bytes = input.readNBytes(limit);
            return new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return "";
        }
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="5q6xlh"
Use ProcessBuilder args.
Do not build shell command string from user input.
Always set timeout.
Read stdout/stderr to avoid process blocking.
```

---

## 27. Safe Command Builder

```java id="a4fwj0"
public final class FfmpegCommandBuilder {

    public List<String> buildAudioExtractionCommand(Path input, Path output) {
        return List.of(
                "-y",
                "-i", input.toAbsolutePath().toString(),
                "-vn",
                "-ac", "1",
                "-ar", "16000",
                "-c:a", "pcm_s16le",
                output.toAbsolutePath().toString()
        );
    }

    public List<String> buildThumbnailCommand(Path input, Path output, String timestamp) {
        return List.of(
                "-y",
                "-ss", timestamp,
                "-i", input.toAbsolutePath().toString(),
                "-frames:v", "1",
                "-q:v", "2",
                output.toAbsolutePath().toString()
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="54q0v1"
Only application code builds command args.
User can choose high-level options, not arbitrary FFmpeg flags.
```

---

## 28. Worker Flow

```text id="veagzx"
media_processing_job claimed
  │
  ▼
load source asset
  │
  ▼
download to temp workspace
  │
  ▼
run ffprobe
  │
  ▼
validate metadata against profile
  │
  ▼
build FFmpeg command args
  │
  ▼
execute with timeout
  │
  ├── success
  │      ├── verify output exists
  │      ├── probe output
  │      ├── upload output asset
  │      ├── persist relationship
  │      └── mark completed
  │
  └── failure
         ├── classify stderr/exit/timeout
         ├── retry if transient
         └── fail if permanent
```

---

## 29. Media Worker Skeleton

```java id="ukkz6m"
public final class MediaProcessingWorker {

    private final MediaProcessingJobRepository jobRepository;
    private final MediaAssetRepository assetRepository;
    private final ObjectStorageClient storageClient;
    private final FfprobeService ffprobeService;
    private final FfmpegCommandFactory commandFactory;
    private final FfmpegRunner ffmpegRunner;
    private final MediaProcessingProfileRepository profileRepository;

    public void execute(UUID jobId) {
        MediaProcessingJob job = jobRepository.get(jobId);

        MediaAsset sourceAsset = assetRepository.get(job.sourceAssetId());
        MediaProcessingProfile profile = profileRepository.get(job.processingProfile());

        Path workspace = createWorkspace(job.id());

        try {
            Path input = storageClient.downloadTo(workspace, sourceAsset.objectRef());

            MediaProbeResult inputMetadata = ffprobeService.probe(input, Duration.ofSeconds(30));

            validateInput(job, profile, inputMetadata);

            Path output = workspace.resolve(resolveOutputFileName(job, profile));

            List<String> commandArgs = commandFactory.build(job, profile, input, output, inputMetadata);

            MediaProcessingRun run = jobRepository.startRun(job, inputMetadata, commandArgs);

            FfmpegRunResult result = ffmpegRunner.run(
                    commandArgs,
                    Duration.ofMillis(profile.timeoutMs())
            );

            if (!result.success()) {
                handleFailure(job, run, result);
                return;
            }

            verifyOutput(output);

            MediaProbeResult outputMetadata = ffprobeService.probe(output, Duration.ofSeconds(30));

            MediaAsset outputAsset = storageClient.uploadDerivedMedia(job, sourceAsset, output, outputMetadata);

            assetRepository.createRelationship(sourceAsset.id(), outputAsset.id(), "DERIVED_FROM");

            jobRepository.markCompleted(job.id(), outputAsset.id(), outputMetadata);

        } finally {
            cleanupWorkspace(workspace);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 30. Workspace Isolation

Each job should use its own workspace:

```text id="t3q2hp"
/tmp/media-worker/{jobId}/
  ├── input/
  ├── output/
  ├── scratch/
  └── logs/
```

Rules:

```text id="hmdj4n"
- generated directory name
- no user filename as path
- strict permissions
- cleanup after job
- max disk usage
- avoid shared temp filenames
```

Kubernetes:

```yaml id="eywcq3"
volumeMounts:
  - name: worker-tmp
    mountPath: /tmp/media-worker

volumes:
  - name: worker-tmp
    emptyDir:
      sizeLimit: 10Gi
```

---

## 31. Timeout Strategy

Timeout should depend on input duration and job type.

Example:

```text id="tl1qh1"
thumbnail:
  base 30s

audio extraction:
  max(2 min, inputDuration * 0.5)

transcode:
  max(5 min, inputDuration * 3)

redaction:
  max(10 min, inputDuration * 5)
```

Table:

| Job Type | Timeout Thinking |
|---|---|
| thumbnail | short |
| extract audio | medium |
| stream copy trim | short |
| re-encode trim | proportional |
| transcode | proportional |
| blur/redaction | expensive |
| subtitle burn | re-encode, proportional |
| frame extraction many frames | proportional to output count |

---

## 32. Retry Strategy

| Failure | Retry? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| temp disk full | Yes after infra fix | transient infra |
| object storage read timeout | Yes | transient |
| FFmpeg process timeout | Limited | maybe input too heavy |
| unsupported codec | No | permanent unless fallback |
| corrupt media | No | permanent |
| output upload failed | Yes | transient |
| invalid filter graph | No | bug/config |
| OOM killed | Limited | lower profile or fail |
| worker pod killed | Yes | job can be reclaimed |
| input missing | No/ops | asset lifecycle issue |

---

```java id="1b1jub"
public enum MediaProcessingFailureClass {
    INPUT_ASSET_MISSING,
    UNSUPPORTED_CONTAINER,
    UNSUPPORTED_CODEC,
    AUDIO_STREAM_MISSING,
    VIDEO_STREAM_MISSING,
    MEDIA_CORRUPT,
    FFMPEG_TIMEOUT,
    FFMPEG_EXIT_ERROR,
    FFMPEG_OOM,
    INVALID_FILTER_GRAPH,
    OUTPUT_NOT_CREATED,
    OUTPUT_VALIDATION_FAILED,
    TEMP_STORAGE_FULL,
    STORAGE_READ_FAILED,
    STORAGE_WRITE_FAILED,
    SECURITY_POLICY_REJECTED,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

Persist:

```json id="j2j2oq"
{
  "errorClass": "UNSUPPORTED_CODEC",
  "errorCode": "VIDEO_CODEC_UNSUPPORTED",
  "message": "Input video codec is not allowed by WEB_MP4_720P profile."
}
```

## 34. Hardware Acceleration

FFmpeg can use hardware acceleration depending on build and environment. FFmpeg general docs mention Intel Quick Sync Video support when FFmpeg is linked/configured appropriately, and similar hardware paths exist for other platforms depending on build. ([FFmpeg](https://ffmpeg.org/general.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use cases:

```text id="lzdiyy"
- high-volume transcoding
- large video workloads
- GPU/accelerator nodes
```

Cautions:

```text id="164pc4"
- output quality may differ
- FFmpeg build must support hardware backend
- Kubernetes node scheduling needed
- GPU/accelerator resource isolation needed
- fallback to CPU should exist
```

Start CPU-only unless volume/cost justifies hardware acceleration.

---

## 35. Artifact Lifecycle

FFmpeg outputs should be `media_asset`.

| Artifact | Type | Retention |
|---|---|---|
| extracted audio | `NORMALIZED_AUDIO` | short unless needed |
| thumbnail | `DERIVED_THUMBNAIL` | medium |
| transcoded video | `DERIVED_VIDEO` | case policy |
| redacted video | `REDACTED_MEDIA` | often longer than raw |
| clipped evidence | `EXPORT_FILE` / `CASE_ARTIFACT` | case/legal policy |
| burned subtitle video | `EXPORT_FILE` | output policy |
| frame extraction | `DERIVED_FRAME` | short unless reviewed |

Relationship examples:

```text id="l4eec5"
RAW_VIDEO → NORMALIZED_AUDIO
RAW_VIDEO → THUMBNAIL
RAW_VIDEO → TRANSCODED_VIDEO
RAW_VIDEO → REDACTED_VIDEO
TRANSCRIPT_SRT → SUBTITLE_BURNED_VIDEO
```

---

## 36. Idempotency Key

Media processing can be expensive. Use deterministic key.

```text id="hpkmn7"
tenant:{tenantId}
sourceAsset:{assetId}
sourceChecksum:{sha256}
jobType:{TRANSCODE_VIDEO}
profile:{WEB_MP4_720P}
optionsHash:{hash(options)}
pipelineVersion:{v1}
```

If same request comes again:

```text id="hrk0s8"
- return existing completed output
- or existing in-progress job
- do not spawn duplicate transcode
```

---

## 39. Security Guardrails

FFmpeg processes complex untrusted binary formats. Treat it as risky.

Checklist:

```text id="esal5y"
[ ] input already MIME/file validated
[ ] file size/duration/resolution limited
[ ] malware scan if required
[ ] FFmpeg runs in worker container
[ ] worker has CPU/memory/temp disk limit
[ ] no shell command string
[ ] no user-controlled raw FFmpeg flags
[ ] timeout enforced
[ ] temp workspace private
[ ] output verified before upload
[ ] logs sanitized
[ ] raw media and outputs private
```

OWASP upload guidance around validating file type, not trusting `Content-Type`, generated filenames, file size limits, authorization, and safe storage applies directly before invoking media parsers/transcoders. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 40. Kubernetes Deployment Notes

Separate media worker from API.

```yaml id="rqwvb1"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: media-processing-worker
spec:
  replicas: 3
  template:
    spec:
      containers:
        - name: worker
          image: company/media-processing-worker:1.0.0
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "1"
              memory: "1Gi"
              ephemeral-storage: "5Gi"
            limits:
              cpu: "4"
              memory: "8Gi"
              ephemeral-storage: "20Gi"
          volumeMounts:
            - name: worker-tmp
              mountPath: /tmp/media-worker
      volumes:
        - name: worker-tmp
          emptyDir:
            sizeLimit: 20Gi
```

Ops principles:

```text id="5kcw8a"
- scale worker by queue lag, CPU, and processing duration
- keep Jersey API pods latency-sensitive
- isolate heavy FFmpeg workload
- use node pool/GPU pool if needed
- implement graceful shutdown
```

## 44. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Run FFmpeg in Jersey request | timeout/API collapse | async worker |
| Accept raw FFmpeg args from user | command abuse/security risk | controlled profiles |
| Shell-concat command | injection risk | `ProcessBuilder` args |
| No timeout | stuck worker | timeout + kill |
| No ffprobe before processing | wrong routing/cost | metadata inspection |
| No temp disk limit | node pressure | `emptyDir` size limit |
| Store output outside media_asset | lifecycle/audit gap | output asset |
| No output validation | broken artifact | probe/verify output |
| Always transcode | wasted CPU | stream copy when safe |
| Always `-c copy` | incompatible output | profile-driven decision |
| No idempotency | duplicate expensive jobs | deterministic key |
| Keep all extracted frames | storage explosion | frame cap/retention |
| Logs include signed URLs | secret leak | sanitize logs |

## 45. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="kl4edx"
Architecture
[ ] FFmpeg runs in worker, not Jersey resource
[ ] job state machine exists
[ ] processing profiles exist
[ ] provider/tool version stored
[ ] outputs stored as media_asset

Input
[ ] file validated before FFmpeg
[ ] ffprobe metadata extracted
[ ] duration/size/resolution limits enforced
[ ] audio/video stream presence checked
[ ] unsupported codec/container handled

Execution
[ ] command args built by application
[ ] no raw user FFmpeg flags
[ ] ProcessBuilder used
[ ] timeout enforced
[ ] stdout/stderr consumed
[ ] workspace isolated
[ ] temp files cleaned
[ ] output verified

Artifacts
[ ] output asset created
[ ] source-output relationship stored
[ ] retention policy applied
[ ] checksum calculated
[ ] metadata stored
[ ] signed URL used for download

Operations
[ ] queue lag monitored
[ ] failure by codec tracked
[ ] temp disk monitored
[ ] CPU/memory limits configured
[ ] retries classified
[ ] poison jobs handled

Security
[ ] no shell injection
[ ] no public raw/output files
[ ] logs sanitized
[ ] sensitive media access controlled
[ ] redaction outputs audited
```

```text id="9ieq4f"

   - EXTRACT_AUDIO
   - GENERATE_THUMBNAIL
   - TRANSCODE_VIDEO
   - TRIM_MEDIA
7. output media_asset creation
8. idempotency key
9. audit events
10. metrics
```

Mulai dari 4 job type:

```text id="p16u0x"
EXTRACT_AUDIO
GENERATE_THUMBNAIL
TRANSCODE_VIDEO
TRIM_MEDIA
```

Lalu tambah:


## 47. Roadmap Singkat Setelah Seri 15

Urutan berikutnya:

```text id="uk5fwf"
Seri 16 → Face Detection vs Face Verification vs Face Recognition
Seri 17 → Face ID, Passkey, WebAuthn, dan biometric boundary
Seri 18 → Biometric Privacy, Consent, Audit, Retention, and Compliance Guardrails
Seri 19 → Provider Abstraction: AWS/Azure/Google/on-prem engine switching
Seri 20 → Production Hardening: observability, cost control, queue lag, GPU/Kubernetes, failure handling
```

FFmpeg adalah fondasi media processing. Yang paling penting untuk senior engineer: jangan hanya tahu command, tapi tahu **execution boundary, isolation, lifecycle artifact, retry, audit, security, dan cost control**.
