# Seri 16/20 - Face Detection vs Face Verification vs Face Recognition  
## Konsep, Use Case, Embedding, Matching, Threshold, Liveness, Error Model, API Boundary, Privacy, dan Audit

## 1. Introduction

Dalam domain face AI, tiga istilah ini sering tercampur, padahal konsekuensi teknis, risiko, dan desain sistemnya berbeda:

```text id="ixp9aj"
Face Detection
→ Apakah ada wajah? Di mana lokasinya?

Face Verification
→ Apakah wajah A dan wajah B milik orang yang sama?
→ 1:1 matching

Face Recognition / Identification
→ Siapa orang ini dari database banyak orang?
→ 1:N search
```

Azure Face service menjelaskan face detection sebagai langkah awal untuk skenario lain, dengan output rectangle/koordinat wajah; AWS Rekognition `CompareFaces` membandingkan wajah pada source image dengan wajah pada target image dan mengembalikan similarity score, bounding box, landmarks, pose, quality, dan confidence. Face matching bersifat probabilistik, sehingga AWS juga merekomendasikan human review sebelum hasil dipakai untuk keputusan yang memengaruhi hak, privasi, atau akses seseorang. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/face/overview-identity))

Dalam sistem enterprise, rekomendasi default:

```text id="jsm8fh"
Detection boleh relatif umum.
Verification harus consent-based dan thresholded.
Recognition / Identification harus dianggap high-risk.
```

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="rx97n8"
Face Detection:
image/video frame
→ face boxes

Face Verification:
reference face + probe face
→ similarity score
→ match / non-match / review

Face Recognition:
probe face
→ search against enrolled face database
→ candidate list
→ threshold + review
```

Diagram sederhana:

```text id="5ltxd5"
Input Image / Video Frame
        │
        ▼
Face Detection
        │
        ├── no face
        │     └── reject / ask recapture
        │
        ├── one face
        │     ├── quality check
        │     ├── liveness optional
        │     ├── verification 1:1
        │     └── recognition 1:N if allowed
        │
        └── multiple faces
              ├── choose largest? risky
              ├── require user selection
              ├── reject for verification
              └── group analysis if allowed
```

---

## 3. Core Differences

| Capability | Pertanyaan | Input | Output | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Face Detection | “Ada wajah di gambar?” | image/frame | bounding box, landmarks, quality | rendah-sedang |
| Face Analysis | “Pose/blur/brightness cukup?” | detected face | quality, pose, attributes | sedang; jangan overinterpret |
| Face Verification | “Ini orang yang sama?” | reference + probe | similarity score / match decision | tinggi |
| Face Recognition / Identification | “Orang ini siapa dari database?” | probe + gallery | candidate list | sangat tinggi |
| Liveness Detection | “Ini orang hidup di depan kamera?” | camera/video flow | real/spoof decision | tinggi, anti-spoof |
| Face Search | “Cari wajah mirip di koleksi” | probe + collection | ranked candidates | sangat tinggi |
| Face Clustering | “Kelompokkan wajah mirip” | many faces | clusters | tinggi |
| Face Redaction | “Blur semua wajah” | image/video | redacted media | privacy-positive, tapi tetap sensitive |

---

## 4. Diagram Arsitektur di Java Jersey/JAX-RS

```text id="9oa1os"
Client / Mobile / Web
        │
        ▼
Jersey API
        │
        ├── POST /face/detection-jobs
        ├── POST /face/verification-jobs
        ├── POST /face/liveness-sessions
        ├── GET  /face/jobs/{jobId}
        └── GET  /face/jobs/{jobId}/result
        │
        ▼
Job + Asset Metadata
PostgreSQL / MyBatis
        │
        ▼
Object Storage
raw image / cropped face / result JSON
        │
        ▼
Face Worker
        │
        ├── metadata validation
        ├── image normalization
        ├── face detection
        ├── quality gate
        ├── optional liveness
        ├── embedding extraction
        ├── 1:1 verification / 1:N search
        └── result persistence
        │
        ▼
Audit + Review + Decision
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="a6wrgc"
Jersey API = control plane.
Face worker = execution plane.
Database = state/audit/result.
Object storage = raw image/cropped face/artifacts.
```

---

## 5. Practical Skill Map

| Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| Face detection | bbox, landmarks, confidence | langkah awal semua pipeline |
| Face quality | blur, brightness, pose, occlusion, size | mencegah false reject/false accept |
| Face crop/alignment | normalize face region | input embedding lebih stabil |
| Embedding | vector representation wajah | dasar matching |
| Similarity metric | cosine/euclidean/provider score | menentukan match |
| Threshold | match vs non-match vs review | trade-off security vs usability |
| 1:1 verification | compare reference/probe | identity verification |
| 1:N recognition | search gallery | high-risk identification |
| Liveness | anti-spoof photo/video/mask | remote onboarding/access |
| Enrollment | reference image capture/quality/consent | source of truth identity |
| Error metrics | FMR, FNMR, FAR, FRR | evaluasi sistem biometrik |
| Demographic evaluation | error rate per group | fairness/risk |
| Human review | borderline/high-impact decision | mengurangi keputusan otomatis salah |
| Privacy/retention | consent, deletion, legal basis | biometric governance |
| Audit | who/what/why/threshold/model | defensibility |

ISO/IEC 19795-1 berlaku untuk empirical performance testing biometric systems/algorithms melalui analysis matching scores dan error rates; untuk face recognition, NIST juga mempublikasikan evaluasi performa dan demographic effects seperti variasi false match/false non-match berdasarkan kondisi image, algorithm, dan demographic group. ([ISO](https://www.iso.org/obp/ui/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Face Detection

Face detection menjawab:

```text id="a5cbtc"
Ada wajah di gambar?
Jika ada, di mana?
Berapa banyak?
Seberapa yakin?
```

Output minimal:

```json id="e4qa23"
{
  "faces": [
    {
      "faceIndex": 0,
      "confidence": 0.998,
      "bbox": {
        "x": 120,
        "y": 80,
        "width": 300,
        "height": 360,
        "unit": "PIXEL"
      },
      "landmarks": {
        "leftEye": { "x": 190, "y": 190 },
        "rightEye": { "x": 270, "y": 188 },
        "nose": { "x": 230, "y": 240 },
        "mouth": { "x": 230, "y": 310 }
      },
      "quality": {
        "brightness": 0.82,
        "sharpness": 0.76,
        "poseYaw": 3.2,
        "posePitch": -1.1,
        "poseRoll": 0.5
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

Azure Face detection dapat mengembalikan rectangle coordinates dan face-related attributes seperti head pose, glasses, dan quality-related signals, tetapi attribute seperti age/emotion adalah prediction umum, bukan classification faktual yang aman untuk keputusan sensitif. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/face/overview-identity))

---

## 7. Face Verification 1:1

Face verification menjawab:

```text id="xfl8ed"
Apakah probe face cocok dengan reference face?
```

Contoh use case:

```text id="zjt25y"
- selfie vs government ID photo
- user login step-up
- remote onboarding
- employee access verification
- account recovery with human review
```

Flow:

```text id="2lw3d4"
Reference Image
        │
        ▼
Detect face
        │
        ▼
Quality gate
        │
        ▼
Embedding / Provider reference
        │
        ▼
Probe Image
        │
        ▼
Detect face + quality gate + liveness
        │
        ▼
Compare 1:1
        │
        ├── score >= accept threshold → MATCH
        ├── score <= reject threshold → NON_MATCH
        └── gray zone → HUMAN_REVIEW / RETRY_CAPTURE
```

AWS `CompareFaces` default-nya mengembalikan face matches dengan similarity score ≥ 80%, tetapi threshold bisa diubah dengan `SimilarityThreshold`; AWS juga menekankan operasi ini probabilistik dan high-impact decisions sebaiknya melalui human review. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/rekognition/latest/APIReference/API_CompareFaces.html))

---

## 8. Face Recognition / Identification 1:N

Face recognition atau identification menjawab:

```text id="g4z86o"
Probe face ini paling mirip dengan siapa dari database/gallery?
```

Flow:

```text id="1xq8ot"
Probe face
  │
  ▼
Embedding
  │
  ▼
Search gallery / vector index / provider collection
  │
  ▼
Top-K candidates
  │
  ▼
Threshold + review
  │
  ├── candidate accepted
  ├── no match
  └── human review
```

Output:

```json id="bwnxzo"
{
  "probeFaceId": "face_probe_123",
  "candidates": [
    {
      "subjectId": "person_001",
      "similarity": 0.94,
      "rank": 1
    },
    {
      "subjectId": "person_087",
      "similarity": 0.88,
      "rank": 2
    }
  ],
  "decision": "REVIEW_REQUIRED"
}
```

Recommendation:

```text id="s5hulk"
1:N recognition jangan jadi default.
Gunakan hanya jika ada lawful basis, consent/policy, purpose limitation, audit, retention, dan human review.
```

---

## 9. Detection vs Verification vs Recognition Table

| Dimension | Detection | Verification 1:1 | Recognition 1:N |
|---|---|---|---|
| Output utama | face box | match/non-match score | candidate list |
| Perlu enrollment | tidak | reference image | gallery/database |
| Risiko privacy | sedang | tinggi | sangat tinggi |
| Cocok untuk | crop, blur, quality check | onboarding/login | controlled identification |
| Threshold | detection confidence | similarity threshold | search threshold + top-K |
| Human review | biasanya tidak | untuk gray zone/high impact | hampir wajib untuk high impact |
| Biometric data | cropped face possible | reference/probe biometric | biometric database |
| Abuse risk | lower | medium-high | high |
| Governance | needed | strict | very strict |

---

## 10. Embedding Model

Face embedding adalah vector numerik yang merepresentasikan karakteristik wajah.

```text id="c3765g"
face crop
→ model
→ embedding vector
→ compare/search
```

Example conceptual:

```json id="3y76fc"
{
  "faceEmbedding": [0.012, -0.093, 0.221, "..."],
  "modelName": "face-embedding-model",
  "modelVersion": "v3",
  "dimension": 512
}
```

Important:

```text id="sx3dh5"
Embedding adalah biometric data.
Treat embedding at least as sensitive as face image.
```

Simpan embedding hanya bila benar-benar dibutuhkan. Untuk banyak use case verification, Anda bisa memakai provider stateless compare API atau menyimpan reference image/embedding dengan retention ketat.

---

## 11. Similarity Score dan Threshold

Matching menghasilkan score.

```text id="pnm5sy"
Higher score
→ wajah lebih mirip
→ belum tentu pasti orang sama
```

Decision model yang lebih aman:

```text id="dazs7z"
score >= acceptThreshold
→ match

score <= rejectThreshold
→ non-match

rejectThreshold < score < acceptThreshold
→ gray zone / human review
```

Example:

```json id="t1wgrv"
{
  "similarityScore": 0.87,
  "acceptThreshold": 0.92,
  "rejectThreshold": 0.75,
  "decision": "REVIEW_REQUIRED"
}
```

Threshold tidak boleh “asal 80%”. Threshold harus dikalibrasi terhadap:

```text id="jsgxda"
- use case risk
- dataset internal
- false accept tolerance
- false reject tolerance
- image quality
- provider/model version
- demographic performance
- liveness availability
```

Biometric evaluation sebaiknya melaporkan error rate pada operating point yang relevan, bukan hanya accuracy umum; NIST demographic evaluation juga menunjukkan false positive/false negative behavior dapat bervariasi berdasarkan algorithm, image quality, dan demographic factors. ([ISO](https://www.iso.org/obp/ui/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Error Metrics

| Metric | Meaning | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| FMR / FAR | false match / false accept | orang salah diterima |
| FNMR / FRR | false non-match / false reject | orang benar ditolak |
| FTE | failure to enroll | gagal membuat reference |
| FTA | failure to acquire | gagal capture wajah |
| DET curve | trade-off FMR vs FNMR | pemilihan threshold |
| ROC curve | TPR/FPR trade-off | evaluasi umum |
| EER | equal error rate | ringkasan, bukan operating point |
| TAR at FAR | true accept rate pada false accept tertentu | useful untuk security |
| Top-K accuracy | candidate benar masuk top-K | recognition/search |

Untuk security-sensitive verification, metric yang penting biasanya:

```text id="y47xq4"
TAR @ FAR tertentu
FNMR @ FMR tertentu
```

Bukan sekadar:

```text id="n66kxh"
accuracy = 99%
```

---

## 13. Liveness Detection

Liveness detection menjawab:

```text id="s6dlw3"
Apakah wajah ini berasal dari orang yang benar-benar hadir di depan kamera,
bukan foto, video replay, mask, atau spoof?
```

Azure Face liveness detection menjelaskan tujuannya sebagai memastikan sistem berinteraksi dengan orang hidup yang hadir secara fisik pada saat autentikasi, dan menyebut spoof seperti printed photo, recorded video, mask, serta presentasi di phone/laptop sebagai ancaman. Azure juga memisahkan komponen frontend capture flow dan app server/orchestrator yang membuat session serta mengambil short-lived authorization token. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/face/concept-face-liveness-detection))

Flow:

```text id="v6e4le"
Create liveness session
  │
  ▼
Frontend captures guided camera flow
  │
  ▼
Provider evaluates real/spoof
  │
  ▼
App server fetches result
  │
  ├── real + verification match → continue
  ├── spoof → reject/security
  └── uncertain/fail → retry/review
```

Output conceptual:

```json id="jzttzv"
{
  "liveness": {
    "decision": "REAL",
    "mode": "PASSIVE_ACTIVE",
    "provider": "AZURE_FACE",
    "sessionId": "live_123"
  },
  "verification": {
    "decision": "MATCH",
    "similarity": 0.93
  }
}
```

---

## 14. Quality Gate

Sebelum verification/recognition, lakukan quality gate.

| Quality Signal | Bad Condition | Action |
|---|---|---|
| face count | 0 face | ask recapture |
| face count | >1 face | reject for verification |
| face size | too small | ask closer capture |
| blur/sharpness | blurry | recapture |
| brightness | too dark/bright | recapture |
| pose yaw/pitch/roll | too extreme | recapture |
| occlusion | sunglasses/mask/hand | recapture |
| crop margin | face cut off | recapture |
| resolution | too low | recapture |
| liveness | spoof/uncertain | reject/retry |

AWS `CompareFaces` supports a `QualityFilter` parameter that can filter out faces that do not meet a selected quality bar such as `LOW`, `MEDIUM`, or `HIGH`, and low-quality detections can include blurry faces or extreme pose. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/rekognition/latest/APIReference/API_CompareFaces.html))

---

## 15. Enrollment

Enrollment adalah proses membuat reference identity.

```text id="c97orf"
User submits reference face
  │
  ▼
consent + purpose capture
  │
  ▼
quality check
  │
  ▼
liveness optional / supervised capture
  │
  ▼
create face template / embedding / provider face id
  │
  ▼
store with retention + audit
```

Enrollment harus lebih ketat dari verification biasa karena reference yang buruk akan membuat semua matching berikutnya buruk.

Enrollment rules:

```text id="5c665s"
- explicit consent
- purpose limitation
- high-quality capture
- one face only
- no sunglasses/occlusion
- face size sufficient
- provider/model version stored
- retention policy set
- delete/re-enroll supported
```

---

## 16. API Boundary

### Create face verification job

```http id="vih1u5"
POST /face/verification-jobs
Idempotency-Key: tenant-case-faceverify-v1
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json id="5c0ckf"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "referenceAssetId": "ast_id_photo_123",
  "probeAssetId": "ast_selfie_456",
  "options": {
    "requireLiveness": true,
    "thresholdPolicy": "KYC_VERIFICATION_V1",
    "allowHumanReview": true
  }
}
```

Response:

```json id="iry7xu"
{
  "jobId": "job_face_789",
  "status": "SUBMITTED"
}
```

### Result

```json id="bnxvsx"
{
  "jobId": "job_face_789",
  "status": "COMPLETED",
  "decision": "MATCH",
  "similarity": 0.94,
  "thresholdPolicy": {
    "acceptThreshold": 0.92,
    "rejectThreshold": 0.75
  },
  "quality": {
    "reference": "PASSED",
    "probe": "PASSED"
  },
  "liveness": {
    "required": true,
    "decision": "REAL"
  },
  "requiresReview": false
}
```

## 18. Face Recognition API - Use Carefully

For 1:N identification:

```http id="505b8j"
POST /face/recognition-jobs
```

```json id="d91xxv"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "probeAssetId": "ast_probe_face_123",
  "galleryId": "gallery_employee_optin_v1",
  "options": {
    "topK": 5,
    "thresholdPolicy": "ACCESS_CONTROL_RECOGNITION_V1",
    "requireHumanReview": true
  }
}
```

Result:

```json id="lxf6vc"
{
  "decision": "REVIEW_REQUIRED",
  "candidates": [
    {
      "subjectId": "subj_001",
      "rank": 1,
      "similarity": 0.91
    }
  ]
}
```

Default stance:

```text id="qaxp80"
Do not auto-act on 1:N recognition for high-impact scenarios.
Use human-in-the-loop and strong governance.
```

## 19. Provider Abstraction

```java id="4d8ayt"
public interface FaceAiProvider {

    FaceDetectionResult detectFaces(FaceDetectionRequest request);

    FaceVerificationResult verifyFace(FaceVerificationRequest request);

    FaceRecognitionResult searchFace(FaceRecognitionRequest request);

    LivenessSession createLivenessSession(LivenessSessionRequest request);

    LivenessResult getLivenessResult(String providerSessionId);
}
```

Request model:

```java id="kcxfg4"
public record FaceVerificationRequest(
        UUID jobId,
        UUID referenceAssetId,
        UUID probeAssetId,
        FaceThresholdPolicy thresholdPolicy,
        boolean requireLiveness,
        Map<String, Object> providerOptions
) {}
```

Implementations:

```text id="njdr8w"
FaceAiProvider
├── AwsRekognitionFaceProvider
├── AzureFaceProvider
├── LocalOnnxFaceProvider
├── OpenCvDetectionProvider
└── CompositeFaceProvider
```

## 20. Local / On-Prem Strategy

Local/on-prem face pipeline biasanya terdiri dari:

```text id="4t4yrc"
Detection model
→ landmarks
→ alignment
→ embedding model
→ vector matching
```

Komponen:

| Component | Example Role |
|---|---|
| OpenCV/JavaCV | image/video processing |
| ONNX Runtime Java | run embedding/detection model |
| PostgreSQL pgvector / vector DB | embedding search |
| Object storage | face crops/reference images |
| Custom policy engine | threshold/review |
| Audit DB | decisions and traceability |

Caution:

```text id="60qjrz"
Local model bukan otomatis lebih aman.
Anda tetap perlu evaluate accuracy, bias, spoofing, privacy, retention, dan incident response.
```

---

## 21. Database Schema - Face Job

```sql id="11w2pf"
CREATE TABLE face_job (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id               UUID NOT NULL,
    case_id                 VARCHAR(100),

    job_type                VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    status                  VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    reference_asset_id       UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    probe_asset_id           UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    gallery_id               UUID,

    provider_name            VARCHAR(100),
    provider_job_id          TEXT,
    model_name               TEXT,
    model_version            TEXT,

    threshold_policy_id      UUID,
    decision                 VARCHAR(100),
    similarity_score         NUMERIC(8,5),

    requires_review          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    review_reason            TEXT,

    error_class              VARCHAR(100),
    error_code               VARCHAR(100),
    error_message            TEXT,

    created_by               UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at             TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

## 22. Database Schema - Detected Face

```sql id="87hsus"
CREATE TABLE detected_face (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    face_job_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES face_job(id),
    source_asset_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    cropped_face_asset_id    UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),

    face_index               INTEGER NOT NULL,
    confidence               NUMERIC(8,5),

    bbox_x                   NUMERIC(12,4),
    bbox_y                   NUMERIC(12,4),
    bbox_width               NUMERIC(12,4),
    bbox_height              NUMERIC(12,4),
    coordinate_unit          VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    landmarks_json           JSONB,
    pose_json                JSONB,
    quality_json             JSONB,

    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

## 23. Database Schema - Verification Result

```sql id="e1uj13"
CREATE TABLE face_verification_result (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    face_job_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES face_job(id),

    reference_face_id        UUID REFERENCES detected_face(id),
    probe_face_id            UUID REFERENCES detected_face(id),

    provider_name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    model_name               TEXT,
    model_version            TEXT,

    similarity_score         NUMERIC(8,5),
    accept_threshold         NUMERIC(8,5),
    reject_threshold         NUMERIC(8,5),

    decision                 VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    confidence_band          VARCHAR(50),

    liveness_required        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    liveness_decision        VARCHAR(100),
    liveness_result_id       UUID,

    result_json              JSONB NOT NULL,

    created_at               TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (face_job_id)
);
```

## 24. Database Schema - Gallery / Enrollment

```sql id="tf37aq"
CREATE TABLE face_gallery (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id               UUID NOT NULL,
    gallery_key              VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,

    purpose                 VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    sensitivity_class        VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BIOMETRIC',

    active                  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    retention_days           INTEGER,

    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (tenant_id, gallery_key)
);
```

```sql id="jd9t0s"
CREATE TABLE face_subject (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id               UUID NOT NULL,
    gallery_id               UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES face_gallery(id),

    subject_reference        TEXT NOT NULL,
    consent_status           VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    consent_record_id        UUID,

    active                  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    enrolled_at              TIMESTAMPTZ,
    retention_until          TIMESTAMPTZ,

    metadata_json            JSONB,

    UNIQUE (gallery_id, subject_reference)
);
```

```sql id="io2zax"
CREATE TABLE face_template (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    subject_id               UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES face_subject(id),

    provider_name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    provider_face_id          TEXT,
    model_name               TEXT,
    model_version            TEXT,

    reference_asset_id        UUID REFERENCES media_asset(id),
    embedding_encrypted_ref   UUID,

    quality_json             JSONB,
    active                  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

---

## 25. Threshold Policy

```sql id="b9mk7h"
CREATE TABLE face_threshold_policy (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    policy_key               VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,

    use_case                 VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    provider_name            VARCHAR(100),
    model_name               TEXT,
    model_version            TEXT,

    accept_threshold          NUMERIC(8,5) NOT NULL,
    reject_threshold          NUMERIC(8,5) NOT NULL,

    require_liveness          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    require_human_review_gray_zone BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    max_false_accept_rate_target NUMERIC(12,10),
    max_false_reject_rate_target NUMERIC(12,10),

    active                  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    UNIQUE (policy_key)
);
```

Example policies:

| Policy | Use Case | Accept | Reject | Liveness |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| `LOW_RISK_PHOTO_DEDUP` | duplicate photo | 0.85 | 0.65 | no |
| `KYC_SELFIE_ID_V1` | onboarding | 0.92 | 0.75 | yes |
| `ACCESS_CONTROL_V1` | access control | 0.95 | 0.80 | yes |
| `MANUAL_REVIEW_ONLY` | investigation/review | no auto accept | no auto reject | optional |

---

## 26. Decision Model

```java id="61idv8"
public enum FaceDecision {
    MATCH,
    NON_MATCH,
    REVIEW_REQUIRED,
    RECAPTURE_REQUIRED,
    LIVENESS_FAILED,
    QUALITY_FAILED,
    MULTIPLE_FACES_DETECTED,
    NO_FACE_DETECTED,
    REJECTED,
    ERROR
}
```

Decision logic:

```java id="aj2x6c"
public FaceDecision decide(
        double similarity,
        FaceThresholdPolicy policy,
        FaceQualityResult quality,
        LivenessResult liveness
) {
    if (!quality.passed()) {
        return FaceDecision.RECAPTURE_REQUIRED;
    }

    if (policy.requireLiveness() && !liveness.isReal()) {
        return FaceDecision.LIVENESS_FAILED;
    }

    if (similarity >= policy.acceptThreshold()) {
        return FaceDecision.MATCH;
    }

    if (similarity <= policy.rejectThreshold()) {
        return FaceDecision.NON_MATCH;
    }

    return FaceDecision.REVIEW_REQUIRED;
}
```

---

## 27. Worker Flow - Verification

```text id="qq0pi4"
Claim face verification job
  │
  ▼
Load reference/probe assets
  │
  ▼
Detect faces on both
  │
  ├── no face → recapture/reject
  ├── multiple faces → reject/review
  └── one face each → continue
  │
  ▼
Quality gate
  │
  ├── failed → recapture
  └── passed → continue
  │
  ▼
Liveness check if required
  │
  ├── spoof/failed → reject
  └── real → continue
  │
  ▼
Compare face
  │
  ▼
Apply threshold policy
  │
  ├── match
  ├── non-match
  └── review
  │
  ▼
Persist result + audit
```

---

## 28. Java Worker Skeleton

```java id="nk37tx"
public final class FaceVerificationWorker {

    private final FaceJobRepository jobRepository;
    private final MediaAssetRepository assetRepository;
    private final FaceAiProviderRegistry providerRegistry;
    private final FaceThresholdPolicyRepository thresholdPolicyRepository;
    private final FaceResultRepository resultRepository;

    public void execute(UUID jobId) {
        FaceJob job = jobRepository.get(jobId);

        FaceThresholdPolicy policy = thresholdPolicyRepository.get(job.thresholdPolicyId());

        FaceAiProvider provider = providerRegistry.resolve(job, policy);

        MediaAsset reference = assetRepository.get(job.referenceAssetId());
        MediaAsset probe = assetRepository.get(job.probeAssetId());

        FaceDetectionResult referenceDetection = provider.detectFaces(
                FaceDetectionRequest.forAsset(job.id(), reference.id())
        );

        FaceDetectionResult probeDetection = provider.detectFaces(
                FaceDetectionRequest.forAsset(job.id(), probe.id())
        );

        FaceQualityGateResult quality = validateQuality(referenceDetection, probeDetection);

        if (!quality.passed()) {
            resultRepository.persistQualityFailure(job, quality);
            jobRepository.markCompleted(job.id(), "RECAPTURE_REQUIRED");
            return;
        }

        LivenessResult liveness = policy.requireLiveness()
                ? getOrRunLiveness(job, provider)
                : LivenessResult.notRequired();

        FaceVerificationResult verification = provider.verifyFace(
                new FaceVerificationRequest(
                        job.id(),
                        reference.id(),
                        probe.id(),
                        policy,
                        policy.requireLiveness(),
                        Map.of()
                )
        );

        FaceDecision decision = decide(
                verification.similarityScore(),
                policy,
                quality,
                liveness
        );

        resultRepository.persistVerification(job, verification, quality, liveness, decision);

        if (decision == FaceDecision.REVIEW_REQUIRED) {
            jobRepository.markNeedsReview(job.id(), "FACE_VERIFICATION_GRAY_ZONE");
        } else {
            jobRepository.markCompleted(job.id(), decision.name());
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 30. Failure Classification

| Failure | Meaning | Retry? |
|---|---|---:|
| `NO_FACE_DETECTED` | no face in image | no, recapture |
| `MULTIPLE_FACES_DETECTED` | more than one face | no/review |
| `FACE_TOO_SMALL` | face below min size | no, recapture |
| `FACE_BLURRY` | quality poor | no, recapture |
| `FACE_POSE_EXTREME` | yaw/pitch/roll too high | no, recapture |
| `LIVENESS_SPOOF` | spoof detected | no |
| `LIVENESS_UNCERTAIN` | provider could not decide | retry/review |
| `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | provider timeout | yes |
| `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT` | quota/rate limit | yes |
| `PROVIDER_MODEL_CHANGED` | version mismatch | no/manual |
| `EMBEDDING_STORE_FAILED` | DB/storage error | yes |
| `GALLERY_NOT_FOUND` | config error | no |
| `CONSENT_MISSING` | no consent | no |
| `POLICY_REJECTED` | use case not allowed | no |

## 33. Privacy and Governance

Face data adalah biometric data. Governance minimal:

```text id="orz85w"
- explicit consent
- purpose limitation
- access control
- retention period
- deletion support
- audit trail
- human review for high-impact
- no silent repurposing
- model/provider version tracking
```

Azure Face documentation explicitly states that customers processing biometric data are responsible for notice, retention/destruction notice, consent, and deletion as appropriate under applicable data protection requirements. ([Microsoft Learn](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/face/overview-identity))

---

## 34. Security Checklist

```text id="88t5gd"
[ ] raw face images private
[ ] cropped face assets private
[ ] embeddings encrypted or stored via provider reference only
[ ] signed URLs short-lived
[ ] no face image in logs
[ ] no embedding in logs
[ ] consent checked before enrollment/search
[ ] gallery access scoped by tenant/purpose
[ ] recognition disabled unless policy permits
[ ] review UI access audited
[ ] deletion removes template/provider face ID
[ ] retention worker handles biometric artifacts
```

---

## 35. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Campur detection, verification, recognition | desain dan risiko kacau | pisahkan API/job type |
| Similarity > 80 dianggap pasti match | false accept risk | calibrated threshold |
| Tidak ada gray zone | bad auto decision | review band |
| 1:N recognition default | privacy/high-risk | opt-in restricted use |
| Tidak ada liveness untuk remote verification | spoof risk | liveness session |
| Multiple faces tapi pilih largest otomatis | wrong person | reject/user selection |
| Tidak simpan threshold/model version | audit gap | persist policy/model |
| Embedding disimpan tanpa governance | biometric leak | encrypt/retention/consent |
| Tidak ada delete flow | compliance risk | template deletion |
| Human review tanpa bbox/crop | poor reviewer UX | side-by-side crops |
| Log face URLs/embeddings | sensitive leak | redacted logging |
| Evaluate hanya accuracy | misleading | FMR/FNMR at operating point |

---

## Response:
7/13/2026, 10:53:19 PM

# Seri 17/20 - Face ID, Passkey, WebAuthn, dan Biometric Boundary  
## Device-Local Biometric Unlock vs Server-Side Face Recognition, Public Key Credential, Challenge-Response, Risk Boundary, dan Integrasi Java Backend

## 1. Introduction

Topik ini penting karena banyak engineer mencampuradukkan:

```text id="rtspv8"
Face ID / Touch ID / Windows Hello
Passkey
WebAuthn
Biometric login
Face recognition
Face verification
```

Padahal boundary-nya berbeda jauh.

**Face ID / Touch ID / Windows Hello dalam passkey flow bukan berarti server melakukan face recognition.** Server tidak menerima scan wajah, fingerprint, face embedding, atau biometric template. Yang terjadi adalah device melakukan **local user verification**, lalu authenticator memakai private key untuk menandatangani challenge dari server. WebAuthn didefinisikan W3C sebagai API untuk membuat dan memakai public-key credentials yang scoped ke Relying Party; credential dibuat dan disimpan oleh authenticator, dan hanya bisa diakses origin milik Relying Party tersebut. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/webauthn-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Apple juga menjelaskan bahwa Face ID data, termasuk mathematical representation wajah, dienkripsi dan hanya tersedia untuk Secure Enclave; data ini tidak meninggalkan device, tidak dikirim ke Apple, dan tidak termasuk dalam backup. Untuk passkeys, Apple menjelaskan server menyimpan public key, private key tidak diketahui server, dan Face ID/Touch ID dapat dipakai untuk mengotorisasi penggunaan passkey di device. ([Apple Support](https://support.apple.com/guide/security/facial-matching-security-sece151358d1/web?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="x23acn"
Server-side Face Verification:
server receives image/selfie
→ server/provider compares face
→ biometric processing happens server-side/provider-side

Passkey/WebAuthn:
server sends challenge
→ device verifies user locally with Face ID/Touch ID/PIN
→ authenticator signs challenge with private key
→ server verifies signature with public key
```

Jadi:

```text id="hty70e"
Face ID in passkey flow
≠ server-side face recognition

Face ID in passkey flow
= local unlock / user verification for private key use
```

---

## 3. Big Picture Diagram

```text id="05245o"
Passkey / WebAuthn Login
│
├── 1. User opens login page
│
├── 2. Server creates random challenge
│
├── 3. Browser calls navigator.credentials.get()
│
├── 4. Authenticator asks local user verification
│   ├── Face ID
│   ├── Touch ID
│   ├── Windows Hello
│   ├── Android biometric
│   └── device PIN/password
│
├── 5. Authenticator signs challenge
│   └── private key stays in authenticator/device
│
├── 6. Browser returns assertion to server
│
├── 7. Server verifies:
│   ├── challenge
│   ├── origin
│   ├── RP ID
│   ├── signature
│   ├── user presence
│   ├── user verification flag
│   └── signature counter / backup flags as policy
│
└── 8. Server creates authenticated session
```

---

## 4. Practical Skill Map

| Area | Yang perlu dikuasai | Kenapa penting |
|---|---|---|
| WebAuthn terminology | RP, authenticator, client, credential, challenge | Supaya tidak salah desain |
| Passkey concept | public/private key credential | Passwordless/phishing-resistant login |
| User presence | user interacted with authenticator | Minimal anti-silent auth |
| User verification | biometric/PIN local verification | Step-up/security policy |
| Attestation | optional proof of authenticator type | Enterprise/high assurance |
| Assertion | signed login response | Server-side verification |
| Challenge-response | anti replay | Core security |
| Origin/RP ID binding | anti phishing | Credential scoped ke domain |
| Discoverable credential | username-less/passkey UX | Modern passkey login |
| Device-bound vs synced passkey | assurance trade-off | Enterprise policy |
| Biometric boundary | local biometric ≠ server biometric | Privacy/security separation |
| Java backend verification | library/server-side validation | Implementasi aman |
| Session management | after WebAuthn success | WebAuthn bukan session sendiri |
| Recovery flow | lost device/passkey | Real-world usability |
| Audit/risk event | credential registered/used/removed | Security operations |

---

## 5. Terminology

| Term | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Relying Party / RP** | Aplikasi/server yang melakukan authentication |
| **RP ID** | Domain scope credential, misalnya `example.com` |
| **Client** | Browser/platform layer yang memediasi WebAuthn |
| **Authenticator** | Device/security key/platform authenticator yang menyimpan credential |
| **Credential ID** | Identifier credential publik yang disimpan server |
| **Public key** | Disimpan server, dipakai verify signature |
| **Private key** | Tetap di authenticator/device |
| **Challenge** | Random bytes dari server untuk mencegah replay |
| **Attestation** | Bukti saat registration tentang authenticator/credential |
| **Assertion** | Signed response saat login |
| **User Presence / UP** | User melakukan interaksi fisik/gesture |
| **User Verification / UV** | User diverifikasi lokal: biometric/PIN/password |
| **Resident/discoverable credential** | Credential bisa ditemukan tanpa server memberi credential ID |
| **Passkey** | FIDO credential dengan UX passwordless; bisa synced atau device-bound |

FIDO Alliance mendeskripsikan passkeys sebagai FIDO credentials untuk passwordless authentication yang menggantikan password dengan cryptographic key pairs dan digunakan dari device user seperti komputer, ponsel, atau security key. ([FIDO Alliance](https://fidoalliance.org/passkeys/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. Biometric Boundary

Ini boundary paling penting.

```text id="ldc68p"
Biometric happens here:
User face/fingerprint
→ local device Secure Enclave / TPM / authenticator
→ local match result

Cryptographic authentication happens here:
Authenticator private key
→ signs server challenge
→ server verifies signature
```

Server hanya melihat:

```text id="xgu89g"
- credential ID
- authenticator data
- client data JSON
- signature
- public key during registration
- flags: user present / user verified
- optional attestation data
```

Server tidak melihat:

```text id="u3nkvm"
- face image
- fingerprint image
- Face ID template
- biometric embedding
- raw biometric result
- device passcode/PIN
```

Apple’s biometric security docs menjelaskan Secure Enclave memproses, mengenkripsi, dan menyimpan template data; saat matching, Secure Enclave membandingkan incoming biometric data dengan template lokal untuk menentukan apakah unlock atau valid match. ([Apple Support](https://support.apple.com/en-lk/guide/security/sec067eb0c9e/web?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Face ID vs Server-Side Face Verification

| Dimension | Face ID / Passkey | Server-Side Face Verification |
|---|---|---|
| Biometric processing | device-local | server/provider-side |
| Server receives face image? | no | yes |
| Server stores embedding? | no | maybe |
| Auth primitive | public key signature | similarity score |
| Main standard | WebAuthn/FIDO2 | provider/model specific |
| Output to server | signed assertion | match/non-match score |
| Risk | credential/auth risk | biometric/privacy risk |
| Consent complexity | lower biometric exposure | higher |
| Best use | login/passwordless/MFA | KYC/selfie-ID comparison |
| Failure mode | device auth fail | face matching false accept/reject |
| Audit | credential registration/auth | biometric decision trail |

Rule praktis:

```text id="e5p8hz"
Untuk login aplikasi:
gunakan passkey/WebAuthn.

Untuk KYC selfie vs ID:
gunakan face verification dengan consent dan governance.

Jangan memakai server-side face recognition hanya untuk login biasa jika passkey cukup.
```

---

## 8. Registration Ceremony

Registration adalah saat user membuat credential baru.

```text id="3xotz7"
User wants to add passkey
  │
  ▼
Server creates PublicKeyCredentialCreationOptions
  │
  ├── challenge
  ├── rp name/id
  ├── user id/name/displayName
  ├── pubKeyCredParams
  ├── authenticatorSelection
  ├── attestation preference
  └── timeout
  │
  ▼
Browser navigator.credentials.create()
  │
  ▼
Authenticator creates key pair
  │
  ├── private key stays local
  └── public key returned to server
  │
  ▼
Server verifies attestation/client data
  │
  ▼
Server stores credential ID + public key
```

W3C WebAuthn menjelaskan public key credentials dibuat oleh authenticator atas permintaan RP dengan user consent, dan credential kemudian scoped ke origin/RP tersebut. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/webauthn-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9. Authentication Ceremony

Authentication adalah login.

```text id="lvhy4r"
User wants to login
  │
  ▼
Server creates PublicKeyCredentialRequestOptions
  │
  ├── challenge
  ├── rpId
  ├── allowedCredentials optional
  ├── userVerification policy
  └── timeout
  │
  ▼
Browser navigator.credentials.get()
  │
  ▼
Authenticator verifies local user
  │
  ├── Face ID / Touch ID / PIN
  └── user presence
  │
  ▼
Authenticator signs challenge
  │
  ▼
Server verifies signature
  │
  ▼
Authenticated session created
```

MDN describes WebAuthn as an extension of Credential Management API enabling strong authentication with public key cryptography; on the web, passkeys are implemented using Web Authentication API. ([MDN Web Docs](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Web_Authentication_API?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 10. Challenge-Response

Challenge mencegah replay.

```text id="u2z8is"
Server:
generate random challenge

Client/authenticator:
sign challenge with private key

Server:
verify signature with stored public key
verify challenge was issued and unused
```

Properties:

| Property | Reason |
|---|---|
| random | cannot be guessed |
| short-lived | reduce replay window |
| one-time-use | replay protection |
| bound to session/user intent | prevent cross-flow replay |
| stored server-side | verify response |
| expires | cleanup |

Challenge table:

```sql id="r0f30e"
CREATE TABLE webauthn_challenge (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id           UUID NOT NULL,
    user_id             UUID,

    challenge_hash       TEXT NOT NULL,
    ceremony_type        VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    rp_id               TEXT NOT NULL,
    origin              TEXT NOT NULL,

    expires_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    consumed_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,

    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

---

## 11. Backend Data Model - Credential

```sql id="qj65gf"
CREATE TABLE webauthn_credential (
    id                          UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id                   UUID NOT NULL,
    user_id                     UUID NOT NULL,

    credential_id               BYTEA NOT NULL,
    public_key_cose             BYTEA NOT NULL,

    rp_id                       TEXT NOT NULL,
    origin                      TEXT NOT NULL,

    sign_count                  BIGINT,
    user_verified_required       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    authenticator_attachment     VARCHAR(50),
    resident_key                 BOOLEAN,
    backup_eligible              BOOLEAN,
    backup_state                 BOOLEAN,

    attestation_type             VARCHAR(100),
    aaguid                       UUID,

    credential_nickname          TEXT,
    device_type                  VARCHAR(100),

    status                      VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',

    created_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    last_used_at                 TIMESTAMPTZ,
    revoked_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ,

    UNIQUE (tenant_id, credential_id)
);
```

Notes:

```text id="6ij6fd"
credential_id and public_key are not secrets like passwords,
but still sensitive security material and should be protected from tampering.
```

```sql id="eg0ju5"
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id               UUID NOT NULL,

    event_type              VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    credential_id_ref        UUID REFERENCES webauthn_credential(id),

    rp_id                   TEXT,
    origin                  TEXT,

    ip_address              INET,
    user_agent              TEXT,
    risk_json               JSONB,

    result                  VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    failure_code            VARCHAR(100),
    metadata_json            JSONB,

    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Audit events:

```text id="6z8sss"
PASSKEY_REGISTRATION_STARTED
PASSKEY_REGISTRATION_COMPLETED
PASSKEY_REGISTRATION_FAILED

PASSKEY_AUTHENTICATION_SUCCEEDED
PASSKEY_AUTHENTICATION_FAILED
PASSKEY_CREDENTIAL_RENAMED
PASSKEY_CREDENTIAL_REVOKED
PASSKEY_RECOVERY_STARTED
PASSKEY_RECOVERY_COMPLETED
PASSKEY_STEP_UP_REQUIRED
PASSKEY_STEP_UP_COMPLETED
```

## 13. API Shape - Registration

### Start registration

```http id="a5rfqy"
POST /auth/passkeys/registration/options
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json id="okg89s"
{
  "userId": "user_123",
  "displayName": "Budi Santoso",
  "credentialNickname": "MacBook Pro"
}
```

Response returns WebAuthn creation options:

```json id="iipnlp"
{
  "challengeId": "chal_123",
  "publicKey": {
    "challenge": "base64url...",
    "rp": {
      "name": "Company App",
      "id": "app.company.com"
    },
    "user": {
      "id": "base64url-user-handle",
      "name": "budi@example.com",
      "displayName": "Budi Santoso"
    },
    "pubKeyCredParams": [
      {
        "type": "public-key",
        "alg": -7
      }
    ],
    "authenticatorSelection": {
      "residentKey": "preferred",
      "userVerification": "required"
    },
    "attestation": "none",
    "timeout": 60000
  }
}
```

### Finish registration

```http id="tnxm6y"
POST /auth/passkeys/registration/finish
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json id="8wkwpm"
{
  "challengeId": "chal_123",
  "credential": {
    "id": "base64url...",
    "rawId": "base64url...",
    "type": "public-key",
    "response": {
      "clientDataJSON": "base64url...",
      "attestationObject": "base64url..."
    }
  }
}
```

## 14. API Shape - Authentication

### Start login

```http id="j0oroz"
POST /auth/passkeys/authentication/options
Content-Type: application/json
```

Username-based:

```json id="gzdplf"
{
  "username": "budi@example.com"
}
```

Username-less / discoverable:

```json id="8f9z74"
{
  "mediation": "conditional"
}
```

Response:

```json id="37xms4"
{
  "challengeId": "chal_login_123",
  "publicKey": {
    "challenge": "base64url...",
    "rpId": "app.company.com",
    "allowCredentials": [],
    "userVerification": "required",
    "timeout": 60000
  }
}
```

### Finish login

```http id="euaphp"
POST /auth/passkeys/authentication/finish
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json id="w71rmo"
{
  "challengeId": "chal_login_123",
  "credential": {
    "id": "base64url...",
    "rawId": "base64url...",
    "type": "public-key",
    "response": {
      "clientDataJSON": "base64url...",
      "authenticatorData": "base64url...",
      "signature": "base64url...",
      "userHandle": "base64url..."
    }
  }
}
```

Server response:

```json id="maii0e"
{
  "authenticated": true,
  "sessionId": "sess_123",
  "userId": "user_123",
  "amr": ["passkey"],
  "aal": "AAL2"
}
```

## 15. Frontend Registration Sketch

```javascript id="14nx8k"
async function registerPasskey() {
  const optionsResponse = await fetch("/auth/passkeys/registration/options", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      credentialNickname: "My device"
    })
  });

  const options = await optionsResponse.json();

  const publicKey = decodePublicKeyCredentialCreationOptions(options.publicKey);

  const credential = await navigator.credentials.create({ publicKey });

  const payload = encodeCredentialForServer(credential);

  await fetch("/auth/passkeys/registration/finish", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      challengeId: options.challengeId,
      credential: payload
    })
  });
}
```

Implementation detail:

```text id="6ia2pc"
WebAuthn uses ArrayBuffer values.
Backend JSON usually uses base64url encoding.
```

---

## 16. Frontend Authentication Sketch

```javascript id="ylqu4x"
async function loginWithPasskey() {
  const optionsResponse = await fetch("/auth/passkeys/authentication/options", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({})
  });

  const options = await optionsResponse.json();

  const publicKey = decodePublicKeyCredentialRequestOptions(options.publicKey);

  const assertion = await navigator.credentials.get({ publicKey });

  const payload = encodeAssertionForServer(assertion);

  const finishResponse = await fetch("/auth/passkeys/authentication/finish", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      challengeId: options.challengeId,
      credential: payload
    })
  });

  return finishResponse.json();
}
```

---

## 17. Java Backend Library Strategy

Untuk Java backend, jangan parse CBOR/COSE/WebAuthn sendiri kecuali benar-benar ahli. Gunakan library WebAuthn server yang mature, lalu bungkus dengan service internal.

Common architecture:

```text id="s0tzei"
Jersey Resource
  │
  ▼
PasskeyApplicationService
  │
  ├── createRegistrationOptions()
  ├── finishRegistration()
  ├── createAuthenticationOptions()
  ├── finishAuthentication()
  └── revokeCredential()
  │
  ▼
WebAuthn Server Library
  │
  ├── verify challenge
  ├── verify origin/RP ID
  ├── parse attestation/authenticator data
  ├── verify signature
  ├── validate flags
  └── return verified result
  │
  ▼
Credential Repository / Audit / Session
```

What backend must verify:

```text id="t9sw5m"
- challenge matches and not reused
- origin is allowed
- RP ID hash matches
- credential ID exists or is newly created
- signature validates with stored public key
- user presence flag
- user verification flag if required
- sign count / clone signal policy
- attestation policy if required
```

---

## 18. Relying Party ID and Origin

This is anti-phishing core.

Example:

```text id="ka0udc"
RP ID: app.company.com
Allowed origins:
- https://app.company.com
- https://staging-app.company.com if separate RP/policy
```

Do not allow arbitrary origins.

Bad:

```text id="j4bdck"
origin = request Origin header without allowlist
```

Good:

```text id="ungvox"
origin must be in configured allowed origins for tenant/environment
rpId must match configured RP ID
```

WebAuthn credentials are scoped to the RP; W3C explains credentials can only be accessed by origins belonging to that Relying Party. ([W3C](https://www.w3.org/TR/webauthn-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 19. User Verification Policy

WebAuthn options can request user verification:

```text id="123azf"
required
preferred
discouraged
```

Use:

| Policy | Meaning | Use Case |
|---|---|---|
| `required` | require local user verification | login, step-up, sensitive action |
| `preferred` | use if available | lower-risk login |
| `discouraged` | avoid UV | rare; maybe physical key tap only |

For enterprise app:

```text id="b4ou91"
default: userVerification = required
```

Reason:

```text id="3adnm1"
You want cryptographic proof that authenticator was used
and device locally verified the user with biometric/PIN/password.
```

---

## 20. User Presence vs User Verification

| Concept | Meaning | Example |
|---|---|---|
| User Presence | user interacted with authenticator | tap security key |
| User Verification | authenticator verified local user | Face ID, fingerprint, PIN |
| Biometric | one form of user verification | Face ID/Touch ID |
| Device PIN/password | also user verification | Windows Hello PIN |

Important:

```text id="m7yk91"
UV does not tell server whether Face ID, fingerprint, or PIN was used.
It tells server that authenticator says user verification succeeded.
```

Do not design server logic like:

```text id="49dgmv"
if Face ID used then allow X
```

Server usually sees:

```text id="ul8vni"
UV flag true
```

not the exact biometric method.

---

## 21. Attestation

Attestation can prove something about authenticator at registration.

Modes:

| Attestation | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `none` | privacy-preserving default |
| `indirect` | client may anonymize |
| `direct` | RP wants attestation statement |
| `enterprise` | enterprise-managed use cases |

Default for consumer apps:

```text id="lvuy8q"
attestation = none
```

Enterprise high-assurance:

```text id="1ziil2"
may require specific authenticator models / platform authenticators / managed devices
```

Trade-off:

```text id="vu3y5u"
More attestation
→ more assurance
→ more privacy complexity
→ more compatibility issues
```

---

## 22. Synced Passkeys vs Device-Bound Credentials

| Type | Meaning | Pros | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|---|
| Synced passkey | credential syncs across user devices/password manager | great recovery/usability | trust passkey provider ecosystem |
| Device-bound passkey | credential stays on specific authenticator/device | stronger device binding | recovery/device loss harder |
| Security key | external authenticator | high assurance | UX/support burden |
| Platform authenticator | built into device | good UX | device/platform dependency |

FIDO notes passkeys can be securely synced across a user’s devices or bound to a specific device. ([FIDO Alliance](https://fidoalliance.org/passkeys/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Enterprise decision:

```text id="i2wnur"
Consumer-facing app:
- synced passkeys are usually acceptable and improve adoption

High-assurance internal/admin:
- consider device-bound credentials/security keys
- enforce managed device posture
- require step-up for sensitive actions
```

---

## 23. Passkey vs MFA

Passkey can be:

```text id="sbuslb"
- passwordless primary factor
- MFA replacement for phishing-resistant login
- step-up authentication
```

It combines:

```text id="u72qxp"
something you have:
- authenticator/device/private key

something you are/know locally:
- biometric or device PIN/password
```

But application still needs:

```text id="m1smdh"
- session management
- authorization
- risk engine
- recovery
- account lifecycle
- device/credential management
```

Passkey is authentication, not authorization.

---

## 24. Integration with Existing Auth System

Typical stack:

```text id="rdn4t4"
Jersey API
  │
  ▼
Auth Service
  │
  ├── password login legacy
  ├── passkey login
  ├── OAuth/OIDC federation
  ├── session/JWT issuance
  ├── risk/step-up policy
  └── audit
```

After WebAuthn success:

```text id="43xec3"
create server session
or issue token
or complete OIDC auth flow
```

Do not put user roles/permissions inside WebAuthn credential itself.

---

## 25. Step-Up Authentication

Sensitive actions should require fresh authentication.

Examples:

```text id="ujd4a1"
- change bank account
- approve payout
- view highly sensitive document
- enroll new face template
- revoke other credentials
- change password/recovery method
- admin destructive operation
```

Flow:

```text id="4pl0m7"
User already logged in
  │
  ▼
Sensitive action requested
  │
  ▼
Server creates WebAuthn challenge with userVerification=required
  │
  ▼
User verifies locally
  │
  ▼
Server verifies assertion
  │
  ▼
Allow action for short window
```

Store:

```text id="pxwzk3"
lastStrongAuthAt
amr = passkey
stepUpExpiresAt
```

---

## 26. Recovery Flow

Passkey deployments fail if recovery is ignored.

Recovery options:

```text id="7yqdxo"
- multiple passkeys per account
- backup passkey
- recovery codes
- verified email + risk-based checks
- admin reset for enterprise
- identity verification workflow
- support process with audit
```

Recommended baseline:

```text id="yukj36"
Require at least two recovery options for high-value accounts:
1. multiple passkeys/devices
2. recovery code or enterprise admin recovery
```

Avoid:

```text id="82jqb8"
“Lost passkey? Just email link and bypass everything.”
```

That weakens the whole auth posture.

---

## 27. Credential Management API

Endpoints:

```http id="kefyiy"
GET    /auth/passkeys
POST   /auth/passkeys/registration/options
POST   /auth/passkeys/registration/finish
PATCH  /auth/passkeys/{credentialId}
DELETE /auth/passkeys/{credentialId}
```

List response:

```json id="4qu9va"
{
  "credentials": [
    {
      "credentialId": "cred_123",
      "nickname": "MacBook Pro",
      "createdAt": "2026-07-13T20:00:00+07:00",
      "lastUsedAt": "2026-07-13T21:00:00+07:00",
      "authenticatorAttachment": "platform",
      "backupEligible": true,
      "backupState": true,
      "status": "ACTIVE"
    }
  ]
}
```

Revoke:

```http id="qt5q79"
DELETE /auth/passkeys/cred_123
```

Security:

```text id="dfslrk"
Revoking credential should require current session + step-up authentication.
```

## 28. MyBatis Mapper

```java id="3az4lb"
public interface WebAuthnCredentialMapper {

    void insertCredential(WebAuthnCredentialRecord record);

            @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,

    );

    List<WebAuthnCredentialRecord> findActiveByUserId(
            @Param("tenantId") UUID tenantId,

    );

    int updateSignCountAndLastUsed(
            @Param("id") UUID id,
            @Param("newSignCount") Long newSignCount,
            @Param("lastUsedAt") OffsetDateTime lastUsedAt
    );

            @Param("id") UUID id,
            @Param("revokedAt") OffsetDateTime revokedAt
    );
}
```

## 29. Java Service Skeleton

```java id="bpl9f9"
public final class PasskeyAuthenticationService {

    private final WebAuthnCredentialRepository credentialRepository;
    private final WebAuthnChallengeRepository challengeRepository;
    private final WebAuthnServer webAuthnServer;
    private final AuditService auditService;
    private final SessionService sessionService;

    public RegistrationOptions startRegistration(User user, RegistrationRequest request) {
        byte[] challenge = SecureRandomBytes.generate(32);

        challengeRepository.create(
                user.tenantId(),
                user.id(),
                hash(challenge),
                "REGISTRATION",
                expiresInMinutes(5)
        );

        return webAuthnServer.createRegistrationOptions(user, challenge, request);
    }

    public void finishRegistration(FinishRegistrationRequest request) {
        WebAuthnChallenge challenge = challengeRepository.getUnconsumed(request.challengeId());

        VerifiedRegistration verified = webAuthnServer.verifyRegistration(
                challenge,
                request.credential()
        );

        credentialRepository.insert(verified.toCredentialRecord());

        challengeRepository.markConsumed(challenge.id());

        auditService.record("PASSKEY_REGISTRATION_COMPLETED", verified.userId());
    }

    public AuthenticationOptions startAuthentication(AuthenticationStartRequest request) {
        byte[] challenge = SecureRandomBytes.generate(32);

        challengeRepository.create(
                request.tenantId(),
                request.userIdOrNull(),
                hash(challenge),
                "AUTHENTICATION",
                expiresInMinutes(5)
        );

        return webAuthnServer.createAuthenticationOptions(request, challenge);
    }

    public AuthSession finishAuthentication(FinishAuthenticationRequest request) {
        WebAuthnChallenge challenge = challengeRepository.getUnconsumed(request.challengeId());

        VerifiedAuthentication verified = webAuthnServer.verifyAuthentication(
                challenge,
                request.credential()
        );

        credentialRepository.updateSignCountAndLastUsed(
                verified.credentialId(),
                verified.signCount(),
                OffsetDateTime.now()
        );

        challengeRepository.markConsumed(challenge.id());

        auditService.record("PASSKEY_AUTHENTICATION_SUCCEEDED", verified.userId());

        return sessionService.createSession(verified.userId(), List.of("passkey"));
    }
}
```

`WebAuthnServer` above is a wrapper around a real WebAuthn library, not hand-rolled crypto.

---

## 30. Failure Classification

| Failure | Meaning | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `CHALLENGE_EXPIRED` | challenge too old | restart ceremony |
| `CHALLENGE_ALREADY_USED` | replay attempt | reject + audit |
| `ORIGIN_MISMATCH` | origin not allowed | reject + security event |
| `RP_ID_MISMATCH` | wrong RP/domain | reject |
| `CREDENTIAL_NOT_FOUND` | unknown credential | reject |
| `SIGNATURE_INVALID` | assertion invalid | reject |
| `USER_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED` | UV flag missing | reject or step-up |
| `USER_PRESENCE_REQUIRED` | UP flag missing | reject |
| `ATTESTATION_REJECTED` | authenticator not allowed | reject |
| `SIGN_COUNT_REGRESSION` | possible cloned authenticator | risk review |
| `CREDENTIAL_REVOKED` | credential disabled | reject |
| `TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS` | abuse/rate limit | throttle |
| `REGISTRATION_DUPLICATE` | credential already exists | idempotent/reject |
| `SESSION_BINDING_FAILED` | challenge not tied to session | reject |

---

## 31. Rate Limit and Abuse Controls

Protect endpoints:

```text id="8550zl"
POST /registration/options
POST /registration/finish
POST /authentication/options
POST /authentication/finish
```

Controls:

```text id="07rgh9"
- per IP rate limit
- per user rate limit
- per tenant rate limit
- challenge count limit
- failed assertion limit
- device enrollment limit
- audit suspicious origin mismatch
```

Authentication start can leak whether user exists if not designed carefully. For username-based login, normalize responses to reduce enumeration.

---

## 32. Session and Token Issuance

WebAuthn success should create application session.

Session record:

```sql id="mo0h7t"
CREATE TABLE auth_session (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id           UUID NOT NULL,
    user_id             UUID NOT NULL,

    auth_method          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    amr                 TEXT[],
    aal                 VARCHAR(50),

    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    last_seen_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,

    revoked_at          TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

Example:

```text id="iww0pu"
amr = ["passkey"]
aal = "AAL2"
```

For very sensitive systems, define your own assurance model mapped to policy rather than blindly assigning generic AAL.

---

## 33. Risk Engine Integration

Passkey authentication is strong, but risk still matters.

Signals:

```text id="05yr94"
- new device/browser
- unusual IP/country
- impossible travel
- credential first use
- credential backup state changed
- sign count anomaly
- admin action requested
- high-risk tenant/case
```

Actions:

```text id="5d4ecz"
- allow
- require step-up
- require second credential/security key
- notify user
- temporary hold
- admin review
```

Passkey reduces phishing risk, but it does not eliminate malware, session theft, compromised endpoint, or bad recovery flows.

---

## 34. Passkey and Face Verification Together

Sometimes both are needed, but for different purposes.

Example onboarding:

```text id="cy4dgk"
1. User creates account
2. User verifies identity with document + selfie
3. System stores KYC verification result
4. User registers passkey
5. Future logins use passkey, not selfie verification
```

Do not do this every login unless needed:

```text id="fn59q9"
Every login:
- ask selfie
- run server-side face verification
```

Better:

```text id="p716ai"
Every login:
- passkey/WebAuthn

High-risk recovery:
- passkey step-up if available
- otherwise identity verification workflow
```

---

## 35. Deployment Behind Reverse Proxy

WebAuthn depends on origin/RP ID. Reverse proxy mistakes break or weaken it.

Checklist:

```text id="j1427n"
[ ] external HTTPS origin configured exactly
[ ] RP ID configured per environment
[ ] staging and production separated
[ ] no wildcard origin acceptance
[ ] forwarded headers trusted only from proxy
[ ] HSTS enabled for production
[ ] cookies Secure/HttpOnly/SameSite
[ ] CSRF protection for session endpoints
```

Important:

```text id="5cjg7h"
WebAuthn requires secure context in browsers.
Use HTTPS in production.
```

MDN documents WebAuthn as a secure-context web API; modern browser APIs generally require HTTPS except local development exceptions. ([MDN Web Docs](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Web_Authentication_API?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 36. Multi-Tenant Considerations

If app is multi-tenant:

| Model | RP ID | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| shared domain | `app.company.com` | simplest |
| tenant subdomain | `{tenant}.company.com` | RP ID strategy careful |
| custom domain | `login.customer.com` | origin/RP config per tenant |
| admin domain separate | `admin.company.com` | separate credential scope |

Recommendation:

```text id="19t3t0"
Start with one RP ID per application domain.
Do not overcomplicate tenant-specific RP ID unless needed.
```

---

## 37. Native Mobile App Considerations

Mobile app passkeys usually integrate with platform credential APIs.

Backend remains similar:

```text id="6zj7hh"
create options
→ mobile platform credential API
→ finish registration/authentication
→ server verifies WebAuthn/FIDO assertion
```

Need to validate:

```text id="wk1rgh"
- app-associated domain / asset links where platform requires
- origin / RP ID mapping
- package/app identity depending platform
- session token issuance
```

Do not implement custom “face unlock” in app and send “faceOK=true” to server. That is not WebAuthn security.

---

## 38. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Server asks “was Face ID used?” | server cannot reliably know exact biometric | require UV flag |
| Treat passkey as face recognition | privacy/design confusion | passkey = public key auth |
| Store biometric image for login | unnecessary biometric risk | WebAuthn |
| Implement WebAuthn crypto manually | subtle vulnerabilities | mature library |
| Accept any origin | phishing/security break | strict origin allowlist |
| Reuse challenge | replay risk | one-time challenge |
| Long-lived challenge | replay window | short expiry |
| No recovery design | account lockout | multiple passkeys/recovery |
| One passkey only | device loss risk | encourage multiple credentials |
| No credential revoke UI | poor account security | credential management |
| No step-up for sensitive action | session theft risk | fresh WebAuthn assertion |
| Email recovery bypasses all security | weakest link | audited recovery flow |
| Global tenant RP confusion | auth failures/security bug | explicit RP config |

---

## 39. Senior Engineer Checklist

```text id="y13289"
Concept
[ ] Face ID/local biometric boundary understood
[ ] Passkey != server-side face recognition
[ ] WebAuthn ceremonies understood
[ ] RP/client/authenticator terminology clear

Registration
[ ] challenge random/short-lived/one-time
[ ] rpId configured
[ ] origin configured
[ ] public key stored
[ ] attestation policy decided
[ ] audit event recorded

Authentication
[ ] challenge verified
[ ] origin verified
[ ] RP ID verified
[ ] signature verified
[ ] user presence checked
[ ] user verification required where needed
[ ] sign count handled
[ ] session created after success

Data
[ ] credential table exists
[ ] challenge table exists
[ ] audit table exists
[ ] credential revoke supported
[ ] credential nickname/device metadata supported

Security
[ ] no biometric data sent to server
[ ] no custom faceOK boolean
[ ] no raw WebAuthn parsing without library
[ ] HTTPS/origin strict
[ ] rate limits
[ ] recovery flow protected
[ ] step-up for sensitive actions

Ops
[ ] registration failure metrics
[ ] authentication failure metrics
[ ] origin mismatch alerts
[ ] revoked credential attempts monitored
[ ] recovery usage monitored
```

---

## 40. Minimal Implementation untuk Tahap Awal

Implementasi awal yang realistis:

```text id="1d0tl2"
1. Choose WebAuthn Java server library
2. Configure RP ID and allowed origins
3. Create webauthn_credential table
4. Create webauthn_challenge table
5. Create registration options endpoint
6. Create registration finish endpoint
7. Create authentication options endpoint
8. Create authentication finish endpoint
9. Require userVerification=required
10. Create session after successful assertion
11. Add credential list/revoke UI
12. Add audit events and rate limits
```

Start dengan:

```text id="wz31uz"
password + passkey as additional sign-in method
```

Lalu evolve ke:

```text id="1fd951"
passwordless primary login
+ recovery flow
+ step-up auth
+ admin/high-assurance policy
```

---

## 41. Roadmap Singkat Setelah Seri 17

Urutan berikutnya:

```text id="l380hl"
Seri 18 → Biometric Privacy, Consent, Audit, Retention, and Compliance Guardrails
Seri 19 → Provider Abstraction: AWS/Azure/Google/on-prem engine switching
Seri 20 → Production Hardening: observability, cost control, queue lag, GPU/Kubernetes, failure handling
```

Kesimpulan utama: **Face ID/passkey/WebAuthn adalah authentication berbasis public key dengan biometric lokal sebagai user verification. Ini bukan server-side face recognition.** Untuk login, gunakan WebAuthn/passkey. Untuk KYC/selfie-ID matching, gunakan face verification dengan consent, threshold, liveness, retention, dan audit yang jauh lebih ketat.

**Seri 17/20 selesai.**
